paḷikkaṟai maṇṭapa muṇṭata ṉuḷḷatu
65		tūniṟa māmaṇic cuṭaroḷi virinta
tāmaraip pīṭikai tāṉuṇ ṭāṅkiṭiṉ
arumpaviḻ ceyyum alarntaṉa vāṭā
curumpiṉa mūcā tolyāṇṭu kaḻiyiṉum
maṟantēṉ ataṉtiṟam mātavi kēḷāy
70		kaṭampūṇ ṭōrteyvaṅ karuttiṭai vaittōr
āṅkava raṭikkiṭiṉ avaraṭi tāṉuṟum
nīṅkā tiyāṅkaṇum niṉaippila rāyiṭiṉ
īṅkitaṉ kāraṇam eṉṉai yeṉṟiyēl
cintai yiṉṟiyuñ ceyviṉai yuṟumeṉum
75		ventiṟal nōṉpikaḷ viḻumaṅ koḷḷavum
ceyviṉai cintai yiṉṟeṉiṉ yāvatum
eytā teṉpōrk kētu vākavum
payaṅkeḻu māmala riṭṭuk kāṭṭa
mayaṉpaṇ ṭiḻaitta marapiṉa tatutāṉ
80		avvaṉa malla taṇiyiḻai niṉmakaḷ
cevvaṉañ celluñ cemmai tāṉilaḷ
maṇimē kalaiyoṭu māmalar koyya
aṇiyiḻai nallāy yāṉum pōvaleṉ
ṟaṇippūṅ kompar avaḷoṭuṅ kūṭi
85		maṇittēr vītiyiṟ cutamati celvuḻīic
cimilik karaṇṭaiyaṉ nuḻaikōṟ pirampiṉaṉ
tavalaruñ ciṟappiṉ arāntā ṇattuḷōṉ
nāṇamum uṭaiyum naṉkaṉam nīttuk
kāṇā uyirkkuṅ kaiyaṟ ṟēṅki
90		uṇṇā nōṉpō ṭuyavavi yāṉaiyiṉ
maṇṇā mēṉiyaṉ varuvōṉ ṟaṉṉai 
vantī raṭikaṇum malaraṭi toḻutēṉ
entam aṭikaḷ emmurai kēṇmō
aḻukkuṭai yākkaiyiṟ pukunta nummuyir
95		puḻukkaṟaip paṭṭōr pōṉṟuḷam varuntā
timmaiyum maṟumaiyum iṟutiyi liṉpamum
taṉvayiṟ ṟarūumeṉ talaimaka ṉuraittatu
kolaiyu muṇṭō koḻumaṭaṟ ṟeṅkiṉ
viḷaipūn tēṟaliṉ meyttavat tīrē
100		uṇṭu teḷintiv yōkat tuṟupayaṉ
kaṇṭā lemmaiyuṅ kaiyutirk koṇmeṉa
uṇṇā nōṉpi taṉṉoṭuñ cūḷuṟ
ṟuṇmeṉa irakkumōr kaḷimakaṉ piṉṉarum
kaṇaviri mālai kaṭṭiya tiraṇaiyaṉ
105		kuvimuki ḻerukkiṟ kōtta mālaiyaṉ
citavaṟ ṟuṇiyoṭu cēṇōṅku neṭuñciṉait
tatarvīḻ poṭittuk kaṭṭiya uṭaiyiṉaṉ
veṇpali cānta peymmuḻu tuṟī ip
paṇpil kiḷavi palaroṭum uraittāṅku
110		aḻūum viḻūum araṟṟuṅ kūum
toḻūum eḻūuñ cuḻalaluñ cuḻalum
ōṭalu mōṭum oruciṟai yotuṅki
nīṭalum nīṭum niḻaloṭu maṟalum
maiya luṟṟa makaṉpiṉ varuntik
115		kaiyaṟu tuṉpaṅ kaṇṭuniṟ kunarum
curiyaṟ ṟāṭi maruḷpaṭu pūṅkuḻal
pavaḷac cevvāyt tavaḷa vāḷnakai
oḷḷari neṭuṅkaṇ veḷḷiveṇ tōṭṭuk
karuṅkoṭip puruvattu maruṅkuvaḷai piṟainutal
120		kāntaḷañ ceṅkai ēntiḷa vaṉamulai
akaṉṟa alkul annuṇ maruṅkul
ikanta vaṭṭuṭai eḻutuvarik kōlattu
vāṇaṉ pērūr maṟukiṭait tōṉṟi
nīṇila maḷantōṉ makaṉmuṉ ṉāṭiya
125		pēṭik kōlattup pēṭukāṇ kunarum
vampa mākkaḷ kampalai mūtūrc
cuṭuma ṇōṅkiya neṭunilai maṉaitoṟum
maiyaṟu paṭivattu vāṉavar mutalā
evvakai uyirkaḷum uvamaṅ kāṭṭi
130		veṇcutai viḷakkattu vittaka riyaṟṟiya
kaṇkavar ōviyaṅ kaṇṭuniṟ kunarum
viḻavāṟṟup paṭutta kaḻiperu vītiyiṟ
poṉṉāṇ kōtta naṉmaṇik kōvai
aiyavi yappiya neyyaṇi mucci
135		mayirppuṟañ cuṟṟiya kayiṟkaṭai mukkāḻ
polampiṟaic ceṉṉi nalampeṟat tāḻac
cevvāyk kutalai meypeṟā maḻalai
cintupu ciṉṉīr aimpaṭai naṉaippa
aṟṟaṅ kāvāc cuṟṟuṭaip pūntukil
140		toṭuttamaṇik kōvai uṭuppoṭu tuyalvarat
taḷarnaṭai tāṅkāk kiḷarpūṭ putalvaraip
polantēr mīmicaip pukarmuka vēḻat
tilaṅkutoṭi nallār cilarniṉ ṟēṟṟi
ālamar celvaṉ makaṉviḻāk kālkōḷ
145		kāṇmi ṉōveṉak kaṇṭuniṟ kunarum
virāṭaṉ pērūr vicayaṉām pēṭiyaik
kāṇiya cūḻnta kampalai mākkaḷiṉ
maṇimē kalaitaṉai vantupuṟañ cuṟṟi
aṇiyamai tōṟṟat taruntavap paṭuttiya
150		tāyō koṭiyaḷ takavilaḷ īṅkivaḷ
māmalar koyya malarvaṉan tāṉpukiṉ
nalliḷa vaṉṉam nāṇā tāṅkuḷa
valluna kollō maṭantai taṉṉaṭai
māmayi lāṅkuḷa vantumuṉ niṟpaṉa
155		cāyalkaṟ paṉakolō taiyal taṉṉuṭaṉ
paiṅkiḷi tāmuḷa pāvaitaṉ kiḷavik
keñcala kollō icaiyuna valla
eṉ ṟivai colli yāvarum iṉaintukac
centaḷirc cēvaṭi nilamvaṭu vuṟāmal
160		kuravamum maravamuṅ kuruntuṅ koṉṟaiyum
tilakamum vakuḷamuṅ ceṅkāl veṭciyum
narantamum nākamum parantalar puṉṉaiyum
piṭavamun taḷavamum muṭamuṭ ṭāḻaiyum
kuṭacamum vetiramuṅ koḻuṅkā lacōkamum
165		ceruntiyum vēṅkaiyum peruñcaṇ pakamum
erimala rilavamum virimalar parappi
vittaka riyaṟṟiya viḷaṅkiya kaiviṉaic
cittirac ceykaip paṭāmpōrt tatuvē
oppat tōṉṟiya uvavaṉan taṉṉait
170		toḻutaṉaḷ kāṭṭiya cutamati taṉṉoṭu
malarkoyyap pukuntaṉaḷ maṇimē kalaiyeṉ.
urai  
1-4.		vayanta mālaikku mātavi uraitta - vayanta mālaiyiṉiṭam mātavi kūṟiya, uyaṅku nōy varuttattu uraimuṉ tōṉṟi - piṟarum varuntutaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya mikka tuṉpantarum moḻi yiṉiṭamāka utittu, māmalar nāṟṟampōl-ciṟanta malariṭattu nāṟṟampōl, maṇimēkalaikku ētu nikaḻcci etirntuḷatu ātaliṉ-maṇimēkalaikku ētu nikaḻcci etirppaṭṭatātalāl; 
uraitta urai yeṉka. ētu nikaḻcci-payaṉukku ētuvākiya viṉaiyiṉ nikaḻcci; viṉai payaṉkoṭuttaṟkut tōṉṟuvatu eṉṟa paṭi; 1"ētu nikaḻcci yīṅkiṉ ṟātaliṉ" 2"ētu nikaḻcci yāvum palavuḷa" eṉa innūluḷḷē piṉṉarum ippeyar payiṉṟu varukiṉṟatu. māmalar nāṟṟampōl eṉṟa uvamaiyāl nalviṉai payaṉaḷittaṟ ketirntuḷa teṉpatu peṟṟām. malariṉkaṇ nāṟṟam aṭaṅkiyiruntu piṉ veḷippaṭutal pōl kaṉmamum maṟaintiruntu payaṉaḷikkuṅ kālam vantuḻi veḷippaṭu meṉka.
 5-10.	tantaiyum tāyum tām naṉi uḻanta - taṉ tantai tāyarāṉa kōvalaṉum kaṇṇakiyum uṟṟu mika varuntiya, ventuyar iṭumpai ceviyakam vetuppa-koṭiya tuyariṉai viḷaikkum tuṉpa moḻikaḷ kātiṉuḷḷē cuṭa, kātal neñcam kalaṅkik kārikai - maṇimēkalai avarpāl aṉpu mikka uḷḷaṅ kalakka muṟṟu, mātar ceṅkaṇ vari vaṉappu aḻittu - aḻakiya civanta kaṇkaḷil mai eḻutiṉa aḻakiṉaic citaittu, pulampu nīr uruṭṭi-varuttattā luṇṭākiya kaṇṇīrai utirttu, poti aviḻ naṟumalar ilaṅku itaḻ mālaiyai iṭṭu nīrāṭṭa - kaṭṭa viḻntu viḷaṅkukiṉṟa itaḻkaḷaiyuṭaiya naṟiya malar mālaiyiṉ mītu vīḻtti ataṉai nīrāṭṭa; 
iṭumpai-mātavi kūṟiya tuṉpa moḻikaḷ, mātar-kātal; īṇṭu aḻaku. vari-maiyeḻutiya kīṟṟu; 1"varivaṉap puṟṟa" eṉpatu puṟam. pulampunīr-taṉittu vīḻkiṉṟa nīrt tuḷiyumām. nīrāṭṭa - naṉaikka eṉṟapaṭi.
11-17.	mātavi maṇimēkalai mukam nōkki-mātavi maṇimēkalaiyiṉ mukattiṉaip pārttu, tāmarai taṇmati cērntatupōlak kāmar ceṅkaiyil kaṇṇīr māṟṟi-centāmaraimalar kuḷircci poruntiya muḻumatiyaic cērntatupōlat taṉ viruppam poruntiya civantakaiyiṉāl maṇimēkalaiyiṉ kaṇṇiṉiṉ ṟoḻukum nīrait tuṭaittu, tūnīr mālai tūttakai iḻantatu - tūya nīrmaiyaiyuṭaiya naṟumalar mālai kaṇṇīrāl naṉaintu tūya taṉmaiyai iḻantatu ākaliṉ, nikarmalar nīyē koṇarvāy eṉṟalum-vēṟu mālai toṭuttaṟku oḷi poruntiya malarkaḷai nīyē ceṉṟu koṇṭuvaruka eṉa uraittalum, matumalark kuḻaliyoṭu māmalar toṭukkum-tēṉporuntiya malar kaḷai yaṇinta kūntalaiyuṭaiya maṇimēkalaiyuṭaṉ malar toṭuttuk koṇṭirukkum, cutamati kēṭṭut tuyaroṭum kūṟum - cutamati yeṉpavaḷ ataṉaik kēṭṭut tuyaruṭaṉ kūṟukiṉṟāḷ; 
mātavi nōkki, māṟṟi, koṇarvāy eṉṟalum, cutamati kēṭṭuk kūṟumeṉka.  pōla - pōlumpaṭi, tāmarai malar mātavi kaikkum, mati maṇimēkalai mukattiṟkum uvamai. kāmar - viruppam; uṭaiyāḷatu viruppam kaiyiṉmēl ēṟṟappaṭṭatu; aḻakumām. nikar-oḷi; 2"nīrvār nikar malar" 3“arumpaviḻ mullai  nikar malar" eṉpaṉa kāṇka. nīyē koṇarvāy eṉṟatu kaḻuvāy kūṟuvatu pōṉṟu avaḷatu tuyariṉai māṟṟuvatō rupāyaṅ karutiyām.
 18-25.	kuravarkku uṟṟa koṭun tuyar kēṭṭu-tāy tantaiyarkku nērnta koṭiya tuṉpattiṉaik kēṭṭu, taṇiyāt tuṉpam talaittalai eytum-āṟāt tuyariṉai mēṉmēl aṭaiyum, maṇimēkalai taṉ matimukam taṉṉuḷ-maṇimēkalaiyiṉatu matipōlum mukattiṉuḷ, aṇitikaḻ nīlattu āymalar ōṭṭiya-aḻaku viḷaṅkukiṉṟa melliya nīlamalarai veṉṟa, kaṭaimaṇi ukunīr kaṇṭaṉaṉ āyiṉ - kaṇṇiṉatu karumaṇiyiṉ kaṭaiyiṉiṉṟu cintukiṉṟa nīraik kaṇṭaṉaṉāyiṉ, paṭaiiṭṭu naṭuṅkum kāmaṉ-kāmaṉ taṉ paṭaiyiṉai eṟintu naṭuṅkuvaṉ, pāvaiyai āṭavar kaṇṭāl akaṟalum uṇṭō - pāvaiyaṉaiyāḷai āṭavar kāṇiṉ viṭṭu nīṅkutalum uṇṭō?, pēṭiyar aṉṟō peṟṟiyiṉ niṉṟiṭiṉ - aṅṅaṉam tam iyaṟkai tiriyāmal niṟparēl avartām pēṭiyar allarō; 
 āy-ārāynteṭutta eṉṟumām. 1"āviṉ kaṭaimaṇi yukunīr" eṉpatu īṇṭu aṟiyaṟpālatu. avaḷāṟ pakaivellak karutiyirunta kāmaṉ iṉi vellutalariteṉṟu paṭaiyai eṟintu naṭuṅkuvā ṉeṉka. uṇṭō, ō ; etirmaṟai. peṟṟi - iyalpu. peṟṟiyil niṟṟalāvatu - ivaḷai virumpātu niṟṟal. āṭavar eṉṟatu īṇṭu iṉpattuṟai niṟpāraik karutiṟṟu. pēṭiyar - peṇṇiṉpam tuykkun takutiyillātavar.
 26-27.	āṅṅaṉam aṉṟiyum - aḵtaṉṟiyum, aṇiyiḻai kēḷāy īṅku innakarattu yāṉ varuṅ kāraṇam - innakarattiṉ kaṇ yāṉ vanta kāraṇattai mātavi kēṭpāyāka;
āṅṅaṉam: cuṭṭu. īṅku: acai.
 28-32.	pārāvāram palvaḷam paḻuniya-kaṭal tarum pala vaḷaṅ kaḷum mutirnta, kārāḷar caṇpaiyil - kārāḷaruṭaiya caṇpai nakarattil, kaucikaṉ eṉpōṉ iru piṟappāḷaṉ oru makaḷ uḷḷēṉ-kaucikaṉ eṉṉum antaṇaṉukku orē makaḷāyuḷḷayāṉ, oru taṉi añcēṉ ōrā neñcamōṭu - taṉṉantaṉiyē celvataṉai añcāmal oṉṟaiyum ārāyāta uḷḷattōṭu, ārāmattiṭai alar koyvēṉtaṉai-upa vaṉattilē malar koyvēṉāyiṉēṉ; aṅṅaṉam koyta eṉṉai; 
pārāvārap palvaḷam eṉpatu melintu niṉṟatu. kārāḷar-ōr cātiyār; vēḷāḷarumām. caṇpai - oru nakaram; cīkāḻiyumām.
33-41. 	mārutavēkaṉ eṉpāṉ ōr viñcaiyaṉ - mārutavēkaṉ eṉṉum oru vittiyātaraṉ, tiru viḻai mūtūr tēvarkōṟku eṭutta - tirumakaḷum virumpum ik kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam intiraṉukkuc ceyyaluṟṟa, peruviḻāk kāṇum peṟṟiyiṉ varuvōṉ - periya viḻāviṉaik kāṇum niṉaivuṭaṉ varukiṉṟavaṉākiya, tāraṉ mālaiyaṉ tamaṉiyap pūṇiṉaṉ pārōr kāṇāp palar toḻu paṭimaiyaṉ - pūmālaiyai yuṭaiyavaṉum maṇimālaiyaiyuṭaiyavaṉum poṟpūṇiṉai yuṭaiyavaṉum pūmiyiluḷḷōr, kāṇalākāta palarun toḻum teyva vaṭiviṉai yuṭaiyavaṉumākiya avaṉ, eṭuttaṉaṉ eṟkoṇṭu eḻuntaṉaṉ vicumpil paṭuttaṉaṉ - eṉṉai eṭuttuk koṇṭu vicumpil eḻuntu akap paṭuttik koṇṭāṉ; āṅku avaṉ pāṉmaiyēṉ āyiṉēṉ - appoḻutu yāṉ avaṉuṭaiya iṉpattiṉ pakutiyai yuṭaiyēṉāyiṉēṉ; āṅkavaṉ īṅku eṉai akaṉṟu kaṇmāṟi nīṅkiṉaṉ taṉpati neṭṭiṭai āyiṉum-avviñcaiyaṉ taṉ ūr cēymaikkaṇ uḷḷatāṉālum viḻitta kaṇ imaikkum aḷavilē eṉṉai innakaril viṭṭu maṟaintu nīṅkiṉaṉ;
tāraṉ mālaiyaṉ pūṇiṉaṉ paṭimaiyaṉāy varuvōṉākiya viñcaiyaṉ eṉṟiyaikka. 1"tāraṉ ......paṭimaiyaṉ," eṉṉum ivviraṇṭaṭiyum cilappatikārattum vantuḷḷamai aṟika. kāṇāp paṭimaiyaṉ palartoḻu paṭimaiyaṉ eṉka. kaṇmāṟi - kaṇṇōṭṭamiṉṟi eṉṟumām.
 42-43.	maṇippūṅ kompar maṇimēkalaitāṉ - māṇikkap pūṅkompaṉaiya maṇimēkalai, taṉittu alarkoyyum takaimaiyaḷ allaḷ - taṉiyē ceṉṟu malar koyyum takuti vāyntava ḷallaḷ;
nīṅkiṉaṉ; makaḷirkku iṅṅaṉam iṭukkaṇ nikaḻvatu uṇṭākaliṉtaṉit talar koyyun takaimaiya ḷallaḷ eṉa viritturaikka.
 44-46.	paṉmalar aṭukkiya naṉmarap pantar - pala malarkaḷai niraitta nalla maraniḻalaiyuṭaiya, ilavantikaiyiṉ eyiṟpuṟam pōkiṉ - ilavantikaiyiṉ matiṟpuṟattē ceṉṟāl, ulaka maṉṉavaṉ uḻaiyōr āṅkuḷar - cōḻa maṉṉaṉatu maruṅkiluḷḷōr āṇṭuṟaivar ; 
marap pantar - maraṅkaḷākiya pantar eṉṟum, cōlai eṉṟumām. ilavantikai - iyantira vāvi; 1"niṟaikkuṟi ṉiṟaintu pōkkuṟiṟ pōkum, poṟippaṭai yamainta poṅkila vantikai," eṉpatu kāṇka, 2"ilavantikai-nīrāviyaic cūḻnta vayantac cōlai; aḵtu aracaṉum urimaiyumāṭum kāvaṟcōlai," eṉpar aṭiyārkku nallār, uḻaiyōr-urimai makaḷir; kāvalāḷarumām. uḻaiyōr āṅkuḷar eṉṟatu ataṉkaṭ pōkalākātu eṉṟapaṭiyām.
 47-52.	viṇṇavar kōmāṉ viḻākkoḷ nalnāḷ - intiraṉukku viḻācceyyum nalla nāṭkaḷil, maṇṇavar viḻaiyār vāṉavar allatu - tēvaraiyaṉṟi makkaḷ virumpār ātaliṉ, pāṭu vaṇṭu imirā-pāṭukiṉṟa vaṇṭukaḷ olikkātaṉavāy, paṉmaram yāvaiyum -āṇṭuḷḷa pala maraṅkaḷum, vāṭā māmalar mālaikaḷ tūkkaliṉ - perumai poruntiya vāṭāta pūmālaikaḷait toṅka viṭutaliṉāl, kaipey pācattup pūtam kākkum eṉṟu - kaiyiṉiṭattuk koṇṭa pācattiṉaiyuṭaiya pūtam kākkumeṉṟu, uyyāṉattiṭai uṇarntōr cellār - uyyāṉattiṉkaṇ aṟintōr cellamāṭṭārkaḷ;
vāṉavar viḻaitalaṉṟi maṇṇavar viḻaiyār eṉka. vāṉavar virumputalāl vaṇṭu imirāvāyiṉa; 3"curumpu mūcāc cuṭarppūṅkāntaḷ" eṉpatu kāṇka. vāṭāta malar mālaikaḷ tēvarkaḷāl nirumikkappaṭṭaṉa. mālaikaḷ tūkkaliṉ pūtaṅ kākkumeṉṟu karutuvārāvar. uyyāṉam - uttiyāṉam ; aracar viḷaiyāṭuñcōlai.
53-58. veṅkatir vemmaiyiṉ viriciṟai iḻanta-ñāyiṟṟiṉ katir veppattāl virinta ciṟakiṉai iḻanta, campāti irunta campāti vaṉamum-campāti eṉpavaṉ irunta campāti vaṉamum, tavā nīrk kāvirip pāvai taṉ tātai - aḻiyāta nīrpperuk kiṉaiyuṭaiya kāviri naṅkaiyiṉ tantaiyākiya, kavēraṉ āṅku irunta kavēra vaṉamum - kavēraṉ eṉapavaṉ irunta kavēra vaṉamum, mūppuṭai mutumaiya tākkaṇaṅku uṭaiya - mikka mutumaiyuṭai yaṉavākiya tīṇṭi varuttumpeṇ teyvaṅkaḷai uṭaiyaṉavātaliṉ, yāppuṭaittāka aṟintōr eytār - aṟivuṭaiyōr avaṟṟai uṟutiyuṭaiyavākak karuti avaṟṟiṉkaṭ cellār;
campāti - kaḻukaracaṉ ; caṭāyuviṉuṭaṉ piṟantavaṉ; ivaṉ vāṉilē paṟantu ñāyiṟṟu maṇṭilattiṉ aṇimaiyiṟ ceṉṟa kālai ñāyiṟṟiṉ vemmaiyāṟ ciṟai tīyappeṟṟaṉaṉ eṉpar. campāti, caṭāyu eṉṉum iruvarum iṟaivaṉaip pūcitta iṭam tiruppuḷ ḷirukkuvēḷūr eṉṉum patiyākum; 1"taḷḷāya campāti caṭāyeṉ pār tāmiruvar, puḷḷāṉārk karaiyariṭam puḷḷirukku vēḷūrē," eṉpatu kāṇka. pukār nakaram appatikku aṇittākaliṉ campāti āṇṭu vantiruttal iyalpēyākum. vēttu muṉiyākiya kavēraṉ eṉpavaṉ piramaṉaik kuṟittu aruntavam purintu avaṉaruḷāl viṭṭuṇu māyaiyaip putalviyākap peṟṟu mutti yeytiṉaṉeṉṟum, piṉpu  akkaṉṉi piramaṉ kaṭṭaḷaiyāl nati vaṭivuṟṟamaiyāl annati kāvēriyeṉap peyar peṟṟateṉṟum purāṇaṅ kūṟumeṉpar. yāppuṭaittāka-iḻukkillaṉavāka; yāppuṭaittāka aṇaṅ kuṭaiya eṉṟiyaittalumām.
 59-62.	aruḷum aṉpum āruyir ōmpum oru perum pūṭkaiyum oḻiyā nōṉpiṉ - karuṇaiyum nēyamum āruyirkaḷaip pātukākkum oppaṟṟa periya mēṟkōḷum nīṅkāta nōṉ piṉaiyuṭaiya, pakavaṉatu āṇaiyiṉ palmarampūkkum uvavaṉam eṉpatu oṉṟu uṇṭu - putta tēvaṉatu āṇaiyāl pala maraṅkaḷum iṭaiyaṟātu pūkkiṉṟa uvavaṉam eṉṉum peyaruḷḷa cōlai oṉṟuṇṭu; 
aruḷ - aṉpu kāraṇamākat tōṉṟum aḷi. aṉpu - aruṭku mutalāki maṉattil nikaḻum nēyam. pūṭkai - mēṟkōḷ. 
 62-66.	ataṉ uḷḷatu-accōlaiyiṉ uḷḷiṭattatākiya, viḷippu aṟai pōkātu meypuṟattu iṭūum paḷikkaṟai maṇṭapam uṇṭu - ōcai veḷiyē pōkāmal taṉṉaic cārntāruṭampai maṭṭum puṟattē tōṟṟuvikkum paḷikku maṇṭapam oṉṟu uṇṭu; ataṉ uḷḷatu - ammaṇṭapattiṉ uḷḷiṭattatākiya, tūniṟa māmaṇic cuṭaroḷi virinta tāmaraippīṭikai tāṉ uṇṭu- tūya niṟamuṭaiya māṇikkattiṉ mikka oḷiparanta patumapīṭam oṉṟuṇṭu;
ataṉ uḷḷatākiya maṇṭapam eṉṟum, ataṉ uḷḷatākiya pīṭikai eṉṟum oṭṭuka. aṟai pōtal-veḷiyē pōtal. taṉṉuḷ eytiṉavaratu ōcaiyai veḷippaṭuttātu, uruviṉai maṭṭum veḷip paṭuttuvateṉṟu paḷiṅku maṇṭapattiṉ iyalpu kūṟiyapaṭi. paḷikkaṟai maṇṭapam-paḷiṅkaṟaiyākiya maṇṭapam; paḷiṅkup pāṟaiyālākiya maṇṭapamumām. tāmaraip pīṭikai-puttaṉ pātapīṭam. "puttaṉ pātattai maṇipatmam eṉṟalum, ataṉai vaḻipaṭuvōr atupaṟṟi, ‘ōm maṇipatmē hum' eṉṟa mantirattai japittalum pautta camaya marapu," eṉpar.
66-72.	āṅku iṭiṉ arumpu aviḻ ceyyum-appīṭattil iṭṭāl arumpukaḷ malarā niṟkum, alarntaṉa vāṭā curumpiṉam mūcā tol yāṇṭu kaḻiyiṉum-alarnta malarkaḷ pala āṇṭukaḷ ceṉṟālum vāṭamāṭṭā, avaṟṟiṉ kaṇ vaṇṭiṉaṅkaḷum moykkā, maṟantēṉ ataṉ tiṟam mātavi kēḷāy - mātavi ataṉ iyalpu oṉṟiṉai naṉku maṟantēṉ ippoḻutu ataṉaik kēṭpāyāka, kaṭam pūṇṭu-kāṇikkai celuttalai mēṟkoṇṭu, ōr teyvam karuttiṭai vaittōr - oru teyvattai maṉattilē vaittu, āṅku avar aṭikku iṭiṉ avar aṭi tāṉ uṟum- appīṭattiṉkaṇ avaraṭiyiṉ poruṭṭu malarai iṭṭāl ammalar atteyvattiṉaṭiyaic ceṉṟu cērum; nīṅkātu yāṅkaṇum niṉaip pilarāy iṭiṉ - niṉaip poṉṟumiṉṟi iṭṭāl ammalar yāṇṭum cellātu avaṇ taṅkum;
tol yāṇṭu-pal yāṇṭu; kaḻinta āṇṭukaḷ toṉmaiyavātaliṉ 'tol yāṇṭu', eṉṟār - tolyāṇṭu kaḻiyiṉum vāṭā mūcā veṉka. maṟantēṉ eṉṟatu ulaka vaḻakkup paṟṟi. kaṭam - kaṭaṉ; kāṇikkai. vaittōr ; muṟṟeccam. malarai iṭiṉ eṉka.
 73-79. 	īṅku itaṉ kāraṇam eṉṉai eṉṟiyēl-itaṟkuriya kāraṇam yāteṉa viṉavutiyāyiṉ, cintai iṉṟiyum ceyviṉai uṟum eṉum-maṉattoṭu kūṭāta vaḻiyum ceytaviṉai piṉvantu payaṉṟarum eṉkiṉṟa, ventiṟal nōṉpikaḷ viḻumam koḷḷavum -valiyiṉaiyuṭaiya nōṉpikaḷ varuttaṅ koḷḷavum, ceyviṉai cintai iṉṟeṉil yāvatum eytātu eṉpōrkku ētuvākavum- maṉattoṭu kūṭāviṭiṉ ceyta viṉai ciṟitum vantu payaṉaḷiyātu eṉpōrkkuk karuviyākavum, payamkeḻu mā malar iṭṭuk kāṭṭa mayaṉ paṇṭu iḻaitta marapiṉatu atutāṉ - malarkaḷai iṭṭuk kāṭṭumāṟu mayaṉ eṉ pōṉāl muṉṉarc ceyyappaṭṭa payaṉ ciṟanta muṟaimaiyai yuṭaiyatākum attāmaraip pīṭam;
payaṅkeḻu marapiṉatu eṉak kūṭṭuka. maṉattoṭu kūṭāvaḻi viṉai payaṉṟarā teṉpataṉai, 1"iṉṉā veṉattā ṇuṇarntavai tuṉṉāmai vēṇṭum" 2"maṉattāṉām māṇā ceyyāmai talai" eṉṉun tirukkuṟaḷkaḷāṉum, avaṟṟiṟku muṟaiyē, "aṟamum pāvamum uḷavāvatu maṉamuḷaṉāya vaḻiyākalāṉ "uṇarntavai eṉṟār" eṉavum, "īṇṭu maṉattā ṉākāta vaḻip pāvamillai yeṉpatu peṟṟām" eṉavum parimēlaḻakar kūṟiya uraiyāṉum aṟika.
80-85	avvaṉam allatu-accōlaiyiṉkaṇallatu, aṇiyiḻai - mātaviyē, niṉmakaḷ cevvaṉam cellum cemmaitāṉ ilaḷ- niṉmakaḷ vēṟākac cellum takutiyillātavaḷ; maṇimēkalai yoṭu māmalar koyya - maṇimēkalaiyuṭaṉ malar koyyumāṟu, aṇiyiḻai nallāy yāṉum pōval eṉṟu-aḻakiya aṇikalaṉaiyuṭaiya mātavi, yāṉum celvēṉ eṉṟu, aṇippūṅ kompar avaḷoṭum kūṭi - aḻakiya pūṅkompaṉaiya maṇimēkalaiyuṭaṉ cērntu, maṇittēr vītiyil cutamati celvuḻīi - maṇikaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya tērkaḷ cellum vītiyiṉkaṇ cutamati cellumpoḻutu;
cevvaṉam - vēṟāka; 3"tirumaka ḷirukkai cevvaṉaṅ kaḻintu" eṉpuḻi, cevvaṉam kaḻintu eṉapataṟku, vēṟākak kaḻintu eṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār poruḷuraittuḷḷamai kāṇka; nērāka eṉṟumām. cemmai - īṇṭut takuti.
86-103. cimilik karaṇṭaiyaṉ-uṟiyilē vaitta kuṇṭikaiyai yuṭaiyavaṉum, nuḻaikōl pirampiṉaṉ-nuṇṇitāyt tiraṇṭa pirampiṉai yuṭaiyavaṉum, tavalarum ciṟappiṉ arāntāṇattu uḷōṉ - kēṭillāta mēṉmaiyaiyuṭaiya arukaṉ kōṭṭattē yuḷḷavaṉum, nāṇamum uṭaiyum naṉkaṉam nīttu - nāṇattaiyum āṭaiyaiyum aṟavē akaṟṟi, kāṇā uyirkkum kaiyaṟṟu ēṅki- kaṇkaḷāṟ kāṇa viyalāta ciṟṟuyirkkum naṭattalāti taṉ ṟoḻilkaḷāl tuṉpamuṇṭāmō eṉac ceyalaṟṟu ēṅki, uṇṇā nōṉ pōṭu - uṇṇā viratattuṭaṉ, uyaval yāṉaiyiṉ maṇṇā mēṉiyaṉ varuvōṉ taṉṉai- varuntu talaiyuṭaiya yāṉaiyaip pōla varuvōṉumākiya kaḻuvāta uṭalai yuṭaiyavaṉai, vantīr aṭikaḷ num malaraṭi toḻutēṉ - vārum aṭikēḷ nummuṭaiya malarpōlum tiruvaṭikaḷai vaṇaṅkiṉēṉ, entam aṭikaḷ emurai kēṇmō- emmuṭaiya perumāṉē aṭiyēṉ moḻiyaik kēḷum, aḻukkuṭai yākkaiyil pukunta num uyir - aḻukkuc ceṟinta uṭaliṉkaṭ pukunta nummuṭaiya uyirāṉatu, puḻukkaṟaip paṭṭōr pōṉṟu uḷam varuntātu - puḻukkat tiṉait tarum aṟaiyil akappaṭṭōraippōla uḷḷam varuntā vaṇṇam, immaiyum maṟumaiyum iṟutiil iṉpamum taṉ vayiṉ tarūum eṉ talaimakaṉ uraittatu-em talaivaruraittatākiya immaiyiṉpattiṉaiyum maṟumai yiṉpattiṉaiyum muṭivillāta mutti yiṉpattiṉaiyum taṉṉiṭattiruntu tarukiṉṟatāṉa, kolaiyum uṇṭō koḻumaṭal teṅkiṉ viḷaipūn tēṟaliṉ - koḻuviya maṭalkaḷaiyuṭaiya teṉṉaiyiṉ kaṇ viḷaikiṉṟa iṉiya matuvil kolaiyeṉpatum uṇṭō?, meyt tavattīrē - uṇmait tavamuṭaiya aṭikaḷē, uṇṭu teḷintu ivyōkattu uṟupayaṉ kaṇṭāl - itaṉai uṇṭu teḷivu peṟṟu it tavaneṟiyil itaṉiṉ mikka payaṉaik kaṇṭāl, emmaiyum kaiyutirk koṇmeṉa - tēṟalaiyaṉṟi emmaiyum akaṟṟiviṭum eṉṟu kūṟi, uṇṇā nōṉpi taṉṉoṭum cūḷuṟṟu - uṇṇā nōṉpiyākiya caiṉa muṉivaruṭaṉ capataṅ kūṟi, uṇm eṉ irakkum ōr kaḷimakaṉ piṉṉarum - uṇṇum eṉṟu irakkiṉṟa oru kaṭkuṭiyaṉ piṉ niṟpōrum;
cimili-uṟi. karaṇṭai - karaṇṭakam; kuṇṭikai; 1"kalpoḷin taṉṉa viṭṭuvāyk karaṇṭaip palpuric cimili nāṟṟi" eṉpatu kāṇka. arāntāṇam - arukattāṉam. vantīraṭikaḷ, entamaṭikaḷ, meyttavattīr eṉak kaḷimakaṉ palakāṟ kūṟuvatu ikaḻcci. maṇṇā mēṉiyaṉ ākaliṉ aḻukkuṭai yākkai eṉṟāṉ. talaimakaṉ - ācāṉ. kolaiyumuṇṭō eṉṟatu kolai yoṉṟumē kaṭiyaṟpālateṉpataṉai uṭaṉpaṭṭapaṭi. teṅkiṉ viḷaipūn tēṟal eṉṟatu tēṟaliṉ tūymai kūṟiyapaṭiyām. iv yōkattu - nummuṭaiya tavattil. itaṉiṉum uṟupayaṉ eṉa virikka. iṉi, yōkam - tēṟaliṉ cērkkai yeṉṟumām : itaṟku, kaṇṭāl akaṟṟum eṉṟatu kaṇṭapiṉ akaṟṟakillīr eṉṉum kuṟippiṟṟu. kaiyutir koḷḷutal-kaiyai.acaittu vilakkutal; cīlamillāraik kāṇiṉ avaruṭaṉ pēcātu avaraip pōmpaṭi kaiyacaittuk kuṟippittal caiṉa muṉivarkku iyalpu. uṇṇā nōṉpi - iraṇṭuvāvum aṭṭamiyum paṭṭiṉi viṭṭuṇṇum virati; 1"paṭṭiṉi nōṉpikaḷ' eṉpataṉuraiyum, 2"ōvā tiraṇṭuvavu maṭṭamiyum paṭṭiṉiviṭṭoḻukkaṅ kāttal, tāvāttava meṉṟār" eṉpatum kāṇka. karaṇṭaiyaṉum pirampiṉṉum ākiya ārāntāṇattuḷḷōṉ nōṉpōṭu mēṉiyaṉāy nīttu ēṅki varupavaṉai eṉṟiyaittulumām. taruvatum uraittatumākiya tēṟalil kolaiyumuṇṭō eṉak kūṭṭuka. tarūum talaimakaṉ eṉalumām. koḷḷum, uṇṇum eṉṉum ēval muṟṟukkaḷil īṟṟumicai yukaram meyyoṭuṅ keṭṭatu. peruṅ kataiyiṟ kaḷimakaṉ iyalpākak kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷa, 3"tuṟakkam kūṭiṉun tuṟantiva ṇīṅkum, piṟappō vēṇṭēṉ yāṉeṉak kūṟi, ārtta yāya ṉūrkkaḷi mūrkkaṉ, cevvaḻik kītañ citaiyap pāṭi, avvaḻi varumō rantaṇāḷaṉaic, cella lāṇai nilliva ṇīyeṉa, eytac ceṉṟu vaitavaṇ vilakki, vaḻuttiṉē muṇṇumiv vaṭinaṟun tēṟalaip, paḻittuk kūṟu niṉ pārppaṉak kaṇamatu, col
lāyāyiṟ pulluveṉ yāṉeṉak, kaiyalait tōṭumōr kaḷimakaṟ kāṇmiṉ" eṉpatu īṇṭu aṟiyaṟpālatu.
104-115.	kaṇaviri mālai kaṭṭiya tiraṇaiyaṉ-alarippūvāl tiraṇaiyākak kaṭṭappaṭṭa mālaiyai yuṭaiyaṉāy, kuvimukiḻ erukkil kōtta mālaiyaṉ-erukkiṉatu kuvinta mukaikaḷāṟ kōkkap peṟṟa tāriṉai yuṭaiyaṉāy, citaval tuṇiyoṭu cēṇōṅku neṭuñciṉait tatarvīḻpu oṭittuk kaṭṭiya uṭaiyiṉaṉ-citariṉa tuṇiyōṭu vāṉilē uyarnta periya marak kiḷaikaḷiluḷḷa ceṟinta cuḷḷikaḷai oṭittuk kaṭṭiya uṭaiyai yuṭaiyaṉāy, veṇpali cāntam meymuḻutu uṟīi-veṇṇīṟuñ cantaṉamum uṭal muḻutum pūcik koṇṭu, paṇpil kiḷavi palaroṭum uraittāṅku - payaṉillāta coṟkaḷaip palaroṭuṅ kūṟik koṇṭu, aḻūum viḻūum araṟṟum kūum-aḻutum viḻuntum araṟṟiyum kūviyum, toḻūum eḻūum cuḻalalum cuḻalum - toḻutum eḻuntum cuḻalutalaic ceytum,. ōṭalum ōṭum - ōṭiyum, oruciṟai otuṅki nīṭalum nīṭum-oru pakkamāka otuṅki neṭitu niṉṟum, niḻaloṭu maṟalum-niḻaluṭaṉ pakaimai koṇṭum niṟkiṉṟa, maiyaluṟṟa makaṉpiṉ varuntik kaiyaṟu tuṉpam kaṇṭu niṟkunarum- pittuṟṟavaṉatu ceyalaṟṟa tuṉpattiṉaik kaṇṭu varunti avaṉ piṉṉē niṟpōrum; 
kaṭṭiya tiraḷ puyaṉ eṉpatum, mey muḻuturīi eṉpatum pāṭam. urīi-uruvi; pūci. tatar-kottumām; 1"citarnaṉai murukkiṉ cēṇōṅku neṭuñciṉait tatar" eṉpatum kāṇka. veṇpali-veṇ ṇīṟu; cāmpal. veṇpaliyākiya cāntameṉak koṇṭu valloṟṟu vikārattāṟ ṟokka teṉiṉumām. cuḻalalum cuḻalum-cuḻalutalaiyum ceyyum; piṉvaruvaṉa vaṟṟiṟkum iṅṅaṉam poruḷ koḷka; 2"iyaṅkalu miyaṅku mayaṅkalu mayaṅkum" eṉpatūum atu. aḻūum mutaliyavaṟṟai viṉaiyeccappaṭuttu, niṟkiṉṟa eṉa oru col virittu muṭikka; iṉi, maiyaluṟṟa makaṉ eṉpataṉai eḻuvāyākki, aḻūum mutaliya viṉaikaḷōṭu taṉittaṉi muṭittu, avaṉ eṉa oru col varuvitturaittalumām.
116-125.	curiyal tāṭi maruḷpaṭu pūṅkuḻal - curuṇṭa tāṭiyum iruṇṭa aḻakiya kūntalum, pavaḷac cevvāy tavaḷa vāḷnakai-pavaḷam pōṉṟa civanta vāyum veṇmai mikka oḷḷiya paṟkaḷum, oḷ ari neṭuṅkaṇ-oḷiporuntiya aripaṭarnta periya kaṇkaḷum, veḷḷiveṇ tōṭṭu-veḷḷiya caṅkiṉāṟ ceyta kātaṇiyum, karuṅkoṭip puruvattu maruṅkuvaḷai piṟai nutal - kariyatāy vaḷainta puruvattiṉ pakkalil vaḷaintuḷḷa piṟai pōṉṟa neṟṟiyum, kāntaḷ am ceṅkai - kāntaḷ malar pōluñ civantakaiyum, ēntu iḷa vaṉamulai - ēntiya aḻakiya iḷaṅ koṅkaikaḷum, akaṉṟa alkul am nuṇ maruṅkul - periya alkulum aḻakiya nuṇṇiya iṭaiyum, ikanta vaṭṭuṭai - muḻantāḷaḷavāka uṭukkappaṭum uṭaiyum, eḻutuvarik kōlattu - tōḷ mutaliyavaṟṟil eḻutappaṭṭa pattik kīṟṟum uṭaiya, vāṇaṉ pērūr maṟukiṭait tōṉṟi-vāṇaṉ eṉṉum acuraṉatu periya cōnakarattiṉ vītiyil niṉṟu, nīṇilam aḷantōṉ makaṉmuṉ āṭiya - nilamaḷanta neṭumāliṉ makaṉākiya kāmaṉ muṉṉar āṭiya, pēṭik kōlattup pēṭu kāṇkunarum- pēṭik kōlattaiyuṭaiya pēṭu eṉṉuṅ kūttiṉaik kāṇpōrum:
curiyal - curuḷal; tāṭi - mōvāy mayir. maruḷ-iruḷ. koṭi oḻuṅkumām. puruvamum iraṇṭu pakkattum vaḷaintu nutalum eṉṟuraittalum poruntum. ikanta-tāḷ muḻuvatum illāta. tāṭi mutaliyavaṟṟaiyuṭaiya kōlam eṉavum, āṭiya pēṭik kōlam eṉavum oṭṭuka; aṉṟi, tāṭi mutaliya vaṟṟaiyuṭaiya pēṭik kōlattuṭaṉ āṭiya pēṭu eṉṟumām. muṉ āṭiya pēṭu ippoḻutu naṭikkappaṭuva taṉai yeṉka. pēṭu-patiṉōrāṭaluḷ oṉṟu;ap patiṉoṉṟaiyum, avaṟṟai nikaḻttiyavarkaḷaiyum.
"kaṭaiyamayi rāṇimarak kālvintai kantaṉ, kuṭaituṭimāl alliyamaṟ kumpam - cuṭar viḻiyāṟ, paṭṭamataṉ pēṭutirup pāvaiyaraṉ pāṇṭaraṅkam, koṭṭiyivai kāṇpatiṉōr kūttu" eṉṉum veṇpāvāṉaṟika. pēṭu - uḻai kāraṇamāka vāṇaṉāṟ ciṟaivaikkappaṭṭa taṉ makaṉ aniruttaṉaic ciṟaimīṭṭuk kāmaṉ cō nakarat tāṭiya āṭal; uḻai-vāṇaṉ makaḷ. 1"āṇmai tirinta peṇmaik kōlattuk, kāma ṉāṭiya pēṭi yāṭalum" 2"curuḷiṭu tāṭi maruḷpaṭu pūṅkuḻal, ariparan toḻukiya ceḻuṅkaya ṉeṭuṅkaṇ, viriveṇṭōṭṭu veṇṇakait tuvarvāyc, cūṭaka varivaḷai yāṭamaip paṉaittōḷ, vaḷariḷa vaṉamulait taḷariyaṉ miṉṉiṭaip, pāṭakac cīṟaṭi yāriyap pēṭi" eṉpaṉa aṟiyaṟpālaṉa.
 126-131.	vampamākkaḷ kampalai mūtūr-putiyarāy varum makkaḷiṉ muḻakkam mikunta toṉmaiyuṭaiya pukār nakaril, cuṭumaṇ ōṅkiya neṭunilai maṉaitoṟum-ceṅkalāṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭu uyarnta periya māṭaṅkaḷtōṟum, maiyaṟu paṭivattu vāṉavar mutalā evvakai uyirkaḷum uvamam kāṭṭi- kuṟṟamaṟṟa teyva vaṭiviṉaiyuṭaiya viṇṇavar mutalāka evvakaippaṭṭa uyirkaḷaiyum oppumai kāṭṭi, veṇ cutai viḷakkattu vittakar iyaṟṟiya - viḷakkattaiyuṭaiya veḷḷiya cutaiyiṉāl kaitērnta ōviyakkārar ceyta, kaṇkavar ōviyam kaṇṭu niṟkunarum - kaṇkaḷaik kavarum vaṉappuṭaiya ōviyaṅkaḷaik kaṇṭu niṟpōrum ;
cuṭumaṇ-ceṅkal; 3"cuṭuma ṇōṅkiya neṭunakar varaippu" eṉpatu perumpāṇ. paṭivam - teyva vaṭivam. uvamam-oppumai; 4"evvakaic ceytiyu muvamaṅ kāṭṭi, nuṇṇiti ṉuṇarnta nuḻainta nōkkiṟ, kaṇṇuḷ viṉaiñarum," 1"mayaṉeṉak koppā vakutta pāvaiyiṉ, nīṅkē ṉiyāṉ" eṉpataṉa kāṇka. vittakar-ciṟpācāriyar; 2"vittaka riyaṟṟiya" eṉpar piṉṉum.
132-145.	viḻavu āṟṟuppaṭutta kaḻiperu vītiyil - viḻaviṉaik koṇṭāṭiya mikapperiya vītiyil, poṉ nāṇ kōtta naṉ maṇik kōvai-poṉṉālākiya kayiṟṟiṟ kōtta nalla maṇik kōvaiyākiya, aiyavi appiya ney aṇi mucci-veṇ ciṟu kaṭukiṉai appiya ney yaṇinta ucciyil, mayirppuṟam cuṟṟiya kayiṟkaṭai mukkāḻ-mayirppuṟattiṟ cuṟṟappaṭṭa kokkiyiṉaiyuṭaiya mūṉṟu caraṅkaḷ, polam piṟaic ceṉṉi nalampeṟat tāḻa - aḻakiya piṟai pōṉṟa aṇiyiṉai yaṇinta ceṉṉiyil aḻaku peṟat toṅkavum, cevvāyk kutalai meypeṟā maḻalai - vaṭivutiruntāta maḻalaiyākiya iḷañ collai yuṭaiya civanta vāyiṉiṉṟum, cintupu ciṉṉīr aimpaṭai naṉaippa-ciṟitākiya nīrcinti mārpilaṇinta aimpaṭait tāliyai naṉaikkavum, aṟṟam kāvāccuṟṟuṭaip pūntukil - aṟṟattiṉai maṟaik kāmaṟ cuṟṟappaṭṭa aḻakiya āṭai, toṭutta maṇikkōvai uṭuppoṭu tuyalvara - toṭukkappaṭṭa maṇikaḷiṉ kōvaiyākiya uṭuppoṭu acaiyavum, taḷarnaṭai tāṅkāk kiḷarpūṇ putalvarai-taḷarnta naṭaiyaiyum poṟukkalāta oḷi poruntiya aṇikalaṉ aṇinta putalvarai, polantēr mīmicaip pukarmuka vēḻattu- poṟṟēriṉ mītuḷḷa puḷḷi poruntiya mukattiṉai yuṭaiya yāṉaiyiṉ mēl, ilaṅkutoṭi nallār cilar niṉṟu ēṟṟi-viḷaṅkukiṉṟa vaḷaiyalkaḷai yaṇinta makaḷir cilar niṉṟu ēṟṟi, āl amar celvaṉ makaṉ viḻāk kālkōḷ kāṇmiṉō eṉ-āliṉ kīḻamarnta civaperumāṉ tirumaintaṉākiya murukavēḷiṉ viḻāk kāl koḷḷutalaik kāṇpīrāka eṉṟu kūṟa, kaṇṭu niṟkunarum-ataṉaik kaṇṭu niṟpōrum;
kōvaiyākiya mukkāḻ eṉka. pēy tīṇṭāvaṇṇam kuḻantaikaḷiṉ muṭiyil neyyaṇintu veṇciṟu kaṭukai apputal marapu; ataṉālē veṇciṟu kaṭukiṟkuk kaṭippakai eṉpatum peyarāyiṟṟu; kaṭi-pēy. kayiṟkaṭai-kokki ; kokkiṉ vāypōlvatākaliṉ kokki eṉappaṭṭatu. piṟaic ceṉṉi-piṟaivaṭuvākiya aṇiyiṉ iru kōṭukaḷil eṉṟuraip pārumuḷar. mey - colliṉ vaṭivu, kutalai- iḷañcol; 1"kutalaic cevvāyk kuṟuntoṭi makaḷir, mutiyōr moḻiyiṉ muṉṟi ṉiṉṟaḻa" eṉpatu kāṇka. ciṟitāya nīraic ciṉṉīr eṉṟal ōr ilakkaṇai vaḻakku. aimpaṭai - kāttaṟ kaṭavuḷāṉa tirumāliṉ caṅku, cakkaram, katai, vāḷ, vil eṉṉum aintu paṭaikaḷiṉ vaṭivākiya aṇi; itu tāli eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum; itaṉaic ciṟuvarkku aṇital marapu; 2"tāli kaḷain taṉṟu milaṉē pālviṭ, ṭayiṉiyu miṉṟayiṉṟaṉaṉē" eṉpatu kāṇka. tērmicai yāṉaiyai vaittu ūrtal 3"poṟciṟu tēr micaip paimpoṟ pōtakam, naṟciṟā rūrtaliṉ" eṉpataṉāṉum aṟiyappaṭum kālkōḷ- toṭakkam. vītiyil nallār cilar, kōvaiyaiyum mucciyaiyum kutalaiyaiyum maḻalaiyaiyum pūṇaiyumuṭaiya putalvarai vēḻattu mukkāḻ tāḻac ciṉṉīr naṉaippat tukil tuyalvara ēṟṟi eṉak kūṭṭi yuraippārumuḷar
 146-150.	virāṭaṉ pērūr vicayaṉām pēṭiyaik kāṇiya cūḻnta kampalai mākkaḷiṉ-virāṭaṉatu periya nakarattiṉkaṇ aruccuṉaṉākiya pēṭiyaik kāṇumāṟu cūḻnta muḻakkat tiṉaiyuṭaiya makkaḷaip pōla, maṇimēkalaitaṉai vantu puṟam cuṟṟi - vantu maṇimēkalaiyaic cuṟṟilum cūḻntu, aṇi amai tōṟṟattu aruntavap paṭuttiya tāyō koṭiyaḷ takavilaḷ - eḻilamainta nalluruvattiṉai ariya tavaneṟiyiṟ paṭuttiya tāyō koṭiyavaḷum takutiyillāta vaḷumāvaḷ;
pāṇṭavar aivarum pāñcāliyum virāṭaṉatu nakarattiṟ karantuṟaintakālai aruccuṉaṉ pēṭiyuruk koṇṭu pirukannaḷai eṉṉum peyaruṭaṉ āṇṭiruntamai pāratattāl aṟiyalāvatu. kampalai mākkaḷ - vēṟu cila kāṭci kaṇṭu muḻaṅkit tiriyumavar. aṇi amai tōṟṟattu - aṇikaḷ illāta tōṟṟattuṭaṉ eṉṟumām. cīvaka cintāmaṇiyil 4"uppamai kāmattuppiṉ" "nikaramainta muḻantāḷum" eṉṉumiṭaṅkaḷil ‘amai' eṉpataṟku ipporuḷ kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka.
150-158. 	īṅkivaḷ mā malar koyya malarvaṉam tāṉ pukil - im maṇimēkalai ciṟanta malar koytaṟkup pūmpoḻilil nuḻaintāl, nal iḷa aṉṉam nāṇātu āṅkuḷa valluna kollō maṭantai taṉ naṭai-aṅkēyuḷḷa nalla iḷaiya aṉṉap paṟavaikaḷ nāṇāmal ivaḷ naṭaipōl naṭakka vallaṉavō, māmayil āṅkuḷa vantu muṉniṟpaṉa cāyal kaṟpaṉa kolō taiyal taṉṉuṭaṉ - āṇṭuḷḷa aḻakiya mayilkaḷ innaṅkaiyiṉ etirē vantu niṟpaṉavāki ivaḷuṭaṉ cāyalaik kaṟpaṉavākumō, paiṅkiḷi tām uḷa pāvaitaṉ kiḷavikku eñcala kollō - aṅkirukkum paciya kiḷikaḷ immaṅkaiyiṉ moḻikaṭkut tōṟpaṉa vallavō, icaiyuna alla - ivai yāvum oppaṉavalla, eṉṟu ivai colli yāvarum iṉaintu uka-eṉṟu ivaikaḷaik kūṟi aṉaivarum varuntik keṭa,
āṅkuḷa aṉṉam nāṇātu valluna kollō eṉavum, āṅkuḷa māmayil vantu muṉṉiṟpaṉavāyk kaṟpaṉakolō eṉavum iyaiyum. mayilkaḷ kaṟṟaṟkuttāṉum muṉvantu niṟkamāṭṭā eṉṟapaṭi. cāyal - meṉmai. eñcutal - kuṟaital; tōṟṟal.
159. 		centaḷirc cēvaṭinilam vaṭu uṟāmal - civanta taḷir pōlum civanta aṭikaḷāl nilattilē vaṭuvuṇṭākāmaṟ ceṉṟu; 
vaṭu - cuvaṭu aṭiyum nilamum vaṭuvuṟāmal eṉṟumām. ceṉṟu eṉa oru col varuvikka.
160-171. 	kuravamum maravamum kuruntum koṉṟaiyum - kurāvum veṇkaṭampum kuruntum koṉṟaiyum, tilakamum vakuḷamum ceṅkāl veṭciyum - mañcāṭiyum makiḻum civanta kālaiyuṭaiya veṭciyum, narantamum nākamum parantu alar puṉṉaiyum - nārattaiyum curapuṉṉaiyum parantu malarkiṉṟa puṉṉaiyum, piṭavamum taḷavamum muṭamuḷ tāḻaiyum-piṭavamum cemmullaiyum vaḷainta muḷḷaiyuṭaiya tāḻaiyum, kuṭacamum vetiramum koḻuṅkāl acōkamum- veṭpālaiyum mūṅkilum parutta aṭiyiṉaiyuṭaiya acōkamum, ceruntiyum vēṅkaiyum peruñ caṇpakamum- ceruntiyum vēṅkaiyum periya caṇpakamum, erimalar ilavamum virimalar parappi-neruppaippōṟ civanta pūkkaḷaiyuṭaiya ilavamum ākiyavai virinta malarkaḷaip parappa, vittakar iyaṟṟiya viḷaṅkiya kaiviṉai - kait toḻilil mēmpaṭṭa vittakarāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭa, cittirac ceykaip paṭām pōrttatuvē oppat tōṉṟiya-cittirat toḻilamainta tukir pōrttatupōlat tōṉṟiya, uvavaṉam taṉṉai - uvavaṉattai, toḻutaṉaḷ kāṭṭiya cutamati taṉṉoṭu - toḻutu kāṭṭiya cutamatiyuṭaṉ, malar koyyap pukuntaṉaḷ maṇimēkalai - maṇimēkalai malar koytaṟku aṭaintaṉaḷ eṉka,.
piṭavam-kuṭṭip piṭavam eṉṉum koṭi; 1"putaṉmicait taḷaviṉitaṉmuṭ cennaṉai, neruṅkukulaip piṭavamō ṭoruṅku piṇiyaviḻa" eṉpatu akam. parappi - parappa veṉka. puttaṉ pātapīṭikaiyuḷḷa tākaliṉ vaṉattait toḻutu kāṭṭiṉaḷ eṉka.
 mātavi yuraitta uraimuṉ ṟōṉṟi maṇimēkalaikku ētu nikaḻcci etirntuḷa tākaliṉ, akkārikai vetuppak kalaṅki uruṭṭi āṭṭa, mātavi nōkki māṟṟik koṇarvāy eṉṟalum, ataṉaik kēṭṭut tuyaroṭu kūṟum cutamati, 'kaṇṇīraik kaṇṭaṉaṉāyiṉ kāmaṉ naṭuṅkum; āṭavar akaṟalumuṇṭō; niṉṟiṭiṟ pēṭiya raṉṟō ; aṉṟiyum, yāṉ varuṅ kāraṇam kēḷāy; koyvēṉai viñcaiyaṉ eṭuttaṉaṉ, eḻuntaṉaṉ, paṭuttaṉaṉ; āyiṉēṉ; avaṉ akaṉṟu nīṅkiṉaṉ; ātalāl maṇimēkalai taṉmaiyaḷallaḷ; pōkiṉ, uḻaiyōr āṅkuḷar; cellār; eytār; uvavaṉameṉpa toṉṟuṇṭu; uṇṭu; uṇṭu; marapiṉatu atutāṉ; cemmai tāṉilaḷ; yāṉum pōval; eṉṟu kūṭic celvuḻi, piṉṉarum niṟkunarum kāṇku narum niṟkunarum niṟkunarum maṇimēkalaiyaic cuṟṟi iṉaintuka, maṇimēkalai cutamati taṉṉoṭu malar koyyap pukuntaṉaḷ eṉa muṭikka. 
malarvaṉam pukka kātai muṟṟiṟṟu. 
 4. paḷikkaṟai pukka kātai 
uvavaṉattilum, ataṉkaṇ uḷḷa poykaiyilum nikaḻum iyaṟkaik kāṭcikaḷaic cutamati kāṭṭa maṇimēkalai kāṇpāḷāyiṉāḷ. avaḷ āṅku avvāṟirukka, mataveṟiyāl vītitōṟum kalakkuṟuttut tirinta kālavēkameṉṉum yāṉaiyiṉ matattai yaṭakkit tērilēṟit tāṉai cūḻa varum utayakumaraṉ kaṇikaiyar teruvil oru māṭattil malaraṇai mēṟ kātaṟparattai yoruttiyuṭaṉ makara yāḻiṉ kōṭṭiṉait taḻuvik koṇṭu mayaṅkip pāvaipōl acaivaṟṟirunta eṭṭikumaraṉaic cāḷara vaḻiyāṟ kaṇṭu, ‘nī iṅṅaṉam mayaṅki
yirukkum kāraṇam yātu,' eṉṟu viṉaviṉaṉ. atu kēṭṭalum avaṉ virainteḻuntu arukaṇaintu toḻutu, ‘vāṭiya aḻakuṭaṉ ivvīti vaḻiyē malarvaṉattiṟkuc cellum maṇimēkalaiyaik kaṇṭēṉ; kāṇṭalum, avaḷ tantaiyuṟṟa koṭuntuyar eṉ niṉaivilē tōṉṟi eṉ maṉavuṟutiyai māṟṟi, ivviyāḻiṉ pakai narampil eṉ kaiyaic celuttiyatu; ituvē yāṉuṟṟa tuṉpam,' eṉṟaṉaṉ. eṉṟalum pala nāḷāka maṇimēkalaiyai virumpiyirunta utayakumaraṉ makiḻcciyuṟṟu, ‘avaḷai eṉ tērmītēṟṟikkoṇṭu varuvēṉ,' eṉṟu avaṉukkuk kūṟiviṭṭut tēruṭaṉ ceṉṟu uvavaṉattiṉ matilvāyilai aṭaintāṉ.
maṇimēkalai attēroliyaik kēṭṭuc cutamatiyai nōkki, ‘utayakumaraṉ eṉpāl mikka viruppa muṭaiyaṉeṉṟu muṉpu mātavikku vayantamālai kūṟiyataik kēṭṭuḷēṉ; iḵtu avaṉ tēroli pōlum; itaṟkiyātu ceyvēṉ,' eṉṟāḷ. ataikkēṭṭa cutamati avaḷai āṅkuḷḷa paḷikkaṟaiyiṟ pukutti, uḷḷē tāḻiṭṭukkoṇṭu irukkac ceytu, tāṉ ap paḷikkaṟaikku aintu viṟkiṭai tūrattē niṉṟāḷ. aṅṅaṉam niṉṟavaḷait tērai niṟuttic cōlaiyiṉūṭē vanta utayakumaraṉ kaṇṭu, ‘nī maṇimēkalaiyuṭaṉ vantāy eṉpataṉai aṟintēṉ,' eṉṟu kūṟi, maṇimēkalaiyaik kuṟittut taṉatu vēṭkai pulappaṭap palavāṟu viṉāviṉaṉ. appoḻutu cutamati potiyaṟaippaṭṭōr pōṉṟuḷam varunti, avaṉai nōkki, "iḷamai nāṇi mutumai yeyti, urai muṭivu kāṭṭiya uravōṉ marukaṉākiya niṉakku aṟivum cālpum araciyal vaḻakkum makaḷir kūṟumāṟum uṇṭō? āyiṉum oṉṟu kūṟuvēṉ; makkaḷ yākkai ivviyalpiṉai uṭaiyatu; ataṉaip puṟamaṟiyākap pārāy," eṉṟu ataṉ iḻiviṉai yeṭutturaittāḷ; avvurai utayakumaraṉ ceviyai aṭaiyumuṉ paḷikkaṟaiyiṉuḷḷirunta maṇimēkalaiyiṉ uruvam avaṉ kaṇṇetirppaṭṭatu. (ikkātaiyiṉ mutaṟkaṇ uḷḷa iyaṟkaip puṉaivukaḷ iṉpam viḷaippaṉa. iṟutikkaṇ uṭampiṉ iḻi takaimai naṉku viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.)  
		paritiyañ celvaṉ virikatirt tāṉaikku
iruḷvaḷaip puṇṭa maruḷpaṭu pūmpoḻil
kuḻalicai tumpi koḷuttik kāṭṭa
maḻalai vaṇṭiṉam nalliyāḻ ceyya
5		veyil nuḻai paṟiyāk kuyilnuḻai potumpar
mayilā ṭaraṅkiṉ mantikāṇ paṉakāṇ
mācaṟat teḷinta maṇinī rilañcip
pācaṭaip parappiṟ paṉmala riṭainiṉ
ṟorutaṉi yōṅkiya viraimalart tāmarai
10		araca vaṉṉam āṅkiṉi tiruppak
karainiṉ ṟālum orumayil taṉakkuk
kampuṭ cēvaṟ kaṉaikuraṉ muḻavāk
kompa riruṅkuyil viḷippatu kāṇāy
iyaṅkutēr vīti yeḻutukaḷ cērntu
15		vayaṅkoḷi maḻuṅkiya mātarniṉ mukampōl
viraimalart tāmarai karainiṉ ṟōṅkiya
kōṭuṭait tāḻaik koḻumaṭa laviḻnta
vālveṇ cuṇṇam āṭiya titukāṇ
mātar niṉkaṇ pōteṉac cērntu
20		tātuṇ vaṇṭiṉa mītukaṭi ceṅkaiyiṉ
añciṟai viriya alarnta tāmaraic
ceṅkayal pāyntu piṟaḻvaṉa kaṇṭāṅku
eṟintatu peṟāa tiraiyiḻantu varunti
maṟintu nīṅku maṇicciral kāṇeṉap
25		poḻilum poykaiyuñ cutamati kāṭṭa
maṇimē kalaiyam malarvaṉaṅ kāṇpuḻi
matimaruḷ veṇkuṭai maṉṉavaṉ ciṟuvaṉ
	utaya kumaraṉ urukeḻu mītūr
	mīyāṉ naṭuṅka naṭuvuniṉ ṟōṅkiya
30			kūmpumutaṉ muṟiya vīṅkupiṇi yaviḻntu
	kayiṟukāl pariya vayiṟupāḻ paṭṭāṅku
	itaicitai tārppat tiraiporu munnīr
	iyaṅkuticai yaṟiyā tiyāṅkaṇu mōṭi
	mayaṅkukāl eṭutta vaṅkam pōlak
35			kāḻōr kaiyaṟa mēlō riṉṟip
	pākiṉ piḷavaiyiṟ paṇaimukan tuṭaittuk
	kōviyaṉ vītiyum koṭittēr vītiyum
	pīṭikait teruvum peruṅkalak kuṟuttāṅku
	irupāṟ peyariya vurukeḻu mūtūr
40		orupāṟ paṭāa toruvaḻit taṅkātu
	pākum paṟaiyum paruntiṉ pantarum
	ātula mākkaḷum alavuṟṟu viḷippa
	nīla mālvarai nilaṉoṭu paṭarnteṉak
	kāla vēkaṅ kaḷimayak kuṟṟeṉa
45			viṭuparik kutiraiyiṉ viraintuceṉ ṟeytik
	kaṭuṅkaṇ yāṉaiyiṉ kaṭāttiṟa maṭakki
	aṇittērt tāṉaiyo ṭaraciḷaṅ kumaraṉ
	maṇittērk koṭiñci kaiyāṟ paṟṟik
	kāralar kaṭampaṉ alla ṉeṉpatu
50			āraṅ kaṇṇiyiṟ cāṟṟiṉaṉ varuvōṉ
	nāṭaka maṭantaiyar nalaṅkeḻu vīti
	āṭakac ceyviṉai māṭat tāṅkaṇ
	cāḷaram poḷitta kālpōku peruvaḻi
	vītimaruṅ kiyaṉṟa pūvaṇaip paḷḷit
55			takarak kuḻalāḷ taṉṉoṭu mayaṅki
makara yāḻiṉ vāṉkōṭu taḻīi
	vaṭṭikaic ceytiyiṉ varainta pāvaiyiṉ
	eṭṭi kumaraṉ iruntōṉ ṟaṉṉai
	mātar taṉṉoṭu mayaṅkiṉai yiruntōy
60			yātunī yuṟṟa iṭukkaṇ eṉṟalum
āṅkatu kēṭṭu vīṅkiḷa mulaiyoṭu
	pāṅkiṟ ceṉṟu taṉṟoḻu tētti
	maṭṭavi ḻalaṅkaṉ maṉṉa kumaraṟku
	eṭṭi kumaraṉ eytiya turaippōṉ
65			vakaivaric ceppiṉuḷ vaikiya malarpōl			takainalam vāṭi malarvaṉam pukūum
	mātavi payanta maṇimē kalaiyoṭu
	kōvala ṉuṟṟa koṭuntuyar tōṉṟa
	neñciṟai koṇṭa nīrmaiyai nīkki
70			vempakai narampiṉ eṉkaic celuttiyatu
	ituyā ṉuṟṟa iṭumpai yeṉṟalum
	matumalart tārōṉ maṉamakiḻ veyti
	āṅkavaḷ taṉṉaiyeṉ aṇittē rēṟṟi
	īṅkiyāṉ varuvēṉ eṉṟavaṟ kuraittāṅku
75			ōṭumaḻai kiḻiyum matiyam pōla
	māṭa vītiyiṉ maṇittēr kaṭaiik
	kāraṇi pūmpoḻiṟ kaṭaimukaṅ kuṟukavat
	tēroli mātar cevimutal icaittalum
	cittirā patiyō ṭutaya kumaraṉuṟ
80			ṟeṉmēl vaitta uḷḷat tāṉeṉa
	vayanta mālai mātavik korunāḷ
	kiḷanta māṟṟam kēṭṭē ṉātaliṉ
	āṅkavaṉ tēroli pōlum āyiḻai
	īṅkeṉ cevimutal icaittateṉ ceykeṉa
85		amutuṟu tīñcol āyiḻai yuraittalum
cutamati kēṭṭut tuḷakkuṟu mayilpōl 
paḷikkaṟai maṇṭapam pāvaiyaip pukukeṉ
ṟoḷittaṟai tāḻkōt tuḷḷakat tirīi
āṅkatu taṉakkōr aiviliṉ kiṭakkai
90		nīṅkātu niṉṟa nēriḻai taṉṉaik
kalleṉ tāṉaiyoṭu kaṭuntēr niṟuttip
paṉmalarp pūmpoḻiṟ pakaṉmuḷait tatupōl
pūmarac cōlaiyum puṭaiyum poṅkarum
tāmaraic ceṅkaṇ parappiṉaṉ varūum
95		araciḷaṅ kumaraṉ ārumil oruciṟai
orutaṉi niṉṟāy uṉṟiṟa maṟintēṉ
vaḷariḷa vaṉamulai maṭantai melliyal
taḷariṭai yaṟiyun taṉmaiyaḷ kollō
viḷaiyā maḻalai viḷaintu melliyal
100		muḷaiyeyi ṟarumpi muttunirait taṉakol
ceṅkayal neṭuṅkaṇ cevimaruṅ kōṭi
veṅkaṇai neṭuvēḷ viyappuraik kuṅkol
mātava ruṟaiyiṭam orīimaṇi mēkalai
tāṉē tamiyaḷiṅ keytiya turaiyeṉap
105			potiyaṟaip paṭṭōr pōṉṟuḷam varunti
matumalark kūntaṟ cutamati yuraikkum
iḷamai nāṇi mutumai yeyti
uraimuṭivu kāṭṭiya uravōṉ marukaṟku
aṟivuñ cālpum araciyal vaḻakkum
110		ceṟivaḷai makaḷir ceppalu muṇṭō
aṉaiya tāyiṉum yātoṉṟu kiḷappal
viṉaiviḷaṅku taṭakkai viṟalōy kēṭṭi
viṉaiyiṉ vantatu viṉaikkuviḷai vāyatu
puṉaivaṉa nīṅkiṟ pulālpuṟat tiṭuvatu
115		mūttuviḷi vuṭaiyatu tīppiṇi irukkai
paṟṟiṉ paṟṟiṭaṅ kuṟṟak koḷkalam
puṟṟaṭaṅ karaviṟ ceṟṟac cēkkai
avalak kavalai kaiyā ṟaḻuṅkal
tavalā uḷḷan taṉpā luṭaiyatu
120		makkaḷ yākkai ituveṉa uṇarntu
mikkōy itaṉaip puṟamaṟip pārāy
eṉṟava ḷuraitta icaipaṭu tīñcol
ceṉṟava ṉuḷḷañ cērā muṉṉarp
paḷiṅkupuṟat teṟinta pavaḷap pāvaiyiṉ
125		iḷaṅkoṭi tōṉṟumāl iḷaṅkō muṉṉeṉ.
urai 
1-6.		paritiyañ celvaṉ virikatirt tāṉaikku - ātittaṉatu virinta katirkaḷākiya cēṉaikku, iruḷ vaḷaippuṇṭa maruḷpaṭu pūmpoḻil-iruḷ muṟṟappaṭṭā lotta maruṭkaiyuṇṭākum aḻakiya poḻiliṉkaṇ, kuḻalicai tumpi koḷuttik kāṭṭa- tumpikaḷ vēyṅkuḻaliṉicaiyaip poruttik kāṭṭa, maḻalai vaṇṭiṉam nalliyāḻ ceyya-iḷaiya vaṇṭiṉaṅkaḷ nalla yāḻiṉicaiyai murala, veyil nuḻaipu aṟiyāk kuyil nuḻai potumpar-ñāyiṟṟiṉ katir tōṉṟuṅkāl toṭṭu maṟaiyum varai ciṟitum cellutal aṟiyāta kuyilum nuḻaintu cellumāṟu aṭarnta iḷamarak
kāvākiya, mayil āṭu araṅkil manti kāṇpaṉakāṇ - araṅkil mayilkaḷ āṭuvataik kāṇpaṉavākiya manti
kaḷaik kāṇpāyāka ;
paritiyākiya celvaṉ eṉka. tāṉaikku-tāṉaiyāl, ñāyiṟṟiṉ katirāl turattappaṭṭa ulakiṉiruḷellām pukuntiruntāṟ pōlum iruṭciyaiyuṭaiya poḻil eṉka. maruḷ-maruṭkai; viyappu. tumpi - vaṇṭiṉ ōr kiḷai. 1"kompart tumpi kuḻalicai kāṭṭap, poṅkar vaṇṭiṉa nalliyāḻ ceyya" eṉap piṉṉarum varutal kāṇka. ilai kaḷiṉ ceṟivālē kuyilkaḷ nuḻaintu cellum iḷamarakkā veṉka; 2"veyiṉuḻai paṟiyāk kuyiṉuḻai potumpar" eṉpatu perumpāṇ. kāṇpaṉa : kāṇṭal eṉat toḻiṟ peyarumām.
 7-13. 	mācaṟat teḷinta maṇinīr ilañci-maṟuvaṟṟut teḷinta paḷiṅkupōlum nīriṉaiṭaiya poykaiyil, pācaṭaip parappil paṉmalar iṭainiṉṟu-paciya ilaikaḷiṉ parappiṉ kaṇuḷḷa pala malarkaḷiṉiṭaiyē niṉṟum, oru taṉi ōṅkiya virai malart tāmarai - taṉiyē uyarntu viḷaṅkiya maṇam mikka oru tāmarai malaril, araca aṉṉam āṅkiṉitu iruppa - aṉṉap puḷ iṉitāka aracu vīṟṟiruppa, karai niṉṟu ālum oru mayil taṉakku - avvilañciyiṉ karaiyil niṉṟu aracaṉ muṉ āṭum melliyal pōla āṭukiṉṟa oppaṟṟa aḻakiṉaiyuṭaiya mayilukku, kampuṭ cēval kaṉaikural muḻavā - campaṅ kōḻic cēvaliṉatu mikka kural muḻavoliyāka, kompar iruṅkuyil viḷippatu kāṇāy - kiḷaiyiṉkaṇ uḷḷa kariya kuyil pāṭuvataik kāṇ pāyāka;
maṇi - nīlamaṇiyumām. oru taṉi - oppaṟṟa taṉi; taṉṉan taṉi; "oru taṉi niṉṟāy," "oru taṉi yirunta, "oru taṉi yēṟi" (4 : 96 : 16 : 33 ; 17:86.) eṉa innūluḷḷē oru taṉiyeṉpatu payiṉṟu varukiṉṟatu. mayil taṉakku-mayiliṉatu āṭṭattiṟ kiyaiya. viḷittal-pāṭutal; 1"viḷiyātāṉ kūttāṭṭu" 2"viḷittaliṉṉamir tuṟaḻ kītam" eṉpuḻi viḷittal ipporuḷpaṭutal kāṇka.
14-18,	iyaṅku tēr vīti eḻu tukaḷ cērntu - tērkaḷ cañcarikkiṉṟa vītiyiṉiṉṟum eḻunta tukaḷ cērntu, vayaṅku oḷimaḻuṅkiya mātar niṉmukampōl - viḷaṅkukiṉṟa oḷi maḻuṅkappeṟṟa niṉatu aḻakiya mukattaippōla, virai malarttāmarai karai niṉṟu ōṅkiya kōṭu uṭait tāḻaik koḻumaṭal aviḻnta vālveṇ cuṇṇam āṭiyatu itukāṇ - karaiyil niṉṟu vaḷarnta kiḷaikaḷaiyuṭaiya tāḻaiyiṉatu koḻuviya maṭaliṉiṉṟum aviḻnta veḷḷiya makarantap poṭi aḷainta maṇam poruntiya it tāmarai malaraik kāṇpāyāka;
mātar - aḻaku. 3"kōṭuṭait tāḻai" eṉṉum pāṭattiṟkuc  caṅku uṭaintāṟ pōlum tāḻaiyiṉ maṭal eṉka; vāl veṇ: oru poruḷirucol; tūya veḷḷiya eṉṟumām; 4"ūrti vāl veḷ ḷēṟē" eṉpatu kāṇka. itu malar eṉak kūṭṭi im malar eṉa uraikka.
 19-26.	mātar niṉkaṇ pōtu eṉac cērntu tātu uṇ vaṇṭiṉam mītu kaṭi ceṅkaiyiṉ - niṉṉuṭaiya aḻakiya kaṇkaḷai nīlamalareṉa niṉaintu tātuṇṇumāṟu aṭainta vaṇṭiṉaṅkaḷai akaṟṟukiṉṟa niṉ civanta kaiyaippōla, am ciṟai viriya alarnta tāmaraic ceṅkayal pāyntu piṟaḻvaṉa kaṇṭu āṅku eṟintu - malarnta tāmarai malarilē pāyntu piṟaḻvaṉavākiya ceṅkayalkaḷaik kaṇṭu aḻakiya ciṟaku viriyumāṟu appoḻutē pāyntu, atu peṟāatu irai iḻantu varunti - am mīṉkaḷaip peṟāmal iraiyai iḻantu varunti, maṟintu nīṅku maṇicciral kāṇ - mīṇṭum nīṅkukiṉṟa nīlamaṇipōlum cicciliyaik kāṇpāyāka; eṉa - eṉṟu colli, poḻilum poykaiyum cutamati kāṭṭa - cutamati poḻilaiyum poykaiyaiyum kāṭṭāniṟka, maṇimēkalai am malarvaṉam kāṇpuḻi - maṇimēkalai  appūmpoḻiliṉ vaḷattiḷaik kāṇumpoḻutu;
añciṟai viriya eṟintu eṉaviyaiyum. atu, cāti yorumai; piṟaḻvaṉaveṉa mēl vantamaiyiṉ. irai iḻantu - peṟāmaiyāl avviraiyai yiḻanteṉka. maṇi - nīlamaṇi; 1"pulavukkaya leṭutta poṉvāy maṇicciral" eṉpatu kāṇka. ciral - ciccilippuḷ; itu mīṉ kotti yeṉṟum kūṟappaṭum. mukam tāmaraikkum, kaṇ ceṅkayalukkum, kai ciralukkum uvamam; 2"poruḷē yuvamañ ceytaṉar moḻiyiṉum, maruḷaṟu ciṟappiṉaḵtuvama mākum" eṉpataṉāl poruḷkaḷ uvamamākak kūṟappaṭṭaṉa. poykai vaṉattiṉ uṟuppākaliṉ, vaṉaṅkāṇpuḻi yeṉa atilaṭakkik kūṟiṉar.
 27-28. 	matimaruḷ veṇkuṭai maṉṉavaṉ ciṟuvaṉ utayakumaraṉ - tiṅkaḷai yotta veṇkuṭaiyaiyuṭaiya aracaṉ putalvaṉākiya utayakumaraṉ; 
maruḷ : uvamavurupu ; 3"matimaruḷ veṇkuṭai" eṉpatu puṟam. maṉṉavaṉ - cōḻaṉ.
28-34.	urukeḻumītu ūr mīyāṉ naṭuṅka - accam poruntiya mēliṭattiṉ ūrntirukkiṉṟa mālumi naṭuṅka, naṭuvuniṉṟu ōṅkiya kūmpu mutal muṟiya - naṭuvil niṉṟu ōṅkiya pāymaram aṭiyilē muṟiya, vīṅkupiṇi aviḻntu kayiṟu kāl pariya - iṟukiṉa piṇippu aviḻntu pāy kaṭṭiṉa kayiṟu aṟupaṭa, vayiṟu pāḻpaṭṭāṅku itai citaintu ārppa - appoḻutu naṭuviṭam pāḻākip pāy pīṟuṇṭu olippa, tiraiporu munnīr iyaṅku ticai aṟiyātu - alaikaḷ porukiṉṟa kaṭaliṉkaṇ cellun ticai aṟiyāmal, yāṅkaṇum ōṭi mayaṅku kāl eṭutta vaṅkam pōla - evviṭattum ōṭi mayaṅkukiṉṟa kaṭuṅkāṟṟiṉālalaikkap paṭṭa marakkalam pōla;
mūtūr eṉṉum pāṭattiṟkup pukāriṉkaṇ eṉṟuraikka,  mīyāṉ - mālumi ; nīyāṉ eṉpatum pāṭam. naṭuṅka - muṟiyap pariya ārppa ōṭiyeṉka : ōṭi mayaṅku vaṅkam eṉṟiyaikka ; mayaṅku kāl - cuḻal kāṟṟu eṉalumām ; itaṟku, cuḻal kāṟṟiṉāl eṭukkappaṭṭu ōṭiya vaṅkampōla eṉappirittuk kūṭṭuka.
35-44.	kāḻōr kaiyaṟa - kuttukkōṟkārar ceyalaṟa, mēlōr iṉṟi - pākarum iṉṟi, pākiṉ piḷavaiyiṟ paṇaimukam tuṭaittu - pākaṉāṟ kuttappaṭṭa piḷavaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya pariya mukattaik kaiyāṟṟuṭaittu, kōviyaṉ vītiyum koṭittēr vītiyum pīṭikait teruvum peruṅkalak kuṟuttu - aracar perunteruvaiyum koṭi yaṇinta tērōṭum vītiyaiyum kaṭai vītiyaiyum periya kalakka muṇṭākki, irupāṟ peyariya urukeḻu mūtūr orupāṟpaṭātu oruvaḻit taṅkātu-iruvakaip peyariṉaiyuṭaiyatāyp pakaivarkku accattait tarum mūtūriṉkaṇ oru neṟip paṭāmal ōriṭattiṟ ṟaṅkāmal, pākum paṟaiyum paruntiṉ pantarum - pākaṉum paṟaiyaṭippōrum paruntiṉ tokutiyum, ātula mākkaḷum alavuṟṟu viḷippa - mikka vaṟumai yuṭaiyōrum cuḻaṉṟu kūppiṭa, nīla mālvarai nilaṉoṭu paṭarn teṉak kālavēkam kaḷimayakku uṟṟeṉa - periya nīla malai nilattiṉ kaṇ tirivatupōlak kālavēkam eṉṉum paṭṭattiyāṉai mata mayakkuṟṟut tirintatāka;
mēlōr iṉṟi-pākaraivīci yeṉṟapaṭi. piḷavai -aṅkucat tāṟ piḷakkappaṭṭa puṇ. pākiṉ piḷavai-pākaṉuṭaiya uṭaṟ piḷavu eṉṟumām. iruvakaip peyariṉai, 1"irupāṟ peyariya vurukeḻu mūtūr" eṉpataṉ uraiyā laṟika. pāku - pākaṉ paṟai - paṟaiyaṭip pōr; yāṉaikku muṉpu paṟaiyaṭikkappaṭutal marapeṉpataṉai, 2"niṟaiyaḻi kolyāṉai nīrkkuviṭ ṭāṅkup. paṟaiyaṟain tallatu cellaṟka veṉṉā, iṟaiyē tavaṟuṭai yāṉ" eṉpataṉālaṟika. ātulamākkaḷ teruvilē tirivarākaliṉ avaraiyuṅ kūṟiṉār. paruntiṉ pantar - uṇaviṉporuṭṭup pantariṭṭāṟpōl mēlē cellum paruntiṉam, varai nilaṉoṭu paṭarnteṉa eṉpatu il poruḷuvamam. nilaṉoṭu - nilamicai. kaḷi - matam. 
vaṅkampōlak kāḻōr kaiyaṟap pākaṉ mutaliyōr  alavuṟṟu viḷippa mūtūriṉkaṇ mēlōriṉṟit tuṭaittup peruṅ kalakkuṟuttup paṭātu taṅkātu paṭarnteṉak kālavēkam kaḷimayakkuṟṟatāka eṉa muṭikka; vaṅkampōlak kālavēkam kaḷimayakkuṟṟeṉa eṉa viyaiyum. ivvāṟē maturaikkāñciyil, 3"vīṅkupiṇi nōṉ kayiṟāiyitai puṭaiyūk, kūmpumutaṉ muruṅka veṟṟik kāyntuṭaṉ, kaṭuṅkāṟ ṟeṭuppak kalporu turaii, neṭuñcuḻip paṭṭa nāvāy pōla, irutalaip paṇila mārppac ciṉañciṟantu, kōlōrkkoṉṟu mēlōr vīci, meṉpiṇi vaṉṟoṭar pēṇātu kāḻcāyttuk, kantunīt tuḻitaruṅ kaṭāa yāṉaiyum" eṉa vantiruntal kāṇka.
45-50.	viṭuparik kutiraiyiṉ viraintu ceṉṟu eyti-celuttu kiṉṟa virainta celaviṉaiyuṭaiya kutiraiyiṉmītu kaṭitiṟ ceṉṟu cērntu, kaṭuṅkaṇ yāṉaiyiṉ kaṭāttiṟam aṭakki- taṟukaṇmaiyuṭaiya yāṉaiyatu matattiṉ kūṟupāṭṭiṉai aṭakki, aṇittērt tāṉaiyoṭu - aḻakiya tēr mutaliya paṭaiyuṭaṉ, araciḷaṅ kumaraṉ-, maṇittērkkoṭiñci kaiyālpaṟṟi - tāṉ ēṟiyuḷḷa tēriṉkaṇ uḷḷa aḻakiya koṭiñciyaik kaiyāṟ piṭittukkoṇṭu, kāralar kaṭampaṉ allaṉ eṉpatu āraṅkaṇṇiyil cāṟṟiṉaṉ varuvōṉ - tāṉ kārkālattu malarkiṉṟa kaṭappa mālaiyaiyuṭaiya murukavēḷ allaṉ eṉpataṉaittāṉ aṇinta ātti mālaiyiṉāl aṟivittu varupavaṉ;
pari - celavu, utayakumaraṉākiya araciḷaṅkumaraṉ eṉak  kūṭṭuka. ‘koṭiñci-tāmaraippū vaṭivamākap paṇṇit tērt taṭṭiṉ muṉṉē naṭuvatu,' eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. 1"koṭiñci neṭuntēr," eṉpataṉ urai kāṇka. itu tāmarai moṭṭu vaṭivamuḷḷateṉṟu kūṟuvārumuḷar. ic colliṉuruvam koṭuñci yeṉavum kāṇappaṭum. aḻaku, iḷamai, āṇmaikaḷāl ivaṉai murukakkaṭavuḷeṉṟeṇṇi, kaṭappa mālaiyiṉmaiyāṉum āttimālai yuṇmaiyāṉum ivaṉ murukaṉallaṉ, cōḻaṉmakaṉ eṉṟu kaṇṭōr tuṇivareṉa utayakumaraṉiṉ aḻaku mutaliyaṉa ciṟappikkap paṭṭaṉa veṉka. ār - ātti.
51-58.	nāṭaka maṭantaiyar nalaṅkeḻu vīti-nāṭakak kaṇikaiyaratu aḻaku poruntiya vītiyil, āṭakac ceyviṉai māṭat tāṅkaṇ - āṭakappoṉṉāṟ ceytoḻilamainta māḷikaiyiṉ kaṇ, cāḷaram poḷitta kālpōku peruvaḻi-kāṟṟu cellu kiṉṟa periya vaḻiyākiyatuḷaiceyyappaṭṭa cāḷarattiṉiṭa māka, vīti maruṅku iyaṉṟa pūvaṇaippaḷḷi - vītiyiṉ pakkalil iyaṟṟiya malaraṇaic cēkkaiyil, takarakkuḻalāḷ taṉṉōṭu mayaṅki - mayirccāntiṉai yaṇinta kuḻaliṉai yuṭaiya kaṇikaiyoruttiyuṭaṉ mayakkamuṟṟu, makarayāḻiṉ vāṉkōṭu taḻīi - makara yāḻiṉatu ciṟanta kōṭṭait taḻuvi, vaṭṭikaic ceytiyiṉ varainta pāvaiyiṉ eṭṭikumaraṉ iruntōṉ taṉṉai - eḻutu kōliṉāl eḻutappaṭṭa ōviyap pāvai pōla acaivaṟṟu irunta eṭṭikumaraṉai,
āṭakam-nālvakaip poṉṉuḷ oṉṟu.poḷitta -  tuḷaiceyta. kālpōku peruvaḻiyākiya poḷitta cāḷaram eṉka. makarayāḻ - nālvakai yāḻiṉuḷoṉṟu; patiṉēḻu narampuṭaiyatu eṉpar. kōṭu - yāḻttaṇṭu. vaṭṭikai - eḻutukōl. eṭṭi - vaṇikar peṟum paṭṭap peyar; "eṭṭip pūppeṟ ṟirumup patiṟṟiyāṇ ṭoṭṭiya celvat tuyarntō ṉāyiṉaṉ." (22; 113-4) eṉpar. piṉṉum; 1"eṭṭi kāvitip paṭṭan tāṅki," eṉpatuṅ kāṇka; 2'eṭṭi kāvitiyeṉpaṉa tēya vaḻakkākiya ciṟappup peyar" eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
 59-64.	mātar taṉṉoṭu mayaṅkiṉai iruntōy yātu nī uṟṟa iṭukkaṇ eṉṟalum - mātiṉōṭum mayakkamuṟṟirup pōy nī aṭainta tuṉpam yātu eṉa viṉavutalum, āṅkatu kēṭṭu vīṅkiḷa mulaiyoṭu pāṅkiṟceṉṟu tāṉ toḻutu ētti-ataṉaik kēṭṭu ak kārikaiyuṭaṉ avaṉ pakkaliṟ ceṉṟu vaṇaṅkit tutittu, maṭṭu aviḻ alaṅkal maṉṉa kumaraṟku-tēṉ aviḻum malarmālaiyiṉai yuṭaiya araca kumaraṉukku, eṭṭi kumaraṉ eytiyatu uraippōṉ - tāṉ uṟṟa tuṉpattiṉ kāraṇattaik kūṟukiṉṟa eṭṭi kumaraṉ;
mayaṅkiṉai: eccamuṟṟu. mayaṅki mātar taṉṉoṭum iruntōy eṉka. pāṅkil - muṟaimaiyāl eṉṟumām.
 65-71.	vakaivaric ceppiṉuḷ vaikiya malarpōl - tiṟappaṭa amainta ceppiṉ uḷḷē vaikkappaṭṭa naṟumalaraip pōla,  takai nalam vāṭi - mikka aḻaku vāṭṭamuṟṟu, malarvaṉam pukūum - pūmpoḻilukkuc cellum, mātavi payanta maṇimēkalai yoṭu kōvalaṉ uṟṟa koṭuntuyar tōṉṟa - mātavi peṟṟa maṇimēkalaiyaik kaṇṭatum kōvalaṉ aṭainta koṭiya tuṉpam niṉaivil vara aḵtu, neñcu iṟaikoṇṭa nīrmaiyai nīkki - maṉam taṅkutal koṇṭa taṉmaiyai nīkki, vempakai narampiṉ eṉ kaic celuttiyatu, koṭiya pakai narampiṉ kaṇ eṉ kaiyaic celuttiyatu - itu yāṉ uṟṟa iṭumpai eṉṟalum - ituvē yāṉ aṭainta tuṉpam eṉṉalum;
ceppiṉuḷ vaikiya malar puḻukkattāl vāṭum; 3"maṭaimāṇ ceppiṟṟamiya vaikiya, peyyāp pūviṉ mey cāyiṉaḷē," 4"vakai varic ceppiṉuḷ vaikiya kōtaiyēm," 5"vēyātu ceppiṉaṭaittut tami vaikum vīyiṉaṉṉa, tīyāṭi ciṟṟam palamaṉaiyāḷ," eṉpaṉa kāṇka. maṇimēkalaiyaik kaṇṭavuṭaṉ kōvalaṉuṟṟa tuṉpam niṉaiviṟku vantamaiyiṉ, ‘maṇimēkalai yoṭu...tuyartōṉṟa,' eṉṟāṉ iṟaikoṇṭa-yāḻilē taṅkiya. pakai narampu-niṉṟa narampiṟku āṟāvatu narampu;āṟāvatum mūṉṟāvatumākiya narampukaḷ eṉṟu muraippar; "niṉṟa narampiṟ kāṟu mūṉṟuñ, ceṉṟu peṟa niṟpatu kūṭamākum," eṉpatu kāṇka. kōvalaṉ taṉ kulattōṉṟalākaliṉ avaṉuṟṟa tuyarai niṉaintu mayaṅkiṉēṉeṉṟāṉeṉka.
72-76. 	matumalart tārōṉ maṉamakiḻvu eyti-tēṉ poruntiya malar mālaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya utayakumaraṉ uḷamakiḻcciyaṭaintu, āṅkavaḷ taṉṉai eṉ aṇittēr ēṟṟi - am maṇimēkalaiyai eṉ aḻakiya tēriṉmītēṟṟi, īṇṭu yāṉ varuvēṉ eṉṟu avaṟku uraittāṅku-īṇṭu yāṉ varuvēṉ eṉṟu eṭṭi kumaraṟkukkūṟi, ōṭu maḻai kiḻiyum matiyampōla - ōṭukiṉṟa mukil kiḻitaṟkuk kāraṇamākiya matiyampōla, māṭavītiyil maṇittēr kaṭaii - māṭaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya vītiyiṉkaṇ aḻakiya tēraic celutti, kār aṇi pūmpoḻiṟ kaṭaimukam kuṟuka - vāṉaḷāviya pūñcōlaiyiṉ vāyiliṉiṭam aṭaiya;
muṉivar uṟaiviṭattiṉīṅki malarvaṉamaṭainta maṇimēkalaiyai iṉi eḷitiṉeytalāmeṉa maṉamakiḻntāṉeṉka. māṭaṅkaṭku maḻaiyum tērukku matiyamum uvamai; matiyiṉai utayakumaraṉukku uvamai yākkalumām . kalai nirampiṉamaiyiṉ; itaṟku matiyam pōlak kuṟuka veṉṟiyaikka. kār aṇi - mēkattai mītu aṇinta.
 77-85.	at tēr oli mātar cevimutal icaittalum - antat tēr oliyāṉatu maṇimēkalaiyiṉ ceviyiṭam ceṉṟu icaittalum, cittirāpatiyōṭu utayakumaraṉ uṟṟu eṉmēl vaitta uḷḷattāṉeṉa-utayakumaraṉ eṉpāl vaitta maṉamuṭaiyāṉ eṉṟu cittirāpatiyiṉiṭamāka aṟintu, vayantamālai mātavikku orunāḷ kiḷanta māṟṟam kēṭṭēṉ ātaliṉ - vayantamālai mātavipāl orunāḷ kūṟiya moḻiyaik kēṭṭirukkiṉṟēṉākaliṉ, āṅkavaṉ tēroli pōlum āyiḻai īṅku eṉ cevimutal icaittatu-cutamati av vutayakumaraṉatu tērōcaiyē pōlum ippoḻutu eṉ ceviyiṭam olittatu, eṉ ceyku eṉa amutuṟu tīñcol āyiḻai uraittalum - itaṟku eṉ ceyvēṉ eṉa amutiṉu miṉiya moḻiyiṉaiyuṭaiya maṇimēkalai kūṟutalum;  
cittirāpatiyōṭu uṟṟu eṉak kūṭṭic cittirāpatiyāl aṟintu eṉṟuraikka; vayantamālai cittirāpatiyuṭaṉ aṭaintu eṉṟalumām. āyiḻai: viḷi. 
86-90. cutamati kēṭṭut tuḷakkuṟu mayilpōl - cutamati atu kēṭṭut taḷarcciyuṟṟa mayilaippōla, paḷikkaṟai maṇṭapam pāvaiyaip pukuka eṉṟu oḷittu aṟai tāḻkōttu uḷḷa kattu irīi-maṇimēkalaiyaip paḷikkaṟai maṇṭapattiṉ uḷḷiṭattē pukuka eṉṟu kūṟi oḷittu irutti aṟaiyiṉ tāḻaik kōttu, āṅkatu taṉakku ōr aiviliṉ kiṭakkai nīṅkātu niṉṟa nēriḻaitaṉṉai - appaḷikku maṇṭapattiṟku aintu viṟkiṭai tūrattil nīṅkāmal niṉṟa cutamatiyai; 
oḷittu aṟai-oḷiyaiyuṭaiyatākiya aṟaiyeṉṟumām. paṇiyāḷar aintuviṟkiṭai tūrattē niṟṟal marapu; 1"aiviliṉakala niṉṟāṅ kaṭitoḻu taṟaiñciṉāṟku," eṉpatu kāṇka. nīṅkātu niṉṟaṉaḷ; aṅṅaṉam niṉṟa nēriḻaiyai eṉa aṟutturaittu, nēriḻaiyaik kaṇṭu eṉa oru col viritturaikka. 
 91-96.	kalleṉ tāṉaiyoṭu kaṭuntēr niṟutti - oliyiṉai yuṭaiya cēṉaiyoṭu virainta celaviṉaiyuṭaiya tēraiyum niṟutti, paṉmalarp pūmpoḻil pakal muḷaittatu pōl-pala malarkaḷaiyuṭaiya pūñcōlaiyil ñāyiṟu tōṉṟiyatu pōla, pūmaraccōlaiyum puṭaiyum poṅkarum - pūmpoḻililum pakkalilum kaṭṭumalaikaḷilum, tāmaraic ceṅkaṇ parappiṉaṉ varūum araciḷaṅkumaraṉ - tāmarai malar pōṉṟa civanta kaṇkaḷāl nōkkivarukiṉṟa maṉṉiḷam putalvaṉ, ārum il oru ciṟai oru taṉi niṉṟāy uṉ tiṟam aṟintēṉ - yārumillāta oru pakkattil nī oruttiyāy niṟkiṉṟāy niṉ iyalpiṉai aṟintēṉ ; 
poṅkar - īṇṭuc ceykuṉṟu. araciḷaṅkumaraṉ cutamatiyaika kaṇṭu uṉ tiṟam aṟintēṉ eṉṟu kūṟiyeṉka. 
  97-104.	vaḷariḷa vaṉamulai maṭantai melliyal - vaḷar kiṉṟa vaṉappuṭaiya iḷaṅ koṅkaikaḷaiyum meṉmait taṉmaiyaiyum uṭaiya maṇimēkalai, taḷar iṭai aṟiyum taṉmaiyaḷ kollō - āṭavar taḷarnta camayattai yaṟiyum taṉmaiyai yuṭaiyavaḷō, viḷaiyā maḻalai viḷaintu melliyal muḷai yeyiṟu arumpi muttu nirait taṉakol-mutirāta maḻalaimoḻi mutirntu melliyalukku muḷai eyiṟukaḷ arumpi muttukkaḷai niraittaṉa pōṉṟaṉavō, ceṅkayal neṭuṅkaṇ cevimaruṅku ōṭi veṅkaṇai neṭuvēḷ viyappu uraikkumkol-civanta kayal mīṉ pōṉṟa nīṇṭa kaṇkaḷ ceviyiṉ pakkattē ōṭik koṭiya kaṇaikaḷaiyuṭaiya kāmaṉiṉ viyappiṉai uraik kiṉṟaṉavō, mātavar uṟaiviṭam orīi maṇimēkalai tāṉē tamiyaḷ iṅku eytiyatu urai eṉa-maṇimēkalai caṅkattār uṟaikiṉṟa viṭat tiṉiṉṟum nīṅkit tāṉē taṉiyaḷāy ivaṇ eytiya kāraṇattai uraippāyāka eṉa;
iṭai- celvi; 1"uṭaiyār pōla iṭaiyiṉṟu kuṟuki" eṉpatu kāṇka. muḷai eyiṟu - muḷaipōlum eyiṟu; muḷaitta eyiṟumām. veṅkaṇai-viruppattaic ceyyuṅ kaṇaiyumām. viyappu- viyakkuñ ceyti. cevimaruṅkōṭutal - uraippatu pōlu meṉṟāṉeṉka. caṅkattai nīṅkiṉaḷātalait tamiyaḷ eṉṟāṉ.
 105-110	potiyaṟaip paṭṭōrpōṉṟu uḷam varunti matu malark kūntaṟ cutamati uraikkum - tēṉ poruntiya malar kaḷaiyaṇinta kuḻaliṉaiyuṭaiya cutamati cāḷaramillāta nilavaṟaiyiṟ paṭṭōr pōla maṉam varuntik kūṟukiṉṟāḷ, iḷamai nāṇi mutumai eytu uraimuṭivu kāṭṭiya uravōṉ marukaṟku iḷamaip paruvattai nāṇi mutumaipparuvattai aṭaintu tammuḷ māṟu koṇṭu vantār iruvaruṭaiya collai ārāyntu aṟintu avarkaṭku avaṟṟiṉ muṭivai viḷakkiya pēraṟivuṭaiyōṉākiya karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉatu vaḻittōṉṟalākiya niṉakku, aṟivum cālpum araciyal vaḻakkum ceṟivaḷai makaḷir ceppalum uṇṭō-nallaṟiviṉaiyum amaitiyaiyum araciyal nītiyaiyum ceṟinta vaḷaiyaiyumuṭaiya makaḷir kūṟumāṟum uṇṭō;
kaṟikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉ urai muṭivu kāṭṭiya ivvaralāṟu 2urai muṭivukāṇā ṉiḷamaiyōṉeṉṟa, naraimutu makkaḷuvappa - narai muṭittuc collāṉ muṟaiceytāṉ cōḻaṉ kula viccai, kallāmaṟ pākam paṭum" eṉṉum paḻamoḻi veṇ pāvālum, "tam muḷ maṟutalaiyāyiṉā riruvar tamakku muṟai ceyya vēṇṭi vantu cila coṉṉāl ac coṉ muṭivu kaṇṭē ārāyntu muṟai ceyya aṟivu nirampāta iḷamaip paruvattāṉeṉṟikaḻnta naraimutu makka ḷuvakkumvakai narai muṭittuvantu, muṟaivēṇṭivanta irutiṟattārum colliya coṟkoṇṭē ārāyntaṟintu muṟaiceytāṉ karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉeṉṉuñ cōḻaṉ; ātalāl tattam kulattukkut takka viccaikaḷ kaṟpataṟku muṉṉē cempāka muḷavām eṉṟavāṟu" eṉṉum ataṉ uraiyālum aṟiyappaṭum: porunarāṟ ṟuppaṭaiyilum 1"mutiyō, ravaipuku poḻutiṟṟam pakaimuraṇ celavum" eṉa itu kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu. marukaṟku muṉṉilaiyiṟ paṭarkkai.
111-125.	aṉaiyatu āyiṉum yāṉ oṉṟu kiḷappal viṉai viḷaṅku taṭakkai viṟalōy kēṭṭi - aṅṅaṉamāyiṉum pōriṉkaṇ viḷaṅkukiṉṟa taṭantōḷ valiyuṭaiyāy yāṉ oṉṟu kūṟuvēṉ ataṉaik kēṭpāyāka, viṉaiyiṉ vantatu viṉaikku viḷaivāyatu - kaṉmattāl uṇṭāṉatu kaṉmattiṟku viḷai nilamākavuḷḷatu. puṉaivaṉa nīṅkil pulāl puṟattu iṭuvatu - puṉaiyap paṭuvaṉavākiya maṇap poruḷkaḷ nīṅkap paṭumāṉāl pulāl nāṟṟattai veḷikkuk kāṭṭuvatu, mūttu viḷivuṭaiyatu - mutumai yaṭaintu cātalaiyuṭaiyatu, tīppiṇi irukkai - koṭiya nōykaṭku iruppiṭamākavuḷḷatu, paṟṟiṉ paṟṟiṭam - paṟṟukkaḷukkup paṟṟum iṭamāyatu, kuṟṟak koḷkalam - kuṟṟaṅkaṭkuk koḷkalamāyuḷḷatu, puṟṟu aṭaṅku araviṉ ceṟṟac cēkkai - pāmpu aṭaṅkiyuḷḷa puṟṟaippōlac ceṟṟattiṟkut taṅkumiṭamāyatu, avalak kavalai kaiyāṟu aḻuṅkal tavalā uḷḷam taṉpāl uṭaiyatu-avalam mutaliya nāṉkum nīṅkātatākiya uḷḷattait taṉṉiṭat tuṭaiyatu, makkaḷ yākkai ituveṉa uṇarntu mikkōy itaṉaip puṟamaṟippārāy - mēlōṉē makkaḷ uṭampu ittakaiyateṉpataṉai aṟintu itaṉaip puṟamaṟiyākap pārppāyāka, eṉṟu avaḷ uraitta-eṉṟu avaḷ kūṟiya, icaippaṭu tīñcol-pukaḻiṉaiyuṇṭākkukiṉṟa iṉiya moḻikaḷ, ceṉṟu avaṉ uḷḷam cērāmuṉṉar - utayakumaraṉ uḷḷattiṟ ceṉṟu aṭaivataṟku muṉṉarē, paḷiṅku puṟattu eṟinta pavaḷappāvaiyiṉ - paḷiṅkaṟaiyiṉ puṟattē viḷaṅkukiṉṟa pavaḷappāvaiyaippōla, iḷaṅkoṭi tōṉṟumāl iḷaṅkō muṉ eṉ - iḷamai poruntiya koṭiyaṉaiya maṇimēkalaiyiṉ uruvam iḷaṅkōviṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟiya teṉka.
yākkai viṉaiyiṉ kāriyamāyum, viṉaikkuk kāraṇamāyum uḷḷa teṉṟapaṭi; "piṟavip peruṅkaṭal" "eṉpataṉuraiyil, kāraṇa kāriyat toṭarcciyāyk karaiyiṉṟi varutaliṉ piṟavip peruṅkaṭaleṉṟār" eṉap parimēlaḻakar kūṟiyatu īṇṭu aṟiyaṟpālatu. puṉaivaṉa - cantaṉam, malar mutaliyaṉa. mūppu eṉpatum pāṭam. aravu aṭaṅku puṟṟiṉ eṉa māṟuka. ceṟṟam - pakaimai neṭuṅkālam nikaḻvatākiya ciṉam, avalam - varut tantōṉṟi niṟṟal; kavalai - yātu ceyvaleṉṟal; kaiyāṟu - mūrccittal; aḻuṅkal - vāyviṭ ṭaḻutal; itu araṟṟu eṉavum paṭum. puṟamaṟip pārāy - puṟamaṟiyākap pārppāyāka; eṉṟatu, paiyai uḷpuṟamāka maṟittup pārttal pōl uṭampaiyum pārkkaveṉṟapaṭi; pārppiṉ itaṉ tūytu aṉmai viḷaṅkum eṉṟavāṟu; 1"maṟṟataṉaip paimmaṟiyāp pārkkappaṭum" 2"paimmaṟiyā nōkkap paruntārkkun takaimaittu" eṉpaṉa kāṇka. eṉṟāḷ; eṉṟu avaḷuraitta col eṉa vēṟaṟut turaikka. "paḷiṅku......pāvai" eṉṉuṅ karuttu; 3"paḷikkaṟaip pavaḻap pāvai pariceṉat tikaḻuñ cāyal" eṉac cīvaka cintā maṇiyil vantuḷḷamai kāṇka.
cutamati kāṭṭa maṇimēkalai malarvaṉam kāṇpuḻi, yāṉaiyiṉ kaṭāttiṟam aṭakkivaruvōṉākiya utayakumaraṉ eṭṭikumaraṉai nōkkiyātu nī yuṟṟa iṭukkaṇ eṉṟalum, avaṉ itu yāṉuṟṟa iṭumpai yeṉṟalum, tārōṉ makiḻveytip pūmpoḻiṟ kaṭaimukam kuṟuka, tēroli icaittalum, eṉceyku eṉa āyiḻaiyuraittalum, pāvaiyai irīi niṉṟa, cutamatiyaik kaṇṭu araciḷaṅkumaraṉ maṇimēkalai eytiyatu uraiyeṉa, avaḷ varunti makkaḷ yākkai itu veṉa uṇarntu puṟamaṟippārāy eṉṟuraitta col avaṉuḷḷañ cērā muṉṉar iḷaṅkoṭi iḷaṅkōmuṉ tōṉṟum eṉa muṭikka.
	paḷikkaṟai pukka kātai muṟṟiṟṟu.	


5. maṇimēkalāteyvam vantu 
tōṉṟiya kātai 
maṇimēkalaiyiṉ uruvattai ōviyameṉak karuti ōviyaṉatu kaittiṟattai viyanta utayakumaraṉ piṉpu ataṉai avaḷ vaṭiveṉat tuṇintu, paḷikkaṟaiyuṭ pukutaṟku ataṉ vāyil kāṇap paṭāmaiyāl paḷikkuc cuvariṉaik kaiyālē taṭavik koṇṭu varupavaṉ cutamatiyai nōkki, ‘maṇimēkalai ettiṟattiṉaḷ ? kūṟuti' eṉṟāṉ. eṉṉalum, cutamati, ‘avaḷ tavavoḻukkiṉaḷ; kuṟṟam purintāraic capikkum āṟṟalumuṭaiyavaḷ; kāma vikāram illātavaḷ ; nī avaḷai virumputal takkataṉṟu' eṉṟu kūṟiṉaḷ. kūṟalum avaṉ, ‘ciṟaiyumuṇṭō ceḻumpuṉal mikkuḻi, niṟaiyumuṇṭō kāmam kāḻkkoḷiṉ? avaḷ eppaṭiyum eṉakkuriyavaḷāvaḷ' eṉṟu collik koṇṭu celpavaṉ, camaṇa muṉivarkaḷiṉ iruppiṭattil ōr vittiyātaraṉāl iṭappaṭṭavaḷeṉṟu kūṟappaṭum cutamati pautta caṅkattaic cārnta mātavi makaḷuṭaṉ vanta varalāṟṟai avaḷai viṉāvit terintukoṇṭu, ‘maṇimēkalaiyaic cittirāpatiyāl iṉi aṭaital kūṭum' eṉṟu collip pōyiṉaṉ. pōṉavuṭaṉ maṇimēkalai veḷiyē vantu cutamatiyai nōkki, aṉpilaḷ; tavavuṇarcciyillātavaḷ, poruḷ vilaiyāṭṭi,' eṉṟu eṉṉai ikaḻntaṉaṉ eṉṉātu avaṉ piṉṉē eṉ neñcu cellaluṟṟatu; ‘ituvō kāmattiyaṟkai ; itaṉ taṉmai keṭuvatāka;' eṉṟu collikkoṇṭu niṉṟāḷ. appoḻutu intira viḻāvaik kāṇutaluṟṟa maṇimēkalā teyvam avarkaṭkut terinta ōr maṭantai vēṭam pūṇṭu appoḻilaiyaṭaintu, paḷikkaṟaiyiluḷḷa pātapīṭikaiyai valaṅkoṇṭu mēl eḻumpi niṉṟu palavāṟu tutittatu. appoḻutu pakaṟpoḻutu nīṅka antimālai vantuṟṟatu. (itaṉkaṇ cutamati varalāṟu kūṟum vāyilākac camaṇariṉum puttarkaḷ aruḷuṭaiyō reṉpatu valiyuṟuttappaṭukiṉṟatu. putta tēvaṉaip paravutal naṉkamain tuḷḷatu. mēṟṟicaiyil ñāyiṟu vīḻak kīḻtticaiyil niṟai mati tōṉṟutalai uruvakappaṭutti yiruppatum, mālaippoḻutiṉ nikaḻccikaḷ kūṟuvatum kaviyiṉ kaṟpaṉait tiṟattiṟkuc ciṟanta eṭuttuk kāṭṭukkaḷām.)
		iḷaṅkōṉ kaṇṭa iḷampoṟ pūṅkoṭi
viḷaṅkoḷi mēṉi viṇṇavar viyappap	
porumukap paḷiṅkiṉ eḻiṉi vīḻttut
tiruviṉ ceyyōḷ āṭiya pāvaiyiṉ
5		viraimala raiṅkaṇai mīṉa vilōtaṉattu
uruvi lāḷaṉo ṭuruvam peyarppa
ōviya ṉuḷḷat tuḷḷiyatu viyappōṉ
kāviyaṅ kaṇṇi yākutal teḷintu
tāḻoḷi maṇṭapan taṉkaiyil taṭaiic
10		cūḻvōṉ cutamati taṉmuka nōkkic
cittirak kaiviṉai ticaitoṟuñ ceṟintaṉa
ettiṟat tāḷniṉ iḷaṅkoṭi yuraiyeṉak
kurukupeyark kuṉṟaṅ koṉṟō ṉaṉṉaniṉ
murukac cevvi mukantutaṉ kaṇṇāl
15		parukā ḷāyiṉip paintoṭi naṅkai
ūḻtaru tavattaḷ cāpa caratti
kāmaṟ kaṭanta vāymaiya ḷeṉṟē
tūmalark kūntaṟ cutamati yuraippac
ciṟaiyu muṇṭō ceḻumpuṉal mikkuḻi
20		niṟaiyu muṇṭō kāmaṅ kāḻkkoḷiṉ
cevviya ḷāyiṉeṉ cevviya ḷākeṉa
avviya neñcamō ṭakalvō ṉāyiṭai
añceñ cāya larāntā ṇattuḷōr
viñcaiya ṉiṭṭa viḷaṅkiḻai yeṉṟē
25		kalleṉ pērūrp pallō ruraiyiṉai
āṅkava ruṟaiviṭam nīṅki yāyiḻai
īṅkivaḷ taṉṉo ṭeytiya turaiyeṉa
vārkaḻal vēntē vāḻkaniṉ kaṇṇi
tīneṟip paṭarā neñciṉai yākumati
30		īṅkivaḷ taṉṉō ṭeytiya kāraṇam
vīṅkunīr ñāla māḷvōy kēṭṭaruḷ
yāppuṭai yuḷḷat temmaṉai yiḻantōṉ
pārppaṉa mutumakaṉ paṭima vuṇṭiyaṉ
maḻaivaḷan tarūum aḻalōm pāḷaṉ
35		paḻaviṉaip payattāl piḻaimaṇa meytiya
eṟkeṭut tiraṅkit taṉtaka vuṭaimaiyiṉ
kuraṅku ceykaṭaṟ kumariyam peruntuṟaip
parantuceṉ mākkaḷoṭu tēṭiṉaṉ peyarvōṉ
kaṭaṉmaṇṭu peruntuṟaik kāviri yāṭiya
40		vaṭamoḻi yāḷaroṭu varuvōṉ kaṇṭīṅ
	kiyāṅṅaṉam vantaṉai yeṉmaka ḷeṉṟē
tāṅkāk kaṇṇī reṉṟalai yutirttāṅ
kōta lantaṇark kovvē ṉāyiṉum
kātala ṉātaliṟ kaiviṭa līyāṉ
45		irantūṇ talaikkoṇ ṭinnakar maruṅkil
parantupaṭu maṉaitoṟun tirivō ṉorunāḷ
puṉiṟṟāp pāynta vayiṟṟup puṇṇiṉaṉ
kaṇaviri mālai kaikkoṇ ṭeṉṉa
niṇanīṭu peruṅkuṭar kaiyakat tēnti
50		eṉmaka ḷirunta iṭameṉ ṟeṇṇit
taṉṉuṟu tuṉpan tāṅkātu pukuntu
camaṇīr kāḷnuñ caraṇeṉ ṟōṉai
	ivaṇī rallaveṉ ṟeṉṉoṭum vekuṇṭu
maiyaṟu paṭivattu mātavar puṟatemaik
55		kaiyutirk kōṭaliṉ kaṇṇiṟai nīrēm
aṟavō ruḷirō ārumi lōmeṉap
puṟavōr vītiyiṟ pulampoṭu cāṟṟa
maṅkuṟōy māṭam maṉaitoṟum pukūum
aṅkaiyiṟ koṇṭa pāttira muṭaiyōṉ
60		katircuṭum amayattup paṉimati mukattōṉ
poṉṉiṟ ṟikaḻum polampū vāṭaiyaṉ
eṉṉuṟ ṟaṉirō eṉṟemai nōkki
aṉpuṭa ṉaḷaiiya aruṇmoḻi yataṉāl
añcevi niṟaittu neñcakaṅ kuḷirppittut
65		taṉkaip pāttira meṉkait tantāṅ
kentaik kuṟṟa iṭumpai nīṅka
eṭuttaṉaṉ ṟaḻīik kaṭuppat talaiyēṟṟi
mātava ruṟaiviṭaṅ kāṭṭiya maṟaiyōṉ
cātuyar nīkkiya talaivaṉ ṟavamuṉi
70		caṅka tarumaṉ tāṉemak karuḷiya
eṅkō ṉiyalkuṇaṉ ētamil kuṇapporuḷ
ulaka nōṉpiṟ palakati uṇarntu
taṉakkeṉa vāḻāp piṟarkkuri yāḷaṉ
iṉpac cevvi maṉpatai yeyta
75		aruḷaṟam pūṇṭa oruperum pūṭkaiyiṉ
aṟakkati rāḻi tiṟappaṭa uruṭṭik
kāmaṟ kaṭanta vāmaṉ pātam
takaipā rāṭṭuta lallatu yāvatum
mikainā villēṉ vēntē vāḻkeṉa
80		añco lāḻiyai niṉtiṟa maṟintēṉ
vañci nuṇṇiṭai maṇimē kalaitaṉaic
cittirā patiyāṟ cērtalu muṇṭeṉ
ṟappoḻi lāṅkavaṉ ayarntu pōyapiṉ
paḷikkaṟai tiṟantu paṉimati mukattuk 
85		kaḷikkayal piṟaḻāk kāṭciya ḷākik
kaṟput tāṉilaḷ naṟṟava uṇarvilaḷ
varuṇak kāppilaḷ poruḷvilai yāṭṭiyeṉ
ṟikaḻntaṉa ṉāki nayantō ṉeṉṉātu
putuvōṉ piṉṟaip pōṉateṉ ṉeñcam
90		ituvō aṉṉāy kāmat tiyaṟkai
ituvē yāyiṟ keṭukataṉ ṟiṟameṉa
matumalark kuḻalāḷ maṇimē kalaitāṉ
cutamati taṉṉoṭu niṉṟa ellaiyuḷ
intira kōṭaṇai viḻāvaṇi virumpi
95		vantu kāṇkuṟuu maṇimēkalā teyvam
patiyakat tuṟaiyumōr paintoṭi yāki
maṇiyaṟaip pīṭikai valaṅkoṇ ṭōṅkip
pulavaṉ ṟīrttaṉ puṇṇiyaṉ purāṇaṉ
ulaka nōṉpiṉ uyarntō yeṉkō 
100		kuṟṟaṅ keṭuttōy ceṟṟañ ceṟuttōy
muṟṟa uṇarnta mutalvā eṉkō
kāmaṟ kaṭantōy ēma māyōy
tīneṟik kaṭumpakai kaṭintōy eṉkō
āyira vārat tāḻiyaṉ ṟiruntaṭi
105		nāvā yiramilēṉ ēttuva tevaṉeṉ
ṟerimaṇip pūṅkoṭi irunila maruṅkuvan
torutaṉi tirivatot tōtiyi ṉotuṅki
nilavarai yiṟantōr muṭaṅkunā nīṭṭum
pulavarai yiṟanta pukāreṉum pūṅkoṭi
110		paṉmalar ciṟanta naṉṉī rakaḻip
puḷḷoli ciṟanta teḷḷaric cilampaṭi
ñāyi liñci nakaimaṇi mēkalai
vāyiṉmaruṅ kiyaṉṟa vāṉpaṇait tōḷi
tarunila vacciram eṉairu kōṭṭam
115		etireti rōṅkiya katiriḷa vaṉamulai
ārpuṉai vēntaṟkup pēraḷa viyaṟṟi
ūḻi yeṇṇi nīṭuniṉ ṟōṅkiya
oruperuṅ kōyiṟ ṟirumuka vāṭṭi
kuṇaticai maruṅkiṉ nāṇmutir matiyamum
120		kuṭaticai maruṅkiṟ ceṉṟuvīḻ katirum
veḷḷiveṇ tōṭṭoṭu poṟṟō ṭāka
eḷḷaṟu tirumukam poliyap peytalum
aṉṉac cēval ayarntuviḷai yāṭiya
taṉṉuṟu peṭaiyait tāmarai yaṭakkap
125		pūmpoti citaiyak kiḻittup peṭaikoṇ
ṭōṅkirun teṅkiṉ uyarmaṭa lēṟa
aṉṟiṟ pēṭai arikkura laḻaiic
ceṉṟuvīḻ poḻutu cēvaṟ kicaippap
pavaḷac ceṅkāṟ paṟavaik kāṉattuk
130		kuvaḷai mēynta kuṭakkaṭ cētā
mulaipoḻi tīmpāl eḻutuka ḷavippak
kaṉṟuniṉai kurala maṉṟuvaḻip paṭara
anti yantaṇar centīp pēṇap
paintoṭi makaḷir palarviḷak keṭuppa
135		yāḻōr marutat tiṉṉaram puḷarak
kōvalar mullai kuḻaṉmēṟ koḷḷa
amaraka maruṅkiṟ kaṇavaṉai yiḻantu
tamarakam pukūum orumakaḷ pōlak
katirāṟṟup paṭutta mutirāt tuṉpamō
140		ṭanti yeṉṉum pacalaimey yāṭṭi
vantiṟut taṉaḷāl mānakar maruṅkeṉ.
urai 
1-6.		iḷaṅkōṉ kaṇṭa iḷampoṟ pūṅkoṭi-araciḷaṅ kumaraṉ kaṇṇuṟṟa aḻakiya iḷam pūṅkoṭiyaṉaiya maṇimēkalai, viḷaṅku oḷi mēṉi viṇṇavar viyappa-viḷaṅkukiṉṟa pēroḷi poruntiya tirumēṉiyaiyuṭaiya vāṉavarkaḷum viyakkumāṟu, porumukap paḷiṅkiṉ eḻiṉi vīḻttu - paḷiṅkiṉālāya porumuka veḻiṉiyai vīḻttu, tiruviṉ ceyyōḷ āṭiya pāvaiyiṉ - tirumakaḷāṭiya pāvaiyaippōla, viraimalar aiṅkaṇai mīṉa vilōtaṉattu uruvilāḷaṉoṭu uruvam peyarppa - maṇam poruntiya malarākiya aintampukaḷaiyum mīṉakkoṭiyaiyum uṭaiya anaṅkaṉuṭaṉ taṉ uruvattai veḷippaṭutta;
poṟpūṅkoṭi - kāmavalliyumām; itu poṉṉulakat tuḷḷa teṉpar. eḻiṉi - tiraiccīlai; porumuka veḻiṉi-mūvakait tiraic cīlaikaḷuḷ oṉṟu; araṅkiṉ valattūṇ iraṇṭilum uruvu tirai yāy amaivatu; eḻiṉi mūṉṟaṉaiyum 1"orumuka veḻiṉiyum - porumuka veḻiṉiyum; karantuvara leḻiṉiyum purintuṭaṉ vakuttu"eṉ pataṉālaṟika. tiruviṉ ceyyōḷ - tiruvākiya ceyyōḷ; tirumakaḷ- iṉ: cāriyai. āṭiya pāvaiyiṉ - āṭutaṟkuk koṇṭa pāvai yuruvaip pōla; kollip pāvaiyurukkoṇṭu āṭiṉamaiyiṉ ak kūttum pāvai yeṉappaṭum; atu pōr ceytaṟkuc camainta kōlattuṭaṉ avuṇar mōkittu viḻumpaṭi tirumakaḷāṭiyatu; patiṉōrāṭaluḷoṉṟu; 2"ceruveṅ kōla mavuṇar nīṅkat, tiru viṉ ceyyō ḷāṭiya pāvaiyum' eṉpatu kāṇka. "uruvi lāḷaṉo ṭuruvam peyarppa" eṉpataṉ karuttu ivaḷuruvaik kaṇṭavuṭaṉ iḷaṅkōvaṉukkuk kāmavupātai uṇṭāyiṟ ṟeṉpatām. 
7-12.	ōviyaṉ uḷḷattu uḷḷiyatu viyappōṉ - (maṇimēkalaiyiṉ uruvattai ōviyam eṉa niṉaintu) ōviyakkāraṉ ataṉai eḻutumāṟu maṉatil amaittatai viyakkiṉṟavaṉāy utayakumaraṉ, kāviyaṅ kaṇṇi yākutal teḷintu - avvaṭivam nīlamalaraṉaiya kaṇkaḷaiyuṭaiya maṇimēkalai yātalait teḷintu, tāḻ oḷi maṇṭapam taṉ kaiyil taṭaiic cūḻvōṉ - taṅkiya oḷiyuṭaiya paḷikku maṇṭapattait taṉ kaiyālē taṭavip pārttuc cuṟṟi varupavaṉ, cutamati taṉmukam nōkki - cutamatiyiṉ mukattaip pārttu, cittira kaiviṉai ticaitoṟum ceṟintaṉa ettiṟattāḷ niṉ iḷaṅkoṭi urai eṉa - cittirak kaittoḻil eppakkamum niṟaintirukkiṉṟaṉa niṉ iḷaṅkoṭi pōlpavaḷ evviṭattāḷ kūṟuka eṉa;
tāḻtal-taṅkutal. vāyil kāṇṭaṟku maṇṭapattait taṭavic cūḻntaṉaṉeṉka. cūḻntu varuṅkāl maṇimēkalaiyiṉ uruvam etticaiyiṉum veḷippaṭṭamaiyiṉ "cittirak kaiviṉai ticaitoṟuñ ceṟintaṉa" eṉṟāṉ ; maṇṭapattiluḷḷa ōviyappāvai palavaṟṟuṭaṉ maṇimēkalaiyiṉ uruvam vēṟupāṭiṉṟi yiruntamaiyiṉ aṅṅaṉam kūṟiṉāṉeṉalumām; 1"mēviya paḷiṅkiṉ viruntiṟ pāvaiyiḵ tōviyac ceytiyeṉ ṟoḻivēṉ muṉṉar" 2"īṅkim maṇṇīṭṭiyāreṉa vuṇarkēṉ" eṉap piṉvaruvaṉa kāṇka. tiṟam-iyalpumām. cutamati kāvaṟ peṇṭākaliṉ 'niṉ iḷaṅkoṭi' eṉṟāṉ. ōviyaṉ eṉpataṟku utayakumaraṉ cittiram pōṉṟavaṉāy niṉṟeṉṟuraittal īṇṭaikkup poruntāmaiyōrka.
13-18.	kuruku peyark kuṉṟaṅ koṉṟōṉ aṉṉa niṉ murukac cevvi - kiravuñca malaiyai eṟinta murukavēḷai yotta niṉatu iḷamai yaḻakiṉai, mukantu taṉ kaṇṇāl parukāḷ 3āyiṉ paintoṭi naṅkai-ārāyumiṭattup paciya vaḷaiyalkaḷai yaṇinta maṇimēkalai taṉ kaṇkaḷāl mukantu parukāḷ, ūḻtaru tavattaḷ - muṟaiyākap peṟṟa tavattiṉaiyuṭaiyaḷ, cāpacaratti-cāpamākiya ampaiyuṭaiyavaḷ, kāmaṟ kaṭanta vāymaiyaḷ eṉṟē - kāmaṉai veṉṟa meymmaiyai yuṭaiyavaḷ eṉṟu, tūmalark kūntal cutamati uraippa-tūya malaraṇinta kūntalaiyuṭaiya cutamati kūṟa;
kuruku-aṉṟil; kiravuñcam; ataṉ peyar peṟṟa kuṉṟameṉka; 4"kurukupeyarkkuṉṟaṅ koṉṟa neṭuvēlē" "kurukupeyarkkuṉ ṟaṅkoṉṟāṉ" eṉpaṉa kāṇka. muruku-iḷamai; īṟu tirintatu; ūḻtaru tavattaḷ- ūḻiṉālē tarap paṭṭa tavattiṉaḷ eṉṟumām; eṉṉai? 5tavamun tavamuṭaiyārk kākum" eṉpavākaliṉ. cāpacaratti - villaiyum ampaiyum uṭaiyāḷ eṉpatōr poruḷum tōṉṟa niṉṟatu; cāpam - vil. āyiṉ, naṅkai parukāḷ; tavattaḷ; caratti; vāymaiyaḷ; eṉṟu uraippaveṉka.
19-27. ciṟaiyum uṇṭō ceḻumpuṉal mikkuḻi- vaḷaviya nīr mikunta viṭattu ataṉait tāṅkum araṇum uṇṭō, niṟaiyum uṇṭō kāmam kāḻkkoḷiṉ-avvāṟē kāmam aṭippaṭiṉ ataṉai niṟuttum taṉmaiyum uḷatākumō, cevviyaḷ āyiṉ eṉ cevviyaḷ ākeṉa - cevviyai yuṭaiyaḷāyiṉ eṉṉai? avaḷ cevviyaḷākaṭṭum eṉṟu, avviya neñcamoṭu akal vōṉ-poṟāmai koṇṭa uḷḷattōṭum nīṅkuvōṉ, āyiṭai-appoḻutu, amceñ cāyal - aḻakiya civantacāyalaiyuṭaiyāy arāntāṇattuḷ-camaṇap paḷḷiyil, ōr viñcaiyaṉ iṭṭa viḷaṅkiḻai eṉṟē-oru viñcaiyaṉāliṭappaṭṭa melliyal eṉṟē, kalleṉ pērūrp pallōr uraiyiṉai-kalleṉṉum oliyiṉaiyuṭaiya nakariṉkaṇ pallōrāluṅ kūṟappaṭuvāy, āṅkavar uṟaiviṭam nīṅki āyiḻai-āyiḻāy nī accamaṇa muṉivar vāḻviṭattai nīṅki, īṅkivaḷ taṉṉōṭu eytiyatu urai eṉa - im maṇimēkalaiyuṭaṉ īṇṭu eytiya kāraṇattaik kūṟuvāyāka eṉa;
ciṟai-aṇai. niṟai-kāmattai uḷḷēyaṭakki niṟuttutal; maṉattai niṟuttalumām; kāḻkkoḷiṉ-vairamēṟiṉ ; mutirntāl eṉṟapaṭi; 1 "nīr mikiṟ ciṟaiyumillai" 2"nīrmikiṉillai ciṟai" 3"ciṟaiyeṉpa tillaic cevvē cempuṉal peruku māyiṉ, niṟai yeṉpa tillaik kāma nērniṉṟu ciṟakkumāyiṉ" 4"piṟitu mākupa kāmaṅkāḻk koḷiṉē" eṉpaṉa īṇṭu aṟiyaṟpālaṉa. tavattaḷ, caratti, vāymaiyaḷ eṉpavaṟṟaic cevviyaḷ eṉa aṭakkik kūṟiṉāṉ. ākeṉa, vikāram, avviyam-piṟark kuriyaḷātal kūṭā teṉṉum poṟāmai; kōṭṭamumām. añceñ cāyal, viḷi. arāntāṇam eṉpataṟku mēl (3 : 87) uraittamai kāṇka. 
28-31.	vār kaḻal vēntē vāḻka niṉ kaṇṇi - nīṇṭa vīrakkaḻalai yaṇinta aracē niṉ kaṇṇi vāḻvatāka, tīneṟippaṭarā neñciṉai ākumati-tīyavaḻiyiṟ cellāta uḷḷamuṭaiyai āvāyāka, īṅkivaḷ taṉṉōṭu eytiya kāraṇam vīṅkunīrñālam āḷvōy kēṭṭaruḷ-im maṇimēkalaiyuṭaṉ yāṉ vanta kāraṇattaik kaṭalcūḻnta nilavulakiṉaiyāḷum maṉṉavaṉē kēṭṭaruḷ vāyāka;
32-40.	yāppuṭai uḷḷattu em aṉai iḻantōṉ pārppaṉa mutumakaṉ-eṉ tāyai iḻantōṉum uṟutiporuntiya uḷḷattai yuṭaiya pārppaṉa mutiyōṉum, paṭima uṇṭiyaṉ-paṭṭiṇiviṭṭuṇṇum nōṉpiṉai yuṭaiyōṉum, maḻaivaḷam tarūum aḻal ōmpāḷaṉ - maḻaivaḷattait tarum muttīyōmpu vōṉumākiya entai, paḻaviṉaip payattāl piḻaimaṇam eytiya eṟkeṭuttu iraṅki-muṉ ceyviṉaiyiṉ payaṉāl māruta vēkaṉiṭam akappaṭṭup piḻaimaṇam eytiya eṉṉaik kāṇāmaiyāl varunti, taṉ takavuṭaimaiyiṉ - taṉatu takuti yuṭaimaiyāl, kuraṅkucey kaṭal kumariyam peruntuṟaip parantu cel mākka ḷoṭu-kuraṅku ceyta kumarikkaṭaliṉ periya tuṟaikkaṇ nīrāṭutaṟku mikkuc cellum makkaḷōṭē, tēṭiṉaṉ peyarvōṉ - eṉṉait tēṭic celpavaṉāy, kaṭal maṇṭu peruntuṟaik kāviri āṭiya-iṭaiyē kāviri kaṭaliṟ kalakkum periya caṅka mukattuṟaiyil nīrāṭutaṟku, vaṭamoḻi yāḷaroṭu varuvōṉ- pārppaṉarōṭu varupavaṉ;
maḻaivaḷan tarūum aḻal eṉka; 1"maḻaikkaru vuyirkku maḻaṟṟika ḻaṭṭiṉ, maṟaiyō rākkiya vāvuti naṟumpukai" eṉpatu kāṇka. māruta vēkaṉukkut tāṉ cilanāḷ uriyaḷāṉatu paṟṟi, "piḻaimaṇa meytiya" eṉṟāḷ. kumari-kaṉṉiyākumari ; 2"toṭiyōḷ pauvamum" eṉpataṉ urai nōkkuka. vaṭamoḻiyāḷar-pārppaṉar ; pārppaṉiyai "vaṭamoḻiyāṭṭi" (13:73) eṉṟu piṉṉark kūṟuvar.
40-46.	kaṇṭu īṅku-eṉṉai innakarattiṟ kaṇṭu, yāṅṅaṉam vantaṉai eṉmakaḷ eṉṟē tāṅkāk kaṇṇīr eṉṟalai utirt tāṅku-eṉ makaḷē īṇṭu evvāṟu vantāy eṉṟu kūṟip perukiya kaṇṇīrai eṉmītu corintu, ōtal antaṇarkku ovvēṉ āyiṉum-yāṉ maṟaiyōtum antaṇarkaḷuṭaṉirut taṟkut takāta vaḷāyiṉum, kātalaṉ ātaliṉ kaiviṭalī yāṉ - eṉmītu mikunta aṉpuṭaiyaṉātalāl eṉṉaik kaiviṭāṉāy, irantūṇ talaikkoṇṭu-irantuṇṭu vāḻtalai mēṟkoṇṭu, innakar maruṅkil-iv'd2vūriṉ kaṇ, parantupaṭu maṉaitoṟum tirivōṉ - parantu tōṉṟum illaṅkaḷ tōṟum ceṉṟu ēṟpōṉ; 
kaiviṭalīyāṉ-kaiviṭāṉ; "kāvalaṉ makaṉō kaiviṭalīyāṉ" (19:32) eṉpar piṉṉum; "kāṭṭiyatātaliṟ kaiviṭalīyāṉ" (13:85) eṉpatu cilappatikāram.
46-52.	orunāḷ puṉiṟṟuā pāynta vayiṟṟup puṇṇiṉaṉ-oru nāḷ īṉṟaṇimaiyaiyuṭaiya pacuvoṉṟu pāyntamaiyāl vayiṟṟiluṇṭāṉa puṇṇiṉai yuṭaiyaṉāy, kaṇaviri mālai kaik koṇṭeṉṉa - alarimālaiyaik kaiyiṟ
koṇṭāṟpōla,  niṇamnīṭu peruṅkuṭar kaiyakattu ēnti-niṇattuṭaṉ nīṇṭa periya kuṭaraik kaiyil ēntik
koṇṭu, eṉmakaḷ irunta iṭa meṉṟu eṇṇi-paṇṭu eṉmakaḷ irunta iṭamākum eṉṟu niṉaintu, taṉ uṟu tuṉpam tāṅkātu pukuntu - taṉatu mikka tuṉpattiṉait tāṅka viyalāmaṟ pukuntu, camaṇīrkāḷ num caraṇ eṉṟōṉai - camaṇarkaḷē ummuṭaiya aṭaikkalam eṉṟu kūṟiyavaṉai; 
pāynta puṇ: peyareccam kāraṇap poruṭṭāyatu; āṟu ceṉṟa viyar eṉpuḻippōla. 1"niṇavarik kuṟaintavatar toṟuṅ, kaṇavira mālai yiṭūukkaḻin taṉṉai, puṇṇumiḻ kuruti parippak kiṭantōr" eṉa uvamaṅ kūṟiyiruppatu aṟiyaṟpālatu. iṭameṉṟu-iṭa mākaliṉ purantaruḷvareṉṟu.
53-57.	ivaṇ nīr alla eṉṟu eṉṉoṭum vekuṇṭu - iv viṭattiṟkuriya taṉmai aṉṟeṉṟu kūṟi eṉṉaiyum ciṉantu, maiyaṟu paṭivattu mātavar puṟattu emaik kaiyutirk kōṭaliṉ- kuṟṟamaṟṟa tavavaṭivattai yuṭaiya camaṇa muṉivarkaḷ puṟattē pōkumāṟu emmaik kaiyai acaittuk kuṟittaliṉāl, kaṇ niṟai nīrēm-kaṇkaḷil niṟainta nīriṉaiyuṭaiyēmāy, aṟavōr uḷīrō ārum ilōm eṉa - aṟampurivīr irukkiṉṟīrō pātukāppōr yārumillātēm eṉṟu, puṟavōr vītiyil pulampoṭu cāṟṟa - puṟattiluḷḷa oru vītiyil varuttattuṭaṉ kūṟa;
ivaṇīralla - ivviṭattiṟkuriyīrallīr eṉṟumām ; alla eṉpatu pālkuṟiyātu vaḻakkuppaṟṟi niṉṟatu. eṉṉoṭum - eṉṉaiyum; ummai: eccam; tantaiyai eṉṉoṭum eṉṟumām. paṭivam - tava vēṭam. ōr puṟavītiyi leṉka ; puṟavōr - puṟattiluḷḷōr eṉṟuraippārumuḷar.
58-70.	maṅkul tōy māṭa maṉaitoṟum pukūum aṅkaiyiṟ koṇṭa pāttiram uṭaiyōṉ - vāṉaḷāviya māṭaṅkaḷai yuṭaiya maṉaikaḷtōṟum cellum kaiyiṟkoṇṭa piccaip pāttirattaiyuṭaiyavaṉum, katircuṭum amayattup paṉimati mukattōṉ-ñāyiṟu kāyum naṇpakaṟ poḻutil kuḷircci poruntiya matipōlum mukattiṉai yuṭaiya vaṉum, poṉṉil tikaḻum polampū āṭaiyaṉ-poṉ pōl viḷaṅkum aḻakiya āṭaiyai uṭaiyavaṉumāki, eṉ uṟṟaṉirō eṉṟu emai nōkki-eṉṉa tuṉpam eytiṉīr eṉṟu eṅkaḷai nōkki, aṉpuṭaṉ aḷaiiya aruḷ moḻi ataṉāl - aṉpoṭu kalanta aruḷ mikunta iṉ moḻikaḷāl, añcevi niṟaittu neñcakam kuḷirppittu - ceviyakattai niṟaittu uḷḷattaik kuḷiracceytu, taṉkaip pāttiram eṉkait tantu-taṉ kaiyiluḷḷa pāttirattai eṉ kaiyil koṭuttuviṭṭu, entaikku uṟṟa iṭumpai nīṅka - eṉ tantaikku eytiya tuṉpam nīṅkumāṟu, eṭuttaṉaṉ taḻīik kaṭuppat talaiyēṟṟi-viraiyat taḻuvi eṭuttut taṉ uṭampiṉiṭattē cumantu ceṉṟu, mātavar uṟaiviṭam kāṭṭiya maṟaiyōṉ-putta muṉivarkaḷ uṟaiviṭattaik kāṭṭiya maṟaiyavaṉākiya, cātuyar nīkkiya talaivaṉ-entaiyiṉ iṟapput tuṉpattai nīkkik kāppāṟṟiya talaivaṉ, tavamuṉi caṅkatarumaṉ - caṅkatarumaṉ eṉṉum tavattaiyuṭaiya muṉivaṉāvāṉ; 
illaṟattōr yāvarum uṇṭapiṉ uccippoḻutiṟ piccaikkuc celvatu puttamatat tuṟaviyar iyalpākaliṉ, "katircuṭumama yattu" eṉṟār ; "veṅkati ramayattu viyaṉpoḻi lakavayiṉ, vantu tōṉṟalum," "veyilviḷaṅ kamayattu viḷaṅkit tōṉṟiya, cātu cakkaraṉ ṟaṉṉaiyāṉūṭṭiya" (10 : 27 - 8 ; 11 : 102 -3) eṉap piṉṉar varutaluṅ kāṇka. marutan tuvaril tōykkappaṭṭu mañcaḷ niṟamuḷḷatākaliṉ "poṉṉiṟ ṟikaḻuma polampū vāṭai" eṉap paṭṭatu. aṉpum aruḷumuṭaiya moḻiyā leṉka. kaṭuppa-viraiya. talai-iṭam. āṭaiyaṉāy nōkki niṟaittuk kuḷirppittut tantu ēṟṟik kāṭṭiya maṟaiyōṉākiya talaivaṉ caṅkatarumaṉ āvāṉeṉka. 
70-79.	tāṉ emakku aruḷiya -avaṉ emakku aṟivuṟutta, eṅkōṉ iyalkuṇaṉ ētamil kuṇapporuḷ - emmuṭaiya iṟaivaṉ iyalpākavē tōṉṟiya kuṇaṅkaḷai yuṭaiyavaṉ kuṟṟamaṟṟa kuṇaṅkaḷiṉ poruḷāyuḷḷōṉ, ulaka nōṉpiṉ palakati uṇarntu taṉakkeṉa vāḻāp piṟarkkuriyāḷaṉ - ulakacīlattuṭaṉ pala piṟappukkaḷilum piṟantu piṟantu uṇarntu uyirkaṭku nallaṟivu pukaṭṭiya taṉakkeṉṟu uyir vāḻāta piṟarkkē urimaipūṇṭoḻukupavaṉ, iṉpaccevvi maṉpatai eyta-niruvāṇamaṭaitaṟkuriya paruvattai uyirppaṉmaikaḷ aṭaiya, aruḷ aṟam pūṇṭa oru perum pūṭkaiyiṉ - aruḷākiya aṟattiṉaippūṇṭa oppaṟṟa periya mēṟkōḷiṉālē, aṟakkatir āḻi tiṟappaṭa uruṭṭi-aṟamākiya katirkaḷaiyuṭaiya āḻiyai ulakattiṟ palavakaiyāka uruṭṭi, kāmaṟ kaṭanta vāmaṉ pātam - māraṉai veṉṟa puttaṉatu tiruvaṭikaḷai, takai pārāṭṭutal allatu yāvatum mikainā illēṉ - pārāṭṭit tutittalaiyaṉṟi vēṟuporuḷkaḷaip paṟṟic ciṟitum pēcāta nāviṉaiyuṭaiyēṉ, vēntē vāḻka eṉa-maṉṉava vāḻvāyāka eṉṟu cutamati kūṟa; 
ulaka nōṉpu-ilvāḻkkaiyiliruntu ceyyum nōṉpu, oḻukkam; ataṉāl illaṟattār ulakanōṉpikaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. kati- piṟappu. puttaṉ aruḷālē palavakaip piṟappum eṭuttāṉeṉap puttacātakak kataikaḷ kūṟāniṟkum: 1"eṟumpukaṭai yayaṉmutalā veṇṇiṟanta veṉṟuraikkap piṟantiṟanta yōṉitoṟum piriyātu cūḻpōki, evvuṭampi levvuyirkkum yātoṉṟā liṭareytiṉ, avvuṭampi luyirkkuyirā yaruḷpoḻiyun tiruvuḷḷam" 2"vāṉāṭum pariyāyu mariṇa māyum vaṉkēḻaṟ kaḷiṟāyu meṇkāṟ puṇmāṉ, tāṉāyum paṇaiyerumai yoruttalāyun taṭakkaiyiḷaṅ kaḷiṟāyuñ caṭaṅka māyum, mīṉāyu muyalāyu maṉṉa māyu mayilāyum piṟavāyum velluñ ciṅka, māṉāyuṅkolai kaḷavu kaṭpoy kāmam varaintavartā muṟaintapati māṉā 'd2vūrē" eṉpaṉa kāṇka. puttaṉ taṉak keṉa vāḻāp piṟarkkuriyāḷaṉ eṉṟa karuttu "piṟarkkaṟa muyalum periyōy," "taṉṉuyirk kiraṅkāṉ piṟavuyi rōmpu ; maṉṉuyir mutalvaṉ" (11 ; 45 ;55; 114-7) eṉa innūluḷḷē piṉṉa rum vantuḷḷaṉa; 3"iruṭpāra viṉainīkki yevvuyirkkuṅ kāvaleṉa, aruṭpāran taṉicumanta vaṉṟumuta liṉṟaḷavum, matuvoṉṟu malaraṭikkīḻ vantaṭaintōr yāvarkkum, potuvaṉṟi niṉak kurittō puṇṇiyaniṉ ṟirumēṉi," "tītiyal puliyatu pacikeṭu vakainiṉa tiruvuru varuḷiya tirumali perumaiyai," eṉpaṉavum kāṇka. ciṟukuṭikiḻāṉ paṇṇaṉ eṉpāṉaic ciṟappikku miṭattu akappāṭṭilē (54) ittoṭar muḻutum vantuḷḷatu; aruḷaṟam- aruḷākiya aṟam ; aruḷē ellā aṟaṅkaḷilum mēlāyatu eṉpatu 1"oṉṟāka nallatu kollāmai" "ahimsā paramōtarma:" eṉpa vaṟṟāṉ aṟiyappaṭum. katir - tikiriyiṉ ār. aṟattai āḻiyeṉṟa taṟkēṟpa ‘uruṭṭi' eṉṟār ; palavakai yālum avaṉ aṟavupatēcañ ceytamaiyiṉ ‘tiṟampaṭa' eṉṟār; "āti mutalva ṉaṟavāḻi yāḷ vōṉ" (10 : 31 eṉpār piṉṉum; itupaṟṟiyē puttaṉait taruma cakkarap piravarttaṉācāriyaṉ eṉpar. kāmaṉ - māraṉ; aṟattiṟku māṟāṉa viruppaṅkaḷai maṉattiluṇṭākkum oru tēvaṉ; māpōti virukkattiṉ kīḻē nōṟṟirukkaiyil ivaṉ ceyta iṭaiyūṟukaḷai yellām veṉṟu viḷaṅkiṉamaiyiṉ "kāmaṟkaṭanta vāmaṉ" eṉṟār. vāmaṉ - aḻakuḷḷavaṉ ; puttaṉ. takaipāraṭṭutal-tutittal; oru col. mikai nā - mikaikkiṉṟa nā ; 2"kāmaṉai veṉṟō ṉāyiratteṭṭu, nāma mallatu navilā teṉṉā" eṉpatu aṟiyaṟ pālatu.
80-83.	añcol āyiḻai niṉtiṟam aṟintēṉ-aḻakiya moḻiyaiyuṭaiya āyiḻāy niṉ varalāṟṟiṉai aṟintēṉ, vañcinuṇ iṭai maṇimēkalaitaṉai - koṭipōlum nuṇṇiya iṭaiyaiyuṭaiya maṇimēkalaiyai. cittirāpatiyāl cērtalum uṇṭeṉṟu-cittirāpatiyāl aṭaitaluṅkūṭum eṉṟu kūṟi, ap poḻil āṅkavaṉ ayarntu pōyapiṉ - utayakumaraṉ ap poḻiliṉiṉṟum kāmattāl taḷarcci yuṭaiyaṉāyc ceṉṟa piṉṉar; 
84-93.	paḷikkaṟai tiṟantu-maṇimēkalai paḷikku maṇṭapattait tiṟantu, paṉimati mukattuk kaḷikkayal piṟaḻāk kāṭciyaḷ  āki- kuḷirmati pōlum mukattilē matartta kayalpōluṅ kaṇkaḷ piṟaḻāta kāṭciyaiyuṭaiyaḷāy, kaṟputtāṉ ilaḷ naṟṟava uṇarvilaḷ varuṇak kāppu ilaḷ poruḷ vilaiyāṭṭi eṉṟu-kaṟpillātavaḷ nalla tavavuṇarcciyillātavaḷ marapiṟkēṟṟa kāvalaṟṟavaḷ poruḷ koṭuppārkkut taṉṉai viṟkum vilaimakaḷ eṉṟu ivvāṟāka, ikaḻntaṉaṉ āki nayantōṉ eṉṉātu-paḻitturait tavaṉāy virumpiṉōṉ eṉa niṉaiyāmal, putuvōṉ piṉṟaip pōṉatu eṉ neñcam - ētilāṉ piṉṉē eṉṉuṭaiya uḷḷam ceṉṟatu; ituvō aṉṉāy kāmattu iyaṟkai - aṉṉaiyē iṅṅaṉa muḷḷatō kāmattiṉ iyalpu, ituvē āyiṉ keṭuka taṉtiṟam eṉa-ivvāṟāyiṉ itaṉvali keṭuvatāka eṉṟu, matumalark kuḻalāḷ maṇimēkalai tāṉ cutamati taṉṉoṭu niṉṟa ellaiyuḷ-tēṉaviḻum malarkaḷai yaṇinta kūntalaiyuṭaiya avaḷ cutamatiyōṭum kūṟiniṉṟa poḻutil;
cutamatiyai nōkkiya pārvai vilakātu niṉṟāḷeṉpar ‘kaḷik kayal piṟaḻāk kāṭciyaḷāki' eṉṟār. varuṇak kāppu - varuṇa mākiya kāval eṉṟumām. maṇimēkalai tiṟattu kāṭciyaḷākik keṭuka taṉṟiṟameṉa niṉṟa vellaiyuḷ eṉka.
94-97.	intira kōṭaṇai viḻāaṇi virumpi vantukāṇkuṟūum maṇimēkalāteyvam - intirakōṭaṇaiyākiya viḻāviṉ eḻilaik kāṇutaṟku virumpi vanta maṇimēkalā teyvam, patiyakattu uṟaiyum ōr paintoṭiyāki - appatiyiṉ kaṇirukkiṟa oru peṇ vaṭivattuṭaṉ, maṇiyaṟaip pīṭikai valaṅkoṇṭu ōṅki - paḷikkaṟaiyiluḷḷa pīṭikaiyai valamvantu vicumpil uyarntu; 
kōṭaṇai - muḻakkam; palavakai āravāraṅkaḷai yuṭaimaiyiṉ, intira viḻā-intira kōṭaṇai yeṉappaṭṭatu. "intira kōṭaṇai yinnakark kāṇa," "intira kōṭaṇai viḻavaṇi varunāḷ" (7. 17, 17; 59) eṉap piṉ varutal kāṇka. maṇi-paḷiṅku, 
98-108.	pulavaṉ-meyyaṟi vuṭaiyōṉē, tīrttaṉ-tūyōṉē, puṇṇiyaṉ - aṟavōṉē, purāṇaṉ - paḻaiyōṉē, ulaka nōṉpiṉ uyarntōy-ulaka nōṉpiṉāl mēm paṭṭavaṉē, eṉkō - eṉpēṉō, kuṟṟam keṭuttōy - mukkuṟṟaṅkaḷaiyum aḻittōy, ceṟṟam ceṟuttōy - kaṭuñciṉattaik kaṭintōy, muṟṟa uṇarnta mutalvā-muḻutum uṇarnta mutaṉmai yuṭaiyōy, eṉkō-eṉpēṉō, kāmaṟ kaṭantōy - māraṉai veṉṟōy, ēmam āyōy-iṉpa mayamāy uḷḷōy, tī neṟik kaṭumpakai kaṭintōy-tīya neṟiyākiya kaṭiya pakaiyai nīkkiṉōy, eṉkō-eṉpēṉō, āyiraārattu āḻiyaṉ tiruntaṭi- āyiram āraṅkaḷaik koṇṭa aṟavāḻiyiṉaiyuṭaiya niṉcevviya tiruvaṭikaḷai, nāāyiram ilēṉ ēttuvatu evaṉ eṉṟu - āyiram nāvillā yāṉ evvāṟu tutikka viyalum eṉṟu, eri maṇip pūṅkoṭi irunila maruṅkuvantu-cuṭar viṭukiṉṟa māṇikkap pūṅkoṭiyāṉatu periya pūmiyiṉ pakkalil vantu, oru taṉi tirivatu ottu-taṉṉan taṉiyē tirivatupōla, ōtiyiṉ otuṅki- meyññāṉat tuṭaṉ otuṅki, nilavarai iṟantu ōr muṭaṅkunā nīṭṭum-nilavellaiyaik kaṭantu taṉatu vaḷainta nāvai nīṭṭu kiṉṟāḷ; 
kuṟṟam-kāma vekuḷi mayakkaṅkaḷ. ēmam - pātukāvalumām. ‘āḻiyan tiruntaṭi' eṉṟa pāṭattiṟku āyiram āraṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya cakkara rēkai poruntiya tiruntiya aṭi eṉṟuraikka. nāvāyira milēṉ ēttuva tevaṉ eṉṟatu eṉ oru nāvil aṭaṅkuva taṉṟu eṉṟapaṭi. ōti - mukkāla uṇarvu. nilavarai iṟantu - puviyiṉ mēlē uyarntu. ōr: acai. nānīṭṭum - collum.
109-118.	pula varai iṟanta pukār eṉum pūṅkoṭi - aṟiviṉ ellaiyaik kaṭanta pukār eṉṉuma pūṅkoṭi, paṉmalar ciṟanta nalnīr akaḻip puḷ oli ciṟanta teḷ aric cilampu aṭi - pala malarkaḷum ciṟantu viḷaṅkukiṉṟa nalla nīriṉaiyuṭaiya puṭkaḷiṉ oli mikka akaḻiyākiya ciṟanta teḷḷiya ariyiṉaiyuṭaiya cilam paṇinta aṭiyaiyum, ñāyil iñci nakai maṇimēkalai - ñāyilaiyuṭaiya matilākiya oḷḷiya maṇikaḷālāya mēkalaiyaiyum, vāyil maruṅku iyaṉṟa vāṉ paṇaittōḷi - vāyiliṉ pakkalil amainta periya tōraṇa kampamākiya tōḷaiyum uṭaiyāḷ, tarunilai vacciram eṉa iru kōṭṭam etir etir ōṅkiyakatir  iḷavaṉamulai-etiretir uyarnta kaṟpakat taru niṟkum kōyil vaccirappaṭai niṟkum kōyil eṉṉumiraṇṭu mākiya oḷi poruntiya iḷaiya aḻakiya koṅkai kaḷaiyum, ār puṉai vēntaṟkup pēraḷavu iyaṟṟi - āttimālai cūṭum cōḻaṉukkup periya aḷaviṉatāka iyaṟṟappaṭṭu; ūḻi eṇṇi nīṭu niṉṟu ōṅkiya - eṇṇappaṭum pala ūḻikkālamāka nilaipeṟṟuyarnta, oru perum kōyil tirumuka vāṭṭi-oppaṟṟa kōyilākiya tirumukattiṉaiyum uṭaiyāḷ: 
pula varai - aṟiviṉ ellai; 1"pulavarai yiṟanta pukaḻcāl tōṉṟal" 2"pulavarai yiṟantōy pōkutal poṟēeṉ" eṉ paṉa kāṇka. malar cilampum, puḷḷoḷi cilampoliyum, akaḻi aṭi yumām. ñāyil - matiliṉ ōruṟuppu; ēppuḻaikku naṭuvāy eytumaṟaiyuñ cūṭṭeṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar; kuruvit talaiyeṉpar aṭiyārkku nallār. paṇai - mūṅkil; īṇṭut tōraṇa kampam. etiretirōṅkiya irukōṭṭameṉka. vacciralai eṉa virittuk koḷka. vēntaṟkēṟpap periya aḷaviṉatāka iyaṟṟappaṭṭa kōyil; 1"perumpeyar maṉṉark koppa maṉai vakuttu" eṉpatuṅ kāṇka-cilampu, mēkalai eṉpavaṟṟiṟkēṟpa aṭaikaḷ puṇartti yuḷḷār.
