119-122.	kuṇaticai maruṅkil nāḷ mutir matiyamum-kīḻtticaiyil nāṉ nirampiya muḻu matiyamum, kuṭaticai maruṅkil ceṉṟu vīḻ katirum-mēṟṟicaiyil ceṉṟu paṭikiṉṟa ñāyiṟum, veḷḷi veṇ tōṭṭoṭu poṉ tōṭāka-veḷḷiyālākiya veṇ tōṭṭuṭaṉ poṉṉālāya tōṭumāka, eḷḷaṟu tirumukam poliyap peytalum-ikaḻtalaṟṟa aḻakiya mukam poliyumāṟu aṇitalum; 
nāḷ-kalai. niṟaiyuvā nāḷil mati kīḻtticaikkaṇ utittum, pariti mēṟṟicaiyiṟ paṭintum kōyiliṉ iru maruṅkum oruṅku tōṉṟutaliṉ, avaṟṟaip pukārc celvi kōyilākiya mukattiṉ iru maruṅkum tōṭāka aṇintaṉaḷ; eṉṟāḷ; eṉavē antip poḻutu vantamai peṟṟām; itu pariyāyavaṇi. tirumukavāṭṭi peytalum eṉka. 
123-141.	aṉṉac cēval ayarntu viḷaiyāṭiya taṉṉuṟu peṭaiyait tāmarai aṭakka - aṉṉac cēvalāṉatu meymmaṟantu viḷaiyāṭiya taṉatu pēṭaiyait tāmarai malar kuvintu taṉṉuḷ aṭakkik koḷḷa pūmpoti citaiyak kiḻittup peṭai koṇṭu ōṅku irun teṅkiṉ uyar maṭal ēṟa-pūliṉatu kaṭṭu aḻiyumāṟu kiḻittup pēṭṭiṉaik koṇṭu uyarnta periya teṉṉaiyiṉatu vaḷarnta maṭaliṟ cēravum, aṉṟil pēṭaiarik kural aḻaiic ceṉṟu vīḻpoḻutu cēvaṟku icaippa-aṉṟiliṉ pēṭu melliya kuralāl cēvalai aḻaittu ñāyiṟuceṉṟu maṟaiyum antip poḻutai uraikkavum, pavaḷac ceṅkāl paṟavaik kāṉattuk kuvaḷai mēynta kuṭakkaṭ cētā-pavaḷampōṟ civanta kālkaḷaiyuṭaiya aṉṉappuṭkaḷ niṟainta kāṉattil kuvaḷai malarai mēynta tiraṇṭa kaṇkaḷaiyuṭaiya cevviya pacukkaḷ, mulaipoḻi tīmpāl eḻu tukaḷ avippa - maṭiyiliruntu poḻikiṉṟa iṉiya pāl nilattiṉiṉṟum eḻukiṉṟa puḻutiyai avippa, kaṉṟu niṉai kurala maṉṟu vaḻip paṭā-kaṉṟukaḷai niṉainta kuralai yuṭaiyaṉavāy maṉṟukaḷiṉ vaḻiyē cellavum, anti antaṇar centīp pēṇa - antaṇarkaḷ antic cen tīyai vaḷarkkavum, paintoṭi makaḷir palar viḷakku eṭuppa - paciya vaḷaiyalaṇinta makaḷir palar viḷakkukaḷai ēṟṟavum, yāḻōr marutattu iṉṉaram puḷara - yāḻiṉaiyuṭaiya pāṇar yāḻiṉ narampiṉai varuṭi iṉiya marutap paṇṇai yeḻuppavum, kōvalar mullai kuḻal mēṟkoḷḷa - kōvalar mullaip paṇṇai vēyṅ kuḻaliṉiṭamāka icaippavum, amaraka maruṅkil kaṇavaṉai iḻantu-pōrkkaḷattil kaṇavaṉai iḻantu, tamar akam pukūum oru makaḷ pōla-tammavariṭam cellum oru maṅkaiyaippōla, katir āṟṟuppaṭutta mutirāt tuṉpamōṭu - ñāyiṟṟiṉaip pōkkiya muṭivillāta tuṉpattuṭaṉ, anti eṉṉum pacalai meyyāṭṭi - antip poḻutu eṉkiṉṟa pacalai pōrtta mēṉiyai yuṭaiyāḷ, vantu iṟuttaṉaḷāl mānakar maruṅku eṉ - periya nakarattiṉ maruṅkē vantu taṅkiṉaḷ eṉka. 
ayarntu - virumpi eṉṟumām. aṭakka - aṭakkalāl. iraviṟ ciṟitum pirintiruttalarumaiyiṉ ‘cēvaṟ kicaippa' eṉṟār. kāṉam - īṇṭu nīrnilai. cētā - cevvip pacu ; cenniṟamuṭaiya pacuvumām. kaṉṟiṉai niṉaintamaiyāṟ pāl poḻivaṉavāyiṉa. maṉṟu-āṉiṉaṅkaḷai aṭaittu vaikkum vēli naṭuvaṇatāya veḷiyiṭam; koṭṭilumām. pakalil mēyacceṉṟa āṉiṉaṅkaḷ mālaippoḻutil kaṉṟai niṉaintu aḻaikkuṅ kuralōṭu pōntu maṉṟilē pukutal iyalpu; 1''āṉkaṇam, kaṉṟu payirkurala maṉṟuniṟai pukutara,'' 2"matavu naṭai nallāṉ, vīṅkumāṇ cerutta ṟīmpāl piliṟṟak, kaṉṟu payir kurala maṉṟuniṟai pukutaru, mālaiyum'' eṉpaṉavuṅ kāṇka. eṭuttal - koḷuttal, 'yāḻōr...puḷara' - īṇṭu marutattiṟkuriya iyaipu pulappaṭṭilatu; ivvaṭi iṭaippukuttup pōlum. 'mullaik kuḻal' eṉṟa pāṭattiṟku mullaiyaik kuḻalukku aṭaiyākkuka ; eṉṉai? 3"mullaik koṭiyāl muppuriyākat teṟṟiya vaḷaiyai vaḷaivāyk kaṭceṟittūtaliṉ mullaik kuḻalāyiṟṟu" eṉa aṭiyārkku nallār uraittaliṉ eṉka. tamar-tantai, tamaiyaṉ mutaliyōr. mutirtal īṇṭu muṭital; mēl mutirtaṟku iṭaṉillāta eṉṟumām. kaṇavaṉaip pirintāṭku mey pacakkumākaliṉ ‘pacalai meyyāṭṭi' eṉṟār; īṇṭup pacalaiyāvatu antip poḻutiṉ puṟkeṉṟa niṟam. tirumuka vāṭṭi tōṭākap peytalum pacalai meyyāṭṭi mānakar maruṅku vantiṟuttaṉaḷ eṉka. 
pūṅkoṭi uruvam peyarppa, cūḻvōṉ cutamati muka nōkki uraiyeṉa, uraippa, akalvōṉ eytiyatu uraiyeṉa, avaḷ caṅkatarumaṉ aruḷiya vāmaṉ pātattaip pārāṭṭu
talallatu mikainā illēṉ eṉa, avaṉ pōyapiṉ, maṇimēkalai cutamatiyōṭu niṉṟa vellaiyuḷ, maṇimēkalā teyvam pain toṭiyākip pīṭikaiyai valaṅkoṇṭu ōṅki otuṅki nānīṭṭum ; nīṭṭuḻi, tirumukavāṭṭi peytalum, pacalai meyyāṭṭi mutirāt tuṉpamoṭu mānakar maruṅku vantiṟuttaṉaḷ eṉṟu muṭikka. 
maṇimēkalā teyvam vantu tōṉṟiya 
kātai muṟṟiṟṟu. 


6. cakkaravāḷakkōṭṭa muraitta kātai 
antimālai vantu nīṅkiyatu. tiṅkaḷmaṇṭilam veḷḷik kuṭattiṉiṉṟu pāl corivatupōl taṇkatirai eṅkaṇum poḻintatu; maṇimēkalā teyvam mīṭṭum putta tēvaṉatu pātapīṭikaiyai ētti, aṅku niṉṟa cutamatiyai nōkki, 'nīṅkaḷ iṅkē niṟṟaṟkuk kāraṇam yātu? eṉṉa tuṉpamuṟṟīrkaḷ?' eṉa viṉava, avaḷ utayakumaraṉ vantu kūṟic ceṉṟataic colliṉaḷ. collalum, atteyvam avaḷai nōkki, "utayakumaraṉukku maṇimēkalaipāluḷḷa vēṭkai ciṟitum taṇintilatu; aṟavōr vaṉameṉṟu itiṉiṉṟu avaṉ akaṉṟaṉaṉāyiṉum, itaṉaik kaṭantu celliṉ puṟattuḷḷa vītiyil ivaḷai akappaṭuttuvaṉ; nīṅkaḷ ivvaṉattaic cūḻnta matiliṉ mēṟṟicaikkaṇṇatākiya ciṟiya vāyil vaḻiyē ceṉṟu mātavar uṟaiyum cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭattai aṭaiyiṉ yātoru tuṉpamum aṇukātu: ākaliṉ aṅkē ceṉmiṉ." eṉṟuraittatu. uraittalum, cutamati, 'yāvarum ataṉaic cuṭukāṭṭuk kōṭṭa meṉṟu kūṟāniṟpar; māruta vēkaṉum nīyumē cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭa meṉṟu kūṟuvīr; ataṟkuk kāraṇam yātu? colluka.' eṉṟāḷ. 
eṉṟalum at teyvam, kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉam tōṉṟiya kālattu uṭaṉṟōṉṟiyatāṉa appuṟaṅkāṭṭiṉ iyalpaṉaittaiyum viḷaṅkak kūṟi, "tavattuṟai mākkaḷ mikapperuñ celvar, īṟṟiḷam peṇṭir āṟṟāp pālakar, mutiyōr eṉṉāṉ iḷaiyōr eṉṉāṉ, koṭun toḻilāḷaṉ koṉṟaṉaṉ kuvippa iv aḻalvāyc cuṭalai tiṉṉak kaṇṭum, kaḻiperuñ celvak kaḷḷāṭṭayarntu, mikka nallaṟam virumpātu vāḻum, makkaḷiṟ ciṟanta maṭavō ruṇṭō?" eṉa aṟañ ceyyātār poruṭṭu iraṅki, "attaṉmaiyatāṉa cuṭukāṭṭil taṉiyē ceṉṟu pēykōṭpaṭṭu iṟanta cārṅkala ṉeṉṉum pārppaṉac ciṟuvaṉ tāyākiya kōtamaiyiṉ muṟaiyīṭṭaik kēṭṭuvanta campāpati yeṉṉum teyvam, "aṇaṅkum pēyum āruyir kavarā ; niṉ makaṉatu aṟiyāmaiyē paṟṟukkōṭāka iḻavūḻ avaṉuyiraik kavarntuceṉṟatu; niṉ uyirai vāṅkikkoṇṭu avaṉuyirai aḷittal iyalpaṉṟu ! uyir uṭampiṉiṉṟu nīṅkiṉ atu viṉaivaḻiyē ceṉṟu vēṟu piṟappaṭaivatil aiyamillai; ‘ulaka maṉṉavarkku uyirkkuyir īvōr ilarō, inta īmap puṟaṅkāṭṭu, aracark kamaintaṉa āyiraṅkōṭṭam' allavō ? ic cakkaravāḷat tuḷḷa tēvarkaḷil yārēṉum nī kēṭṭa varam koṭuppāruḷarāyiṉ yāṉum ataṉait tarutaṟ kuriyēṉāvēṉ," eṉṟuraittu, pirama kaṇaṅkaḷ mutalākac cakkaravāḷattiluḷḷa varaṅkoṭukkum āṟṟaluṭaiya tēvarkaḷai yellāmvaruvittuk kōtamaikku muṉṉiṟutti, ivaḷuṟṟa tuṉpam itu ; ivaḷatu varuttattaip pōkkuvīrāka, eṉṟu nikaḻntavaṟṟaic colla, attēvaraṉaivarum campāpatikūṟiya vāṟē kūṟiṉar; ataṉaik kēṭṭu uṇmai yaṟintu oruvāṟu varutta moḻinta kōtamai makaṉaip puṟaṅkāṭṭiliṭṭu iṟantu pōyiṉaḷ.
piṉpu campāpatiyiṉ āṟṟalai yāvarkkum pulappaṭuttal vēṇṭi, ellāt tēvarkaḷum kūṭiyaviṭattu avarkaḷ kūṭiyataṟku aṟikuṟiyāka, ulakiṉ naṭuvēyuḷḷa mērumalaiyum, ataṉaic cūḻnta eḻuvakaik kuṉṟaṅkaḷum nāṉku peruntīvukaḷum, iraṇṭāyiram ciṟu tīvukaḷum, ēṉaiya iṭa vakaikaḷum ākiya ivaṟṟaip pulappaṭutti, āṅkāṅku vāḻum uyirkaḷaiyum maṇṇīṭṭiṉāl vakuttu mayaṉāl nirumikkappaṭṭa tākaliṉ cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭameṉap peyarpeṟṟatu; itu cuṭukāṭṭaic cūḻnta matiṟ puṟattuḷḷatākaliṉ ‘cuṭukāṭṭukkōṭṭa'meṉṟu yāvarāṉum kūṟappaṭum; itaṉ varalāṟu ituvākum" eṉṟuraikkak kēṭṭukkoṇṭirunta maṇimēkalai ‘makkaḷ vāḻkkai ittakaiyatu,' eṉa iraṅkikkūṟi yirukkaiyil, cutamati tūṅkutaluṟṟaṉaḷ. appoḻutu maṇimēkalā teyvam maṇimēkalaiyait taḻuvi yeṭuttu, ākāyavaḻiyē muppatu yōcaṉai teṟkē ceṉṟu, kaṭalcūḻnta maṇi pallavam eṉṉum tīvil avaḷai vaittakaṉṟatu. (itil pukār nakariṉ puṟaṅ kāṭṭiṉiyalpukaḷum, akkālattil iṟantōrai aṭakkañ ceyyumuṟaikaḷum kūṟiyiruppaṉa ciṟappāka aṟiyat takkavai. 
		anti mālai nīṅkiya piṉṉar
vantu tōṉṟiya malarkatir maṇṭilam
cāṉṟōr taṅkaṇ eytiya kuṟṟam
tōṉṟuvaḻi viḷaṅkun tōṟṟam pōla
5		mācaṟu vicumpiṉ maṟuniṟaṅ kiḷara
ācaṟa viḷaṅkiya vantīn taṇkatir
	veḷḷiveṇ kuṭattup pālcori vatupōl
	kaḷḷaviḻ pūmpoḻil iṭaiyiṭai coriya
uruvu koṇṭa miṉṉē pōlat
10		tiruvi liṭṭut tikaḻtaru mēṉiyaḷ
āti mutalvaṉ aṟavāḻi yāḷvōṉ
pāta pīṭikai paṇintaṉa ḷēttip
patiyakat tuṟaiyumōr paintoṭi yākic
cutamati nallāḷ taṉmukam nōkki
15		īṅku niṉṟīr eṉṉuṟ ṟīreṉa
āṅkava ḷāṅkavaṉ kūṟiya turaittalum 
araciḷaṅ kumaraṉ āyiḻai taṉmēl
taṇiyā nōkkan tavirntila ṉāki
aṟattōr vaṉameṉ ṟakaṉṟaṉa ṉāyiṉum
20		puṟattōr vītiyiṟ poruntuta loḻiyāṉ
perunteru voḻittip peruvaṉañ cūḻnta
tirunteyiṟ kuṭapāṟ ciṟupuḻai pōki
mikka mātavar virumpiṉa ruṟaiyum
cakkara vāḷak kōṭṭam pukkāl
25		kaṅkul kaḻiyiṉuṅ kaṭunavai yeytātu
aṅkunīr pōmeṉ ṟarunteyva muraippa
vañca viñcaiyaṉ māruta vēkaṉum
añceñ cāyal nīyu mallatu
neṭunakar maruṅkiṉ uḷḷō rellāñ
30		cuṭukāṭṭuk kōṭṭa meṉṟala turaiyār
cakkara vāḷak kōṭṭa maḵteṉa
mikkōy kūṟiya uraipporu ḷaṟiyēṉ
īṅkitaṉ kāraṇa meṉṉai yōveṉa
āṅkataṉ kāraṇam aṟiyak kūṟuvaṉ
35		mātavi makaḷoṭu valliruḷ variṉum
nīkē ḷeṉṟē nēriḻai kūṟumin
nāmap pērūr taṉṉoṭu tōṉṟiya
īmap puṟaṅkā ṭīṅkita ṉayalatu
ūrā naṟṟēr ōviyap paṭuttut
40		tēvar pukutarūuñ ceḻuṅkoṭi vāyilum
nelluṅ karumpu nīruñ cōlaiyum
nalvaḻi yeḻutiya nalaṅkiḷar vāyilum
veḷḷi veṇcutai iḻukiya māṭat
	tuḷḷuru veḻutā veḷḷiṭai vāyilum
45		maṭitta cevvāyk kaṭutta nōkkiṉ
toṭutta pācattup piṭitta cūlattu
neṭunilai maṇṇīṭu niṉṟa vāyilum
nāṟperu vāyilum pāṟpaṭ ṭōṅkiya
kāppuṭai yiñcik kaṭivaḻaṅ kāriṭai
50		ulaiyā uḷḷamō ṭuyirkkaṭaṉ iṟuttōr
talaitūṅku neṭumaran tāḻntupuṟañ cuṟṟip
pīṭikai yōṅkiya perumpali muṉṟil
kāṭamar celvi kaḻiperuṅ kōṭṭamum
aruntavark kāyiṉum aracark kāyiṉum
55		oruṅkuṭaṉ māynta peṇṭirk kāyiṉum
nālvēṟu varuṇap pālvēṟu kāṭṭi
iṟantōr maruṅkiṟ ciṟantōr ceyta
kuṟiyavu neṭiyavuṅ kuṉṟukaṇ ṭaṉṉa
cuṭuma ṇōṅkiya neṭunilaik kōṭṭamum
60		aruntiṟaṟ kaṭavuṭ ṭiruntupalik kantamum
niṟaikkaṟ ṟeṟṟiyum miṟaikkaḷac cantiyum
taṇṭu maṇṭaiyum piṭittuk kāvalar
uṇṭukaṇ paṭukkum uṟaiyuṭ kuṭikaiyum
tūmak koṭiyuñ cuṭarttō raṇaṅkaḷum
65		īmap pantarum yāṅkaṇum parantu
cuṭuvō riṭuvōr toṭukuḻip paṭuppōr
tāḻvayi ṉaṭaippōr tāḻiyiṟ kavippōr
iravum pakalum iḷivuṭaṉ ṟariyātu
varuvōr peyarvōr māṟāc cummaiyum
70		eñciyōr maruṅkiṉ īmañ cāṟṟi
neñcu naṭukkuṟūum neyta lōcaiyum
tuṟavō riṟanta toḻuviḷip pūcalum
piṟavō riṟanta aḻuviḷip pūcalum
nīṇmuka nariyiṉ tīviḷik kūvum
75		cāvōrp payiruṅ kūkaiyiṉ kuralum
pula'd2vūṇ poruntiya kurāliṉ kuralum
ūṇṭalai tuṟṟiya āṇṭalaik kuralum
naṉṉīrp puṇari naḷikaṭa lōtaiyiṉ
iṉṉā icaiyoli eṉṟuniṉ ṟaṟātu
80		tāṉṟiyum oṭuvaiyum uḻiñcilu mōṅkik
kāṉṟaiyuñ cūraiyuṅ kaḷḷiyu maṭarntu
kāypacik kaṭumpēy kaṇaṅkoṇ ṭīṇṭum
mālamar peruñciṉai vākai maṉṟamum
veṇṇiṇan taṭiyoṭu mānti makiḻciṟantu
85		puḷḷiṟai kūṟum veḷḷiṉ maṉṟamum
cuṭalai nōṉpikaḷ oṭiyā vuḷḷamoṭu
maṭaitī yuṟukkum vaṉṉi maṉṟamum
virata yākkaiya ruṭaitalai tokuttāṅ
kiruntoṭarp paṭukkum iratti maṉṟamum
90		piṇantiṉ mākkaḷ niṇampaṭu kuḻiciyil
viruntāṭ ṭayarum veḷḷiṭai maṉṟamum
aḻaṟpey kuḻiciyum puḻaṟpey maṇṭaiyum
veḷḷiṟ pāṭaiyum uḷḷīṭ ṭaṟuvaiyum
parinta mālaiyum uṭainta kumpamum
95		nellum poriyuñ cilpali yariciyum
yāṅkaṇum paranta ōṅkirum paṟantalai
tavattuṟai mākkaḷ mikapperuñ celvar
īṟṟiḷam peṇṭi rāṟṟāp pālakar
mutiyō reṉṉāṉ iḷaiyō reṉṉāṉ
100		koṭun toḻi lāḷaṉ koṉṟaṉaṉ kuvippaviv
aḻalvāyc cuṭalai tiṉṉak kaṇṭum
kaḻiperuñ celvak kaḷḷāṭ ṭayarntu
mikka nallaṟam virumpātu vāḻum
makkaḷiṟ ciṟanta maṭavō ruṇṭō
105		āṅkatu taṉṉaiyō raruṅkaṭi nakareṉac
cārṅkala ṉeṉpōṉ taṉivaḻic ceṉṟōṉ
eṉpun taṭiyu mutiramu miyākkaiyeṉ
ṟaṉpuṟu mākkaṭ kaṟiyac cāṟṟi
vaḻuvoṭu kiṭanta puḻuvūṉ piṇṭattu
110		alattakam ūṭṭiya aṭinari vāykkoṇ
ṭulappi liṉpamō ṭuḷaikku mōtaiyum
kalaippuṟa alkul kaḻukukuṭain tuṇṭu
nilaittalai neṭuviḷi yeṭukku mōtaiyum
kaṭakañ ceṟinta kaiyait tīnāy
115		uṭaiyak kavvi yoṭuṅkā vōtaiyum
cāntan tōynta ēntiḷa vaṉamulai
kāyntapaci yeruvai kavarntū ṇōtaiyum
paṇpukoḷ yākkaiyiṉ veṇpali yaraṅkattu
maṇkaṇai muḻava māka āṅkōr
120		karuntalai vāṅkik kaiyakat tēnti
irumpē ruvakaiyiṉ eḻuntōr pēymakaḷ
puyalō kuḻalō kayalō kaṇṇō
kumiḻō mūkkō itaḻō kavirō
pallō muttō veṉṉā tiraṅkātu
125		kaṇṭoṭ ṭuṇṭu kavaiyaṭi peyarttut
taṇṭāk kaḷippi ṉāṭuṅ kūttuk
kaṇṭaṉaṉ verīik kaṭunavai yeyti
viṇṭōr ticaiyiṉ viḷittaṉaṉ peyarntīṅ
kemmaṉai kāṇāy īmac cuṭalaiyiṉ
130		vemmutu pēykkeṉ uyirkoṭut tēṉeṉat
tammaṉai taṉmuṉ vīḻntumey vaittalum
pārppāṉ ṟaṉṉoṭu kaṇṇiḻan tiruntavit
tīttoḻi lāṭṭiyeṉ ciṟuvaṉ ṟaṉṉai
yārumil tamiyē ṉeṉpatu nōkkātu
135		āruyi ruṇṭa taṇaṅkō pēyō
tuṟaiyu maṉṟamun tolvali māṉum
uṟaiyuḷuṅ kōṭṭamuṅ kāppāy kāvāy
takavilai kollō campā patiyeṉa
makaṉmey yākkaiyai mārpuṟat taḻīi
140		īmap puṟaṅkāṭ ṭeyiṟpuṟa vāyilil
kōtamai yeṉpāḷ koṭuntuyar cāṟṟak
kaṭivaḻaṅku vāyiliṟ kaṭuntuya reyti
iṭaiyiruḷ yāmat teṉṉaiyīṅ kaḻaittaṉai
eṉṉuṟ ṟaṉaiyō eṉakkurai yeṉṟē
145		poṉṉiṟ polinta niṟattāḷ tōṉṟa
ārumi lāṭṭiyeṉ aṟiyāp pālakaṉ
īmap puṟaṅkāṭ ṭeytiṉōṉ ṟaṉṉai
aṇaṅkō pēyō āruyi ruṇṭatu
uṟaṅkuvāṉ pōlak kiṭantaṉaṉ kāṇeṉa
150		aṇaṅkum pēyum āruyi ruṇṇā
piṇaṅkunūṉ mārpaṉ pētukan tāka
ūḻviṉai vantivaṉ uyiruṇṭu kaḻintatu
māperun tuṉpanī oḻivā yeṉṟalum
eṉṉuyir koṇṭivaṉ uyirtan taruḷiṉeṉ
155		kaṇṇil kaṇavaṉai ivaṉkāt tōmpiṭum
ivaṉuyir tanteṉ uyirvāṅ keṉṟalum
mutumū tāṭṭi iraṅkiṉaḷ moḻivōḷ
aiya muṇṭō āruyir pōṉāl
ceyviṉai maruṅkiṟ ceṉṟupiṟap peytutal
160		āṅkatu koṇarntuniṉ āriṭar nīkkutal
īṅkeṉak kāvatoṉ ṟaṉṟunī yiraṅkal
kolaiyaṟa māmeṉuṅ koṭuntoḻiṉ mākkaḷ
avalap paṭiṟṟurai yāṅkatu maṭavāy
ulaka maṉṉavark kuyirkkuyi rīvōr
165		ilarō inta īmap puṟaṅkāṭ
ṭaracark kamaintaṉa āyiraṅ kōṭṭam
nirayak koṭumoḻi nīyoḻi yeṉṟalum
tēvar taruvar varameṉ ṟorumuṟai
nāṉmaṟai yantaṇar naṉṉū luraikkum
170		māperun teyva nīyaru ḷāviṭiṉ
yāṉō kāvēṉ eṉṉuyi rīṅkeṉa
ūḻi mutalvaṉ uyirtari ṉallatu
āḻit tāḻi yakavarait tirivōr
tāntariṉ yāṉun tarukuvaṉ maṭavāy
175		īṅke ṉāṟṟaluṅ kāṇpā yeṉṟē
nālvakai marapi ṉarūpap piramarum
nāṉāl vakaiyi ṉurūpap piramarum
iruvakaic cuṭarum irumū vakaiyiṟ
peruvaṉap peytiya teyvata kaṇaṅkaḷum
180		palvakai yacurarum paṭutuya ruṟūum
eṇvakai narakarum iruvicum piyaṅkum
paṉmī ṉīṭṭamum nāḷuṅ kōḷum
taṉṉakat taṭakkiya cakkara vāḷattu
varantaraṟ kuriyōr tamaimuṉ niṟutti
185		arantai keṭumiva ḷaruntuya rituveṉac
campā patitāṉ uraittaam muṟaiyē
eṅkuvāḻ tēvarum uraippak kēṭṭē
kōtamai yuṟṟa koṭuntuyar nīṅki
īmac cuṭalaiyiṉ makaṉaiyiṭ ṭiṟantapiṉ
190		campā patitaṉ āṟṟal tōṉṟa
eṅkuvāḻ tēvaruṅ kūṭiya iṭantaṉil
cūḻkaṭal vaḷaiiya āḻiyaṅ kuṉṟattu
naṭuvu niṉṟa mēruk kuṉṟamum
puṭaiyi ṉiṉṟa eḻuvakaik kuṉṟamum
195		nālvakai marapiṉ māperun tīvum
ōrī rāyirañ ciṟṟuṭait tīvum
piṟavum āṅkataṉ iṭavakai yuriyaṉa
peṟumuṟai marapiṉ aṟivuvarak kāṭṭi
āṅkuvā ḻuyirkaḷum avvuyi riṭaṅkaḷum
200		pāṅkuṟa maṇṇīṭṭiṟ paṇpuṟa vakuttu
mikka mayaṉāl iḻaikkap paṭṭa
cakkara vāḷak kōṭṭamīṅ kitukāṇ
iṭupiṇak kōṭṭat teyiṟpuṟa mātaliṉ
cuṭukāṭṭuk kōṭṭa meṉṟala turaiyār
205		itaṉvara vituveṉ ṟirunteyva muraikka
mataṉiṉ neñcamoṭu vāṉṟuya reytip
piṟantōr vāḻkkai ciṟantō ḷuraippa
iṟantiruḷ kūrnta iṭaiyiruḷ yāmattut
tūṅkutuyi leytiya cutamati yoḻiyap
210		pūṅkoṭi taṉṉaip poruntit taḻīi
antaram āṟā āṟain tiyōcaṉait
teṉṟicai maruṅkiṟ ceṉṟutirai yuṭutta
maṇipal lavattiṭai maṇimē kalāteyvam
aṇiyiḻai taṉṉaivait takaṉṟatu tāṉeṉ.
urai
1-8.		antimālai nīṅkiya piṉṉar - antippoḻutākiya mālaik kālam kaḻinta piṉṉar, vantu tōṉṟiya malar katir maṇṭilam- vantu utitta paranta kiraṇaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya tiṅkaḷ maṇṭilam, cāṉṟōr taṅkaṇ eytiya kuṟṟam - uyarkuṭit tōṉṟiya mēṉmakkaḷpālaṭainta kuṟṟamāṉatu, tōṉṟuvaḻi viḷaṅkum tōṟṟampōla - tōṉṟumiṭattu viḷaṅkumiyal piṉaippōla, mācu aṟu vicumpil maṟu niṟam kiḷara - vicumpiṉkaṇ mācaṟṟa niṟattiṉiṭam kaḷaṅkam viḷaṅka, ācu aṟa viḷaṅkiya amtīm taṇ katir - kuṟṟamaṟṟu viḷaṅkukiṉṟa aḻakiya tīviya kuḷirnta katiriṉai, veḷḷi veṇkuṭattup pāl corivatupōl - veḷḷik kuṭat tiṉiṉṟu pālaic corivatupōla, kaḷaviḻ pūmpoḻil iṭaiyiṭai coriya - tēṉ aviḻukiṉṟa malarc cōlaiyil iṭaiyiṭaiyē poḻiya;
antimālai-antiyākiya mālai. tōṉṟuvaḻi viḷaṅkum eṉṟatu cāṉṟōrpāl kuṟṟam uṇṭāya appoḻutē atu yāvarum aṟiya veḷippaṭum eṉṟavāṟām; avar veḷippaṭu miṭattu atuvum veḷippaṭum eṉṟumām: tiṅkaḷ tūymai uṭaiyatākalāṉum ataṉkaṇ kaḷaṅkam ataṟku māṟāka iruttalāṉum atu viḷaṅkit tōṉṟiṟṟu. 1"kuṭippiṟantār kaṇviḷaṅkuṅ kuṟṟam vicumpiṉ, matikkaṇ maṟuppō luyarntu" eṉpataṉāṟ cāṉṟōriṭaik kuṟṟam yāvarumaṟiya niṟṟal kāṇka. īṇṭup poruḷ uvamamākkap paṭṭatu; eṉṉai? 2poruḷē yuvamañ ceytaṉar moḻiyiṉum, maruḷaṟu ciṟappiṉaḵ tuvama mākum" eṉpavākalāṉ. cāṉṟōr kuṟṟam viḷaṅkun tōṟṟampōlak katir maṇṭilam maṟuniṟaṅ kiḷarat taṇ katiraip pāl corivatupōl iṭaiyiṭaic coriya veṉka. tiṅkaḷukku veḷḷikkuṭamum taṇkatirkkup pālum uvamai.
9-15. 	uruvu koṇṭa miṉṉē pōla-peṇṇuruvaṅkoṇṭa  miṉṉalaip pōlavum, tiruvil iṭṭut tikaḻtaru mēṉiyaḷ - intira villaippōlavum oḷiviṭṭu viḷaṅkukiṉṟa tirumēṉiyai yuṭaiya atteyvam, ātimutalvaṉ aṟavāḻi āḷvōṉ pāta pīṭikai paṇintaṉaḷ ētti - aṟavāḻiyiṉai uruṭṭukiṉṟa āti mutalvaṉatu pātapīṭikaiyai vaṇaṅkit tutittu, patiyakattu uṟaiyum ōr paintoṭiyāki-ap patiyiluṟaikiṉṟa oru peṇ vaṭivukoṇṭu, cutamati nallāḷ taṉmukam nōkki - cutamatiyiṉ mukattai nōkki, īṅku niṉṟīreṉ uṟṟīr eṉa-īṇṭu niṟkiṉṟavarkaḷē nīvir aṭainta tuṉpam yātu? eṉa viṉava;
uruvu koṇṭa miṉ:ilporuḷuvamai. miṉpōlavum tiruvil pōlavum eṉka. tiruvil - vāṉavil. 3tiruvi liṭṭut tikaḻtaru mēṉiyaṉ" eṉa varutal kāṇka. kaṭavuḷar iṉṉa kālattu ivvāṟu tōṉṟuvar eṉpatu pulappaṭāmaiyāṉ, aṅṅaṉam tōṉṟum vāṉavil avarkku uvamaiyāyiṟṟu. tiruvil - aḻakiya oḷiyeṉṟumām. āti mutalvaṉ - puttaṉ. aṟavāḻi - aṟamākiya tikiri. pātapīṭikai- puttatēvaṉ tiruvaṭi yamainta pīṭam; uvavaṉattiṟ paḷikkaṟaiyil uḷḷatu. paṇintaṉaḷ: muṟṟeccam. muṉṉarum 1"patiyakat tuṟaiyumōr paintoṭi yāki" eṉa vantamai kāṇka. niṉṟīr : viṉaippeyar; atu maṇimēkalaiyaiyum uḷappaṭuttiṟṟu.
16. 		āṅku avaḷ āṅku avaṉ kūṟiyatu uraittalum-akkālaic cutamati av vutayakumaraṉ moḻintataṉaik kūṟiya vaḷavil; avaṉ kūṟiyatu - cittirāpatiyāṟ cērtalu muṇṭu eṉpatu. 
17-26.	araciḷaṅ kumaraṉ āyiḻai taṉmēl taṇiyāvuḷḷam tavirntilaṉ āki - maṉṉavaṉ putalvaṉ maṇimēkalaiyiṭattuk koṇṭa ācai nīṅkāta uḷḷattiṉait tavirntilaṉāy, aṟattōr vaṉam eṉṟu akaṉṟaṉaṉ āyiṉum-tavattōr kaḷuṭaiya poḻil eṉṟu iṅku niṉṟum nīṅkiṉaṉāyiṉum, puṟattōr vītiyil poruntutal oḻiyāṉ - puṟat tiluḷḷavarkaḷatu teru viṉkaṇṇē cērtalai nīṅkāṉ, perunteru oḻittu ipperuvaṉañ cūḻnta - ātalāl perun teruvukaḷai viṭṭu intap periya cōlaiyaic cūḻntuḷḷa, tiruntu eyiṟ kuṭapāl ciṟu puḻai pōki - tiruttamuṟṟa matiliṉ mēṟṟicaiyiṉiṭat tēyuḷḷa ciṟiya vāyiliṉ vaḻiyē ceṉṟu, mikka mātavar virumpiṉar uṟaiyum-tavattāṉ mikka muṉivarkaḷ virumpi yuṟaiyum, cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭam pukkāl - cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭattai yaṭaintāl, kaṅkul kaḻiyiṉum kaṭunavai eytātu - aṅkē iraviṉaik kaḻippiṉum koṭiya tuṉpa muṇṭākātu, aṅkunīr pōm eṉṟu arunteyvam uraippa - ākaliṉ nīvir aṅkē celvīrāka eṉṟu ariya teyvam kūṟiyaruḷa;
aṟattōr tuṟavikaḷāṉamaiyiṉ, puṟattōr eṉpataṟku avarkkup puṟamākavuḷḷa illaṟa neṟiyiṉar eṉa vuraittalum amaiyum; puṟattu ōr vītiyil eṉṟalumām. puḻai-nuḻai vāyil. kaḻiyiṉum - kaḻippiṉum. iṉi, āṇṭē irākkālam kaḻivatāyiṉum eṉalumām kaṭunavai - utayakumaraṉāṟ paṟṟappaṭutal.
27-33.	vañca viñcaiyaṉ māruta vēkaṉum amceñcāyal nīyum allatu-vañcamuṭaiya viñcaiyaṉākiya mārutavēkaṉ eṉpāṉum aḻakiya civanta meṉmait taṉmaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya nīyum aṉṟi, neṭunakar maruṅkiṉ uḷḷōr ellām - ip periya nakariṉkaṇ uḷḷōraṉai varum, cuṭukāṭṭuk kōṭṭam eṉṟalatu uraiyār - cuṭukāṭṭuk kōṭṭam eṉpatēyaṉṟi vēṟu peyar kūṟār, cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭam aḵtu eṉa - ataṉaic cakkara vāḷakkōṭṭam eṉṟu, mikkōy kūṟiya uraipporuḷ aṟiyēṉ - mēmpāṭuṭaiya nī kūṟiya moḻiyiṉ poruḷai aṟiyēṉ, īṅku itaṉ kāraṇam eṉṉaiyō eṉa - iṅṅaṉam kūṟuvataṉ kāraṇam yātō eṉṟu cutamati kēṭpa;
vañca viñcaiyaṉ eṉṟāḷ, taṉṉaik 1kavarntu ceṉṟu piṉṉart taṉṉaiviṭṭup pirintamaiyāṉ. neṭunakar-kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉam.
34-36.	āṅku ataṉ kāraṇam aṟiyak kūṟuval - aḵtu appeyar peṟṟataṉ kāraṇattait teḷiya uraippēṉ, mātavi makaḷoṭu valliruḷ variṉum nīkēḷ eṉṟē nēriḻai kūṟum - mikunta iruḷaiyuṭaiya yāmam variṉum nī mātavi makaḷuṭaṉ ataṉaik kēṭpāyāka eṉṟu maṇimēkalā teyvam kūṟukiṉṟatu; 
36-38.	in nāmap pērūr taṉṉoṭu tōṉṟiya - pakaivarkku accattai viḷaikkum ip periya nakarattōṭē uṇṭāya, īmap puṟaṅkāṭu īṅku itaṉ ayalatu - piṇañcuṭum viṟakaṭuk kiṉaiyuṭaiya cuṭukāṭu iv vuvavaṉattiṉ pakkattatu; nāmam - pukaḻumām. itaṉ - iv vuvavaṉattiṉ. 
39-49.	ūrā naltēr ōviyappaṭuttu - ōviyampōla vimāṉam vicumpiṉkaṇṇē niṟkumāṟu ceytu, tēvar pukutarūum ceḻuṅ koṭi vāyilum - viṇṇavar nuḻaintu celluñ ceḻuṅ koṭiyiṉaiyuṭaiya vāyilum, nellum karumpum nīrum cōlaiyum nalvaḻi eḻutiya nalaṅkiḷar vāyilum - neṟpayirum karumpum poykaiyum poḻilum naṉkaṉa meḻutiya nalañciṟanta vāyilum, veḷḷi veṇcutai iḻukiya māṭattuḷ - mikka veṇmai yuṭaiya cutaiyāṟ pūcappaṭṭa māṭattil, uru eḻutā veḷḷiṭai vāyilum- vaṭivaṅkaḷ eḻutappeṟāta veḷiyāṉa iṭattiṉaiyuṭaiya vāyilum, maṭitta cevvāyk kaṭutta nōkkil toṭutta pācattup piṭitta cūlattu - maṭikkappaṭṭa civanta vāyaiyum ciṉanta nōkkaiyum piṟaraittoṭutta pācattaiyum kaiyiṟ piṭitta cūlappaṭaiyaiyumuṭaiya, neṭunilai maṇṇīṭu niṉṟa vāyilum - nīṇṭa tōṟṟattaiyuṭaiya pūtavaṭivam niṟkiṉṟa vāyilum ākiya, nāṟperu vāyilum pāṟpaṭṭu ōṅkiya - nāṉku periya vāyilkaḷum pakutippaṭṭu uyarnta, kāppuṭai iñcik kaṭi vaḻaṅku āriṭai - kāvaluṭaiya matil cūḻnta pēykaḷ naṭamāṭukiṉṟa ariya iṭattil;
ūrāt tēr-pākarāṟ celuttappaṭātu tāṉē cellun tēr, vimāṉam. vimāṉattuṭaṉ tēvar pukuvatāka ōviyappaṭutta vāyil eṉṟalumām. nīr - poykai mutaliyaṉa. maṇṇīṭu - cutaiyāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa pāvai; "maṇṇīṭṭiṟ paṇpuṟa vakuttu," "īṅkim maṇṇīṭṭu" (6: 200; 18 - 156.) eṉa mēl innūluḷ varutal kāṇka. kaṭi - pēy. 
50-53.	ulaiyā uḷḷamōṭu uyirkkaṭaṉ iṟuttōr-taḷarāta uḷḷattōṭu uyirākiya kaṭaṉaik koṭuttōr kaḷiṉ, talai tūṅku neṭumaram tāḻntu puṟañcuṟṟi - talaikaḷ toṅkukiṉṟa nīṇṭa maraṅkaḷ tāḻntu puṟattē cūḻappeṟṟu, pīṭikai ōṅkiya perum pali muṉṟil kāṭamar celvi kaḻiperuṅ kōṭṭamum- periya palipīṭam ōṅkiya muṉṟilaiyuṭaiya kāṭukiḻāḷiṉ mikap periya kōyilum;
uyirkkaṭaṉ iṟuttalaic cilappatikārattu 1intira viḻa'd2vūreṭutta kātaiyāṉum aṟika. iḵtu avippali eṉappaṭum. pali koṭuppōr cikaiyai marattil muṭintuviṭṭut talaiyai arivarākaliṉ "talaitūṅku neṭumaram" eṉṟār ; 2"vīṅkutalai neṭuṅkaḻaiyiṉ micaitōṟun ticaitōṟum viḻittu niṉṟu, tūṅkutalai, cirippaṉa kaṇ ṭuṟaṅkutalai maṟantirukkuñ cuḻalkaṭ cūrppēy" eṉpatu kāṇka. kāṭamar celvi - kāṭukiḻāḷ ; turkkai.
54-59. 	aruntavarkku āyiṉum aracarkku āyiṉum, oruṅkuṭaṉ māynta peṇṭirkku āyiṉum-ariya tavattāṉuyarn tōrkkum aracarkkum kaṇavaṉuṭaṉ oruṅkē uyirnītta kaṟpuṭai makaḷirkkum, nālvēṟu varuṇap pālvēṟu kāṭṭi - nālvakai marapiṉ pakuti vēṟupāṭṭiṉaik kāṭṭi, iṟantōr maruṅkil ciṟantōr ceyta-iṟantōrkaḷiṉ uṭalaip putaittaviṭattē avarkaṭkuc ciṟantōr amaitta, kuṟiyavum neṭiyavum kuṉṟu kaṇṭaṉṉa cuṭumaṇ ōṅkiya neṭunilaik kōṭṭam-kuṟiyaṉavum neṭiyaṉavum ākiya kuṉṟukaḷaik kaṇṭāṟpōṉṟaṉavāyc ceṅkaṟkaḷāṟ ceyyappaṭṭu uyarnta kōṭṭaṅkaḷum;
āyiṉum eṉpatu ōr eṇṇiṭaiccol; 1"potiyi lāyiṉum imaya māyiṉum......pukārēyāyiṉum" eṉpuḻippōla. ciṟantōr - urimaiyuṭaiyōr. cuṭumaṇ-ceṅkal. kōṭṭam-aṟai; kōyilumām.
60-65.	aruntiṟal kaṭavuḷ tiruntu palik kantamum - ariya tiṟalaiyuṭaiya kaṭavuḷuṭaiya tiruntiya paliyiṭutaṟkuriya tūṇkaḷum, niṟaikal teṟṟiyum - niṟuttappaṭṭa kallālākiya tiṇṇaiyum, miṟaikkaḷac cantiyum-vaḷainta iṭattiṉai yuṭaiya pala vaḻikaḷuñ cērnta cantukaḷum, taṇṭum maṇṭaiyum piṭittuk kāvalar uṇṭu kaṇpaṭukkum uṟaiyuṭ kuṭikaiyum - kaiyiṟ piṭikkum kōlum uṇṇum kalamum piṭittu īmaṅ kāppōr uṇṭu tuyilum uṟaiviṭamākiya kuṭilkaḷum, tūmakkoṭiyum cuṭart tōraṇaṅkaḷum-pukaiyoḻuṅ kākiya koṭiyum cuṭarākiya tōraṇaṅkaḷum, īmappantarum yāṅkaṇum parantu - īmattil iṭṭa pantarkaḷum evviṭattum parakkappeṟṟu;
kantiṉkaṇ teyvamuṟaitalai 2"kalikeḻu kaṭavuḷ kantaṅ kaiviṭa" eṉpataṉāṉum aṟika. miṟai - vaḷaivu ; 3miṟaikkoḷi tiruttiṉāṉē" eṉṟār piṟarum. maṇṭai-uṇṭaṟkuriya maṇkalam; irakkuṅ kalamumām; 4"ēlātu kaviḻnta eṉ iravaṉ maṇṭai" eṉpatu puṟam. kuṭikai-kuṭicai. cuṭar-tīkkoḻuntu; viḷakkumām.
66-69.	cuṭuvōr iṭuvōr toṭukuḻip paṭuppōr - piṇaṅkaḷaic cuṭuvōrum vāḷā iṭṭuppōvōrum tōṇṭappaṭṭa kuḻiyiliṭuvōrum, tāḻvayiṉ aṭaippōr tāḻiyiṟ kavippōr - tāḻnta iṭaṅkaḷil aṭaittu vaippōrum tāḻiyiṉālē kavippōrum, iravum pakalum iḷivuṭaṉ tariyātu - iravum pakalum aruvaruppuṭaṉ taṅkāmal, varuvōr peyarvōr māṟāc cummaiyum - varuvōrum pōvōru mākiyavarkaḷiṉ nīṅkāta oliyum;
tāḻvayiṉ - paḷḷamākiya iṭam. tāḻiyiṟ kavittalaip puṟa nāṉūṟṟiṉ 227, 237, 255, 363-ām ceyyuṭkaḷāl aṟika. itu mutumakkaṭ ṭāḻi eṉappaṭum. cuṭuvōr mutaliyōrāy varuvōr peyarvōr eṉka.
70-79. 	eñciyōr maruṅkiṉ īmañcāṟṟi neñcu naṭukkuṟūum neytal ōcaiyum-uyir vāḻntiruppōriṭam īmam uṇṭeṉpataik kūṟi uḷḷam naṭuṅkumāṟu puriyum neytaṟ paṟaiyiṉ oliyum, tuṟavōr iṟanta toḻuviḷip pūcalum-tuṟaviyar iṟantataṉāṟ pōnta tutimuḻakkamum, piṟavōr iṟanta aḻuviḷip pūcalum-illaṟattōr iṟantataṉā lākiya aḻukai yoliyum, nīḷmuka nariyiṉ tīviḷikkūvum-nīṇṭa mukattaiyuṭaiya nariyiṉ tīya oliyiṉaiyuṭaiya kūppīṭum. cāvōrp payirum kūkaiyiṉ kuralum-iṟappōrai yaḻaikkum pērāntaiyiṉ kuralum, pula'd2vūṇ poruntiya kurāliṉ kuralum - pulālākiya uṇavaip poruntiya kōṭṭāṉiṉ oliyum, ūṇtalai tuṟṟiya āṇṭalaik kuralum - uṇavākat talai mūḷaiyaik kaṭittuṇṭa āṇṭalaip paṟavaiyiṉ muḻakkamum, naṉṉīrp puṇari naḷikaṭal otaiyiṉ iṉṉā icaioli eṉṟum niṉṟu aṟātu-tūnīrc cērkkai yiṉaiyuṭaiya ceṟinta kaṭal ōcaiyaippōl iṉṉātaṉavākiya muḻakkam eṉṟum niṉṟu nīṅkāmal;
neytal - cāppaṟai ; aḵtu īmañcāṟṟutalai 1"maṇaṅ koṇṭīṇ, ṭuṇṭuṇṭuṇ ṭeṉṉum uṇarviṉāṟ cāṟṟumē, toṇṭoṇṭoṇ ṇeṉṉum paṟai" eṉpataṉālumaṟika. muṉ tuṟavōr eṉṟamaiyiṉ piṟavōr illaṟattārāyiṟṟu. toḻuviḷiyākiya pūcal, aḻuviḷiyākiya pūcal eṉka ; viḷi - ōcai. payirtal - aḻaittal, 2"pōḻvāykkūkai, cuṭṭuk kuviyeṉac cettōrp payirum, kaḷḷiyam paṟantalai" eṉpataṉāl pērāntai iṟantōrai aḻaikkumeṉpatum pōtarum. āṇṭalai - āṇmakaṉatu talai pōṉṟa vaṭiva muḷḷa oruvakaip paṟavai; itaṉai, 3"nīṇṭa pali pīṭattilarintu vaitta neṭuṅkuñcic cirattaittaṉ ṉiṉameṉṟeṇṇi, āṇṭalaippuḷ arukaṇaintu pārkku mālō" eṉpataṉāṉaṟika. neytalōcai mutaliya iṉṉā icaiyoli kaṭalōtaiyiṉ eṉṟum niṉṟaṟāteṉka.
80-91.	tāṉṟiyum oṭuvaiyum uḻiñcilum ōṅki - tāṉṟiyum oṭuvaiyum uḻiñcilum uyarntu, kāṉṟaiyum cūraiyuṅ kaḷḷiyum aṭarntu - kāṉṟaiyuñ cūraiyuṅ kaḷḷiyum ceṟintu, kāypacik kaṭumpēy kaṇaṅkoṇṭu īṇṭum - kāykiṉṟa paciyaiyuṭaiya koṭiya pēykaḷ kūṭṭamākat tiraṇṭirukkum, māl amar peruñciṉai vākai maṉṟamum - mukil taṅkukiṉṟa peruṅ kiḷai kaḷaiyuṭaiya vākai maram niṟkum maṉṟamum, veḷ niṇam taṭiyoṭu mānti makiḻ
ciṟantu - veḷḷiya niṇattiṉait tacaiyuṭaṉ uṇṭu makiḻcci mikuntu, puḷ iṟai kūrum veḷḷil maṉṟamum- paṟavaikaḷ taṅkukiṉṟa viḷāmaram niṟkum maṉṟamum, cuṭalai nōṉpikaḷ oṭiyā uḷḷamoṭu maṭaitīyuṟukkum vaṉṉi
maṉṟamum - kāpālikar taḷarāta uḷḷattuṭaṉ cōṟaṭukiṉṟa tīyaiyuṭaiya vaṉṉimaram niṟkum maṉṟamum. virata yākkaiyar uṭaitalai tokuttāṅku iruntoṭarp paṭukkum iratti maṉṟamum - virataṅ kākkum uṭampiṉai yuṭaiyōr uṭainta talaikaḷait tokuttup periya mālaikaḷākac ceyyum ilantaimaram niṟkum maṉṟamum, piṇam tiṉ mākkaḷ - piṇattiṉait tiṉṉum mākkaḷāyiṉār, niṇampaṭu kuḻiciyil viruntāṭṭu ayarum - niṇam poruntiya pāṉaiyil viruntu ceykiṉṟa, veḷḷiṭai maṉṟamum - veḷiyāṉa iṭattaiyuṭaiya maṉṟamum ākiya ivviṭaṅkaḷil;
oṭuvai-oṭumaram. uḻiñcil - vākai; uṉṉamumām. tāṉṟi mutaliya mūṉṟum maramum, kāṉṟai mutaliya mūṉṟum ceṭiyumām. māl-mēkam. iṟaikūrtal-taṅkutal. cuṭalai nōṉpikaḷ-mayāṉattiliruntu nōṉpiyaṟṟuvōr; kāpālika camayattōr. virata yākkaiyar -māvirata camayattōr. palar kūṭumiṭam maṉṟa meṉappaṭum; paṇṭai nāḷil maṉṟaṅkaḷ perumpālum marattaṭiyil iruntaṉa; pēykaḷ kūṭutaliṉ vākaiyaṭi maṉṟa māyiṟṟu; iṅṅaṉamē ēṉaiyavum koḷka.
92-96.	aḻal pey kuḻiciyum puḻal pey maṇṭaiyum - tīppeyta pāṉaiyum, puḻal eṉṉum paṇṇiyam iṭṭa kalaṉum, veḷḷila pāṭaiyum uḷḷīṭṭaṟuvaiyum-veḷḷilākiya pāṭaiyum uḷḷē paṇṭaṅkaḷ iṭap paṭṭa uṟiyum, parinta mālaiyum uṭainta kumpamum -pāṭaiyiṟkaṭṭi aṟutteṟinta mālaikaḷum uṭainta kuṭaṅkaḷum, nellum poriyum cilpali ariciyum-nellum poriyum ciṟupaliyākiya ariciyum, yāṅkaṇum paranta ōṅkirum paṟantalai - evviṭattum parantu kiṭakkum mikapperiya pāḻiṭattil;
puḻal-oruvakaic ciṟṟuṇṭi. veḷḷiṟpāṭai: irupeya roṭṭu. uḷḷīṭṭaṟuvai-uḷḷīṭṭaiyuṭaiya kiḻiyumām. cilpali - ciṟupali; 
1"tūveḷḷarici, cilpalic ceytu" eṉpatu kāṇka. vākai maṉṟam mutaliya maṉṟaṅkaḷ yāṅkaṇum kuḻici mutaliyaṉa paranta paṟantalai yeṉka. 
97-104.	tavattuṟai mākkaḷ mikap peruñ celvar-tava neṟiyiṟ cellun tuṟaviyar mikka peruñ celva muṭaiyōr, īṟṟiḷam peṇṭir āṟṟāppālakar - īṉṟaṇimaiyai yuṭaiya iḷamakaḷir āṟṟāta iḷañciṟār, mutiyōr eṉṉāṉ iḷaiyōr eṉṉāṉ - āṇṭil mutirntōr eṉṉāmalum iḷaiyōr eṉṉāmalum, koṭun toḻilāḷaṉ koṉṟaṉaṉ kuvippa - koṭun toḻilai yuṭaiya kālaṉ koṉṟu kuvippa, iv aḻalvāyc cuṭalai tiṉṉakkaṇṭum - aḻal vāyiṉaiyuṭaiya cuṭalai tiṉṉak kaṇṭum, kaḻiperuñcelvak kaḷḷāṭṭu ayarntu - mikka peruñcelvamākiya kaḷḷaiyuṇṭu viḷaiyāṭutalaic ceytu, mikka nallaṟam virumpātu vāḻum - mēṉmai tarum nallaṟaṅkaḷai virumpāmal vāḻkiṉṟa, makkaḷil ciṟanta maṭavōr uṇṭō - makkaḷiluñ ciṟanta aṟivilikaḷ uḷarō;
eṉṉāṉ eṉpataṉait tavattuṟaimākkaḷ mutaliya vaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka. koṉṟaṉaṉ; muṟṟeccam. aḻalākiya vāy eṉa uruvakam. ikaraccuṭṭu ataṉ koṭumaiyai uṇarttiṟṟu. āriṭaiyil paṟantalaiyil cuṭalai (49, 66, 101) eṉka. paṟantalai yākiya cuṭalai yeṉṉalumām. aṟivai mayakkutaliṉ celvattaik kaḷ eṉṟār. ‘tavat tuṟaimākkaḷ......maṭavōruṇṭō' eṉpatu teyvaṅ kūṟiyatu ; kavi kūṟiyatumām.
105-106.	āṅkatu taṉṉai ōr aruṅkaṭi nakar eṉa - aṅṅaṉamākiya puṟaṅkāṭṭai ōr ariya kāppamainta nakarameṉṟeṇṇi, cārṅkalaṉ eṉpōṉ taṉivaḻic ceṉṟāṉ - taṉivaḻiyē ceṉṟa cārṅkalaṉ eṉṉum ciṟuvaṉ;
107-109.	eṉpum taṭiyum utiramum yākkai eṉṟu - eṉpun tacaiyum kurutiyumākiyavaṟṟai uṭampu eṉṟu, aṉpuṟu mākkaṭku aṟiyac cāṟṟi - aṉpu vaikkiṉṟa makkaḷukku aṟiyakkūṟi, vaḻuvoṭu kiṭanta puḻu'd2vūṉ piṇṭattu - vaḻuvuṭaṉ kiṭanta puḻukkaḷ malinta ūṉ piṇṭamākiya uṭalil;
aṉpuṟu mākkaḷ-uṭampiṉiṭattu aṉpumikka mākkaḷ; mākkaḷ eṉṟār aimpula vuṇarcciyaṉṟi maṉavuṇarcci yillātavar eṉaṟku, vaḻu - vaḻumpu. yākkai eṉpatu eṉpu mutaliyaṉavē yeṉa mākkaṭkuc cāṟṟikkiṭanta piṇṭam eṉka;    
	1"kuṭaruṅ koḻuvuṅ kurutiyu meṉpum     	toṭaru narampoṭu tōlum--iṭaiyiṭaiyē vaitta taṭiyum vaḻumpumā maṟṟivaṟṟuḷ  ettiṟattā ḷīrṅkōtai yāḷ."
eṉpatu ottu nōkkaṟpālatu.
110-119.	alattakam ūṭṭiya aṭi nari vāykkoṇṭu - cem pañcuk kuḻampu ūṭṭappeṟṟa aṭikaḷai nari vāyilē kavvik koṇṭu, ulappil iṉpamōṭu uḷaikkum ōtaiyum - kēṭillāta iṉpattuṭaṉ ūḷaiyiṭukiṉṟa oliyum, kalaippuṟa alkul kaḻuku kuṭaintuṇṭu nilattalai neṭuviḷi eṭukkum ōtaiyum - kalaiyai yoḻitta alkulaik kaḻuku kuṭaintu uṇṭu nilattiṉ kaṇ periya kūvutalaic ceyyum ōcaiyum, kaṭakam ceṟinta kaiyait tī nāy uṭaiyak kavvi oṭuṅkā ōtaiyum-kaṭakamaṇinta kaiyait tīya nāy uṭaiyumāṟu kavvikkoṇṭu iṭum oṭuṅkāta muḻakkamum, cāntam tōynta ēntiḷa vaṉamulai - cantaṉam pūcappeṟṟa nimirnta iḷaṅ koṅkaiyai, kāynta paci eruvai kavarntūṇ ōtaiyum-mikka paciyaiyuṭaiya paruntu kavarntuṇṇukiṉṟa ōcaiyum, paṇpukoḷ yākkaiyiṉ veṇpali araṅkattu- iṉimaiyuṭaiya uṭalkaḷ venta cāmpaṟ kuvaiyākiya araṅkil, maṇkaṇai muḻavam āka - mārccaṉai ceṟinta taṇṇumai muḻakkamāka;
kalai - uṭai; mēkalaiyumām. nilaittalai eṉpatu pāṭamāyiṉ taṉṉiṭattu nilaipeṟṟa eṉka. tī nāy - puṟaṅkāṭṭiṟ ṟiriyum oru vakai nāyumām. paṇpu - iṉimai ; aḻakumām. ōtai palavum muḻavamāka veṉka.
119-121.	āṅku ōr karuntalai vāṅkik kaiyakattu ēnti - aṅkuḷḷa ōr peṇpiṇattiṉ kariya talaiyaip paṟittuk kaiyilē ēnti, irumpēr uvakaiyiṉ eḻuntu ōr pēy makaḷ-ōr peṇ pēy mikka periya makiḻcciyuṭaṉ eḻuntu ;
122-126.	puyalō kuḻalō-mukilō aṉṟik kūntalō, kayalō kaṇṇō - kayal mīṉō aṉṟik kaṇṇō, kumiḻō mūkkō - kumiḻampūvō aṉṟi mūkkō, itaḻō kavirō - utaṭō aṉṟi muruṅkai malarō, pallō muttō - paṟkaḷō aṉṟi muttukkaḷō, eṉṉātu iraṅkātu-eṉṟu karutāmalum iraṅkāmalum, kaṇtoṭṭu uṇṭu - kaṇkaḷait tōṇṭi uṇṭu, kavai aṭi peyarttu - kavaitta aṭikaḷaip peyarttu, taṇṭāk kaḷippiṉ āṭum kūttu - taṇiyāta kaḷippuṭaṉ āṭukiṉṟa kūttiṉai;
eṉṉātu - eṉṟu karuti aticayikkātu ; iḵtu aiyavati cayattiṉ pāṟpaṭum: 1"kavaiyaṭip pēymakaḷ" eṉṟār piṟarum.
127-131. 	kaṇṭaṉaṉ verīi - kaṇṭu añci, kaṭu navai eyti - ap pēyāṟ piṭikkappaṭṭu, viṇṭu - avviṭattiṉ nīṅki, ōr ticaiyiṉ viḷittaṉaṉ peyarntu - ōr ticaiyiṟ kūppiṭṭuk koṇṭu ceṉṟu, īṅku emmaṉai kāṇāy - em aṉṉaiyē iṅkē kāṇpāyāka, īmaccuṭalaiyiṉ - īmamākiya mayāṉattiṉ kaṇ uḷḷa, vemmutu pēykku eṉ uyir koṭuttēṉ eṉa - koṭiya mutu pēykku eṉ uyiraik koṭuttuviṭṭēṉ eṉṟu kūṟi, tam aṉai taṉ muṉ vīḻntu mey vaittalum - taṉ tāyiṉ muṉṉē vīḻntu uyir tuṟakkavum;
cārṅkala ṉeṉpōṉ taṉivaḻic ceṉṟōṉ kaṇṭaṉaṉ verīi eṉka. kaṭu navai-mikka tuṉpam ; āvatu-pēyk kōṭpaṭutal. 2"kaḻalkaṭ kūḷik kaṭunavaip paṭṭōr" eṉpatukāṇka. īmac cuṭalai:irupeyaroṭṭu; īmattaiyuṭaiya cuṭalaiyeṉṟumām. īmam- piṇañ cuṭum viṟakaṭukku. mey vaittal - uṭampaip pōkaṭutal; iṟattal.
132-141.	pārppāṉ taṉṉoṭu kaṇ iḻantirunta - kaṇavaṉōṭu kaṇ iḻantirunta, it tīt toḻilāṭṭi eṉ ciṟuvaṉ taṉṉai- ik koṭuviṉaiyēṉuṭaiya putalvaṉai, yārum il tamiyēṉ eṉpatu nōkkātu - yāṉ yārumaṟṟa ēḻai yeṉpataṉaik karutāmal, ār uyir uṇṭatu aṇaṅkō pēyō - aṇaṅkō aṉṟip pēyō ariya uyiraiyuṇṭatu, tuṟaiyum maṉṟamum tol vali maraṉum - nīrttuṟaikaḷum maṉṟaṅkaḷum paḻaiya vali yuṭaiya maraṅkaḷum, uṟaiyuḷum kōṭṭamum kāppāy - taṅku miṭaṅkaḷum kōyilkaḷu mātiyavaṟṟiṉkaṇiruntu kāttu varuvōy, kāvāy takavilai kollō campāpati eṉa-campāpatiyē nī eṉ makaṉuyiraik kāvāmaliruntāy takaviṉmaiyai yuṭaiyaiyō eṉa aḻaittu, makaṉ mey yākkaiyai mārpuṟat taḻīi - makaṉatu uṭalākiya uyiraṟṟa yākkaiyai mārpuṭaṉ taḻuvikkoṇṭu; īmap puṟaṅkāṭṭu eyilpuṟa vāyilil - iṭu kāṭṭiṉ matiṟ puṟattuḷḷa vāyilil niṉṟu, kōtamai eṉpāḷ koṭun tuyar cāṟṟa - kōtamai eṉṉum pārppaṉi koṭiya tuṉpattiṉaik kūṟa;
pārppāṉ - cārṅkalaṉuṭaiya tantai; avaṉ kaṇṇiḻan tiruntamai "kaṇṇil kaṇavaṉai" (6:155.) eṉap piṉ varutalāṉum tuṇiyappaṭum. tīttoḻilāṭṭi-pārppaṉi yeṉṟumām; 1tīttiṟam purintōṉ" eṉap pārppaṉaṉ kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka; aṅki kāriyam ceypavaṉ eṉṟapaṭi. kaṇavaṉum kaṇṇiḻan tāṉākaliṉ ciṟuvaṉai yaṉṟit taṉakkuk kaḷaikaṇ illai yeṉpāḷ ‘yārumil tamiyēṉ' eṉṟāḷ. nōkkātu - karutātu; nōkkaṉōkkam. maṉṟam - palarkūṭum veḷiyiṭam. takavu ilai kollō-niṉṉiṭam takavu illaiyō eṉṟumām; takavu - naṭuvu nilai; evviṭattum yāraiyuṅ kākkun teyvam īṇṭu ivaṉaik kāvāmaiyiṉ ‘takavilai kollō' eṉṟāḷ. kōtamai takavilai kollō eṉa mārpuṟat taḻīi vāyiliṟ cāṟṟaveṉka.
142-145. 	kaṭivaḻaṅku vāyilil kaṭuntuyar eyti iṭaiyiruḷ yāmattu eṉṉai īṅku aḻaittaṉai - iruḷaiyuṭaiya iṭai yāmattil pēykaḷ naṭamāṭukiṉṟa iv vāyiliṉ kaṇiruntu koṭiya tukkattiṉaiyaṭaintu eṉṉai ippoḻutu aḻaittāy, eṉ uṟṟaṉai eṉakku urai eṉṟē-nī eṉṉa tuṉpameytiṉai ataṉai eṉakkuk kūṟuvāyāka eṉṟu, poṉṉiṟ polinta niṟattāḷ tōṉṟa -poṉṉaip
pōl viḷaṅkum niṟattiṉai yuṭaiya campāpati tōṉṟa ;
kaṭi - pēy. poṉ-cūriyaṉ eṉpatu arumpatavurai. tōṉṟi uraiyeṉṟu kūṟa eṉa vikuti pirittoṭṭutalumām.
146-149.	ārumilāṭṭi eṉ aṟiyāp pālakaṉ - ārumaṟṟa vaḷākiya eṉṉuṭaiya aṟiyāc ciṟuvaṉ. īmap puṟaṅkāṭṭu eytiṉōṉ taṉṉai - īmattiṉaiyuṭaiya puṟaṅkāṭṭiṉ vaḻiyē vantōṉai, aṇaṅkō pēyō āruyir uṇṭatu-aṇaṅkō pēyō ariya uyirai uṇṭatu, uṟaṅkuvāṉ pōlak kiṭantaṉaṉ kāṇ eṉa- itō uṟaṅkupavaṉ pōlak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉaṉ kāṇpāyāka eṉṟu pārppaṉi uraittu varunta ;
150-153.	aṇaṅkum pēyum āruyir uṇṇāt - aṇaṅkum pēyum ariya vuyirai uṇṇamāṭṭā, piṇaṅku nūl mārpaṉ pētu kantāka -neruṅkiya muppuri nūlai yaṇinta mārpiṉaiyuṭaiyacārṅkalaṉatu aṟiyāmaiyē paṟṟuk kōṭāka, ūḻviṉai vantu ivaṉ uyiruṇṭu kaḻintatu - ūḻviṉaiyāṉatu vantu ivaṉatu uyirai uṇṭu nīṅkiyatu, māperun tuṉpam nī oḻivāy eṉṟalum-niṉatu mikapperiya tuyarattai nīṅkuvāyāka eṉṟu teyvamuraittalum; 
154-156. 	eṉ uyirkoṇṭu ivaṉ uyir tantaruḷiṉ-eṉṉuṭaiya uyiraip peṟṟu eṉ makaṉatu uyirait tantaruḷvāyāṉāl, eṉ kaṇṇil kaṇavaṉai ivaṉ kāttu ōmpiṭum-kaṇṇiḻanta eṉ kaṇavaṉai ivaṉ pātukāppāṉ ākaliṉ, ivaṉ uyir tantu eṉ uyir vāṅku eṉṟalum-ivaṉ uyirai aḷittu eṉatu uyirai vāṅku vāyāka eṉṟu kōtamai kūṟiyavaḷavil : kāttu ōmpiṭum: oruporuṭ paṉmoḻi.
157-167. 	mutu mūtāṭṭi iraṅkiṉaḷ moḻivōḷ - campāpati irakkamuṟṟuk kūṟukiṉṟavaḷ, aiyam uṇṭō āruyir pōṉāl ceyviṉai maruṅkila ceṉṟu piṟappu eytutal-ariya uyirāṉatu uṭampai viṭṭu nīṅkiṉāl tāṉ ceyta viṉaiyiṉvaḻiyēceṉṟu vēṟu piṟappaṭaitalil aiyam uṇṭō, āṅkatu koṇarntu niṉ āriṭar nīkkutal-ceṉṟa avaṉuyiraik koṇṭuvantu niṉṉuṭaiya ariya tuṉpattai nīkkutal, īṅku eṉakku āva toṉṟu aṉṟu - eṉakku iyalvatoru kāriyamaṉṟu, nī iraṅkal-nī varuntaṟka, kolai aṟam ām eṉum koṭuntoḻil mākkaḷ avalappaṭiṟṟu urai āṅkatu maṭavāy - maṭantaiyē nī kūṟiya atu kolai purivatai aṟam eṉṟu kūṟum koṭun toḻilaiyuṭaiya mākkaḷiṉ tuṉpattait tarum poyyurai yākum, ulaka maṉṉavarkku uyirkku uyir īvōr ilarō - ulakattaiyāḷkiṉṟa aracarkaṭku uyirkku uyir koṭup pōr oruvarumilarō, inta īmap puṟaṅkāṭṭu aracarkku amaintaṉa āyiram kōṭṭam - intac cuṭukāṭṭiṉkaṇ aracarkaḷukku amainta kōṭṭaṅkaḷ aḷavaṟṟaṉa ākaliṉ, nirayak koṭu moḻi nī oḻi eṉṟalum-nirayattiṟkētuvākiya koṭiya moḻikaḷai nī viṭuvāyāka eṉṟu teyvam kūṟutalum;
mutu mūtāṭṭi-mikka mutumaiyai uṭaiyāḷ. maruṅku - vaḻi; 1"ceyviṉai vaḻittā yuyircelu meṉpatu, poyyil kāṭciyōr poruḷurai yātaliṉ" eṉpatu īṇṭaṟiyaṟpālatu. maṉṉavarkku uyirkkuyi rīvōr - aracar uyirukku māṟākat tammuyiraik koṭuppōr. āyiram-aḷavillaṉa eṉṉumporuṭṭu. kōṭṭam-aṟai.
168-171. 	tēvar taruvar varam eṉṟu orumuṟai nāṉmaṟai antaṇar naṉṉūl uraikkum - tēvarkaḷ varantaruvar eṉṟu antaṇar kaḷatu nalla nūlākiya nāṉmaṟai orupeṟṟiyē kūṟā niṟkum; aṅṅaṉamākavum, māperun teyvam nī aruḷāviṭiṉ- mikapperun teyvamākiya nī aruḷpuriyāviṭṭāl, yāṉō kāvēṉ eṉ uyir īṅkeṉa - yāṉ eṉ uyirai īṇṭuk kāppāṟṟāmal viṭuvēṉ eṉṟu kōtamai kūṟa;
172-175.	ūḻimutalvaṉ uyirtariṉ allatu-ūḻimutalvaṉākiya iṟaivaṉ uyirai aḷittālallatu, āḻittāḻi akavarait tirivōr tām tariṉ-cakkaravāḷamākiya tāḻiyiṉkaṇ tiriyun tēvarkaḷ tara vallavarāyiṉ, yāṉum tarukuvaṉ maṭavāy- maṭavāy yāṉum niṉ makaṉuyirait taruvēṉ, īṅku eṉ āṟṟalum kāṇpāy eṉṟu - eṉṉuṭaiya āṟṟalaiyum nī ippoḻutu kāṇpāyāka eṉṟu kūṟi;
ūḻimutalvaṉ - ulaka mutalvaṉ; ivaṉai makāppiramā eṉpar. tāḻipōṟ kavintuḷa teṉpār tāḻi yeṉṟār. mutalvaṉ tariṉ allatu tirivōr tārār. avar tariṉ yāṉun tarukuvaṉ eṉa viritturaikka. 
176-185.	nālvakai marapiṉ arūpap piramarum - nāṉku vakaip paṭṭa muṟaimaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya arūpap piramarkaḷaiyum, nāṉāl vakaiyiṉ urūpap piramarum-patiṉāṟu vakaippaṭṭa urūpap piramarkaḷaiyum, iruvakaic cuṭarum-ñāyiṟu tiṅkaḷ kaḷaiyum, iru mūvakaiyil peruvaṉappu eytiya teyvata kaṇaṅkaḷum - āṟu vakaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya mikka aḻaku viḷaṅku kiṉṟa teyva kaṇaṅkaḷaiyum, palvakai acurarum paṭutuyar uṟūum eṇvakai narakarum- pala tiṟappaṭṭa acurarkaḷaiyum mikka tuṉpattai aṉupavikkiṉṟa eṇvakaippaṭṭa narakar kaḷaiyum, iru vicumpu iyaṅkum paṉmīṉ īṭṭamum nāḷum kōḷum-akaṉṟa vāṉiṉkaṇ tiriyum pala mīṉkaḷiṉ tokutikaḷaiyum nāṭkaḷaiyum kōṭkaḷaiyum, taṉ akattu aṭakkiya cakkaravāḷattu - taṉ uḷḷē aṭakkik koṇṭirukkum cakkaravāḷattil, varan taraṟkuriyōrtamai muṉ niṟutti -varantaruvataṟ kuriyavarkaḷākiya tēvarkaḷaik kōtamaikku muṉṉē niṟutti, arantai keṭum ivaḷ aruntuyar ituveṉa - ivaḷuṟṟa ariya tuyar itu ittuyariṉaip pōkkuvīrāka eṉṟu campāpati kūṟa;
ulakam muppattoṉṟu eṉṟum, avaṟṟuḷ mutalā vatākiya naraka lōkattiṉ pakutikaḷākiya makānirayam, irauravam, kālacūttiram, cañcīvaṉam, makāvīci, tapaṉam, campiratāpaṉam, caṅkatam eṉṉum eṭṭu narakaṅkaḷilum narakarkaḷ vacippareṉṟum, āṟāvatu mutaliya aṟuvakai ulakaṅkaḷilum aṟuvakait teyvakaṇaṅkaḷ vacippareṉṟum, paṉṉiraṇṭāvatu mutaliya patiṉāṟu ulakaṅkaḷil patiṉāṟuvakai uruvappiramarkaḷum, irupatteṭṭāvatu mutaliya nāṉku ulakaṅkaḷil nālvakai aruvappiramarkaḷum vacippareṉṟum kūṟuvar. oṉṟu mutal mēṉmēlulakaṅkaḷil vacippōr paṭippaṭiyē uyarntōrāva reṉpatu aṟika. keṭum : ceyyumeṉṉuñ col; ēvalil vantatu. 
186-189. 	campāpati tāṉ uraitta ammuṟaiyē - kāvaṟṟeyva mākiya campāpati kūṟiya muṟaippaṭiyē, eṅku vāḻ tēvarum uraippak kēṭṭē - ulakeṅkaṇumuḷḷa tēvarkaḷum kūṟak kēṭṭu, kōtamai uṟṟa koṭuntuyar nīṅki - kōtamai tāṉ aṭainta koṭiya tuṉpattiṉiṉṟum nīṅki, īmaccuṭalaiyil makaṉai iṭṭu iṟantapiṉ - iṭukāṭṭil aṭukkappaṭṭa viṟakiṉai yuṭaiya aḻaliṉkaṇ makaṉai iṭṭut tāṉum iṟanta piṉṉar; 
campāpati kūṟiyatāvatu ūḻi mutalvaṉ uyirtariṉallatu ēṉōr tārār eṉṟatu. tēvar eṉṟatu aruvappiramar iṟuti
yākavuḷḷa mēlulakattavar aṉaivarkkum potuppeyar. 
190-202.	campāpati taṉ āṟṟal tōṉṟa - campāpatiyiṉuṭaiya pērāṟṟal veḷippaṭumāṟu, eṅku vāḻ tēvarum kūṭiya iṭantaṉil-eṅkumuṟaikiṉṟa tēvarka ḷellōrum kūṭiya anta iṭattil, cūḻkaṭal vaḷaiiya āḻiyaṅkuṉṟattu - perumpuṟak kaṭalāṟ cūḻappaṭṭa cakkaravāḷa malaiyiṉ uṭpaṭṭa iṭattil, naṭuvu niṉṟa mērukkuṉṟamum - naṭu viṭattē niṉṟatākiya mērumalaiyum, puṭaiyiṉniṉṟa eḻu vakaik kuṉṟamum ataṉ pakkattē cūḻntu niṉṟa eḻuvakaik kulamalaikaḷum, nālvakai marapiṉ māperuntīvum-nāṉku vakaippaṭṭa marapiṉaiyuṭaiya mikapperiya tīvukaḷum, ōrī rāyiram ciṟṟiṭaittīvum - avaṟṟaic cūḻntuḷḷa iraṇṭāyiram ciṟu tīvukaḷum, piṟavum āṅkataṉ iṭavakai uriyaṉa-piṟavumākiya ataṉ uriya iṭavakaikaḷai, peṟu muṟai marapiṉ aṟivuvarak kāṭṭi-avai amainta muṟaippaṭi kāṇpōrkku aṟivurai amaittuk kāṭṭi, āṅkuvāḻ uyirkaḷum avvuyir iṭaṅkaḷum-āṅkē vāḻum uyirkaḷaiyum avai vacikkum iṭaṅkaḷaiyum, pāṅkuṟa maṇṇīṭṭil paṇpuṟa vakuttu-aḻaku porunta maṇṇīṭukaḷāka ilakkaṇappaṭi vakuttu, mikka mayaṉāl iḻaikkappaṭṭa - aṟiviṉ mēmpaṭṭa teyvat taccaṉāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭa, cakkaravāḷakkōṭṭam īṅkitukāṇ-cakkara vāḷakkōṭṭam eṉpatu ituvākum: 
tōṉṟakkūṭiya iṭam eṉka; tōṉṟa iḻaikkappaṭṭa  eṉ ṟiyaittalum poruntum. mērumalai eṇpattīrāyiram yōcaṉai uyaramum, ataṟkut takka parumaiyum uḷḷateṉṟum, ataṉaic cūḻak kulakirikaḷ ēḻum, avaṟṟuḷ ovvoṉṟaṉaiyum cūḻntu avvakkiri yiṉ uyaravaḷavuḷḷa āḻamum akalamumuṭaiya ēḻu kaṭalkaḷum uḷḷaṉaveṉṟum, ivaiyellāvaṟṟaiyum puṟañ cūḻntukiṭakkum perumpuṟakkaṭalil camputtīvu mutaliya nāṉku māperuntīvukaḷum, avaiovvoṉṟaiyum ainnūṟu ainnūṟu ākaccūḻnta iraṇṭāyiram ciṟu tīvukaḷum uḷḷaṉa eṉṟum, camputtīvu teṟkiluḷḷatu eṉṟum kūṟuvar; ippakutikku ṭākṭar u.. vē. cāminātaiyar eḻutiya arumpatavurai kāṇka. 
ciṟu tīvukaḷ antarattīvukaḷ eṉṟum kūṟappaṭum; "antarat tīviṉum akaṉperun tīviṉum" (25 : 224) eṉṟu piṉvarutal kāṇka. uyirkaḷ eṉṟatu avaṟṟukku nilaikkaḷamākiya uṭampu kaḷai. iṭam eṉṟatu nīr, nilam mutaliyavaṟṟai. maṇṇīṭu-cutaiyāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa pāvai mutaliyaṉa. mayaṉ - acurattaccaṉ eṉpārumuḷar.
203-205.	iṭupiṇakkōṭṭattu eyiṟpuṟam ākaliṉ - piṇaṅkaḷai yiṭukiṉṟa puṟaṅkāṭṭiṉ matiṟ puṟattiluḷḷatu ātalāl itaṉai, cuṭukāṭṭukkōṭṭam eṉṟalatu uraiyār - cuṭukāṭṭuk kōṭṭa meṉṟē kūṟuvar, itaṉvaravu itu eṉṟu irunteyvam uraikka - itaṉuṭaiya varalāṟu iḵtu eṉṟu maṇimēkalā teyvam moḻintaruḷa;
iṭutal, cuṭutal iraṇṭiṉaiyum oṉṟāka aṭakkip peyar kūṟiṉār. 
206-214. 	mataṉil neñcamōṭu vāṉtuyar eyti - valiyaṟṟa maṉattuṭaṉē mikka tuṉpattaiyaṭaintu, piṟantōr vāḻkkai ciṟantōḷ uraippa-pūmiyiṟ piṟantōrkaḷatu vāḻkkaiyai maṇimēkalai kūṟa, iṟantiruḷ kūrnta iṭaiyiruḷ, yāmattu - mikavuñceṟinta iruḷ mikunta iṭaiyāmattil, tūṅkutuyil eytiya cutamati oḻiya - mikka uṟakkattiṉaiyaṭainta cutamati avviṭattil nīṅka, pūṅkoṭitaṉṉaip poruntit taḻīi - maṇimēkalaiyaic cērat taḻuvikkoṇṭu, antaram āṟā āṟaintu yōcaṉait teṉṟicai maruṅkil ceṉṟu-vicumpiṉ vaḻiyāka muppatu yōcaṉai tūram teṉṟicaikkaṭ ceṉṟu, tiraiyuṭutta maṇi pallavattiṭai - kaṭalāṟ cūḻappaṭṭa maṇipallava meṉṉum tīviṉkaṇ, maṇimēkalā teyvam aṇiyiḻai taṉṉai vaittu akaṉṟatutāṉ - maṇimēkalaiyai vaittuviṭṭu maṇimēkalā teyvamtāṉ nīṅkiyatu eṉka. 
mataṉ-cerukkumām. kōṭṭattiṉiyalpu kēṭṭamaiyiṉ neñcam mataṉaḻintateṉka. vāḻkkai yeṉṟatu ataṉ nilaiyāmaiyai. 
maṇṭilam coriya, mēṉiyaḷ ētīti nōkki eṉṉuṟṟīreṉa, avaḷ avaṉ kūṟiya turaittalum; arun teyvamuraippa, cutamati eṉṉai yōveṉa, nēriḻai kūṟum ; aṅṅaṉam kūṟukiṉṟa teyvam itaṉ varavu ituveṉṟuraikka, ciṟantōḷuraippa, teyvam iṭai yiruḷ yāmattuc cutamatiyoḻiyap pūṅkoṭitaṉṉait taḻīic ceṉṟu maṇipallavattil vaittu akaṉṟatu eṉa muṭikka.
cakkaravāḷakkōṭṭamuraitta kātai muṟṟiṟṟu. 


7. tuyileḻuppiya kātai  
maṇipallavattiṉiṉṟum nīṅkiya maṇimēkalā teyvam, ‘iravu kaḻintāl maṇimēkalai eṉkai yakappaṭuvāḷ' eṉṟu vēṭkai nōyāl tuyilātirunta utayakumaraṉ muṉtōṉṟi, "maṉṉavaṉ makaṉē, kōl nilai tirintiṭiṉ kōḷnilai tiriyum, kōḷnilai tirintiṭiṉ māri vaṟaṅkūrum, māri vaṟaṅkūriṉ maṉṉuyir illai, maṉṉuyirellām maṇṇāḷ vēntaṉ taṉṉuyireṉṉum takutiyiṉ ṟākum, tavattiṟam pūṇṭōḷ taṉmēl vaitta, avattiṟam oḻika" eṉṟu avaṉukku aṟivurai kūṟiviṭṭu, uvavaṉañ ceṉṟu aṅkē tuyilum cutamatiyai eḻuppi, "yāṉ maṇimēkalā teyvam; intira viḻāk kāṇṭaṟku vantēṉ ; nī-añcātē, maṇimēkalaikkup puttaṉ aṟa neṟiyiṟ cellum naṟpoḻutu vantuṟṟatākaliṉ avaḷai yeṭuttuc ceṉṟu maṇipallavattil vaittēṉ; avaḷ taṉatu paḻam piṟappiṉ nikaḻcciyaiyum aṟintukoṇṭu iṟṟaikku ēḻāvatu nāḷil iṅku vantu cērvāḷ. innakaril vēṟuvaṭivam koḷ vāḷāyiṉum uṉakku avaḷ oḷippāḷallaḷ; avaḷ ippatiyiṟ pukumnāḷil pala aṟputaṅkaḷ nikaḻum; nāṉ vantataiyum maṇimēkalai nalvaḻiyiṟ ceṉṟataiyum mātavikkuc colka: avaḷ muṉṉarē eṉṉai yaṟivāḷ; kōvalaṉ ‘namatu kula teyvattiṉ peyarai ikkuḻantaikku iṭuka' eṉṟu colli, eṉ peyarai ivaḷukkiṭṭa nāḷiṉ iravil mātaviyiṉ kaṉaviṟ ceṉṟu, ‘kāmaṉ ceyalaṟṟēṅka māperun tavakkoṭi yīṉṟaṉai' eṉṟu naṉaviṟ kūṟiyatupōla nāṉ kūṟiyatuṇṭu; itaṉaiyum avaḷukku niṉaippūṭṭuti," eṉṟu kūṟippōyiṟṟu. 
piṉpu cutamati yeḻuntu maṇimēkalaiyiṉ pirivāl varunti. avvaṉattaic cūḻnta matiliṉ mēṟpuṟattiluḷḷa ciṟiya vāyil vaḻiyē ceṉṟu, cakkaravāḷakkōṭṭattai yaṭaintu, āṅkuḷḷa ulakavaṟaviyiṉ orupuṭai yiruntāḷ. appoḻutu avviṭattē kantiṉai iṭamākakkoṇṭuḷḷa teyvappāvaiyāṉatu cutamati maruḷumpaṭi avaḷatu muṟpiṟappiṉ varalāṟṟaiyum ippiṟappiṉ varalāṟṟaiyum kūṟi viḷittu, ‘maṇimēkalai taṉatu muṟpiṟappaiyum uṉatu muṟpiṟappaiyum aṟintukoṇṭu iṟṟaikku ēḻāmnāḷiravil in nakarkkaṇ varuvāḷ; avaḷ pirintatu paṟṟi nī añcal' eṉṟu kūṟiṟṟu. ataṉaik kēṭṭu neñcamnaṭuṅkiya cutamati, iravu muḻutum avviṭattiruntu, katiravaṉ utittavuṭaṉ eḻuntu vītimaruṅkiṟ ceṉṟu mātaviyai aṭaintu, mutalnāḷiravu nikaḻntavaṟṟaic coṉṉavuṭaṉ avaḷ māṇikkattai iḻanta nākam pōṉṟu mikka tuyarat tōṭiruntāḷ. cutamati maṇimēkalaiyiṉ pirivāl iṉṉuyiriḻanta yākkaipōlac ceyalaṟṟiruntāḷ. (itil, irāppoḻutiṉ nikaḻccikaḷum, viṭiyal  nikaḻccikaḷum āciriyaruṭaiya ulakiyalaṟivaiyum, kaṟpaṉaittiṟattaiyum pulappaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa.)  
		maṇimē kalaitaṉai maṇipal lavattiṭai
maṇimē kalāteyvam vaittu nīṅki
maṇimē kalaitaṉai malarppoḻiṟ kaṇṭa
utaya kumaraṉ uṟutuya reytik
5		kaṅkul kaḻiyileṉ kaiyakat tāḷeṉap 
poṅkumel lamaḷiyiṟ poruntā tiruntōṉ 
muṉṉart tōṉṟi maṉṉavaṉ makaṉē
kōlnilai tirintiṭiṟ kōḷnilai tiriyum
kōḷnilai tirintiṭiṉ mārivaṟaṅ kūrum
10		mārivaṟaṅ kūriṉ maṉṉuyi rillai 
maṉṉuyi rellām maṇṇāḷ vēntaṉ
taṉṉuyi reṉṉun takutiyiṉ ṟākum
tavattiṟam pūṇṭōḷ taṉmēl vaitta
avattiṟam oḻikeṉ ṟavaṉvayi ṉuraittapiṉ
15		uvavaṉam pukuntāṅ kuṟutuyil koḷḷum
cutamati taṉṉait tuyiliṭai nīkki
intira kōṭaṇai innakark kāṇa
vantē ṉañcal maṇimē kalaiyāṉ
āticāṉ muṉivaṉ aṟavaḻip paṭuum
20		ētu mutirnta tiḷaṅkoṭik kātaliṉ
viñcaiyiṟ peyarttuniṉ viḷaṅkiḻai taṉṉaiyōr
vañcamil maṇipal lavattiṭai vaittēṉ
paṇṭaip piṟappum paṇpuṟa uṇarntīṅ
kiṉṟēḻ nāḷil innakar maruṅkē
25		vantu tōṉṟum maṭakkoṭi nallāḷa
kaḷippumāṇ celvak kāvaṟ pērūr
oḷitturu veytiṉum uṉtiṟa moḷiyāḷ
āṅkavaḷ innakar pukunta annāḷ
īṅku nikaḻvaṉa ētup palavuḷa
30		mātavi taṉakkiyāṉ vanta vaṇṇamum
ētamil neṟimakaḷ eytiya vaṇṇamum
uraiyāy nīyavaḷ eṉtiṟam uṇarum 
tiraiyirum pauvattut teyvamoṉ ṟuṇṭeṉak
kōvalaṉ kūṟiyik koṭiyiṭai taṉṉaiyeṉ
35		nāmañ ceyta naṉṉāḷ naḷḷiruḷ.
kāmaṉ kaiyaṟak kaṭunavai yaṟukkum
māperun tavakkoṭi īṉṟaṉai yeṉṟē
naṉavē pōlak kaṉavakat turaittēṉ
īṅkiv vaṇṇam āṅkavaṭ kuraiyeṉ
40		ṟantarat teḻuntāṅ karunteyvam pōyapiṉ
ventuya reytic cutamati eḻuntāṅ
kakaṉmaṉai yaraṅkat tāciriyar tammoṭu
vakaiteri mākkaṭku vaṭṭaṇai kāṭṭi
āṭal puṇarkkum araṅkiyaṉ makaḷiril
45		kūṭiya kuyiluvak karuvikaṇ tuyiṉṟu
paṇṇukkiḷai payirum paṇṇiyāḻt tīntoṭai
koḷaival lāyamō ṭicaikūṭ ṭuṇṭu
vaḷaicēr ceṅkai melvira lutaitta
vemmaivey tuṟātu taṉmaiyiṟ ṟiriyavum
50		paṇpil kātalaṉ parattamai nōṉātu
uṇkaṇ civantāṅku olkukoṭi pōṉṟu
teruṭṭavun teruḷā tūṭaloṭu tuyilvōr
viraippūm paḷḷi vīḻtuṇai taḻuvavum
taḷarnaṭai yāyamoṭu taṅkā tōṭi
55		viḷaiyāṭu ciṟutēr īrttumey varunti
amaḷit tuñcum aimpaṭait tālik
kutalaic cevvāyk kuṟunaṭaip putalvarkkuk
kāvaṟ peṇṭir kaṭippakai yeṟintu
tūpaṅ kāṭṭit tūṅkutuyil vatiyavum
60		iṟaiyuṟai puṟavum niṟainīrp puḷḷum
kāvuṟai paṟavaiyum nāvu ḷaḻunti
viḻavukkaḷi yaṭaṅki muḻavukkaṇ tuyiṉṟu
paḻaviṟaṉ mūtūr pāyalkoḷ naṭunāḷ
kōmakaṉ kōyiṟ kuṟunīrk kaṉṉaliṉ
65		yāmaṅ koḷpavar ēttoli yaravamum
uṟaiyuṇiṉ ṟoṭuṅkiya uṇṇā uyakkattu 
niṟaiyaḻi yāṉai neṭuṅkū viḷiyum
tērvaḻaṅku teruvuñ ciṟṟiṭai muṭukkarum
ūrkāp pāḷar eṟituṭi ōtaiyum
70		muḻaṅkunīr muṉṟuṟaik kalampuṇar kammiyar
tuḻantaṭu kaḷḷiṉ ṟōppiyuṇ ṭayarntu
paḻañceruk kuṟṟa aṉantarp pāṇiyum
aravāyk kaṭippakai aiyavik kaṭippakai
viraviya makaḷir ēntiya tūmattup
75		putalvaraip payanta puṉiṟunīr kayakkam
tīrviṉai makaḷir kuḷaṉā ṭaravamum
valitta neñciṉ āṭava riṉṟiyum
pulikkaṇat taṉṉōr pūta catukkattuk
koṭittēr vēntaṉ koṟṟaṅ koḷkeṉa
80		iṭikkuraṉ muḻakkat tiṭumpali yōtaiyum
īṟṟiḷam peṇṭir āṟṟāp pālakar
kaṭuñcūṉ makaḷir neṭumpuṇ uṟṟōr
tantuyar keṭukkum mantira mākkaḷ
maṉṟap pēymakaḷ vantukaik koḷkeṉa
85		niṉṟeṟi paliyiṉ neṭuṅkura lōtaiyum
palvē ṟōtaiyum parantoruṅ kicaippak
kēṭṭuḷaṅ kalaṅki ūṭṭiru ḷaḻuvattu
muruntēr iḷanakai nīṅkip pūmpoḻil
tirunteyiṟ kuṭapāṟ ciṟupuḻai pōki
90		mikkamā teyvam viyanteṭut turaitta
cakkara vāḷak kōṭṭat tāṅkaṇ
palarpukaḻ tiṟanta pakuvāy vāyil
ulaka aṟaviyiṉ orupuṭai iruttalum
kantuṭai neṭunilaik kāraṇam kāṭṭiya
95		anti leḻutiya aṟputap pāvai
maittaṭaṅ kaṇṇāḷ mayaṅkiṉaḷ veruvat
tippiya muraikkun teyvak kiḷaviyiṉ
iravi vaṉmaṉ oruperu makaḷē
turakat tāṉait tuccayaṉ ṟēvi
100		tayaṅkiṇark kōtai tārai cāvuṟa 
mayaṅki yāṉaimuṉ maṉṉuyir nīttōy
kārāḷar caṇpaiyiṟ kaucikaṉ makaḷē
māruta vēkaṉō ṭinnakar pukuntu
tārai tavvai taṉṉoṭu kūṭiya
105		vīrai yākiya cutamati kēḷāy
iṉṟēḻ nāḷil iṭaiyiruḷ yāmattut
taṉpiṟap pataṉōṭu niṉpiṟap puṇarntīṅku
ilakkumi yākiya niṉakkiḷai yāḷvarum
añcaleṉ ṟuraitta tavvurai kēṭṭu
110		neñcam naṭukkuṟūum nēriḻai nallāḷ
kāva lāḷar kaṇṭuyil koḷḷat
tūmeṉ cēkkait tuyilkaṇ viḻippa
valampuric caṅkam vaṟiteḻun tārppap
pulampuric caṅkam poruḷōṭu muḻaṅkap
115		pukarmuka vāraṇam neṭuṅkū viḷippap
poṟimayir vāraṇaṅ kuṟuṅkū. viḷippap
paṇainilaip puravi palaveḻun tālap
paṇainilaip puḷḷum palaveḻun tālap
pūmpoḻi lārkaip puḷḷoli ciṟappap
120		pūṅkoṭi yārkaip puḷḷoli ciṟappap
kaṭavuṭ pīṭikaip pūppali kaṭaikoḷak
kalampakar pīṭikaip pūppali kaṭaikoḷak
kuyiluvar kaṭaitoṟum paṇṇiyam paranteḻak
koṭuppōr kaṭaitoṟum paṇṇiyam paranteḻa
125		ūrtuyi leṭuppa uravunī raḻuvattuk
kāriruḷ cīttuk katiravaṉ muḷaittalum
ēvuṟu maññaiyiṉ niṉaintaṭi varunta
mānakar vīti maruṅkiṟ pōkip
pōya kaṅkuliṟ pukuntatai yellām 
130		mātavi taṉakku vaḻuviṉ ṟuraittalum
naṉmaṇi iḻanta nākam pōṉṟavaḷ
taṉmakaḷ vārāt taṉittuya ruḻappa
iṉṉuyi riḻanta yākkaiyi liruntaṉaḷ
tuṉṉiya turaitta cutamati tāṉeṉ. 
urai
1-7.		maṇimēkalaitaṉai maṇipallavattiṭai maṇimēkalā teyvam vaittu nīṅki-maṇimēkalā teyvam maṇimēkalaiyai maṇipallavattiṉkaṇ vaittu ataṉaiviṭṭu nīṅka, maṇimēkalaitaṉai malarppoḻiṟ kaṇṭa utayakumaraṉ - maṇimēkalaiyaip pūñcōlaiyiṟ kaṇṭa utayakumaraṉ, uṟutuyar eyti - mikka tuṉpa muṟṟu, kaṅkul kaḻiyileṉ kaiyakattāḷ eṉa - iravu nīṅkiṉāl maṇimēkalai eṉ kaiyiṉiṭattē uḷḷāḷ eṉṟu, poṅku mel amaḷiyil poruntātu iruntōṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟi- ciṟanta melliya paṭukkaiyiṭattuk kaṇṭuyilātu irukkiṉṟavaṉ muṉṉē tōṉṟi,  
maṇimēkalaiyai eytap peṟāmaiyāṉ uṟutuyar eytiṉāṉ eṉka. poruntātu-kaṇ poruntātu : uṟaṅkātu. iruntōṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟi eṉka. 
7-14. 	maṉṉavaṉ makaṉē-aracaṉ putalvaṉē, kōl nilai tirintiṭiṉ kōḷ nilai tiriyum-aracaṉatu kōl kōṭu vatāyiṉ kōṭ kaḷiṉ nilai vēṟupaṭum, kōḷnilai tirintiṭiṉ māri vaṟamkūrum- aṅṅaṉam kōṭkaḷ nilai tavaṟiṉāl maḻai peyyāmaṟ kaḻiyuṅkālam mikum, māri vaṟaṅ kūriṉ maṉṉuyir illai - maḻai vaḷaṅ kuṟaintāl nilaipeṟṟa uyirkaḷ illaiyām, maṉṉuyir ellām maṇṇāḷ vēntaṉ taṉuyir eṉṉum takuti iṉṟu ākum- nilaipeṟṟa uyirkaḷ aṉaittum maṇṇulakattai āḷkiṉṟa maṉṉavaṉatu uyireṉṟu kūṟappaṭum takaimaiilatām ākaliṉ, tavattiṟam pūṇṭōḷ taṉmēl vaitta - tavattiṉ kūṟupāṭṭiṉai mēṟkoṇṭa maṇimēkalaipāl vaitta, avattiṟam oḻi keṉṟu avaṉ vayiṉ uraittapiṉ-tīya viruppiṉ taṉmaiyai oḻivāyāka eṉṟu utayakumaraṉiṭam moḻinta piṉṉar;  
kōlnilai tiriyiṉ kōḷnilai tirital mutaliyaṉa nikaḻumeṉ pataṉai, 1"kōṇilai tiriyāk kōlō ṉākuka,' 2"kōṇilaitirintu nāḻi kuṟaipaṭap pakalkaṇ miñci, nīṇila māri yiṉṟi viḷaivaḵkip paciyu nīṭip, pūṇmulai makaḷir poṟpiṟ kaṟpaḻin taṟaṅkaṇ māṟi, āṇaiyiv vulaku kēṭā maracukōl kōṭi ṉeṉṟāṉ" eṉpavaṟṟāṉum aṟika. avattiṟam - kēṭu ceyyum tīya viḻaivu. 
15-22.   uvavaṉam pukuntu āṅku uṟutuyil koḷḷum-uvavaṉattiṟ pukuntu āṇṭu mikunta uṟakkattuṭaṉirukkum, cutamati taṉṉait tuyiliṭai nīkki - cutamatiyai uṟakkat tiṉiṉṟum eḻuppi, intirakōṭaṉai innakark kāṇa vantēṉ añcal maṇimēkalai yāṉ-yāṉ maṇimēkalā teyvam añcātē innakariṉ intira viḻākkāṇa vantēṉ, āticāṉ muṉivaṉ aṟa vaḻippaṭūum-putta tēvaṉiṉ aṟaneṟiyiṟ cellum, ētu mutirntatu iḷaṅkoṭikku ātaliṉ- ētu nikaḻcci maṇimēkalaikku muṟṟiyatākaliṉ viñcaiyiṟ peyarnnu niṉ viḷaṅkiḻaitaṉṉai-niṉ melliyalai eṉ vittaiyiṉālē peyarttu, ōr vañcam il maṇipallavattiṭai vaittēṉ-oru vakaiyāṉa vañcaṉaiyum illāta maṇi pallavattiṉ kaṇ vaittuḷēṉ;  tuyiliṭai : urupu mayakkam, kōṭaṇai - muḻakkam ; palvakai muḻakkattiṉaiyuṭaiya viḻāviṟkāyiṟṟu. viṉaikaḷ tam payaṉaiyūṭṭa etirppaṭuvataṉai, ētu nikaḻcci allatu ētu eṉpatu pauttanūl vaḻakku. 
23-29.	paṇṭaip piṟappum paṇpuuṟa uṇarntu-muṟpiṟaviyaiyum naṉkaṟintu, īṅku iṉṟu ēḻunāḷil innakar maruṅkē- iṟṟaikku ēḻām nāḷilē ivviṭattu innakarattiṉkaṇ, vantu tōṉṟum maṭakkoṭi nallāḷ - maṭappam poruntiya koṭipōlum maṇimēkalai vantu tōṉṟuvaḷ, kaḷippu māṇ celvak kāvaṟ pērūr - celvak kaḷippāṟ ciṟanta kāvalaiyuṭaiya ip periya nakarattil. oḷittu uruvu eytiṉum uṉ tiṟaṉ oḷiyāḷc - taṉ vaṭivattai maṟaittu vantālum niṉṉiṭam maṟaiyāḷ, āṅku avaḷ innakar pukunta annāḷ - maṇimēkalai innakarattiṟku mīḷavanta annāḷil, īṅku nikaḻvaṉa ētup palavuḷa - īṇṭu nikaḻvaṉa vākiya ētu nikaḻccikaḷ palavuṇṭu;
 tōṉṟum: muṟṟu. kaḷippumāṇ celvam eṉpataṟkuk kaḷippu māṭci yuṟutaṟkuk kāraṇamāya celvam eṉa uraippiṉum amaiyum. pukunta eṉpatu kāla vaḻuvamaiti.
 30-40. 	mātavi taṉakku yāṉ vanta vaṇṇamum-mātavikku yāṉ vanta vakaiyaiyum, ētamil neṟi makaḷ eytiya vaṇṇamum- maṇimēkalai kuṟṟamaṟṟa vaḻiyiṟ cērnta taṉmaiyaiyum, urai yāy nī - nī kūṟuvāyāka, avaḷ eṉ tiṟam uṇarum-am mātavi eṉ varalāṟṟai uṇaruvāḷ, tiraiyirum pauvattut teyvam oṉṟu uṇṭu eṉak kōvalaṉ kūṟi ikkoṭiyiṭai taṉṉai eṉ nāmañ ceyta nalnāḷ - kōvalaṉ alaikaḷaiyuṭaiya periya kaṭaliṉiṭattut teyvam oṉṟu uṇṭeṉṟu kūṟi ip peṇ makaḷukku eṉ peyaraiyiṭṭa nallanāḷil, naḷiruḷ-iraviṉ naṭuyāmattē, kāmaṉ kaiyaṟak kaṭunavai aṟukkum - kāmaṉ ceyalaṟṟuppōkap periya tuṉpaṅkaḷai aṟukkavalla, māperun tavakkoṭi īṉṟaṉai eṉṟē-mikap periya tavamuṭaiya makaḷaip peṟṟaṉai eṉṟu, naṉavēpōlak kaṉavakattu uraittēṉ -  nēriṟ kūṟuvataip pōlak kaṉaviṉkaṇ moḻintēṉ, īṅku ivvaṇṇam āṅku avaṭku urai eṉṟu - ivaṟṟaiyellām mātavikkuk kūṟuvāyāka eṉṟuraittu antarattu eḻuntu āṅku arunteyvam pōyapiṉ - vicumpil eḻuntu avvariya maṇimēkalā teyvam pōyiṉa piṉṉar; 
ētam il neṟi eytiyatu eṉṟatu puttaṉ aṟavaḻippaṭutal eṉka; itaṉai vicuttimārkkam eṉpa. teyvam-eṉkula mutalvaṉaik kēṭṭiṉiṉṟum nīkkip pātukātta teyvam eṉa viritturaittuk koḷka. koṭiyiṭai taṉṉai-koṭiyiṭaikku; koṭiyiṭai: maṇimēkalai. naḷḷiruḷ eṉpatu ceṟinta iruḷaiyuṭaiya iraviṉai yuṇarttiṟṟu. kaṭunavai - avā mutaliyaṉa. naṉṉāḷ naḷḷiruṭ kaṉavakattu uraittēṉ eṉka. 
41.		ventuyar eytic cutamati eḻuntāṅku-cutamati koṭiya tuṉpa maṭaintu eḻuntu;  
42-49.  	akalmaṉai araṅkattu āciriyar tammoṭu- akaṉṟa vīṭākiya araṅkiṉkaṇ āciriyaṉmāroṭu niṉṟu, vakai teri māk kaṭku vaṭṭaṇai kāṭṭi-aviṉaya mutaliya kūṟupāṭukaḷaiyaṟikiṉṟa makkaḷukkuk kamalavarttaṉai kāṭṭi, āṭal puṇarkkum araṅkiyal makaḷiril-āṭal puriyum nāṭaka makaḷiraippōla, kūṭiya kuyiluvak karuvi kaṇ tuyiṉṟu-oruṅkiyainta kuralyāḻ mutaliya kuyiluvak karuvi kaḷellām kaṇṭuyiṉṟu, paṇṇuk kiḷaipayirum paṇṇi yāḻt tīntoṭai-paṇṇiṉ vakaikaḷai icaikkum yāḻiṉ iṉiya narampukaḷ, koḷaival āyamōṭu icai kūṭṭuṇṭu- pāṭṭil valla makaḷiruṭaṉ icaiyaic cērntaṉupavittu, vaḷai cēr ceṅkai melviral utaitta-vaḷaikaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiya civanta kaikaḷiṉ melliya viralkaḷāl teṟitta, vemmai veytuṟātu taṉmaiyil tiriyavum- vemmai naṉku uṟāmaiyāl iyalpi ṉiṉṟu tiriyāniṟkavum;  
āciriyar-iyalāciriyaṉ, āṭalāciriyaṉ, yāḻāciriyaṉ, icaiyā ciriyaṉ, kuḻalāciriyaṉ, taṇṇumai yāciriyaṉ eṉpōr. vaṭṭaṇai- varttaṉai; kamala. varttaṉai; āvatu kaittalaṅ kāṭṭal. kiḷai-tiṟamu mām. tīntoṭai tiriyavum eṉka. 
50-53.	paṇpil kātalaṉ parattamai nōṉātu. kuṇamaṟṟa kātalaṉuṭaiya parattamaiyaip poṟāmal, uṇ kaṇ civantāṅku olku koṭipōṉṟu - acaikiṉṟa koṭi pōla maiyuṇṭa kaṇkaḷ civantu, teruṭṭavum teruḷātu ūṭaloṭu tuyilvōr-teḷi vikkavum teḷiyāmal ūṭaluṭaṉ tuyilkiṉṟa makaḷir, viraip pūmpaḷḷi vīḻtuṇai taḻuvavum - maṇam poruntiya malaraṇaiyil tammāl virumpappaṭukiṉṟa koḻunarait taḻuvavum;  
paṇpu - īṇṭu aṉpu. parattamai-parattaṉ taṉmai; parattaiyai maruvi yoḻukuvōṉ parattaṉ; 1"peṇṇiyalā rellāruṅ kaṇṇiṟ potuvuṇpar, naṇṇēṉ parattaniṉ mārpu" eṉpatu kāṇka. ūṭaloṭu tuyilvōr tāmē tuṇaiyait taḻuvavu meṉka. 
54-59.	taḷarnaṭai āyamoṭu taṅkātu ōṭi viḷaiyāṭu ciṟutēr īrttu meyvarunti - taḷarnaṭaic ciṟārkaḷuṭaṉ ōriṭattil taṅkāmal ōṭi viḷaiyāṭuñ ciṟu tēriṉai iḻuttu uṭalvarunti, amaḷit tuñcum-paṭukkaiyil uṟaṅku kiṉṟa, jampaṭait tālik kutalaic cevvāyk kuṟunaṭaip putalvarkku - jampaṭait tāli yaṇinta mārpum maḻalaic cevvāyum taḷarnaṭaiyumuṭaiya iḷam putalvarkku, kāvaṟ peṇṭir kaṭippakai eṟintu tūpam kāṭṭit tūṅku tuyil vatiyavum - cevilittāyar kaṭippakai vīcip pukai kāṭṭit tūṅkukiṉṟa uṟakkattiṟ poruntavum; 
aimpaṭai - tirumāliṉ pañcāyuta vaṭivamuṭaiya aṇikalaṉ; "cintupu ciṉṉī raimpaṭai naṉaippa" (3;138) eṉpuḻi uraittamai nōkkuka. kaṭippakai - pēykkup pakai; vēmpum, veṇciṟu kaṭukum; ik kātaiyuḷ mēlvarutal kāṇka. kāvaṟpeṇṭir tuyil vatiyavum eṉka. 
60-63.	iṟaiyuṟai puṟavum niṟainīrp puḷḷum - iṟappukaḷil vāḻ kiṉṟa puṟākkaḷum niṟainta nīriluḷḷa paṟavaikaḷum, kā uṟai paṟavaikaḷuma nā uḷ aḻunti-poḻilkaḷil uṟai kiṉṟa puṭkaḷum nā uḷḷē aḻunti, viḻavukkaḷi aṭaṅki muḻavukkaṇ tuyiṉṟu- viḻāviṉāluṇṭākiya kaḷippu aṭaṅki muḻavukaḷiṉ kaṇ tuyiṉṟu, paḻaviṟal mūtūr pāyal koḷ naṭunāḷ - paḻaiya vali yiṉaiyuṭaiya toṉṉakaram tuyiliṉaikkoṇṭa iṭaiyāmattil; 
nāvuḷḷaḻuntalāvatu : oliyātiruttal. aḻunti, aṭaṅki, tuyiṉṟu eṉṉum iṭattu nikaḻporuḷiṉ ṟoḻilkaḷ iṭattiṉ mēl niṉṟaṉa; aḻunti mutaliyavaṟṟaic ceyavaṉeccamākat tirittalumām.
64-65.	kōmakaṉ kōyil kuṟunīrk kaṉṉaliṉ - aracaṉ māḷikaiyil kuṟiya nīriṉaik koṇṭa nāḻikai vaṭṭilāl, yāmam koḷpavar ēttuoli aravamum-nāḻikaik kaṇakkiṭu vōr ēttukiṉṟa oliyākiya muḻakkamum; aracaṉai ētti nāḻikai kūṟuvarākaliṉ ‘ēttoliyaravamum' eṉṟār. oliyākiya aravameṉka ; aravam tamiḻccollē.
 66-67.	uṟaiyuḷ niṉṟu oṭuṅkiya uṇṇā uyakkattu-taṅku miṭattil niṉṟu oṭuṅkiya uṇṇāmaiyā ṉuṇṭākiya varuttattiṉāl, niṟai aḻiyāṉai neṭuṅkūviḷiyum - niṟaiyaḻinta yāṉaiyiṉatu neṭiya kūppīṭākiya muḻakkamum;  
68-69.	tērvaḻaṅku teruvum ciṟṟiṭai muṭukkarum - tērkaḷ cellukiṉṟa periyavītikaḷilum ciṟiya iṭaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya muṭuk kut terukkaḷilum, ūrkāppāḷar eṟituṭi ōtaiyum - ūr kāvalar aṭikkiṉṟa tuṭiyoliyum; 
70-72.	muḻaṅku nīr muṉṟuṟaik kalampuṇar kammiyar - olik kiṉṟa nīriṉaiyuṭaiya kaṭaṟṟuṟaikaḷil marakkalattiṟ cērntu toḻil purivōr, tuḻantaṭu kaḷḷiṉ tōppiyuṇṭu ayarntu - tuḻāvic camaitta kaḷ vakaikaḷil nellāṟ camaitta kaḷḷaiyuṇṭu tammai maṟantu, paḻañcerukkuṟṟa aṉantarp pāṇiyum - paḻaiya cerukkiṉaiyaṭainta mayakkat tuṭaṉ pāṭum pāṭṭiṉōcaiyum; 
kaḷḷākiya tōppi yeṉṟumām. tōppi - nellāṟ camaitta kaḷ. paḻañcerukku-mutirnta kaḷippu. aṉantar-kaḷḷuṇṭa mayakkam. 
73-76.	aravāyk kaṭippakai aiyavik kaṭippakai viraviya makaḷir ēntiya tūmattu-arattiṉ vāypōṉṟa vēmpākiya kaṭip pakaiyum veṇciṟukaṭukākiya kaṭippakaiyum kalantu iṭappaṭṭu makaḷirāl ēntappaṭṭa pukaiyuṭaṉ ceṉṟu, putalvaraip payanta puṉiṟu tīr kayakkam tīrviṉai makaḷir kuḷaṉ āṭu aravamum - putalvaraip peṟṟa puṉiṟu tīrumāṟu kayaṅkutal tīrkiṉṟa makaḷir kuḷaṅkaḷil nīrāṭukiṉṟa oliyum; 
kaṭippakai-pēykkup pakaiyeṉṉum poruḷatu, makaḷir ēntiya aravāyk kaṭippakaiyuṭaṉum aiyavik kaṭippakai viraviya tūmat tuṭaṉum eṉṟuraittalumām. puṉiṟu tīrnta makaḷir mayakkantīrnta makaḷir eṉat taṉittaṉi muṭittalumām. putalvarp payanta puṉiṟu tīrnta makaḷir iraviṟceṉṟu kuḷattil nīrāṭutal 1"kaṇava ruvappap putalvarp payantu, paṇaittēn tiḷamulai yamutamūṟap, pulavuppuṉiṟu tīrntu polinta cuṟṟamoṭu, vaḷamaṉai makaḷir kuḷanī rayara" eṉpataṉālum aṟiyappaṭum. pattāvatu iravil nīrāṭavēṇṭum eṉpar. 
77-80.	valitta neñciṉ āṭavar iṉṟiyum - pakaimaiyaik karutiya uḷḷamuṭaiya vīrar illātirukkavum, pulikkaṇattu aṉṉōr-pulikkūṭṭattiṉai yotta vīrarkaḷ, pūtacatuk kattuk koṭittēr vēntaṉ koṟṟam koḷka eṉa-pūtattaiyuṭaiya  nāṟcantiyilē koṭiyaṇinta tēriṉai yuṭaiya em aracaṉ veṟṟipeṟuka eṉṟu, iṭikkural muḻakkattu iṭumpali ōtaiyum iṭiyaṉaiya kural muḻakkat tuṭaṉ paliyiṭukiṉṟa ōcaiyum;
valitta-pōriṉaik karutiya eṉṟumām. āṭavar-īṇṭup pakaivar.  
81-85.   īṟṟiḷam peṇṭir āṟṟāp pālakar - īṉṟaṇimaiyai yuṭaiya iḷamakaḷir āṟṟāc ciṟuvar, kaṭuñcūl makaḷir neṭumpuṇ uṟṟōr - mutaṟ cūlaiyuṭaiya maṅkaiyar periya puṇkaḷāl varuntiṉōr, tam tuyar keṭukkum mantira mākkaḷ - ākiya ivarkaḷiṉ tuṉpaṅkaḷai nīkkukiṉṟa mantiravātikaḷ, maṉṟap pēy makaḷ vantu kaikkoḷka eṉa-maṉṟattiṉkaṇuḷḷa pēymakaḷ vantu peṟuka eṉṟukūṟi, niṉṟu eṟi paliyiṉ neṭuṅkural ōtaiyum-niṉṟu pali eṟikiṉṟa nīṇṭa kural muḻakkamum; 
kaṭuñcūl - mutaṟcūl; 1"kaṭuñcūṉ muṇṭakam katirmaṇi kaḻāalavum" eṉṟār piṟarum. paliyiṭumpoḻutu neṭuṅkura leṭuttukkūppiṭuvarākaliṉ ‘paliyiṉeṭuṅkural' eṉṟār: 
86.		palvēṟu ōtaiyum parantu oruṅkicaippa - ākiya palavēṟu vakaippaṭṭa ōcaiyum parantu oṉṟāka muḻaṅka;  
87-93.   kēṭṭu uḷam kalaṅki ūṭṭiruḷ aḻuvattu-avaṟṟai yellāṅ kēṭṭu maṉaṅkalaṅki ūṭṭiṉāṟpōṉṟa iruṭ parap pilē, muruntu ēr iḷanakai nīṅkip pūmpoḻil - mayiṟpīliyiṉ muḷaipōṉṟa aḻakiya paṟkaḷaiyuṭaiya cutamati uvavaṉattiṉiṉṟum nīṅki, tiruntu eyiṟ kuṭapāl ciṟu puḻai pōki - tiruntiya matiliṉ mēṟṟicaiyiluḷḷa ciṟiya vāyiliṉ vaḻiyē ceṉṟu, mikka māteyvam viyantu eṭuttu uraitta-perumaiporuntiya maṇimēkalāteyvam viyantu eṭuttukkūṟiya, cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭat tāṅkaṇ - cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭattiṇkaṇ, palar pukat tiṟanta pakuvāy vāyil-palaruñ cellumāṟu tiṟakkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟa periya vāypōlum vāyilaiyuṭaiya, ulaka aṟaviyiṉ orupuṭai iruttalum-ūrampalattil orupakkattil iruttalum; 
ūṭṭutal - pūcutal. aṟavi - ampalam cutamati eḻuntu pal vēṟu ōtaiyum oruṅkicaippak kēṭṭu uḷaṅkalaṅki nīṅkip pōki orupuṭai yiruttalum eṉka. 
94-97.	kantuṭai neṭunilaik kāraṇam kāṭṭiya antil eḻutiya aṟputap pāvai - neṭiya tōṟṟattaiyuṭaiya tūṇākiya av viṭattē paḻaviṉaiyākiya kāraṇattai aṉaivarukkum aṟivikkum poruṭṭu mayaṉ eṉṉum teyvat taccaṉāl eḻutappaṭṭu aṉaivarukkum viyappai yuṇṭupaṇṇum pāvaiyāṉatu, maittaṭaṅ kaṇṇāḷ mayaṅkiṉaḷ veruva-cutamati añci mayaṅkumāṟu, tippiyam uraikkum teyvak kiḷaviyiṉ - etirkālattil nikaḻ vaṉavaṟṟaiyum kūṟum tippiyamākiya teyvak kiḷaviyiṉālē; 
neṭunilai yuṭaik kantākiya antil eṉak kūṭṭuka. antil-āṅkeṉṉum poruṭṭu. kāṭṭiya : ceyyiyaveṉṉum viṉaiyeccam. varuvataṉai uraippatu teyvat taṉmaiyām eṉṟu koṇṭu 'tippiya muraikkum' eṉṟār. teyvakkiḷavi - teyvattiṉ moḻi. 
98-105.	iravivaṉmaṉ oru perumakaḷē - acōtara nakarattaraca ṉākiya iravivaṉmaṉatu oru perumakaḷē, turakat tāṉait tuccayaṉtēvi - kutiraic cēṉaikaḷai yuṭaiyavaṉum kaccaya nakarat taracaṉumākiya tuccayaṉuṭaiya maṉaiviyē, tayaṅku iṇarkkōtai tārai cāvuṟa-viḷaṅkukiṉṟa pūṅkot tiṉālāya mālaiyaiyuṭaiya tārai iṟakkumāṟu, mayaṅki yāṉaimuṉ maṉṉuyir nīttōy- yāṉaiyiṉ muṉṉē mayaṅki uyir tuṟantōy, kārāḷar caṇpaiyil kaucikaṉ makaḷē - kārāḷaruṭaiya caṇpai nakarattil uḷḷa kaucikaṉ eṉpavaṉuṭaiya putalviyē, mārutavēkaṉōṭu innakar pukuntu - mārutavēkaṉ eṉṉum viñcaiyaṉuṭaṉ in nakarattiṟ cērntu, tārai tavvai taṉṉoṭu kūṭiya - tamakkai yākiya tāraiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya, vīraiyākiya cutamati kēḷāy - vīrai yākiya cutamatiyē kēṭpāyāka ; 
iravivaṉmaṉ - cutamatiyiṉ muṟpiṟappilē tantaiyāyiruntavaṉ. tuccayaṉ - cutamatiyiṉ muṟpiṟappiṟ kaṇavaṉāyiruntavaṉ. tārai- cutamatiyiṉ muṟpiṟappil tamakkaiyāyiruntavaḷ. vīrai yāṉaiyāl iṟantatu kēṭṭu ivaḷ iṟantamaiyāl ‘tārai cāvuṟa' eṉṟāḷ ; cāvuṟa : etirkālattil vantatu. kārāḷar - vēḷāḷar ; caṇpai - cīkāḻi ; caṇpai yeṉpatu aṅkanāṭṭiṟ kaṅkaik karaiyiluḷḷa tākiya campā nakaram eṉṟum, kārāḷar eṉpatu karāḷar eṉpataṉ tiripeṉṟum kūṟuvārumuḷar; tārai, vīrai, ilakkumi eṉṉum mūṉṟu cakōtarikaḷuḷ, tārai mātaviyākavum, ilakkumi maṇimēkalai yākavum kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉattiṟ piṟan tiruttalāṉum, intiraviḻāk kāṇṭaṟku vanta mārutavēkaṉ eṉṉum viñcaiyaṉ tāṉ pūk koyyumpoḻutu taṉṉai eṭuttēkiṉaṉeṉṟu mēl (3:28-39) cutamati kūṟutalāṉum pukār nakarukku aṇittākiya cīkāḻiyeṉṟu koḷvatē poruttamām. kaucikaṉ - cutamatiyiṉ tantai.  
106-110.	iṉṟēḻ nāḷil iṭaiyiruḷ yāmattu - iṟṟaikku ēḻāvatu nāḷil iruḷaiyuṭaiya iṭaiyāmattiltaṉ piṟappataṉoṭu niṉ piṟappu uṇarntu īṅku ilakkumi yākiya niṉakku iḷaiyāḷ varum - taṉatu muṟpiṟappuṭaṉ niṉatu muṟpippiṉaiyum aṟintukoṇṭu niṉakku iḷai yāḷum ilakkumiyum ākiya maṇimēkalai īṇṭu varuvāḷ, añcal eṉṟu uraittatu - nī añcātē eṉṟu kūṟiyatu, avvurai kēṭṭu - ammoḻi kēṭṭu, neñcam naṭukkuṟūum nēriḻai nallāḷ - uḷḷam naṭuṅkiya nēriḻaiyākiya cutamati;
pāvai teyvak kiḷaviyiṉ uraittatu eṉka.  
111-126.	kāvalāḷar kaṇ tuyilkoḷḷa - ūrkāvalaruṭaiya kaṇkaḷ uṟakkattaip porunta, tūmeṉ cēkkait tuyilkaṇ viḻippa - tūya melliya paṭukkaiyil tuyilkiṉṟa āṭavar makaḷiruṭaiya kaṇkaḷ viḻippaṭaiya, valampuric caṅkam vaṟitu eḻuntu ārppa-valampuriyākiya caṅkukaḷ poruḷiṉṟi, muḻaṅka, pulam puric caṅkam poruḷoṭu muḻaṅka-aṟiviṉmikka pulavar kūṭṭam poruḷoṭum muḻaṅka, pukarmuka vāraṇam neṭuṅkūviḷippa - puḷḷi poruntiya mukattaiyuṭaiya yāṉaikaḷ nīṇṭa kūppīṭ ṭiṉaic ceyya, poṟi mayir vāraṇam kuṟuṅkū viḷippa-poṟip poṟiyākiya mayirkaḷaiyuṭaiya kōḻikaḷ kuṟiya kūvutalaicceyya, paṇainilaip puravi pala eḻuntu āla - pantiyil niṟṟalaiyuṭaiya kutiraikaḷ pala eḻuntu āṭa, paṇai nilaip puḷḷum pala eḻuntu āla-kiḷaikaḷiluḷḷa pala puṭkaḷum eḻuntu olikka, pūmpoḻil ārkaip puḷoli ciṟappa-pūñcōlaiyil matuvuṇṇutalaiyuṭaiya vaṇṭu kaḷiṉ olimika, pūṅkoṭiyār kaippuḷ oliciṟappa - pūṅkoṭi pōṉṟa makaḷiruṭaiya kaiyilaṇinta vaḷaikaḷiṉ oli mika, kaṭavuṭ pīṭikai pūppali kaṭaikoḷa - kaṭavuḷarkkuriya pīṭaṅkaḷiṟ koṭukkappaṭum polivupeṟṟa palikaḷ muṟṟup peṟa, kalampakar pīṭikaip pūppali kaṭaikoḷa-aṇikalaṅkaḷ viṟkap paṭukiṉṟa āvaṇa vītiyil malarāl ceyyappaṭum pūcaṉaiyaik kaṭaiyiṭaṅkaḷ koḷḷa, kuyiluvar kaṭai toṟum paṇiyam parantu eḻa-tōṟ karuvikaḷaip payil vōr vāyiltōṟum icaiyuṭaṉ kūṭiya iyaṅkaḷiṉ oli paravi mēmpaṭa, koṭuppōr kaṭaitoṟum paṇṇiyam parantu eḻa - koṭaiyāḷarkaḷuṭaiya vāyiltōṟum koṭuttaṟkuriya pala paṇṭaṅkaḷum parantu niṟaiya, ūr tuyil eṭuppa-ūriṉait tuyil eḻuppumāṟu, uravu nīr aḻuvattuk kāriruḷ cīttuk katiravaṉ muḷaittalum-kaṭaṟ parappilē kariya iruḷaik keṭuttu ñāyiṟu tōṉṟutalum;  
kāvalāḷar iraviṭai viḻittōrākaliṉ viṭiyalil tuyilvā rāvar. pulampuriyākiya caṅkameṉka. pukarmuka vāraṇam kavaḷam vēṇṭik kūppiṭāniṟkum. puḷ-paṟavai. vaṇṭu, vaḷaiyal, teyvaṅkaṭku iraviṟ pali koṭuppārākaliṉ atu muṭivuṟṟa teṉṟār, pū - polivu. illuṟai teyvattiṉ poruṭṭuk kālaiyil pūviṭutal marapākaliṉ 'kalampakar pīṭikaip pūppali kaṭaikoḷa' eṉṟār ; kaṭai vāyilumām; 1"pūppali ceytu kāppukkaṭai niṟutti" eṉpatu kāṇka. paṇiyam-paṇṇappaṭṭa vāttiyam eṉṟumām. 
127-134.	ēvuṟu maññaiyiṉ iṉaintu - ampu paṭṭuruviya mayilaippōla uḷḷam naintu, aṭi varunta mānakar vīti maruṅkil pōki - aṭikaḷ varuntak kaṭantu periya nakarattiṉ vītivaḻiyē ceṉṟu, pōya kaṅkulil pukuntatai ellām-ceṉṟa iravil nikaḻntavaṟṟai ellām, mātavi taṉakku vaḻuviṉṟu uraittalum-mātavikku vaḻuvātu kūṟutalum, naṉmaṇi iḻanta nākam pōṉṟu avaḷ-mātavi nalla maṇiyai iḻanta araviṉaippōla, taṉmakaḷ vārāt taṉittuyar uḻappa - taṉ makaḷ vārātatāl oppaṟṟa tuṉpattāl varunta, iṉuyir iḻanta yākkaiyiṉ iruntaṉaḷ tuṉṉiyatu uraitta cutamatitāṉ eṉ - avaṭku nērntataik kūṟiya cutamatitāṉ iṉiya uyiraiyiḻanta uṭalaippōl acai vaṟṟiruntaṉaḷ eṉka. 
pukuntataiyellām: orumaiyiṟ paṉmai. ‘naṉmaṇi yiḻanta nākam pōṉṟu' (25; 95) eṉa mēl innūluḷḷum, 2"iru nitik kiḻavaṉum perumaṉaik kiḻattiyum, arumaṇi yiḻanta nākam pōṉṟatum" eṉac cilappatikārattilum maṇiyiḻanta nākamum, 3"iṉṉuyi riḻanta yākkai yeṉṉat, tuṉṉiya cuṟṟam tuyark kaṭal vīḻntatum" eṉac cilappatikārattil uyiriḻanta yākkaiyum uvamamāka varutal kāṇka.
maṇimēkalā teyvam nīṅkit tōṉṟi avaṉvayi ṉuraitta piṉ pukuntu nīkki uraiyeṉṟu pōyapiṉ, cutamati eḻuntu pal vēṟu ōtaiyum icaippak kēṭṭu uḷaṅkalaṅki nīṅkippōki oru puṭai iruttalum, pāvai teyvakkiḷaviyiṉ "varum, añcal" eṉ ṟuraittatu ; ataṉaik kēṭṭu naṭukkuṟu nallāḷ katiravaṉ muḷaittalum pōki, mātavik kuraittalum, avaḷ tuyaruḻappat tāṉ uyiriḻanta yākkaipōl iruntaṉaḷ eṉa viṉai muṭikka.
tuyileḻuppiya kātai muṟṟiṟṟu. 


8. maṇipallavattut tuyaruṟṟa kātai  
 cutamati pukāriṉkaṇ ceyalaṟṟirukka, maṇipallavattiṟ kaṭalarukē maṇaliṟ ṟuyiṉṟa maṇimēkalai, tuyiluṇarntu tāṉ muṉ paṟinta poruḷ oṉṟum kāṇappaṭātu aṟiyāta poruḷkaḷē kāṇappaṭutaliṉ vēṟiṭattiṟ ceṉṟu piṟanta uyir pōṉṟavaḷākit tikaippuṟum poḻutu katiravaṉ utittāṉ. utittavuṭaṉ avaḷ 'ivviṭam uvavaṉattil muṉṉam kaṇṭaṟiyāta oru pakutiyō! itu naṉavō kaṉavō eṉpatai aṟikiṉṟilēṉ; maṉam naṭuṅku kiṉṟatu: cutamati eṅkoḷittāy ? ōr māṟṟam tarukiṉṟilai; iruḷ kaḻintatu; mātavi varuntuvaḷ ; itu viñcaiyuṭaṉ tōṉṟiya am maṭantai ceyta vañcamō! oṉṟum teriya villaiyē! taṉiyē irukka mikavum añcukiṉṟēṉ; viraintu varuvāyāka,' eṉṟu kūṟik koṇṭu nīrttuṟaikaḷilum maṇaṟkuṉṟu kaḷilum eṅkaṇum ceṉṟu tēṭit taṉakkup pāṅkāyiṉār oruvaraiyum kāṇātavaḷākik kūvi aḻutu varuntupavaḷ, taṉ tantaiyai niṉaintu 'kōṟṟoṭi māta roṭu vēṟṟu nāṭaṭaintu, vaivāḷ uḻanta maṇippūṇ akalattu, aiyāvō!' eṉṟu aḻutaṉaḷ ; aḻupavaḷ muṉṉar, intiraṉāl iṭappaṭṭatum, taricittōrkkup paḻam piṟappaip pulappaṭuttuvatumākiya putta pīṭikai tōṉṟiyatu.  
		īṅkiva ḷiṉṉaṇa māka iruṅkaṭal
vāṅkutirai yuṭutta maṇipal lavattiṭait
tattuvanī raṭaikaraic caṅkuḻu toṭuppiṉ
muttuviḷai kaḻaṉi muricem pavaḷamoṭu
5		viraimara muruṭṭun tiraiyulāp parappiṉ
ñāḻal ōṅkiya tāḻkaṇ acumpiṉ
āmpaluṅ kuvaḷaiyum tāmpuṇarntu mayaṅki
vaṇṭuṇa malarnta kuṇṭunī rilañci
muṭakkāṟ puṉṉaiyum maṭaṟpūn tāḻaiyum
10		veyilvara voḻitta payilpūm pantar
aṟalviḷaṅku nilāmaṇal naṟumalarp paḷḷit
tuñcutuyi leḻūum añci lōti
kātaṟ cuṟṟam maṟantu kaṭaikoḷa
vēṟiṭattup piṟanta uyirē pōṉṟu
15		paṇṭaṟi kiḷaiyoṭu patiyuṅ kāṇāḷ
kaṇṭaṟi yātaṉa kaṇṇiṟ kāṇā
nīla mākkaṭal neṭṭiṭai yaṉṟiyuṅ
kālai ñāyiṟu katirvirittu muḷaippa
uvavaṉa maruṅkiṉil ōriṭaṅ kollitu
20		cutamati oḷittāy tuyarañ ceytaṉai
naṉavō kaṉavō eṉpatai aṟiyēṉ
maṉanaṭuk kuṟūum māṟṟan tārāy
valliruḷ kaḻintatu mātavi mayaṅkum
elvaḷai vārāy viṭṭakaṉ ṟaṉaiyō
25		viñcaiyil tōṉṟiya viḷaṅkiḻai maṭavāḷ
vañcañ ceytaṉaḷ kollō aṟiyēṉ
orutaṉi yañcuveṉ tiruvē vāveṉat
tiraitavaḻ paṟavaiyum viriciṟaip paṟavaiyum
eḻuntuvīḻ cillaiyum oṭuṅkuciṟai muḻuvalum
30		aṉṉac cēval araca ṉākap
paṉṉiṟap puḷḷiṉam parantoruṅ kīṇṭip
pācaṟai maṉṉar pāṭi pōla
vīcunīrp parappiṉ etireti rirukkum
tuṟaiyun tuṟaicūḻ neṭumaṇaṟ kuṉṟamum
35		yāṅkaṇun tirivōḷ pāṅkiṉaṅ kāṇāḷ
kuraṟṟalaik kūntal kulaintupiṉ vīḻa
araṟṟiṉaḷ kūuy aḻutaṉaḷ ēṅki
vīḻtuya reytiya viḻumak kiḷaviyil
tāḻtuya ruṟuvōḷ tantaiyai uḷḷi
40		emmitiṟ paṭuttum vevviṉai yuruppak
kōṟṟoṭi mātaroṭu vēṟṟunā ṭaṭaintu
vaivā ḷuḻanta maṇippūṇ akalattu
aiyā vōveṉ ṟaḻuvōḷ muṉṉar
virintilaṅ kaviroḷi ciṟantukatir parappi
45		uraipeṟu mummuḻam nilamicai ōṅkit
ticaitoṟum oṉpāṉ muḻanila makaṉṟu
vitimā ṇāṭiyiṉ vaṭṭaṅ kuyiṉṟu
patuma catura mīmicai viḷaṅki
aṟavōṟ kamainta ācaṉa meṉṟē
50		naṟumala rallatu piṟamarañ coriyātu 
paṟavaiyum mutirciṟai pāṅkuceṉ ṟatirātu
tēvarkō ṉiṭṭa māmaṇip pīṭikai
piṟappuviḷaṅ kaviroḷi aṟattakai yācaṉam
kīḻnila maruṅkiṉ nākanā ṭāḷum
55		iruvar maṉṉavar oruvaḻit tōṉṟi
ematī teṉṟē eṭukka lāṟṟār
tamperum paṟṟu nīṅkalum nīṅkār
ceṅkaṇ civantu neñcupukai uyirttut
tamperuñ cēṉaiyoṭu veñcamam purināḷ
60		iruñceru oḻimiṉ ematīteṉṟē
peruntava muṉivaṉ iruntaṟa muraikkum
poruvaṟu ciṟappiṟ puraiyō rēttum
taruma pīṭikai tōṉṟiya tāṅkeṉ 
 urai                    
1-12.		īṅku ivaḷ iṉṉaṇamāka - pukār nakarilē cutamati ivvāṟu varunta, iruṅkaṭal vāṅku tirai uṭutta maṇi pallavat tiṭai-periya kaṭaliṉatu vaḷainta alaikaḷāṟ cūḻappaṭṭa maṇipallavattiṉkaṇ, tattunīr aṭaikaraic caṅku uḻutoṭuppiṉ muttu viḷai kaḻaṉi-caṅkukaḷāl uḻutu vitaikkappaṭṭa muttukkaḷ viḷaikiṉṟa mōtunīr cūḻ aṭaikaraiyākiya kaḻaṉiyil, muri cempavaḷamoṭu viraimaram uruṭṭum tirai ulāpparappil-vaḷainta civanta pavaḷaṅkaḷuṭaṉ cantaṉam akil mutaliya maṇamporuntiya maraṅkaḷai uruṭṭukiṉṟa alaikaḷ ulāvum kaṭaṟkaraiyil, ñāḻal ōṅkiya tāḻkaṇ acumpiṉ-pulinakak koṉṟai uyarnta tāḻnta iṭattaiyuṭaiya cēṟṟil, āmpalum kuvaḷaiyum tām puṇarntu mayaṅki - alliyum kuvaḷaiyu mākiya malarkaḷ cērntu kalantu, vaṇṭu uṇa malarnta kuṇṭu nīr ilañci-vaṇṭukaḷ uṇṇumāṟu malarappeṟṟa āḻamākiya nīriṉaiyuṭaiya poykaik karaiyil, muṭakkāl puṉṉaiyum maṭaṟpūn  tāḻaiyum - vaḷainta aṭiyai yuṭaiya puṉṉaiyum maṭalkaḷālākiya pūviṉaiyuṭaiya tāḻaiyum, veyil varavu oḻinta payil pūmpantar-veyiliṉatu varavai nīkkiya aḻakiya pantariṉkaṇ, aṟal viḷaṅku nilāmaṇal naṟumalarp paḷḷi - aṟal viḷaṅkukiṉṟa nilāvaip pōṉṟa veḷḷiya maṇalil naṟiya malarkaḷālākiya paṭukkaiyi liruntu, tuñcu tuyil eḻūum añcil ōti - meymaṟanta uṟakkattiṉiṉṟum eḻunta aḻakiya cilavākiya kūntalai yuṭaiya maṇimēkalai; 
aṭaikaraikkaṇ kaḻaṉiyil eṉṟumām. toṭuppu - vitaippu; 1"toṭuppi ṉāyiram vittiyatu viḷaiya" eṉpatu kāṇka; viḷāk koṇṭu uḻutalumām. muri eṉpataṟkuc citaṟiya eṉṟum, oḷiviṭu meṉṟum uraittalumām. aṟal - nīr arittu oḻukuvatāl maṇal aṟṟu aṟṟu iruppatu. maṇipallavat tiṭaip parappil ilañcik karaiyil maṇaliṟ paḷḷiyil tuyileḻunta maṇimēkalai yeṉka. 
13-27.	kātaṟ cuṟṟam maṟantu kaṭaikoḷa vēṟiṭattup piṟanta uyirē pōṉṟu - aṉpiṉ mikka cuṟṟaṅkaḷai maṟantu muṭivu koḷḷa vēṟiṭattil tōṉṟiya uyiraippōla, paṇṭu aṟi kiḷaiyoṭu patiyum kāṇāḷ-muṉṉart taṉṉālaṟiyap paṭṭa cuṟṟattuṭaṉ nakarattaiyum kāṇātavaḷāki, kaṇṭu aṟiyātaṉa kaṇṇiṟ kāṇā - muṉ kaṇṭaṟiyap paṭātaṉa vaṟṟaik kaṇkaḷāṟ kaṇṭu, nīla māk kaṭal neṭṭiṭai aṉṟiyum - nīlaniṟamuṭaiya periya kaṭalil aṇimaikkaṇ, kālai ñāyiṟu katir virittu muḷaippa - iḷañāyiṟu katirkaḷai virittu utikka, uvavaṉa maruṅkil ōriṭam kol itu-iḵtu uvavaṉattil ōriṭamō, cutamati oḷittāy- cutamatī  oḷintu koṇṭāyō, tuyaram ceytaṉai - tuṉpam ceytaṉai, naṉavō kaṉavō eṉpatai aṟiyēṉ-itu naṉavō kaṉavō eṉpatai aṟikiṉṟilēṉ, maṉam naṭukkuṟūum māṟṟam tārāy-uḷḷam naṭuṅkutaluṟukiṉṟatu maṟumoḻi taruvāy, valliruḷ kaḻintatu mātavi mayaṅkum-valitākiya iruḷ nīṅkiyatu mātavi mayaṅkuvāḷ, elvaḷai vārāy-oḷi poruntiya vaḷaiyiṉai yuṭaiyāy varuvāyāka, viṭṭu akaṉṟaṉaiyō - eṉṉaiviṭṭuc ceṉṟaṉaiyō, viñcaiyil tōṉṟiya viḷaṅkiḻai maṭavāḷ - viḷaṅkukiṉṟa aṇikaḷuṭaṉ vittaiyiṉālē tōṉṟiyamaṭantai, vañcam ceytaṉaṉ kollō aṟiyēṉ - vañcaṉai ceytaṉaḷō aṟiyēṉē, oru taṉi añcuvēṉ tiruvē vā eṉa-oppaṟṟa taṉimaiyai añcukiṉṟēṉ tiruvaṉaiyāy varuka eṉṟu;  
kaṭaikoḷa - muṟṟa; viṉaimuṭi veyta eṉṟumām. neṭṭiṭai aṉṟiyum - neṭuntūram aṉṟāka; aṇimaiyil; um: acai. uvavaṉam eṉpatu mutal maṇimēkalai kūṟṟu. ōriṭaṅkol-muṉ kaṇṭaṟiyāta ōriṭamō veṉka. vārāy - varukiṉṟilai eṉavum, varātu eṉavum uraittalumām. melvaḷai yeṉṟalumām.  
28-35.	tirai tavaḻ paṟavaiyum viriciṟaip paṟavaiyum-alaiyil tavaḻkiṉṟa puṭkaḷum virinta ciṟakiṉaiyuṭaiya paṟavaikaḷum, eḻuntu vīḻ cillaiyum oṭuṅkuciṟai muḻuvalum - eḻuntu vīḻkiṉṟa cillaiyum oṭuṅkiya ciṟakiṉaiyuṭaiya muḻuvalum, aṉṉac cēval aracaṉākap paṉṉiṟap puḷḷiṉam parantu oruṅku īṇṭi - aṉṉaccēvalai aracaṉākak koṇṭu pala  niṟaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiya paṟavai iṉaṅkaḷ parantu oṉṟākak kūṭi, pācaṟai maṉṉar pāṭi pōla - pakaiyaracar iruvaruṭaiya paṭai vīṭukaḷil iruvar paṭaiyum etiretir iruttalpōla, vīcunīrp parappiṉ etir etir  irukkum -vīcukiṉṟa nīrpparappiṉ kaṇṇē etiretirē  irukkiṉṟa, tuṟaiyum tuṟaicūḻ neṭumaṇaṟ kuṉṟamum - nīrt tuṟaikaḷum tuṟaikaḷaic cuṟṟilumuḷḷa periya maṇaṟ kuṉṟukaḷum ākiya,yāṅkaṇum tirivōṉ-evviṭattum tirikiṉṟavaḷ; 
tiraitavaḻ paṟavai - kuḷuvai mutaliyaṉa. viriciṟaip paṟavai - pōtā mutaliyaṉa. eḻuntuvīḻ cillai - ciral mutaliyaṉa. tirai tavaḻ paṟavai mutaliya nāṉkum nīrvāḻ paṟavaikaḷ. paṟavaiyum paṟavaiyum cillaiyum muḻuvalumākiya paṉṉiṟap puḷḷiṉam eṉka. pācaṟai maṉṉar - pōr kuṟittup pācaṟaiyiṟ ceṉṟu taṅkiyirukkum maṉṉar. pāṭipōla - pāṭiyiṟ paṭaikaḷ etiretir iruttalpōla - 1"kampuṭ kōḻiyum kaṉaikural nāraiyum, ceṅkā laṉṉamum paiṅkāṟ kokkum, kāṉak kōḻiyum nīlaniṟak kākkaiyum, uḷḷum ūralum puḷḷum putāvum, velpōr vēntar muṉaiyiṭam pōlap. palvēṟu kuḻūukkural paranta vōtaiyum" eṉpatu īṇṭu ottu nōkkaṟpālatu. 
35-43.	pāṅkiṉam kāṇāḷ-pakkattiluḷḷa iṉaṅkaḷaik kāṇātavaḷāy, kuraltalai kūntal kulaintu piṉ vīḻa - pūṅkottai iṭattēyuṭaiya kuḻal aviḻntu piṉṉē vīḻa, araṟṟiṉaḷ kūuy - kūvip pulampi, aḻutaṉaḷ ēṅki - ēṅki aḻutu, vīḻtuyar eytiya viḻumak kiḷaviyiṉ tāḻtuyar uṟuvōḷ - āḻamākiya tuṉpattiṉkaṇṇē poruntiṉōḷ mikka tuyaramaṭainta tuṉpattaip pulappaṭuttum moḻikaḷāl, tantaiyai uḷḷi - tantaiyākiya kōvalaṉai niṉaintu, em itil paṭuttum vevviṉai uruppa-emmai ivvakait tuṉpattiṉkaṇ akappaṭuttum koṭiya tīviṉaiyāṉatu vantu uruppa, kōṟṟoṭi mātaroṭu vēṟṟu nāṭu aṭaintu-tiraṭciyākiya vaḷaiyalkaḷai yaṇinta eṉ aṉṉai kaṇṇaki yōṭu vēṟu nāṭṭai aṭaintu, vaivāḷ uḻanta maṇippūṇ akalattu aiyāvō eṉṟu aḻuvōḷ muṉṉar - kūriya vāḷiṉāl veṭṭuṇṭu varuntiya maṇippūṇ tāṅkiya mārpiṉaiyuṭaiya aiyāvō eṉṟu aḻukiṉṟavaḷ muṉṉē; 
kural - kūntaliṉ tiraṭciyumām. araṟṟiṉaḷ, aḻutaṉaḷ eṉpaṉa muṟṟeccam. uṟuvōḷ viḻumakkiḷaviyiṉ aḻuvōḷ eṉak kūṭṭuka. uruppa - mutira; vekuḷa eṉṟumām. ōkāram pulampalil vantatu. 
44-53.	virintu ilaṅku aviroḷi ciṟantu - virintu viḷaṅku kiṉṟa pēroḷi mikku, katirparappi-kiraṇaṅkaḷai virittu, urai peṟu mummuḻam nilamicai ōṅki - pukaḻamainta nilattiṉ mītu mūṉṟu muḻa aḷaviṉukku uyarntu, ticaitoṟum oṉpāṉ muḻanilam akaṉṟu-nāṟṟicaiyilum oṉpatumuḻa aḷaviṉaiyuṭaiya iṭam akaṉṟu, vitimāṇ āṭiyiṉ vaṭṭam kuyiṉṟu -viti māṭcimaippaṭṭa paḷiṅkiṉāl vaṭṭañ ceyyappaṭṭu, patuma caturam mīmicai viḷaṅki-patumattaiyuṭaiya caturam mēliṭattil viḷaṅkappeṟṟu, aṟavōṟku amainta ācaṉam eṉṟē-puttaṉukku amainta ākaṉam eṉṟu, naṟumalar allatu piṟa marañ coriyātu - maraṅkaḷ naṟumalar kaḷai aṉṟi vēṟoṉṟaiyum coriyā, paṟavaiyum mutirciṟai pāṅku ceṉṟu atirātu - paṟavaikaḷum appīṭikaiyiṉ pakkattiṟ ceṉṟu ciṟaiyoli ceyyā, tēvarkōṉ iṭṭa māmaṇippīṭikai - intiraṉāliṭappaṭṭa perumai poruntiya maṇikaḷāṉāya pīṭikai, piṟappu viḷaṅku aṟattakai ācaṉam-kāṇpōratu paḻam piṟappu viḷaṅkutaṟku ētuvākiya oḷi poruntiya aṟattiṉiyalpuṭaiya ācaṉam;  
ciṟantu parappi ōṅki akaṉṟu kuyiṉṟu viḷaṅki amainta ācaṉam eṉka. viti - ceykai. patumam - puttaṉ pātamum ām. mīmicai, oru poruṭ paṉmoḻi. maram piṟa coriyātu, paṟavai ciṟai atirātu, avvāṟākat tēvar kōṉ iṭṭa pīṭikaiyeṉa virikka. 
54-63. 	kīḻnila maruṅkiṉ nākanāṭu āḷum - kīḻt ticaikkaṇ uḷḷa nāka nāṭṭiṉai āḷum. iruvar maṉṉavar oruvaḻit tōṉṟi-maṉṉavar iruvar oruṅku tōṉṟi, ematu ītu eṉṟē eṭukkal āṟṟār tam perum paṟṟu nīṅkalu nīṅkār - iḵtu em muṭaiyatu emmuṭaiyatu eṉṟu kūṟi eṭukkavum iyalātavarkaḷāyt tammuṭaiya periya viruppam nīṅku talaiyum ceyyātavarkaḷāy, ceṅkaṇ civantu neñcupukai uyirttu-civanta kaṇkaḷ ciṉattāṟ civantu maṉaṅkotittu, tam perum cēṉaiyōṭu veñcamam purināḷ-tammuṭaiya peruñ cēṉaikaḷuṭam koṭiya pōr puriyunāḷil, iruñceru oḻimiṉ ematu ītu eṉṟē - nīvīr numatu periya pōrai nīṅkumiṉ; iḵtu emmuṭaiyatu eṉṟu kūṟi, peruntava muṉivaṉ iruntu aṟam uraikkum - periya tavamuṉi vaṉākiya puttaṉ iruntu aṟam uraitta, poruvaṟu ciṟappil puraiyōr ēttum-oppaṟṟa ciṟappiṉaiyuṭaiya uyarntōrkaḷ tutikkiṉṟa, taruma pīṭikai tōṉṟiyatu āṅkeṇ - taruma pīṭikai avviṭattil tōṉṟiyatu eṉka.  
nāka ilacciṉai yuṭaimaiyāl nākareṉap peyarpeṟṟa oruvakai makkaḷ vāḻum nāṭu nākanāṭu eṉpar. innāṭṭiṉ peyar piṉṉarum innūliṟ palaviṭattu vantuḷatu. tama, a-āṟaṉurupu. nīṅkalum nīṅkār eṉṉum aṭukkiṉuḷ piṉṉatu potuviṉaiyākum ; muṉpu, "cuḻalaluñ cuḻalum, ōṭalu mōṭum oru ciṟai yotuṅki, nīṭalum nīṭum," (3 : 111-3) eṉa vantamaiyuṅ kāṇka.
māmaṇip pīṭikai, aṟattakaiyācaṉam, taruma pīṭikai eṉpaṉa oru poruḷmēl vantaṉa. aḻuvōḷ muṉṉar pīṭikai tōṉṟiya teṉka.
ivaḷ iṉṉaṇamāka, maṇipallavattiṭait tuyileḻum añci lōti, pōṉṟu kāṇāḷāyk kaṇṭu tiruvē vāveṉṟu aḻuvōḷ muṉṉart taruma pīṭikai āṅkut tōṉṟiyateṉka.  
maṇipallavattut tuyaruṟṟa kātai muṟṟiṟṟu. 


9. pīṭikai kaṇṭu piṟappuṇarnta kātai  
pīṭikaiyaik kaṇṭa maṇimēkalai viyappiṉāl taṉṉai yaṟiyāḷāyiṉāḷ; avaḷ kaikaḷ talaimēṟ kuvintaṉa ; ataṉai mummuṟai valamvantu paṇintu eḻupavaḷ ataṉ kāṭciyāl taṉ paḻampiṟappiṉ nikaḻccikaḷai yuṇarntu, "mātava! kāyaṅkarai yeṉṉum natikkaraiyil nī kūṟiyavellām uṇmaiyātalai aṟintēṉ; kāntāra nāṭṭiṉ aracaṉākiya attipati yeṉṉum aracaṟku maittuṉaṉākiya piramatarumaṉē! nī avaṉpāṟ ceṉṟu aṟamuraikkaiyil, ‘in nāvalan tīvil iṟṟaikku ēḻām nāḷiṟ pūkampa muṇṭākum; appoḻutu in nakarum nākanāṭṭil nāṉūṟu yōcaṉaip parappum pātalattil vīḻntu keṭṭoḻiyum: ātaliṉ itiṉiṉṟum nīṅkuka,' eṉṉa, aracaṉum nakariluḷḷa makkaṭkup paṟaicāṟṟi yaṟivittu iṭavayameṉṉum appatiyai nīṅki, vaṭakkiluḷḷa avanti nakarkkuc celvōṉ iṭaiyē kāyaṅkaraiyiṉ karaiyiṟ pāṭiceytiruppa, nī kuṟitta nāḷil annakar aḻintatu, atukaṇṭa aracaṉum ēṉaiyarum niṉṉaic cūḻntu vaṇaṅka, nī avarkaṭku aruḷaṟattaip pōtittuk koṇṭiruntaṉai; appoḻutu acōtara nakarattaracaṉākiya iravivaṉmaṉtēvi amutapati vayiṟṟiṟ piṟantu ilakkumi yeṉṟu peyareyti, attipati yeṉṉum aracaṟku nīlapati yeṉpavaḷpāṟ piṟanta irākulaṉukku maṉaiviyākap pukka nāṉ eṉ kaṇavaṉuṭaṉ vantu aṟaṅ kēṭṭaṟku vaṇaṅkiyavuṭaṉ, nī eṉṉai nōkki, "ivvirākulaṉ iṟṟaikkup patiṉāṟām nāḷil tiṭṭiviṭameṉṉum pāmpāl iṟappāṉ, nī ivaṉuṭaṉ tīyiṟpukuvāy; piṉpu kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉattiṟ ceṉṟu piṟappāy; aṅku niṉakku oru tuṉpam uṇṭākum; appoḻutu maṇimēkalā teyvam vantu naḷḷiravil uṉṉai eṭuttucceṉṟu teṉṟicai maruṅkiluḷḷa tīvu oṉṟil vaittuccellum; ceṉṟapiṉ āṅkuḷḷa putta pīṭikaiyaik kaṇṭu toḻuvāy; appoḻutē uṉatu muṟpiṟappil nikaḻnta ceytikaḷai aṟintu, iṉṟu yāṉ kūṟiya uraiyiṉait teḷivāy,' eṉṟu colla, "eṉ kātalaṉ piṟappaiyum terivikkavēṇṭum" eṉṟu kēṭṭēṉ; ‘uṉṉaikkoṇṭu ceṉṟa teyvam mīṇṭun tōṉṟi, uṉakku avaṉaip pulappaṭuttum,' eṉṟu kūṟiṉāy; at teyvam varātō?" eṉa ēṅki aḻutukoṇṭiruntaṉaḷ. 
		āṅkatu kaṇṭa āyiḻai aṟiyāḷ
kāntaḷañ ceṅkai talaimēṟ kuvintaṉa
talaimēṟ kuvinta kaiyaḷ ceṅkaṇ
mulaimēṟ kaluḻntumut tattira ḷukuttatiṉ 
5		iṭamuṟai mummuṟai valamuṟai vārāk
koṭimiṉ mukiloṭu nilañcērn teṉṉa
iṟunucup palaca veṟunilañ cērntāṅku
eḻuvōḷ piṟappu vaḻuviṉ ṟuṇarntu
toḻutakai mātava tuṇiporu ḷuṇarntōy
10		kāyaṅ karaiyiṉī yuraittatai yellām
vāyē yākutaṉ mayakkaṟa uṇarntēṉ
kāntāra meṉṉuṅ kaḻiperu nāṭṭup
pūruva tēyam poṟaikeṭa vāḻum
atti patiyeṉum aracāḷ vēntaṉ
15		maittuṉa ṉākiya pirama tarumaṉ
āṅkavaṉ ṟaṉpāl aṇaintaṟa ṉuraippōy
tīṅkaṉi nāval ōṅkumit tīviṭai
iṉṟēḻ nāḷil irunila mākkaḷ
niṉṟunaṭuk keyta nīṇila vēntē
20		pūmi naṭukkuṟūum pōḻtat tinnakar
