kōṟal - koṉṟuṇṭal eṉṟumām. kayakku-kalaṅkutal; īṇṭuk kalakkamāvatu aṟiviṉ tiripu. cātalum piṟattalum, uṟaṅkalum viḻittalum pōlutal  1"uṟaṅkuvatu pōluñ cākkā ṭuṟaṅki, viḻippatu pōlum piṟappu" eṉṉum vāyuṟai vāḻttāṉum aṟiyappaṭum. cātal uṟaṅkalum piṟattal viḻittalu meṉa niraṉiṟai.allaṟam-aṟamallatu; pāvam. 
 91-95.	kaṭu nakai eyti - nākar kuravaṉ peruñ cirippuṭai yaṉāy, uṭampuviṭṭu ōṭum uyir urukkoṇṭu ōr iṭam pukum eṉṟē  emakku īṅku uraittāy-uṭalai viṭṭup pirikiṉṟa uyirāṉatu vēṟu vaṭivu koṇṭu ōr iṭattiṟ pukum eṉṟu emakku ippoḻutu kūṟiṉāy, avvuyir evvaṇam pōyp pukum avvakai cevvaṉam urai eṉa - av vuyir evvāṟu ceṉṟu pukum attiṟattiṉai naṉku kūṟuvāyāka eṉa moḻiya; eḷḷal paṟṟi nakai piṟantatu. cevvaṉam - nērē. 
 95-106.	(ataṉaik kēṭṭa cātuvaṉ,) ciṉavātu itu kēḷ-cīṟṟaṅ koḷḷātu itaṉaik kēṭpāyāka, uṟṟatai uṇarum uṭal uyir vāḻvuḻi - uyirāṉatu taṉṉiṭam uṟaikiṉṟapoḻutu uṭal taṉpāl uṟukiṉṟataṉai uṇarum, maṟṟaiya uṭampē maṉṉuyir pōṉāl-aṅṅaṉamākiya uṭalē taṉṉiṭam nilaipeṟṟa uyir pirintāl, taṭintu eri ūṭṭiṉum tāṉ uṇarātu eṉiṉ - veṭṭit tīyiṉkaṇ iṭṭālum tāṉ aṟiyātu eṉṟāl, uṭampiṭaip pōṉatu oṉṟu uṇṭeṉa uṇar nī - avvuṭampiliruntu pirintu ceṉṟatu oṉṟu uṇṭu eṉpatai nī aṟivāy, pōṉār tamakku ōr pukkil uṇṭu eṉpatu-aṅṅaṉam ceṉṟōrkku oru pukumiṭam uṇṭu eṉpatai, yāṉō allēṉ yāvarum uṇarkuvar-yāṉ maṭṭu maṉṟu ellōrum aṟikuvar. uṭampu īṇṭu oḻiya uyir pala kāvatam kaṭantu cēṇ cēṟal - yākkai iv viṭattil nīṅka uyir pala kāvataṅkaḷ kaṭantu neṭuntūrattiṟ cellutal, kaṉaviṉum kāṇkuvai-kaṉaviṉ kaṇṇum kāṇpāy, āṅṅaṉam pōki avvuyir ceyviṉai pūṇṭa yākkaiyil pukuvatu teḷi nī - aṅṅaṉam ceṉṟu avvuyir tam viṉaip payaṉai nukartaṟkuriya uṭaliṉkaṇ pukuvatait teḷivāy nī, eṉṟu avaṉ uraittalum - eṉṟu cātuvaṉ moḻitalum;
uṟṟatai yuṇartal-taṭpam veppam mutaliya ūṟṟiṉai yuṇartal. maṟṟaiya-muṟkūṟiya eṉṟapaṭi; maṟṟai eṉpataṉai acaiyākki, avvuṭampu eṉṉalumām. eṉiṉ - ātalāl ōriṭattaiviṭṭup pōṉavarkkup piṟitōriṭam uṇṭeṉa ulakiyalāṟ kūṟiṉāṉ, pūṇṭa-ēṟṟukkoṇṭa; ēṟṟu nukar taṟkuriya eṉṟapaṭi.
106-111.	eri viḻi nākaṉum-tīppōluṅ kaṇkaḷaiyuṭaiya nākaṉum, naṉṟu aṟi ceṭṭi nalaṭi vīḻntu - aṟat tiṉaiyaṟinta vaṇikaṉatu nalla aṭiyil viḻuntu vaṇaṅki, kaḷḷum ūṉum kaiviṭiṉ ivvuṭampu uḷḷuṟaivāḻuyir ōmputal āṟṟēṉ - kaḷḷaiyum pulālaiyum kaiviṭṭāl ivvuṭampiṉkaṇ taṅki vāḻkiṉṟa uyiraip pātukātta laṟiyēṉ, tamakku oḻi marapiṉ cāvuṟu kāṟum emakku ām nallaṟam eṭutturai eṉṟalum-tamakku aṟutiyiṭṭa muṟai maiyāl iṟappiṉai eytumaḷavum emakkup poruntiya nalla aṟaṅkaḷai eṭuttuk kūṟuka eṉṟalum; 
eri viḻi-eññāṉṟum ciṉattiṉāṟ civanta kaṇ. naṉṟu - aṟam. ceṭṭi-ceṭṭu uṭaiyavaṉ; cirēṭṭi eṉpataṉ citaivumām. uṟai- uṟaintu eṉa viṉaiyeccamākkuka. tamakku - uyirkaṭṭu.
 112-117.	naṉṟu coṉṉāy-naṉkuraittāy, nal neṟip paṭarkuvai. nalvaḻiyilē celvāy, uṉṟaṉakku ollum neṟi aṟam uraikkēṉ-uṉakku iyalum vaḻiyāl aṟat tiṉaik kūṟuvēṉ, uṭai kala mākkaḷ uyir uyntu īṅkuṟiṉ-kaṭaliṟ kalamuṭaiyappaṭṭa makkaḷ uyir tappi īṇṭuc cērntāl, aṭutoḻil oḻintu avar āruyir ōmpi-kollun toḻilaiviṭṭu avarkaḷuṭaiya ariya uyiraik kāttu, mūttuviḷi mā oḻittu evvuyir māṭṭum tīttiṟam oḻikeṉa - mutumaiyuṟṟu iṟakkum vilaṅkukaḷait tavira vēṟu enta uyiriṉiṭattum kolait toḻilai nīṅkuvāyāka eṉṟu cātuvaṉuraippa; 
mutumaiyuṟṟu iṟakkum vilaṅkukaḷai maṭṭum uṇṇuka eṉṟāṉ. tīttiṟam - kolai. ōmpi oḻikaveṉ ṟiyaiyum.
117-127.   ciṟumakaṉ uraippōṉ - kīḻōṉākiya nākaṉ uraippavaṉ, īṅku emakkākum ivvaṟam ceykēm - īṇṭu emakkup poruntiya ivvaṟattiṉaic ceyyāniṟpēm, āṅku uṉakku ākum arum poruḷ koḷkeṉa - niṉak kēṟṟa ariya poruḷkaḷaik koṇṭu celka veṉṟu, paṇṭum paṇṭum kalaṅkaviḻ mākkaḷai uṇṭēm - muṉpellām marakkalaṅ kaviḻntu vanta makkaḷaik koṉṟu uṇṭōm, avar tam uṟuporuḷ īṅkivai- avar kaḷuṭaiya mikka poruḷkaḷākum iṅkuḷḷa ivaikaḷ, viraimaram meṉtukil viḻunitik kuppaiyōṭu ivai ivai koḷkeṉa - cantaṉa maraṅkaḷum melliya āṭai kaḷum ciṟanta poruṭkuvaikaḷumākiya ivaṟṟaiyellām koḷka eṉṟu kūṟa, eṭuttaṉaṉ koṇarntu - cātuvaṉ avaikaḷai eṭuttuk koṇarntu, cantiratattaṉ eṉṉum vāṇikaṉ vaṅkañ cērntatil - cantiratattaṉ eṉṉum vaṇikaṉatu marakkalam vantatil, vantu uṭaṉēṟi - uṭaṉ ēṟivantu, innakar pukuntu īṅku ivaḷoṭu vāḻntu - in nakarattai yaṭaintu ivvātiraiyuṭaṉ vāḻntu, taṉ maṉai naṉ pala tāṉamum ceytaṉaṉ - taṉatu maṉaiyiṉ kaṇ pala nallaṟaṅkaḷaiyuñ ceytaṉaṉ. 
uraippavaṉ arumporuḷ koḷkeṉap potuviṟ kūṟip piṉpu avaṟṟai vitantu, ivai yivai koḷka veṉṟuraikka veṉka. viraimaram - maṇamuṭaiya cantaṉam akil mutaliya maraṅkaḷ. cērntatākiya vaṅkattileṉka. 
 128-135.	āṅṅaṉamākiya ātirai kaiyāl - at taṉmai yaḷākiya ātiraiyiṉ kaiyāl, pūṅkoṭi nallāy piccai peṟukeṉa 16. ātirai piccaiyiṭṭa kātai - pūṅkoṭiyaṉaiya melliyalē nī piccai peṟuvāyāka veṉṟu uraikka, maṉaiyakam pukuntu maṇimēkalaitāṉ - maṇimēkalai ātiraiyiṉ illattiṟ ceṉṟu, puṉaiyā ōviyampōla niṟṟalum - aṇi ceyyappaṭāta ōviyappāvai pōla niṟṟalum, toḻutu valaṅkoṇṭu - valamvantu vaṇaṅki, tuyaraṟu kiḷaviyōṭu - iṉcoṟkaḷōṭu, amutacurapiyiṉ akaṉcurai niṟai tara - maṇimēkalai kaiyiṟ koṇṭa amutacurapiyiṉatu akaṉṟa uḷḷiṭam niṟaiyumāṟu, pārakam aṭaṅkalum pacippiṇi aṟuka eṉa ātirai iṭṭaṉaḷ āruyir marunteṉ - ātirai nallāḷ āruyir maruntākiya aṉṉattai nilavulaka muḻuvatum paci nōy aṟuka eṉṟu kūṟi iṭṭaṉaḷ eṉka.
 peṟukeṉa - peṟukaveṉṟu kāya caṇṭikai uraikka. puṉaiyā ōviyam - vaṇṇaṅkaḷaik koṇṭeḻutāta vaṭivaik kōṭṭiṉa ōviyam eṉalumām: 1“puṉaiyā vōviyaṅ kaṭuppa'' eṉpuḻi nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uraittamai kāṇka. piccai yēṟkumpoḻutu tuṟavaṟattiṉar mōṉamāka niṟṟalvēṇṭu meṉpavākaliṉ, 'puṉaiyā vōviyam pōla niṟṟalum' eṉṟār. tuyaraṟukiḷavi - iṉcol.
ātirai toḻutu kiḷaviyōṭu pacippiṇiyaṟukeṉak kūṟi niṟai tara iṭṭaṉaḷeṉka.
viñcaiyar pūṅkoṭi mātarkku uraippōḷ, ‘āṅṅaṉamākiya ātirai kaiyāṟ piccai peṟuka' eṉṟu colla, maṇimēkalai maṉaiyakam pukuntu niṟṟalum, ātirai valaṅkoṇṭu āruyir maruntu iṭṭaṉaḷ eṉa muṭikka. 
 ātirai piccaiyiṭṭa kātai muṟṟiṟṟu. 


17. ulakavaṟavi pukka kātai 
(ātirai yaḷitta piccaiyai mutalil maṇimēkalai ēṟṟa piṉpu, amutacurapiyiluḷḷa cōṟṟut tiraḷai, aṟaneṟiyi līṭṭiya poruḷ vaḻaṅkuntōṟum kuṟaiyāmal vaḷarvatupōla eṭukka eṭukkak kuṟaiviṉṟi vaḷarntu ēṟpōr paciyaip pōkki viḷaṅkiṟṟu. atu kaṇṭa kāya caṇṭikai viyantu maṇimēkalaiyai vaṇaṅki, 'aṉṉaiyē! eṉatu tīrāp paciyaiyum tīrttaruḷka'' eṉṟu vēṇṭa, uṭaṉē maṇimēkalai amutacurapiyiliruntu oru piṭi yamutaiyeṭuttu avaḷ kaiyiliṭṭāḷ. ataṉai yuṇṭu paci nōy tīrntu makiḻnta kāyacaṇṭikai “vaṭaticaik kaṇṇē viñcaiya rulakiluḷḷa kāñcaṉapurameṉpatu eṉṉuṭaiya ūr; teṉṟicaiyiluḷḷa potiyiṉ malaiyiṉ vaḷaṅkaḷaik kāṇṭaṟku virumpikkaṇavaṉum yāṉum puṟappaṭṭup pōntu iṭaiyēyuḷḷa kāṉyāṟoṉṟiṉ karaiyiliruntōm; irukkaiyil, viruccikaṉeṉṉum muṉivaṉoruvaṉ pāraṇañ ceytaṟkup paṉaṅkaṉi pōṉṟa parutta nāvaṟ kaṉiyoṉṟait tēkkilaiyil vaittuviṭṭu nīrāṭac ceṉṟāṉ. ak kaṉiyiṉ iyalpiṉai yaṟiyāta nāṉ paḻaviṉaiyāl ataṉaik kālāṟ citaittuk keṭuttēṉ. nīrāṭi mīṇṭu vanta muṉivaṉ ak kaṉi eṉṉāṟ citaintamaiyai aṟintu ciṉantu, eṉṉai nōkki, 'teyvattaṉmai yuṭaiyatum paṉṉīrāṇṭiṟ korumuṟai orē kaṉiyait taruvatumākiya nāval marattiluṇṭāṉatu ikkaṉi; itaṉai yuṇṭōr paṉṉīrāṇṭu paci yoḻintiruppar; yāṉō paṉṉiraṇṭāṇṭu paṭṭiṉiyiruntu orunāḷuṇṇum nōṉpuṭaiyēṉ ; uṇṇum nāḷum innāḷē; uṇṇak karutiya kaṉiyum ikkaṉiyē; itaṉai nī yaḻittuviṭṭāy; ātalāl, nī vāṉ vaḻiyē cellum mantirattai maṟantu, yāṉaittīyeṉṉum nōyāl paṉṉiraṇṭāṇṭu tīrāp paci koṇṭuḻantu, piṉpu ikkaṉiyai yāṉ uṇṇum nāḷil paciyoḻiyap peṟuvāy' eṉṟu kūṟi varuntip pōyiṉaṉ. uṭaṉē perum paci eṉṉaip paṟṟiyatu; ataṉāl mika varuntiṉēṉ; atu kaṇṭa eṉ kaṇavaṉ kāy kaṉi kiḻaṅku mutaliyavaṟṟai mikak koṇarntu eṉṉai uṇpikkavum appaci tīrātāyiṟṟu. antarañ cellum mantiramum eṉ niṉaivukku vantilatu. ataṉāl varuntiya eṉ kaṇavaṉ, ‘nī naṭantu ceṉṟu tamiḻ nāṭṭilē āṟṟā mākkaṭku arun tuṇaiyākic celvarkaḷ vāḻntirukkum kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattai yaṭaintu aṅkē taṅkuvāyāka' eṉṟu colla, nāṉ av vaṇṇamē pōntu iṅkirukkiṉṟēṉ. ovvōrāṇṭilum iṅku intiraviḻā naṭakkumpoḻutu eṉ kaṇavaṉ vantu eṉ varuttattaip pārttut tāṉum varuntip piṉvarum yāṇṭiṉai eṇṇic celvaṉ. taṇiyāta vempaciyait taṇittaṉai. yāṉ eṉ patikkēkuvēṉ. in nakarilē muṉivarkaḷ palar uṟaiyum cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭam eṉpa toṉṟuṇṭu; ataṉ kaṇ palarum vantu pukutaṟkākavē eppoḻutum vāyiṟ katavu tiṟantuḷḷa ulakavaṟavi yeṉṉum ampalam oṉṟuṇṭu; 
	“		ūrū rāṅkaṇ uṟupaci yuḻantōr.
āru miṉmaiyi ṉarumpiṇi yuṟṟōr
iṭuvōrt tērntāṅ kiruppōr palarāl.  
ātaliṉ avviṭattiṟkuc celvāyāka'' eṉṟu kūṟiviṭṭu, avaḷ taṉṉūrukkuc ceṉṟaṉaḷ. piṉ maṇimēkalai vītiyiṉ orupakkattē otuṅkic ceṉṟu ulaka vaṟaviyai aṭaintu mummuṟai valamvantu paṇintu, atilēṟic campāpatiyaiyum kantiṟpāvaiyaiyum vaṇaṅki, veyilāṟ karinta kāṭṭilē maḻai tōṉṟiṉāṟpōlap paciyāl varuntiya makkaṭku amutacurapi yōṭu tōṉṟi, “iḵtu āputtiraṉ kaiyilirunta amutacurapi yākum ; uṇṇutaṟku viruppamuḷḷa yāvarum varuka'' eṉṟu kūṟa, palarum vantu uṇpārāyiṉar ; ātaliṉ av vampalattil uṇṇu moli mikuntatu.  
		pattiṉip peṇṭir pāttūṇ ēṟṟa
piccaip pāttirap peruñcōṟ ṟamalai
aṟattiṉ īṭṭiya oṇporuḷ aṟavōṉ
tiṟattu vaḻippaṭūuñ ceykai pōla
5		vāṅkukai varunta maṉṉuyirk kaḷittut
tāṉtolai villāt takaimai nōkki
yāṉait tīnōy akavayiṟ ṟaṭakkiya
kāyacaṇ ṭikaiyeṉuṅ kārikai vaṇaṅki
neṭiyōṉ mayaṅki nilamicait tōṉṟi
10		aṭalaru munnīr aṭaitta ñāṉṟu
kuraṅkukoṇarn teṟinta neṭumalai yellām
aṇaṅkuṭai yaḷakkar vayiṟupuk kāṅku
iṭṭa tāṟṟāk kaṭṭaḻaṟ kaṭumpacip
paṭṭēṉ eṉṟaṉ paḻaviṉaip payattāl
15		aṉṉai kēḷnī āruyir maruttuvi
tuṉṉiya eṉṉōy tuṭaippā yeṉṟalum
eṭutta pāttirat tēntiya amutam
piṭittavaḷ kaiyil pēṇiṉaḷ peytalum
vayiṟukāy perumpaci nīṅki maṟṟavaḷ
20		tuyaram nīṅkit toḻutaṉaḷ uraikkum
mācil vāḷoḷi vaṭaticaic cēṭik
kācilkāñ caṉapurak kaṭinaka ruḷḷēṉ
viñcaiyaṉ ṟaṉṉoṭeṉ vevviṉai uruppat
teṉṟicaip potiyil kāṇiya vantēṉ
25		kaṭuvara laruvik kaṭumpuṉal koḻitta
iṭumaṇaṟ kāṉiyāṟ ṟiyaintoruṅ kiruntēṉ
purinūṉ mārpil tiripuri vārcaṭai
maravuri yuṭaiyaṉ viruccikaṉ eṉpōṉ
peruṅkulaip peṇṇaik karuṅkaṉi yaṉaiyatōr
30		iruṅkaṉi nāvaṟ paḻamoṉ ṟēntit
tēkkilai vaittuc cēṇāṟu parappiṟ
pūkkamaḻ poykai yāṭac ceṉṟōṉ
tīviṉai uruttaliṉ cerukkoṭu ceṉṟēṉ
kālā lantak karuṅkaṉi citaittēṉ
35		uṇṭal vēṭkaiyiṉ varūum viruccikaṉ
kaṇṭaṉaṉ eṉṉaik karuṅkaṉic citaivuṭaṉ
cīrtikaḻ nāvalil tippiya māṉatu
īrā ṟāṇṭil orukaṉi taruvatu
akkaṉi yuṇṭōr āṟī rāṇṭu
40		makkaḷ yākkaiyiṉ varumpaci nīṅkuvar
paṉṉī rāṇṭil orunā ḷallatu
uṇṇā nōṉpiṉēṉ uṇkaṉi citaittāy
antarañ cellum mantiram iḻantu
tantit tīyāl taṉittuyar uḻantu
45		munnā lāṇṭiṉ mutirkaṉi nāṉīṅku
uṇṇu nāḷuṉuṟupaci kaḷaikeṉa
annā ḷāṅkavaṉ iṭṭa cāpam
innāḷ pōlum iḷaṅkoṭi keṭuttaṉai
vāṭupaci uḻantu māmuṉi pōyapiṉ
50		pāṭimiḻ aruvip payamalai oḻinteṉ
alavalaic ceytik kañciṉaṉ akaṉṟa
ilakoḷi viñcaiyaṉ viḻumamō ṭeyti
āraṇaṅ kākiya aruntavaṉ taṉṉāl
kāraṇam iṉṟiyum kaṭunō yuḻantaṉai
55		vāṉū ṭeḻukeṉa mantiram maṟantēṉ
ūṉuyir nīṅkum uruppoṭu tōṉṟi 
vayiṟukāy perumpaci varuttum eṉṟēṟkut
tīṅkaṉi kiḻaṅku ceḻuṅkāy nallaṉa
āṅkavaṉ koṇaravum āṟṟē ṉāka
60		nīṅka lāṟṟāṉ neṭuntuya reyti
āṅkavaṉ āṅkeṉak karuḷoṭu muraippōṉ
camput tīviṉuḷ tamiḻaka maruṅkil
kampa millāk kaḻiperuñ celvar
āṟṟā mākkaṭ kāṟṟuntuṇai yāki
65		nōṟṟōr uṟaivatōr nōṉakar uṇṭāl
palanā ḷāyiṉum nilaṉoṭu pōki
appatip pukukeṉ ṟavaṉaruḷ ceyya
ippatip pukuntīṅ kiyāṉuṟai kiṉṟēṉ
intira kōṭaṇai viḻavaṇi varunāḷ
70		vantu tōṉṟiyim mānakar maruṅkē
eṉṉuṟu perumpaci kaṇṭaṉaṉ iraṅkip
piṉvarum yāṇṭavaṉ eṇṇiṉaṉ kaḻiyum
taṇivil vempaci tavirttaṉai vaṇaṅkiṉēṉ
maṇimē kalaiyeṉ vāṉpatip paṭarkēṉ
75		tukkan tuṭaikkun tukaḷaṟu mātavar
cakkara vāḷak kōṭṭamuṇ ṭāṅkatil
palarpukat tiṟanta pakuvāy vāyil
ulaka vaṟavi oṉṟuṇ ṭataṉiṭai
ūrū rāṅkaṇ uṟupaci uḻantōr
80		ārum iṉmaiyiṉ arumpiṇi yuṟṟōr
iṭuvōrt tērntāṅ kiruppōr palarāl
vaṭuvāḻ kūntal ataṉpāṟ pōkeṉ
ṟāṅkavaḷ pōkiya piṉṉar āyiḻai
ōṅkiya vītiyiṉ orupuṭai otuṅki
85		valamuṟai mummuṟai vantaṉai ceytav
ulaka vaṟaviyiṉ orutaṉi yēṟip
patiyōr tammoṭu palartoḻu tēttum
mutiyōḷ kōṭṭam mummaiyiṉ vaṇaṅkik
kantuṭai neṭunilaik kāraṇaṅ kāṭṭiya
90		tantuṇaip pāvaiyait tāṉtoḻu tētti 
veyilcuṭa vempiya vēykari kāṉattuk
karuvi māmaḻai tōṉṟiya teṉṉap
pacitiṉa varuntiya paital mākkaṭku
amuta curapiyō ṭāyiḻai tōṉṟi
95		āput tiraṉkai amuta curapiyiḵtu
yāvarum varuka ēṟpōr tāmeṉa
ūṇoli aravat toliyeḻun taṉṟē
yāṇarp pērūr ampala maruṅkeṉ. 
urai
1-6.   		pattiṉip peṇṭir pāttūṇ ēṟṟa - kaṟpiṟciṟanta ātirai nallāḷāṟ pakuttuṇṇum uṇaviṉaip peṟṟa, piccaip pāttirap peruñ cōṟṟu amalai-amutacurapiyiluḷḷa periya cōṟṟuttiraḷai, aṟattiṉ īṭṭiya oṇporuḷ - aṟaneṟiyiṉālīṭṭappaṭṭa oḷḷiya poruḷ, aṟavōṉ tiṟattu vaḻippaṭūum ceykai pōla - aṟañ ceyvōṉ karuttiṉ vaḻiyē ceṉṟu payaṉpaṭumāṟu pōla, vāṅku kai varunta maṉṉuyirkku aḷittu- ēṟkum kaikaḷ varuntumāṟu uyirkaṭku mika aḷittum, tāṉ tolaivu illāt takaimai nōkki-tāṉ kuṟaivupaṭāttaṉmaiyaik kaṇṭu; 
 pāttu - pakuttu eṉpataṉ marūu. pāttūṇ - iyalpuṭaiya mūvarkkum teṉpulattār mutaliya nālvarkkum pakuttuṇṭaṟ kuriya uṇavu ; itaṉāl ātiraiyaḷitta uṇaviṉ takutiyum, ātiraiyiṉ maṉaiyaṟa māṇpum peṟappaṭṭaṉa; 1''paḻiyañcip pāttū ṇuṭaittāyiṉ vāḻkkai, vaḻiyeñca leññāṉṟu mil'' eṉpatu aṟiyaṟ pālatu. ātiraipāṟ peṟṟataṟku aṟattiṉ īṭṭiyatum, maṇimēkalai karutiyavāṟu maṉṉuyirkkaḷikka atu payaṉpaṭutaṟku aṟavōṉ tiṟattu vaḻippaṭutalum, uvamai yāyiṉa. iṉi, aṟavōṉ eṉpataṟkuc caṟpāttiram eṉṟum, tiṟattu vaḻippaṭum eṉpataṟkup peṟuvōr perumaikkut takkapaṭi perukumeṉṟum karuttukkoṇṭaṉar makā makōpāttiyāya ṭākṭar cāminātaiyar; īṇṭu atu poruntu mēṟ koḷka. piṟa uyirkaṭkum aḷikkappaṭiṉum cōṟuṇṭalai iyalpāka vuṭaiyar makkaḷātaliṉ, 'vāṅkukai varunta' eṉṟār. ik karuttu muṉṉarum vantuḷḷamai kāṇka. 
 7-16.   yāṉait tī nōy akavayiṟṟu aṭakkiya kāyacaṇṭikai eṉum kārikai vaṇaṅki - yāṉaittī eṉṉum perumpaci nōyiṉait taṉ vayiṟṟiṟkoṇṭa kāyacaṇṭikai eṉṉum maṭantai maṇimēkalaiyai vaṇaṅki, neṭiyōṉ mayaṅki nilamicai tōṉṟi aṭalarumunnīr aṭaitta ñāṉṟu - tirumāl mayakkattāl nilamicai irāmaṉākat tōṉṟi vellutaṟkariya kaṭalai aṭaitta poḻutu, kuraṅku koṇarntu eṟinta neṭumalai ellām-kuraṅkukaḷ koṇṭuvantu vīciya periya malaikaḷellām, aṇaṅkuṭai aḷakkar vayiṟu pukkāṅku - varuttan tarum kaṭaliṉ vayiṟṟiṉuḷḷē pukuntāṟ pōla, iṭṭatu āṟṟāk kaṭṭaḻal kaṭumpaci paṭṭēṉ eṉṟaṉ paḻaviṉaip payattāl - iṭṭa uṇavukaḷāl taṇiyāta aḻalpōṉṟa koṭiya paciyai eṉṉuṭaiya muṉṉai viṉaippayaṉāl aṭaintēṉ, aṉṉaikēḷ nī - tāyē nī kēṭpāyāka, āruyir maruttuvi tuṉṉiya eṉnōy tuṭaippāy eṉṟālum - ariya uyiraip pātukākka valla maruntiṉaiyuṭaiyāy neruṅkiya eṉatu pacippiṇiyaik kaḷaintaruḷvāy eṉa vēṇṭa; 
 yāṉait tī nōy - perumpaciyaic ceyyum nōy; pasmaka viyāti eṉavum paṭum. mayaṅki - aviccaiyāṉ mayaṅki; 1‘iruṇīṅki yiṉpam payakkum maruṇīṅki, mācaṟu kāṭci yavarkku'' eṉpataṉāl aviccai piṟappiṟkuk kāraṇamāta laṟika; neṭiyōṉ tōṉṟi mayaṅki eṉa māṟṟi, tirumāl irāmāvatārattil paruvata muṉivarum nāratarum amparīṭaṉuk kiṭṭa cāpamākiya iruḷait tāṉ ēṟṟuk koṇṭamaiyāl ataṉāl mūṭappaṭṭut taṉṉai māyaṉeṉ ṟaṟiyātu mayaṅki yiruntaṉaṉ eṉṉum iliṅka purāṇak kataiyai ēṟṟiyuraippārumuḷar. iṉi mayaṅki yeṉpataṟkuc cītaiyaip pirintu kaiyā ṟeyti yeṉ ṟuraittalumām. munnīr - paṭaittal, kāttal, aḻittal eṉṉum mūṉṟu nīrmaiyai yuṭaiyatu; kaṭal. malaikaḷāl kaṭaliṉ vayiṟu nirampāmaipōla uṇavukaḷiṉāl eṉ vayiṟu nirampiṟṟillai yeṉat tāṉuṇṭa uṇaviṉ mikutiyum paciyiṉ koṭumaiyum pulappaṭa uvamaṅ kūṟiṉāḷ. iṭṭatu: viṉaippeyar. āruyir maruntu- aṭicil, maruttuvi - maruntiṉaiyuṭaiyāḷ.
kārikai nōkki vaṇaṅki, 'eṉṉōy tuṭaippā' yeṉṟalu meṉka.
 17-20.  eṭutta pāttirattu ēntiya amutam piṭittu avaḷ kaiyil peytalum-maṇimēkalai tāṉ kaiyiṟ koṇṭa amuta curapiyiluḷḷa aṉṉattai eṭuttup piṭiyāka avaḷ kaiyil iṭa, vayiṟukāy perumpaci nīṅki maṟṟavaḷ tuyaram nīṅkittoḻutaṉaḷ uraikkum-kāyacaṇṭikai vayiṟṟil erikiṉṟa koṭiya paci nīṅkat taṉ tuyaramum nīṅki maṇimēkalaiyait toḻutu kūṟukiṉṟāḷ; 
ēntiya - poruntiya; ēntiya kaiyileṉak kūṭṭutalumām. paci nīṅki - paci nīṅka: eccattiripu.
21-26.	mācu il vāl oḷi vaṭaticaic cēṭi - vaṭaticaiyil mācaṟṟa veḷḷiya voḷiyiṉaiyuṭaiya viñcaiyarulakil, kācu il kāñcaṉapurak kaṭinakar uḷḷēṉ - kuṟṟamaṟṟa kāñcaṉapura meṉṉuṅ kāval poruntiya nakarattiluḷḷa yāṉ, viñcaiyaṉ taṉṉoṭu eṉ vevviṉai uruppa-eṉatu koṭuviṉaiyāṉatu tōṟṟa eṉ kaṇavaṉōṭu, teṉṟicaip potiyil kāṇiya vantēṉ-teṉṟicaiyiluḷḷa potiyiṉ malaiyiṉ vaḷaṅkāṇum poruṭṭu vantēṉ, kaṭuvaral aruvik kaṭumpuṉal koḻitta - virainta celaviṉai yuṭaiya aruviyiṉatu vēkamuḷḷa nīr teḷḷiya, iṭumaṇal kāṉiyāṟṟu iyaintoruṅku iruntēṉ- iṭumaṇalai yuṭaiya kāṉyāṟṟil eṉ kaṇavaṉuṭaṉ kūṭi oruṅkiruntēṉ; 
cēṭi - vittiyātara rulaku; atu veḷḷi malaiyiluḷḷa tākaliṉ vāloḷiyaiyuṭaiyatāyiṟṟu; 1“veḷḷi mālvarai viyaṉperuñ cēṭi'' eṉpatu kāṇka. uruppa - aḻalaveṉṟumām. kāṇiya: ceyyiya veṉṉum viṉaiyeccam. vantēṉ : eccamuṟṟumām. iṭu maṇal - ekkar.
 27-32.	purinūl mārpil tiripuri vārcaṭai maravuri uṭaiyaṉ viruccikaṉ eṉpōṉ - appoḻutu muṟukkiya pūṇūlaṇinta mārpum tirittu muṟukkiya nīṇṭa caṭaiyum maravuri yāṭaiyu muṭaiya viruccikaṉ eṉṉum muṉivaṉ, peruṅkulaip peṇṇaik karuṅkaṉi aṉaiyatōr iruṅkaṉi nāvaṟpaḻam oṉṟu ēnti - periya kulaiyaiyuṭaiya paṉaiyiṉatu kariya kaṉiyai yottatākiya perumai poruntiya kaṉinta nāvaṟpaḻam oṉṟaik kaiyilēnti vantu, tēkkilai vaittuc cēṇ nāṟu parappil pūkkamaḻ poykai āṭac ceṉṟōṉ - ataṉai oru tēkkiṉ ilaiyil vaittuviṭṭu neṭuntūram nāṟumiyalpuṭaiya pūkkaḷ kamaḻum parappiṉaiyuṭaiya poykaiyil nīrāṭac ceṉṟāṉāka;
paṉaṅkaṉi parumaṉ paṟṟi uvamaiyāyatu. ōr : acai. nāṟu pū eṉavum, parappiṟ poykai yeṉavum iyaiyum.
viruccika ṉeṉpōṉ nāvaṟpaḻamoṉṟai ēnti vaittup poykaiyāṭac ceṉṟāṉāka eṉka. 
33-34.   tīviṉai uruttaliṉ cerukkoṭu ceṉṟēṉ - tīviṉai yāṉatu payaṉ koṭuppat tōṟṟutaliṉ yāṉ āṇṭut tarukkoṭu ceṉṟu, kālāl antak karuṅkaṉi citaittēṉ - kāliṉālē anta nāvaṟpaḻattaic citaittēṉ; 
35-42.   uṇṭal vēṭkaiyiṉ varūum viruccikaṉ - uṇṇum vēṭkaiyuṭaṉ nīrāṭip pōnta viruccika muṉivaṉ, kaṇṭaṉaṉ eṉṉaik karuṅkaṉic citaivuṭaṉ - ap paḻattiṉaic citaitta tiṟattiṉōṭu eṉṉaik kaṇṇuṟṟaṉaṉ, cīrtikaḻ nāvalil tippiya māṉatu-ciṟappu viḷaṅkum nāvalilē teyvattaṉmai yuṭaiya toṉṟu, īrāṟu āṇṭil oru kaṉi taruvatu - paṉṉīrāṇṭiṟku oru paḻam koṭuppatu, ak kaṉi uṇṭōr - ap paḻattiṉai yuṇṭōr, āṟīrāṇṭu makkaḷ yākkaiyiṉ varum paci nīṅkuvar- paṉṉīrāṇṭu makkaḷuṭali luṇṭākum paci nīṅkappeṟuvar, paṉṉīrāṇṭil oru nāḷ allatu uṇṇā nōṉpiṉēṉ - yāṉō paṉṉīrāṇṭil orunāḷ uṇpatallatu piṟanāḷil uṇṇāta nōṉpiṉaiyuṭaiyēṉ, uṇkaṉi citaittāy-avviyalpuṭaiya yāṉ iṉṟu uṇṭaṟkuriya ak kaṉiyaik keṭuttāy;
citaivu-citaittu niṉṟanilai; citaintakaṉiyō ṭeṉṟumām. tippiyam-teyvalōkattiliruntu uṇṭāya teṉṟumām. uṇ kaṉi - tippiyamāṉa nāval tanta kaṉiyākiya yāṉ uṇṭaṟkuriya kaṉiyeṉka
 43-48.   antaram cellum mantiram iḻantu-ākaliṉ viṇmītu cellum mantirattai iḻantu, tantit tīyāl taṉittuyar uḻantu - yāṉaittī yeṉṉum pacinōyāl oppaṟṟa tuṉpa maṭaintu varunti, mun nālāṇṭil mutirkaṉi īṅku uṇṇum nāḷ uṉ uṟupaci kaḷaika eṉa - iṉi varum paṉṉiraṇṭāva tāṇṭil mutirkiṉṟa nāvaṟkaṉiyiṉai nāṉ iṅku uṇṇukiṉṟa nāḷil niṉatu mikka paciyaip pōkkuvāy eṉṟu, annāḷ āṅku avaṉ iṭṭa cāpam - yāṉ av varuṅ kaṉi citaitta aṉṟu avviṭattil am muṉivaṉiṭṭa cāpattai, innāḷ pōlum iḷaṅkoṭi keṭuttaṉai - iḷaṅkoṭi pōlum nī iṉṟu keṭuttāy; 
paṉṉīrāṇṭu tāṉ pacittirukkac ceyta tīviṉai paṟṟi avvakaiyē tuyaruḻakkumāṟu cāpamiṭṭaṉaṉeṉka. cāpam keṭuttaṉai yeṉṟiyaiyum. pōlum : oppil pōli. cāpa muṭivellaiyākiya nāḷ innāḷ eṉṟumām.
49-55. 	vāṭu paci uḻantu māmuṉi pōyapiṉ - vāṭutaṟkētu vākiya paciyāl varunti am muṉivaṉ ceṉṟapiṉ, pāṭu imiḻ aruvip payamalai oḻintu-oli muḻaṅkukiṉṟa aruvi kaḷaiyuṭaiya payaṉuṭaiya potiyiṉ malaiyai aṭaivatai viṭuttu, eṉ alavalaic ceytikku añciṉaṉ akaṉṟa - maṉattil tōṉṟiyatai ārāyātu ceyta eṉ ceykaikku añciṉavaṉāy nīṅkiya, ilaku oḷi viñcaiyaṉ viḻumamōṭu eyti-viḷaṅkum oḷiyuṭaiya viñcaiyaṉ tuṉpamō ṭaṭaintu, āraṇaṅkākiya aruntavaṉ taṉṉāl - ariya teyvat taṉmaiyuṭaiya aruntavaṉāl, kāraṇam iṉṟiyum kaṭunōy uḻantaṉai - kāraṇamillāmalum kaṭiya nōyāl varun taluṟṟaṉai, vāṉūṭu eḻuka eṉa - vicumpiṉ mītu eḻuvāyāka eṉṟu kūṟa ;
alavalaic ceyti - ārāyātu ceyta ceyti; alamarutalaic ceyyumceyti yeṉṟumām; 1"alavalaiyuṭaiyai" eṉpuḻi nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uraittamai kāṇka. vilakoḷi eṉap pirittalumām ; vilaku oḷi - viṭṭu viḷaṅkum oḷi. kāraṇa miṉṟiyē kaṉiyaic citaittuk kaṭunōyuḻantaṉai eṉa virit turaittukkoḷka.
55-61.	mantiram maṟantēṉ - vāṉiṟ cellum mantirattai maṟantu viṭṭēṉ, ūṉ uyir nīṅkum uruppoṭu tōṉṟi - uṭampiṉiṉṟum uyir nīṅkutaṟkētuvākiya veppattuṭaṉ tōṉṟi, vayiṟukāy perumpaci varuttum eṉṟēṟku - vayiṟṟiṉaik kāykiṉṟa perum paciyāṉatu mika varuttā niṉṟatu eṉṟu kūṟiya eṉporuṭṭu, tīṅkaṉi kiḻaṅku ceḻuṅkāy nallaṉa āṅkavaṉ koṇaravum āṟṟēṉāka-nallaṉavākiya iṉiya paḻaṅkaḷ kiḻaṅkukaḷ ceḻiya kāykaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟai avaṉ koṇarntu taravum eṉatu paci taṇiyēṉāka, nīṅkal āṟṟāṉ neṭuntuyar eyti āṅkavaṉ āṅku eṉakku aruḷoṭum uraippōṉ - eṉṉaiviṭṭu nīṅkalāṟṟātavaṉākiya eṉ kaṇavaṉmikka tuyarattaiyaṭaintu avviṭattil eṉakku aruḷoṭum kūṟukiṉṟavaṉ; 
maṟantēṉ eṉavum varuttum eṉavum kūṟiṉēṟku eṉka. nallaṉaveṉpataik kaṉi mutaliya ovvoṉṟōṭuṅ kūṭṭuka. āṟṟēṉāka - paci taṇiyappeṟēṉāka. 
62-68.   camput tīviṉuḷ tamiḻaka maruṅkil - nāvalan tīviṉuḷ tamiḻakattilē, kampam illāk kaḻiperuñ celvar - naṭukkamillāta mikka peruñ celvattaiyuṭaiyōr, āṟṟā mākkaṭku āṟṟum tuṇaiyāki - vaṟiyōrkaṭku vēṇṭiyaṉa koṭukkum tuṇaivarāki, nōṟṟōr uṟaiva tōr nōṉ nakar uṇṭāl - muṟpiṟappil tavampurintōr vāḻkiṉṟa perumaiyuṭaiya nakaramoṉṟu uṇṭu, pala nāḷāyiṉum nilaṉoṭu pōki - palanāṭkaḻiyiṉum nī naṭantu ceṉṟu, appatip pukuka eṉṟu avaṉ aruḷ ceyya-an nakarattiṟ cērvāy eṉṟu avaṉ kūṟiyaruḷa, ip patip pukuntu īṅku yāṉ uṟaikiṉṟēṉ-yāṉ avaṉ col vaḻiyē ik kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattai yaṭaintu īṇṭu vatikiṉṟēṉ;
āṟṟutal-koṭuttal; 1"aṟṟārkkoṉ ṟāṟṟātāṉ celvam" eṉpatu kāṇka. nōṟṟōr peruñcelvar tuṇaiyāki uṟaivateṉka. nōṉmai-īṇṭup perumai, nilaṉoṭu pōtal - kālāl naṭantu cellutal.
69-74.	intirakōṭaṇai viḻavaṇi varunāḷ - intira kōṭaṇai yākiya aḻakiya viḻā naṭaipeṟum nāḷil, vantu tōṉṟi immānakar maruṅkē - ip periya nakariṉ maruṅkē av viñcaiyaṉ vantu tōṉṟi, eṉṉuṟu perumpaci kaṇṭaṉaṉ iraṅki-eṉṉai varuttukiṉṟa perum paciyiṉaik kaṇṇuṟṟu iraṅki, piṉvarum yāṇṭu avaṉ eṇṇiṉaṉ kaḻiyum-piṉṉar eṉatu cāpam kaḻikiṉṟa āṇṭukaḷai eṇṇikkoṇṭu avaṉ nīṅkuvāṉ, taṇivil vempaci tavirttaṉai - eṉṉuṭaiya taṇitalillāta koṭiya paciyai nīkkiṉāy, vaṇaṅkiṉēṉ - niṉṉē vaṇakkañ ceytēṉ, maṇimēkalai eṉ vāṉpatip paṭarkēṉ-maṇimēkalaiyē iṉi eṉatu ciṟanta patiyiṉkaṭ celvēṉ;
intirakōṭaṇaiyākiya viḻavu eṉka; palvakai muḻakka muṭaimai paṟṟi viḻā kōṭaṇai yeṉappaṭṭatu; "intira kōṭaṇai viḻāvaṇi virumpi" (5:94) eṉa muṉṉar vantamaiyuṅ kāṇka. āṇṭutōṟum intiraviḻā naṭakkumnāḷil vantu kaṇṭu iraṅkik kaḻiyum eṉka. piṉvarum yāṇṭu - cāpam niṟkum paṉṉīrāṇṭil eñciya āṇṭu. maṇimēkalai tavirttaṉai vaṇaṅkiṉēṉ paṭarkēṉ eṉṟāḷeṉka. maṇimēkalai: viḷi. 
75-83.   tukkam tuṭaikkum tukaḷaṟu mātavar - tukkattaip pōkkum kuṟṟamaṟṟa perun tavattōrkaḷ uṟaikiṉṟa, cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭam uṇṭu-cakkaravāḷak kōṭṭam eṉpatoṉṟuṇṭu, āṅkatil palar pukat tiṟanta pakuvāy vāyil - ataṉkaṇ palarum cellumāṟu tiṟakkappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟa piḷanta vāypōlum vāyiliṉai yuṭaiya, ulakavaṟavi oṉṟuṇṭu-ulakavaṟavi eṉṉum ūrampalam oṉṟu uṇṭu, ataṉiṭai-ataṉkaṇ, ūrūr āṅkaṇ uṟupaci uḻantōr- ūrtōṟum mikka paciyāl varuntiṉōrum, ārum iṉmaiyiṉ arumpiṇi uṟṟōr - pātukāppōr oruvarum iṉmaiyiṉāl ariya nōyuḻantōrumāy, iṭuvōrt tērntu āṅku iruppōr palarāl - aṉṉamiṭuvōrai ārāyntu irukkiṉṟavar palarāvar, vaṭu vāḻ kūntal ataṉpāṟ pōkeṉṟu - vakir poruntiya kuḻaliṉai yuṭaiyāy avviṭam celvāyāka eṉṟu kūṟi, āṅkavaḷ pōkiya piṉṉar-ak kāyacaṇṭikai ceṉṟa piṉpu;
tukkam - piṟavittuṉpam. ūrūr āṅkaṇ - pala ūrkaḷilum, āṅku, āl: acaikaḷ. vaṭu-vakir. kūntal: viḷi.
83-90.	āyiḻai - maṇimēkalai, ōṅkiya vītiyiṉ orupuṭai otuṅki-māṭaṅkaḷ ōṅkiya teruviṉkaṇṇē oru puṟamāka otuṅkic ceṉṟu, valamuṟai mummuṟai vantaṉai ceytu - vala muṟaiyāka mūṉṟumuṟai vantu paṇintu, av vulakavaṟaviyiṉ oru taṉi ēṟi - anta ulaka vaṟaviyiṉkaṇ tāṉ taṉiyē ceṉṟu, patiyōr tammoṭu palar toḻutu ēttum - nakariluḷḷāruṭaṉ palarum tutittu vaṇaṅkum, mutiyōḷ kōṭṭam mummaiyiṉ vaṇaṅki-mutiyavaḷākiya campāpatiyiṉ kōyilai maṉam moḻi meykaḷāl vaṇakkañ ceytu, kantuṭai neṭu nilaik kāraṇam kāṭṭiya tantuṇaip pāvaiyait tāṉ toḻutu ētti - neṭiya nilaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya tūṇiṉ kaṇiruntu paḻaviṉaiyākiya kāraṇaṅ kāṭṭum poruṭṭu eḻutap paṭṭa tam tuṇaiyākiya kantiṟpāvaiyai vaṇaṅkittutittu;
mummaiyiṉ vaṇaṅki-mūṉṟumuṟai vaṇaṅki yeṉṟumām. neṭu nilaiyuṭaik kantu eṉa māṟuka. kāṭṭuvataṟku mayaṉāl eḻutappaṭṭaveṉa viritturaikka. "kantuṭai neṭunilaik kāraṇaṅ kāṭṭiya, anti leḻutiya vaṟputap pāvai" (7:94-5) eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattaṉavāka in nūluṭ piṟāṇṭu varuvaṉa kāṇka. maṇimēkalaiyuṭaṉ maṟṟaiyōraiyum uḷappaṭutti, ‘tantuṇaippāvai' eṉṟār.
91-98.   veyil cuṭa vempiya vēykari kāṉattu - ñāyiṟṟiṉ katirāṟ cuṭappaṭṭu vempiya mūṅkil karinta kāṭṭiṉkaṇ, karuvi māmaḻai tōṉṟiyatu eṉṉa - tokutiyākiya mukilkaḷ tōṉṟiṉāṟpōla; paci tiṉa varuntiya paital mākkaṭku - paciyāṉatu uṭalait tiṉṉa varuntiya tuṉpamuṭaiya makkaḷukku, amutacurapiyōṭu āyiḻai tōṉṟi - maṇimēkalai amutacurapiyōṭu tōṉṟi, āputtiraṉ kai amutacurapi iḵtu-āputtiraṉ kaiyilirunta amutacurapiyākum itu, yāvarum varuka ēṟpōr tām eṉa - ēṟpōr yāvarum varuka eṉṟu kūṟa, ūṇ oli aravattuolieḻuntaṉṟē yāṇarp
pērūr ampalam maruṅkeṉ-uṇavuṇṇum āravāramākiya muḻakkam eḻuntatu putuvaruvā yiṉaiyuṭaiya pērūriṉ ampalattiṉkaṇ eṉka. 
tiṉa eṉṟatu ilakkaṇai vaḻakku. varukaveṉa aḻaittuc coriya vantuṇṇum āravāram eṉa viritturaikka. maḻai tōṉṟip poḻintāṟpōla āyiḻai tōṉṟic coriya veṉka. oliyaravam: oruporu ḷirucol. ampala maruṅku oliyeḻuntaṉṟeṉka.
kāyacaṇṭikai yeṉṉuṅ kārikai, nōkki vaṇaṅki, ‘eṉṉōy tuṭaippāy' eṉṟalum, maṇimēlalai peytalum, avaḷ paci nīṅkit toḻutu uraikkum; uraippavaḷ, ‘ulakavaṟavi yoṉṟuṇṭu; ataṉpāṟ pōka' eṉṟu kūṟip pōkiya piṉṉar, āyiḻai otuṅki vantu vantaṉai ceytu ēṟi vaṇaṅkit toḻutu ēttit tōṉṟi, ēṟpōr yāvarum  varuka' eṉa, ampala maruṅku oliyeḻuntaṉṟu eṉa viṉai muṭivu ceyka. 
 ulakavaṟavi pukka kātai muṟṟiṟṟu. 


18. utayakumaraṉ ampalam pukka kātai 
maṇimēkalai pikkuṇik kōlaṅkoṇṭu pāttiramēnti aiyamēṟṟu ulaka vaṟaviyiṟ ceṉṟāḷeṉpataik kēṭṭa cittirāpati uḷaṅkotittu veytuyirttuk kalaṅki, "maṇimēkalaiyiṉ ic ceykaiyai oḻippēṉ" eṉṟu eṇṇikkoṇṭu, kūttiyal maṭantaiya rellāraiyum pārttu, "kōvalaṉ iṟantatu kēṭṭu mātavi muṉivarkaḷiṉ paḷḷiyai aṭaintu tāpatak kōlam pūṇṭiruttal nakaikkat takkatu; yām kaṇavaṉuṭaṉ iṟakkum pattiṉip peṇṭirallēm; pāṇaṉ iṟantapoḻutu avaṉuṭaṉ iṟavāta yāḻiṉaip pōlvēm; maṟṟum tātiṉai yuṇṭu pūvait tuṟakkum vaṇṭupōlkuvam; tāpatak kōlan tāṅkuvatu namatu kula voḻukkattiṟku ottataṉṟu; mātavi makaḷ maṇimēkalaiyiṉ pikkuṇikkōlattai māṟṟi avaḷ kaiyilēntiya aiyak kaṭiñaiyaip piccaiyēṟpār piṟar kaiyiṟ pōkki, avaḷaip palanāḷāka virumpiyirukkum utayakumaraṉāl avaṉatu tērilēṟṟu vittu varuvēṉ; aṅṅaṉañ ceyyēṉāyiṉ, kuṭikkuṟṟappaṭṭu ēḻu ceṅkalait talaimēlēṟṟik koṇṭu nāṭaka varaṅkaic cuṟṟivantu paḻiyōṭirukkum nāṭaka makaḷirpōla iṉi nāṉ nāṭakak kaṇikaiyar maṉaikaṭkuc cellēṉākuka" eṉṟu cūḷuṟavu ceytu, taṉṉaic cilar cūḻntuvarac ceṉṟu utayakumaraṉ iruppiṭattai yaṭaintu, paḷiṅku maṇṭapattilē tūmalarp paḷḷiyil iruntōṉaik kaṇṭu tutittu, maṇimēkalai ulakavaṟaviyai aṭaintirukkiṟāḷ eṉpataṉai avaṉukkuk kuṟippikka, avaṉ uvavaṉattil maṇimēkalaiyaik kaṇṭatu mutal nikaḻntavaṟṟaik kūṟi avaḷpāluḷḷa ciṟapput taṉmaiyaip pārāṭṭiṉaṉ; cittirāpati avaṉ maṉattai vēṟupaṭuttum moḻikaḷ palavaṟṟaik kūṟi muyala avaṉ uḷḷam piṟaḻntu tērēṟi ulakavaṟaviyai aṭaintu, palarkkum uṇavaḷittuk koṇṭirukkum maṇimēkalaiyaik kaṇṭu arukiṟ ceṉṟu, "nī tāpatak kōlam pūṇṭatu yātu karuti?" eṉṟu viṉāviṉaṉ; maṇimēkalai "paḻam piṟappiṟ kaṇavaṉākavirunta ivaṉai vaṇaṅkutal muṟaiyākum" eṉṟu eṇṇi vaṇaṅki,  
	“		piṟattalu mūttalum piṇippaṭ ṭiraṅkalum
iṟattalu muṭaiyatu iṭumpaik koḷkalam
makkaḷ yākkai yituveṉa vuṇarntu
mikka nallaṟam virumputal purintēṉ" 
eṉṟuraittu, vēṟṟurukkoḷḷa niṉaittu avaṉai nīṅkik kōyiliṉuḷḷē ceṉṟu campāpatiyai vaṇaṅki, muṉpu maṇimēkalā teyvam aruḷic ceyta mantirattai yōtik kāyacaṇṭikai vaṭiva muṟṟu amutacurapiyai ēnti veḷiyē vantu niṉṟāḷ. avaḷ aṅṅaṉam vantatai aṟiyāta utayakumaraṉ "uḷḷē ceṉṟa maṇimēkalai campāpati kōyiliṉuḷḷē oḷittukkoṇṭāḷ" eṉṟu niṉaintu ceṉṟu campāpatiyai vaṇaṅki "piccaip pāttirattaik kāyacaṇṭikaiyiṉ kaiyiṟ koṭuttuviṭṭu oḷittuc ceṉṟa maṇimēkalaiyai iṅkuḷḷa pāvaikaḷuḷḷē yāṉ evvāṟuṇarvēṉ; nī avaḷai eṉakkuk kāṭṭāyāyiṉ paṉṉāḷāyiṉum nāṉ ivviṭattilēyē pāṭu kiṭappēṉ; maṇimēkalaiyai iṅkē viṭuttu nāṉmaṭṭum pōkēṉ; uṉ tiruvaṭiyait toṭṭēṉ" eṉṟu cūḷuraittāṉ.  
		āṅkatu kēṭṭāṅ karumpuṇ akavayiṉ
tīttuṟu ceṅkōl ceṉṟucuṭ ṭāṅkuk
kotitta uḷḷamoṭu kurampukoṇ ṭēṟi
vituppuṟu neñciṉaḷ veytuyirttuk kalaṅkit
5		tīrppaliv vaṟameṉac cittirā patitāṉ
kūttiyaṉ maṭantaiyark kellāṅ kūṟum
kōvalaṉ iṟantapiṉ koṭuntuya reyti
mātavi mātavar paḷḷiyuḷ aṭaintatu
nakutak kaṉṟē naṉṉeṭum pērūr
10		itutak keṉpōrk keḷḷurai yāyatu
kātalaṉ vīyak kaṭuntuya reytip
pōtal ceyyā uyiroṭu pulantu
naḷiyirum poykai yāṭunar pōla
muḷiyerip pukūum mutukuṭip piṟanta
15		pattiṉip peṇṭir allēm palartam
kaittūṇ vāḻkkai kaṭaviya maṉṟē
pāṇmakaṉ paṭṭuḻip paṭūum pāṉmaiyil
yāḻiṉam pōlum iyalpiṉam aṉṟiyum
naṟuntā tuṇṭu nayaṉil kālai
20		vaṟumpūt tuṟakkum vaṇṭu pōlkuvam
viṉaiyoḻi kālait tiruviṉ celvi
aṉaiyē māki āṭavart tuṟappēm
tāpatak kōlan tāṅkiṉa meṉpatu
yāvarum nakūum iyalpiṉa taṉṟē
25		mātavi īṉṟa maṇimē kalaivalli
pōtaviḻ celvi poruntutal virumpiya
utaya kumaraṉām ulakāḷ vaṇṭiṉ
citaiyā uḷḷañ cevvitiṉ aruntak
kaikkoṇ ṭāṅkavaḷ ēntiya kaṭiñaiyaip 
30 		piccai mākkaḷ piṟarkaik kāṭṭi
maṟṟavaṉ ṟaṉṉāl maṇimē kalaitaṉaip
poṟṟērk koṇṭu pōtē ṉākil
cuṭumaṇ ēṟṟi araṅkucūḻ pōki
vaṭuvoṭu vāḻum maṭantaiyar tammōr
35		aṉaiyē ṉāki araṅkak kūttiyar
maṉaiyakam pukāa marapiṉa ṉeṉṟē
vañciṉañ cāṟṟi neñcupukai uyirttu
vañcak kiḷavi māṇpoṭu tērntu
ceṟivaḷai nallār cilarpuṟañ cūḻak
40		kuṟuviyar poṭitta kōlavāṇ mukattaḷ
kaṭuntēr vīti kāliṟ pōki
iḷaṅkō vēntaṉ iruppiṭam kuṟuki
arava vaṇṭoṭu tēṉiṉam ārkkum
tarumaṇaṉ ñemiriya tirunā ṟoruciṟaip
45		pavaḻat tūṇattup pacumpoṟ ceñcuvart
tikaḻoḷi nittilac cittira vitāṉattu
viḷaṅkoḷi paranta paḷiṅkucey maṇṭapattut
tuḷaṅkumā ṉūrtit tūmalarp paḷḷi
veṇṭirai virinta veṇṇiṟac cāmarai
50		koṇṭiru maruṅkuṅ kōtaiyar vīca
iruntōṉ tiruntaṭi poruntiniṉ ṟēttip
tirunteyi ṟilaṅkac cevviyi ṉakkavaṉ
mātavi maṇimē kalaiyuṭaṉ eytiya
tāpatak kōlan tavaṟiṉ ṟōveṉa
55		aritupeṟu ciṟappiṟ kurukukaru uyirppa
orutaṉi yōṅkiya tirumaṇik kāñci
pāṭalcāl ciṟappiṟ paratat tōṅkiya
nāṭakam virumpa naṉṉalaṅ kaviṉik
kāmar cevvik kaṭimalar aviḻntatu
60		utaya kumaraṉeṉum oruvaṇ ṭuṇīiya
viraivoṭu vantēṉ viyaṉperu mūtūr
pāḻmam paṟantalai ampalat tāyatu
vāḻkaniṉ kaṇṇi vāyvāḷ vēnteṉa 
ōṅkiya pauvat tuṭaikalap paṭṭōṉ
65		vāṉpuṇai peṟṟeṉa maṟṟavaṭ kuraippōṉ
mēviya paḷiṅkiṉ viruntiṟ pāvaiyiḵtu
ōviyac ceytiyeṉ ṟoḻivēṉ muṉṉark
kāntaḷañ ceṅkai taḷaipiṇi viṭāa
ēntiḷa vaṉamulai iṟainerit tatūum 
70		ottoḷir pavaḷat tuḷḷoḷi ciṟanta
muttukkūrt taṉṉa muḷḷeyiṟ ṟamutam
aruntē mānta āruyir taḷirppa
viruntiṉ mūral arumpiya tūum
māyitaḻk kuvaḷai malarpuṟat tōṭṭik
75		kāyvēl veṉṟa karuṅkayal neṭuṅkaṇ
aṟivupiṟi tākiya tāyiḻai taṉakkeṉac
ceviyakam pukūuc ceṉṟa cevviyum
paḷiṅkupuṟat teṟinta pavaḷap pāvaiyeṉ
uḷaṅkoṇ ṭoḷittāḷ uyirkkāp piṭṭeṉṟu
80		iṭaiyiruḷ yāmat tiruntēṉ muṉṉarp
poṉṟikaḻ mēṉi orutti tōṉṟic
ceṅkōl kāṭṭic ceytavam purinta
aṅkavaḷ taṉṟiṟam ayarppā yeṉṟaṉaḷ
teyvaṅ kollō tippiyaṅ kollō
85		eyyā maiyalēṉ yāṉeṉ ṟavaṉcolac
cittirā patitāṉ ciṟunakai eyti
attiṟam viṭuvāy araciḷaṅ kuricil
kāmak kaḷḷāṭ ṭiṭaimayak kuṟṟaṉa
tēvark kāyiṉuñ cilavō ceppiṉ
90		mātavaṉ maṭantaikku varuntutuya reyti
āyirañ ceṅkaṇ amararkōṉ peṟṟatum
mēruk kuṉṟat tūṟunīrc caravaṇattu
aruntiṟaṉ muṉivark kāraṇaṅ kākiya
perumpeyarp peṇṭir piṉpuḷam pōkkiya
95		aṅki maṉaiyāḷ avaravar vaṭivāyt
taṅkā vēṭkai taṉaiyavaḷ taṇittatūum
kēṭṭu maṟitiyō vāṭṭiṟaṟ kuricil
kaṉṉik kāvaluṅ kaṭiyiṟ kāvalum 
taṉṉuṟu kaṇavaṉ cāvuṟiṟ kāvalum
100		niṟaiyiṟ kāttup piṟarpiṟark kāṇātu
koṇṭōṉ allatu teyvamum pēṇāp
peṇṭirtaṅ kuṭiyiṟ piṟantāḷ allaḷ
nāṭavar kāṇa nallaraṅ kēṟi
āṭalum pāṭalum aḻakuṅ kāṭṭic
105		curuppunāṇ karuppuvil aruppukkaṇai tūvac
cerukkayal neṭuṅkaṭ curukkuvalaip paṭuttuk
kaṇṭōr neñcaṅ koṇṭakam pukkup
paṇṭēr moḻiyiṟ payaṉpala vāṅki
vaṇṭiṟ ṟuṟakkuṅ koṇṭi makaḷiraip
110		pāṉmaiyiṟ piṇittup paṭiṟṟurai yaṭakkutal
kōṉmuṟai yaṉṟō kumaraṟ keṉṟalum
utaya kumaraṉ uḷḷam piṟaḻntu
viraipari neṭuntēr mēṟceṉ ṟēṟi
āyiḻai yirunta ampala meytik
115		kāṭamar celvi kaṭippaci kaḷaiya
ōṭukaik koṇṭuniṉ ṟūṭṭunaḷ pōlat
tīppaci mākkaṭkuc ceḻuñcō ṟīttup
pāttiram ēntiya pāvaiyaik kāṇṭalum
iṭaṅkaḻi kāmamo ṭaṭaṅkā ṉāki
120		uṭampō ṭeṉṟaṉ uḷḷakam pukunteṉ
neñcaṅ kavarnta vañcak kaḷvi
nōṟṟūṇ vāḻkkaiyiṉ nocitavan tāṅki
ēṟṟūṇ virumpiya kāraṇam eṉṉeṉat
tāṉē tamiyaḷ niṉṟōḷ muṉṉar
125		yāṉē kēṭṭal iyalpeṉac ceṉṟu
nallāy eṉkol naṟṟavam purintatu
collāy eṉṟu tuṇintuṭaṉ kēṭpa
eṉṉamar kātala ṉirākulaṉ īṅkivaṉ
taṉṉaṭi toḻutalum takaveṉa vaṇaṅki
130		aṟaipōy neñcam avaṉpāl aṇukiṉum
iṟaivaḷai muṉkai īṅkivaṉ paṟṟiṉum
toṉṟu kātalaṉ colletir maṟuttal
naṉṟi yaṉṟeṉa naṭuṅkiṉaḷ mayaṅkik 
kēṭṭatu moḻivēṉ kēḷvi yāḷaril
135		tōṭṭa ceviyainī yākuvai yāmeṉil
piṟattalum mūttalum piṇippaṭ ṭiraṅkalum
iṟattalu muṭaiya tiṭumpaik koḷkalam
makkaḷ yākkai ituveṉa uṇarntu
mikka nallaṟam virumputal purintēṉ
140		maṇṭamar murukkuṅ kaḷiṟaṉai yārkkup
peṇṭir kūṟum pēraṟi vuṇṭō
kēṭṭaṉai yāyiṉ vēṭṭatu ceykeṉa
vāṭṭiṟaṟ kuricilai maṭakkoṭi nīṅki
muttai mutalvi mutiyāḷ irunta
145		kuccarak kuṭikai taṉṉakam pukkāṅku
āṭavar ceyti aṟikunar yāreṉat
tōṭalar kōtaiyait toḻutaṉaḷ ētti
māya viñcai mantiram ōtik
kāyacaṇ ṭikaiyeṉuṅ kārikai vaṭivāy
150		maṇimē kalaitāṉ vantu tōṉṟa
aṇimalart tārōṉ avaḷpāṟ pukkuk
kuccarak kuṭikaik kumariyai marīip
piccaip pāttiram perumpaci uḻanta
kāyacaṇ ṭikaitaṉ kaiyiṟ kāṭṭi
155		māyaiyiṉ oḷitta maṇimē kalaitaṉai
īṅkim maṇṇīṭ ṭiyāreṉa uṇarkēṉ
āṅkavaḷ ivaḷeṉ ṟaruḷā yāyiṭiṉ
paṉṉā ḷāyiṉum pāṭu kiṭappēṉ
iṉṉuṅ kēḷāy imaiyōr pāvāy
160		pavaḷac cevvāyt tavaḷavāḷ nakaiyum
añcaṉañ cērāc ceṅkayal neṭuṅkaṇum
muṟintukaṭai neriya vaḷaintacilaip puruvamum
kuvimuṭ karuviyum kōṇamum kūrnuṉaik
kavaimuṭ karuviyum ākik kaṭikoḷak
165		kalvip pākariṟ kāppuvalai yōṭṭi
valvā yāḻiṉ mellitiṉ viḷaṅka
mutukkuṟai mutumoḻi eṭuttuk kāṭṭip
putukkōḷ yāṉai vēṭṭam vāytteṉa 
mutiyāḷ uṉṟaṉ kōṭṭam pukunta
170		mativāḷ mukattu maṇimē kalaitaṉai
oḻiyap pōkēṉ uṉṉaṭi toṭṭēṉ
itukuṟai yeṉṟaṉaṉ iṟaimakaṉ ṟāṉeṉ.  
urai
1-9.		āṅkatu kēṭṭu āṅku - maṇimēkalai aṟattōr kōlam pūṇṭu piccaiyēṟṟu aṉaivarukkum uṇavaḷip pataik kēṭṭu appoḻutē, arumpuṇ akavayiṉ - ariya puṇṇiṉ uḷḷē, tīttuṟu ceṅkōl ceṉṟu cuṭṭāṅku - tīyiṉkaṇ paḻukkak kāytaluṟṟa cevviya kōlāṉatu ceṉṟu cuṭṭāṟpōla, kotitta uḷḷamoṭu - kotip puṟṟa uḷḷattuṭaṉ, kurampu koṇṭu ēṟi - varampu kaṭantu eḻuntu, vituppuṟu neñciṉaḷ veytuyirttuk kalaṅki - naṭukka meytiya maṉattaḷāyc cuṭu mūcceṟintu mayaṅki, tīrppal iv aṟameṉac cittirātipatitāṉ kūttiyal maṭantaiyarkku ellām kūṟum-cittirāpatiyāṉavaḷ maṇimēkalai koṇṭa ivvaṟattiṉai oḻittu viṭuvēṉ eṉa eṇṇi nāṭakak kaṇikaiyar aṉaivarkkum kūṟuvāḷ; 
tī tuṟu - tīyāṉatu paṟṟiya, kōl cuṭṭāṅkut tuṉpattāṟ kotitta uḷḷa meṉka. kurampu - varampu; koṇṭu - tāṇṭi yeṉṉum poruṭṭu; 1"kurampeḻuntu kuṟṟaṅkoṇ ṭēṟār" eṉpatu kāṇka. vituppu-viraivumām. mēl varuvaṉa cittirāpati kūṟṟu.  
7-15.   kōvalaṉ iṟantapiṉ koṭuntuyar eyti mātavi mātavar paḷḷiyuḷ aṭaintatum - mātavi taṉ kātalaṉākiya kōvalaṉ kolaiyuṇṭa piṉṉark koṭiya tuṉpattaiyaṭaintu aṟavaṇavaṭikaḷ uṟaiviṭattai aṭaintatum, nakutakkaṉṟē nalneṭum pērūr - mikac ciṟanta pērūriṉkaṇ cirikkat takkatāyiṟṟu, itu takku eṉpōrkku eḷḷuvarai āyatu - ivvurai takutiyuḷḷateṉṟu kūṟum aṟiñarkaṭku ikaḻurai yākiyatu, kātalaṉ vīyak kaṭuntuyar eyti-kaṇavaṉiṟakka mikunta tuyarattai yaṭaintu, pōtal ceyyā uyirōṭu pulantu - nīṅkāta uyiruṭaṉ veṟuppuk koṇṭu, naḷiirum poykai āṭunarpōla - kuḷirnta nīriṉaiyuṭaiya periya kuḷattiṉkaṭ ceṉṟu nīrāṭuvār pōla, muḷi eri pukūum mutukuṭippiṟanta pattiṉip peṇṭir allēm-viḷakka mikka tīyiṭaik kuḷikkum paḻaṅkuṭiyiṟ piṟanta kaṟpuṭai makaḷi rallēm yām; 
kōvalaṉ kolaiyuṇṭamai kēṭṭu mātavi aṟavaṇavaṭi kaḷai yaṭaintu tuṟavu pūṇṭataṉai, muṉṉar ūralaruraitta kātaiyiṟ kūṟiṉamaiyāṉum, 1"pōtiyiṉkīḻ mātavar muṉ puṇṇiyatāṉam purinta, mātavi taṉṟuṟavuṅ kēṭṭāyō tōḻī" eṉpataṉālum aṟika. pērūr-pērūril. itu-maṇimēkalai pikkuṇik kōlam pūṇṭu piccai yēṟkiṉṟāḷ eṉṉum urai. takku-takkatu: vikāram. takkeṉpōr - takavuṭaimaiyai aṟintu kūṟum aṟiñar. muḷiyeri - kāynta viṟakiṟ paṟṟiya tī yeṉṟumām. 
15-24.	palar tam kaittūṇ vāḻkkai kaṭaviyam aṉṟē - palaruṭaiya kaiyatākiya uṇavaiyuṇṭu vāḻtalākiya urimaiyaiyuṭaiyēm; pāṇmakaṉ paṭṭuḻip paṭūum pāṉmai il yāḻiṉam pōlum iyalpiṉam-pāṇaṉiṟantaviṭattu uṭaṉiṟakkuntaṉmaiyillāta yāḻaip pōṉṟa iyalpuṭai yōm: aṉṟiyum-aḵtaṉṟiyum, naṟuntātu uṇṭu nayaṉ il kālai-maṇamuḷḷa makarantattai nukarntu pacaiyākiya tēṉ illātapoḻutu, vaṟumpūt tuṟakkum vaṇṭu pōlkuvam - vaṟitākiya pūvai viṭṭu nīṅkum vaṇṭiṉaiyum pōlvēm; viṉai oḻi kālait tiruviṉ celvi aṉaiyēm āki āṭavart tuṟappēm-maṟṟum nalviṉai nīṅkum kālattut tirumakaḷaip pōṉṟēm āki āṭavarai viṭṭu nīṅkuvēm; tāpatak kōlam tāṅkiṉam eṉpatu - pikkuṇik kōlam pūṇṭēm eṉpatu, yāvarum nakūum iyalpiṉatu aṉṟē - yāvarum nakaikkum iyalpaiyuṭaiya tallavō ;  
nāṭakak kaṇikaiyātaliṉ pāṇmakaṉaiyum yāḻaiyum uvamai kūṟiṉāḷeṉka. nayaṉ il kālai - āṭavariṭattuc celvamillāta kālattil eṉṟumām. "vaṇṭiṟ ṟuṟakkuṅ koṇṭi makaḷirai" eṉa ik kātaiyiṟ piṉpum kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷatu; maturaik kāñci yuḷḷum 1"iḷampal celvar vaḷantapa vāṅki, nuṇṭā tuṇṭu vaṟumpūt tuṟakkum, meṉciṟai vaṇṭiṉa māṉa" eṉa iv vuvamai vantuḷḷamai aṟiyaṟpālatu. viṉai - nalviṉai. tiruviṉ celvi-tiruvākiya celvi. nalviṉai yulanta poḻutu tirumakaḷ nīṅkuvatu pōlum eṉṉum iv vuvamai 2"tavantīr maruṅkiṟ ṟirumakaḷ pōlap, payantīr maruṅkiṟ paṟṟuviṭ ṭorīi" eṉap peruṅ kataiyuḷ varutalaṟintu iṉpuṟaṟpālatu. tāpatakkōlam - tavakkōlam. 
25-32.   mātavi īṉṟa maṇimēkalai valli - mātavi peṟṟa maṇimēkalaiyākiya pūṅkoṭi, pōtaviḻ cevvi poruntutal virumpiya-pūkkum paruva maṭaitalai virumpiya, utayakumaraṉām ulakāḷ vaṇṭiṉ-utayakumaraṉ eṉṉum ulakiṉaiyāḷ kiṉṟa vaṇṭiṉatu, citaiyā uḷḷam cevvitiṉ arunta-vēṟoṉṟālum aḻiyāta maṉamāṉatu naṉṟāka aruntu māṟu, kaikkoṇṭu - maṇimēkalaiyaik kaiyakappaṭuttu, āṅkavaḷ ēntiya kaṭiñaiyai - avaḷ  kaiyil ēntiya ōṭṭiṉai, piccaimākkaḷ piṟarkaik kāṭṭi - ēṟṟuṇpōrākiya piṟa makkaḷiṉ kaiyilē kāṭṭi, maṟṟavaṉ   taṉṉāl - avvutayakumaraṉāl, maṇimēkalaitaṉaip poṟṟērk koṇṭupōtēṉ ākil - avaḷaip poṟṟēriṉmītu ēṟṟik koṇṭu vārēṉāyiṉ ; 
pōtaviḻ cevvi poruntutal virumpiya eṉpataṟkup pūkkum paruvattil ataṉai yaṭaitaṟku virumpiya eṉṟurait talumām. ikkātaiyuṭ piṉṉarum utayakumaraṉ vaṇṭāka uruvakikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉaṉ. talaivaṉ makaḷir nalattai yuṇṭu tuṟattaṟku vaṇṭiṉai uvamai kūṟutalumuṇṭu; 3"peṇṭir nalamvauvit taṇcāraṟṟātuṇṇum, vaṇṭiṟ ṟuṟappāṉ malai" eṉpatu kāṇka. piṟar kaikkāṭṭi - piṟariṭam paṟittuk koṭutteṉṟapaṭi. 
33-42. 	cuṭumaṇ ēṟṟi araṅku cūḻpōki - ceṅkaṟkaḷait talai mītēṟṟi araṅkiṉaic cuṟṟi vantu, vaṭuvoṭu vāḻum maṭantaiyar tammō raṉaiyēṉ āki - kuṟṟattuṭaṉ kūṭi vāḻum makaḷiraip pōṉṟavaḷāki, araṅkak kūttiyar maṉaiyakam pukāa marapiṉaṉ eṉṟē-araṅkiṉkaṇ āṭukiṉṟa nāṭaka makaḷiruṭaiya illattiṉ kaṇ nuḻaiyāta muṟaimai yaḷāvēṉ eṉṟu, vañciṉam cāṟṟi neñcu pukai uyirttu-vañciṉaṅkūṟi neñcu pukaiyeḻap perumūc ceṟintu, vañcakkiḷavi māṇpoṭu tērntu - vañcam poruntiya collai naṉku ārāyntu, ceṟivaḷai nallār cilar puṟam cūḻa - neruṅkiya vaḷaiyalkaḷai yaṇinta potu makaḷir cilar puṟattē cūḻa, kuṟuviyar poṭitta kōlavāṇ mukattaḷ - ciṟu viyarvainīr arumpiya aḻaku poruntiya oḷḷiya mukattaiyuṭaiyaḷāy, kaṭuntēr vīti kāliṟpōki- virainta celaviṉaiyuṭaiya tērkaḷ cellum vītiyil naṭantu ceṉṟu, iḷaṅkō vēntaṉ iruppiṭam kuṟuki - araciḷaṅ kumaraṉ iruppiṭattai aṭaintu; 
ēṟṟi - ēṟṟappaṭṭu eṉṟumām. patiyilāriṟ kuṭikkuṟṟap paṭṭārai ēḻu ceṅkaṟ cumatti araṅku cūḻvittup puṟattu viṭutal marapākaliṉ, cittirāpati iṅṅaṉam vañciṉañ cāṟṟiṉa ḷeṉka ; 'ya ṅkuñ cūḻpōki' eṉap pāṭaṅ koṇṭu, ūraic cuṟṟi vantu eṉṟuraittalumām; 1"cuṭuma ṇēṟā vaṭunīṅku ciṟappiṉ" eṉpatum ataṉ uraikaḷum kāṇka. maṭantaiyar tammōr; ōr: acai. marapiṉaṉ: aṉvikuti, taṉmaikkaṇ vantatu; 'marapiṉaḷaṉṟeṉa' eṉpatum pāṭam vañcakkiḷavi - maṇimēkalaiyai utayakumaraṉ vayamākkaṟkēṟṟa col. kōla mukattu viyarpoṭittavaḷāy eṉap pirittuk kūṭṭutalumām.  
43-51.	arava vaṇṭoṭu tēṉiṉam ārkkum-olikkiṉṟa vaṇṭukaḷuṭaṉ tēṉiṉamum āravārikkum, tarumaṇal ñemiriya tirunāṟu oru ciṟai - koṇṭuvantiṭṭa maṇal paranta muṉṟilaiyuṭaiya aḻaku tōṉṟum oru pakkattil, pavaḻat tūṇattup pacumpoṉ ceñcuvar-pavaḷaṅkaḷāṟ ceyta tūṇkaḷaiyum paciya poṉṉālākiya cevviya cuvarkaḷaiyum, tikaḻ oḷi nittilac cittira vitāṉattu - oḷi viḷaṅkum muttukkaḷākiya mēṟkaṭṭiyaiyum uṭaiya, viḷaṅku oḷip paranta paḷiṅku cey maṇṭapattu-viḷaṅku kiṉṟa pēroḷi paraviya paḷiṅku maṇṭapattil, tuḷaṅkumāṉ ūrtit tūmalarp paḷḷi - oḷi acaikiṉṟa ariyaṇai yākiya naṟu malar irukkaiyil, veṇtirai virinta veṇṇiṟac cāmarai - veḷḷiya alaikaḷ pōla virinta veṇṇiṟamuṭaiya kavarikaḷai, koṇṭu iru maruṅkum kōtaiyar vīca - makaḷir koṇṭu iru pakkamum vīca, iruntōṉ tiruntaṭi porunti niṉṟu ētti - amarntirunta utayakumaraṉuṭaiya tiruntiya aṭikaḷai vaṇaṅki niṉṟu ētta;
 ārkkum oru ciṟai yeṉka; ārkkum eṉṟataṉāl maṇamuṭaimai peṟappaṭṭatu. maṇal paranta muṟṟattai yuṭaiyaveṉa viritturaittuk koḷka ; 1"tarumaṇaṉ ñemiriya tirunakar muṟṟattu" eṉpatu kāṇka. nāṟutal-tōṉṟutal. muttālākiya calliyaiyum tūkkiṉaiyum uṭaiya cittira vitāṉam eṉka; 2"ōviya vitāṉat turaipeṟu nittilattu, mālait tāmam vaḷaiyuṭa ṉāṟṟi" eṉṟār iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum. māṉ-arimāṉ. ūrti-ēṟutaṟkiṭamākavuḷḷa ātaṉam eṉṉum poruḷ kuṟittu niṉṟatu. maṇṭapattup paḷḷiyil iruntōṉ eṉka. ētti - ēttaveṉat tirikka. 
52-54.	tiruntu eyiṟu ilaṅkac cevviyiṉ nakku avaṉ - utayakumaraṉ aḻakiya paṟkaḷ viḷaṅkumāṟu cevvaiyākac cirittu, mātavi maṇimēkalaiyuṭaṉ eytiya - mātavi maṇimēkalaiyuṭaṉ koṇṭa, tāpatak kōlam tavaṟiṉṟō eṉa - tava vēṭam tavaṟiṉṟi yuḷatō veṉṟu kēṭka ;
tāpatakkōlam-tavavēṭam; eṉṟatu pikkuṇik kōlattai. 
55-63.	aritu peṟu ciṟappil kuruku karu vuyirppa - aritiṟ peṟṟa ciṟappiṉāl paṟavaikaḷ īṉum vaṇṇam, oru taṉi ōṅkiya tirumaṇik kāñci - oppaṟṟuyarnta aḻakiya kariya kāñci maram, pāṭal cāl ciṟappil paratattu ōṅkiya-pāṭal cāṉṟa ciṟappiṉaiyuṭaiya parata kaṇṭattil uyarnta, nāṭakam virumpa naṉṉalam kaviṉi - nāṭukaḷiṉiṭam virumpumāṟu aḻakiya nalaṅkaḷ niṟaintu. kāmar cevvik kaṭimalar aviḻntatu-virumpappaṭum cevviyaiyuṭaiya.  naṟiya malar viriyap peṟṟatu; utayakumaraṉ eṉum oru vaṇṭu uṇīiya - ataṉai utayakumaraṉākiya oppaṟṟa vaṇṭu uṇṇum poruṭṭu, virai voṭu vantēṉ-viraintu vantēṉ; viyaṉperu mūtūrp pāḻmm paṟantalai ampalattāyatu - atu mikka perumaiyai yuṭaiya im mūtūriṉ kaṇṇē pāḻiṭamākiya puṟaṅ kāṭṭaic cārnta ulaka vaṟaviyil uḷḷatu ; vāḻka niṉ kaṇṇi vāyvāḷ vēntu eṉa-kūriya vāy poruntiya vāḷiṉai yuṭaiya maṉṉava niṉ kaṇṇi vāḻvatāka eṉa vuraippa; 
utayakumaraṉ avaik kaḷattiruntā ṉākaliṉ cittirāpati tāṉ kūṟuvataṉaik kuṟippi ṉuṇarntukoḷḷumāṟu cilēṭaiyāṟ kūṟuvāḷāyiṉaḷ. kāñcimarattaip puṉainturaippatu pōṉṟa itaṉkaṇ, ‘kuruku' eṉpatu mātaviyaiyum, 'maṇikkāñci' eṉpatu maṇimēkalaiyaiyum kuṟittu niṉṟaṉa. mātavipeṟa ōṅkiya maṇimēkalai nāṭakam virumpap paruva meytiṉaḷeṉka. kuruku-kurukkatti ; itu mātaviyeṉavum paṭum. kāñci - mēkalaiyātalait tivākaram mutaliyavaṟṟāṉaṟika, nāṭakam - nāṭṭiṉiṭam, nāṭakakkalai. nāṭakam ivaḷāṟ ciṟappeytu mākaliṉ "nāṭakam virumpa" eṉṟār; 1"nāṭaka mēttu nāṭakak kaṇikai" eṉa varutal kāṇka. nalam kaviṉi - nalam niṟaintu eṉṉum poruṭṭu. kāmar-viruppam. ‘utayakumaraṉeṉu moruvaṇṭu' eṉṟatu muṉṉilaip puṟamoḻi. paṟantalai - puṟaṅkāṭu. kuruku karuvuyirppa ōṅkiya kāñci, nalaṅ kaviṉik kaṭimala raviḻntatu; ataṉai utayakumaraṉākiya vaṇṭu uṇṇum poruṭṭu vantēṉ; ak kāñci ampalattāyatu eṉka. kaṇṇi-talaiyiṟ cūṭum mālai. niṉ kaṇṇi vāḻka veṉṟal marapu. 
64-65.	ōṅkiya pauvattu uṭaikalap paṭṭōṉ vāṉpuṇai peṟṟeṉa - periya kaṭalilē kappal uṭaiyappaṭṭavaṉ ciṟanta teppattaip peṟṟāṟpōlak karuti, maṟṟu avaṭku uraippōṉ- cittirāpatikkuk kūṟupavaṉāy. 
uṭaikalap paṭṭōṉ - kalam uṭaiyap paṭṭōṉ eṉa māṟuka. "uṭaikalap paṭṭāṅ koḻintōr tammuṭaṉ" (16:20) eṉa muṉ vantamaiyuṅ kāṇka.  
66-73.	mēviya paḷiṅkiṉ viruntiṉ pāvai iḵtu ōviyac ceyti yeṉṟu oḻivēṉ muṉṉar-paḷikkaṟaiyiṉkaṇ poruntiya putumaiyiṉai yuṭaiya pāvaiyākiya itu cittirac ceykai eṉṟu nīṅkuvēṉ muṉṉē, kāntaḷañ ceṅkai taḷaipiṇi viṭāa ēntu iḷavaṉamulai iṟai nerittatūum - kāntaḷ malar pōṉṟa civanta kaikaḷ piṇitta piṇippu viṭāvāy ēntiya iḷaiya aḻakiya mulaiyai iṟaiyaḷavu neritta ceykaiyum, ottoḷir .  pavaḷattu uḷ oḷi ciṟanta - pavaḷattai yottu oḷirukiṉṟa vāyiṉuḷḷē oḷimikka, muttuk kūrttaṉṉa muḷeyiṟṟu amutam - muttukkaḷ kūrmaiyuṟṟālaṉṉa kūriya paṟkaḷiṉ kaṇ  ūṟukiṉṟa amutiṉai, arunta ēmānta āruyir taḷirppa-aruntu  taṟku ācaippaṭṭa ariya uyirāṉatu taḷirkkumāṟu, viruntiṉ mūral arumpiyatūum - putiya puṉmuṟuval pūttatuvum ;
muṉpu maṇimēkalai taṉṉaik kaṇṭu añcip paḷik kaṟaiyiṟ pukunta poḻutu avaḷai ōviyameṉṟu tāṉ eṇṇiyiruntāṉāka utayakumaraṉ kūṟiṉāṉeṉka. viṭā a:etirmaṟai viṉaiyeccamuṟṟu. iṟai - ciṟitu. arunta eṉṉum peyareccat takaram tokkatu; "aruntē mānta vāruyir mutalvaṉai" (14 : 68) eṉa muṉṉarum iṅṅaṉam pōntatu. 
74-80. 	māyitaḻk kuvaḷai malar puṟattu ōṭṭik kāyvēl veṉṟa karuṅkayal neṭuṅkaṇ - kariya itaḻkaḷaiyuṭaiya kuvaḷai malarkaḷaip puṟattē ōṭacceytu kāykiṉṟa vēliṉai veṉṟa kariya kayalmīṉaip pōṉṟa nīṇṭa kaṇkaḷ, aṟivu piṟitākiyatu āyiḻai taṉakkeṉac ceviyakam pukūuc ceṉṟa cevviyum - maṇimēkalaikku aṟivu vēṟupaṭṭateṉṟu aṟivikkumāṟu kātiṉiṭam ōṭic ceṉṟa aḻakum, paḷiṅku puṟatteṟinta pavaḷappāvai eṉ uḷam koṇṭu oḷittāḷ uyirk kāppiṭṭu eṉṟu-ākiya ivaṟṟaikkoṇṭu paḷikkaṟaiyiṉ puṟattē tōṉṟiya pavaḷappāvai uyiraik kāttal ceytu eṉ uḷattil oḷittāḷ eṉṟu, iṭaiyiruḷ yāmattu iruntēṉ muṉṉar- iruḷaiyuṭaiya iṭaiyāmattil tuyilātirun tēṉ muṉpu;  
ōṭṭi veṉṟa kaṇ, kayal neṭuṅkaṇ eṉka. ceviyil uraikkap pukkatupōṟ ceṉṟa veṉka. kaṇṇiṉ iyalpākiya nīṭciyaiyō allatu mīṭciyaiyō iṅṅaṉaṅ kaṟpittuk kūṟutalāl itutaṟkuṟippēṟṟam. koṇṭu - nerittatūum arumpiyatūum cevviyumākiya ivaṟṟaik koṇṭu, pāvai koṇṭu uyirkkāppiṭṭu eṉ uḷattu oḷittāḷ eṉka. iruntēṉ muṉṉar - tuyilātiruntēṉuṭaiya muṉpu; "poṅku mellamaḷiyiṟ poruntā tiruntōṉ, muṉṉart tōṉṟi"  (7: 6-7) eṉa muṉpu vantiruttal kāṇka. iruntēṉ ; aṅṅaṉa mirunta eṉ muṉṉar eṉa vēṟaṟutturaikka. 
81-85.	poṉtikaḻ mēṉi orutti tōṉṟi - poṉpōla viḷaṅkukiṉṟa tirumēṉiyaiyuṭaiyaoruttitōṉṟi, ceṅkōl kāṭṭi - ceṅkōṉmaiyai aṟivuṟutti, ceytavam purinta aṅkavaḷ taṉtiṟam ayarppāy eṉṟaṉaḷ - tavaneṟiyiṟ cellum maṇimēkalaiyiṉmītu koṇṭa eṇṇattai maṟappāy eṉṟu kūṟiṉaḷ, teyvaṅ kollō tippiyaṅ kollō. eyyā maiyalēṉ yāṉ eṉṟu avaṉ cola-aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiyatu oru teyvantāṉō aṉṟi vāṉavar ulakil tōṉṟiya vēṟu poruḷō iṉṉateṉa aṟiyāta mayakkamuṭaiyēṉ eṉṟu utayakumaraṉ kūṟa; 
orutti eṉṟatu maṇimēkalā teyvattai- eyyāmai - aṟiyāmai: uriccol, tippiyaṅ kollō - viyappiṉai viḷaivikkum vēṟoṉṟō eṉṟumām. 
86-89. 	cittirāpati tāṉ ciṟu nakai eyti - cittirāpati ciṟunakaiyaṭaintu, attiṟam viṭuvāy araciḷaṅkuricil-iḷavaracar peruntakaiyē niṉ kaṉaviṟṟōṉṟi orutti kūṟiyataṉai viṭuvāyāka, kāmak kaḷḷāṭṭiṭai mayakkuṟṟaṉa tēvarkkāyiṉum cilavō ceppiṉ-kūṟuṅkāl vāṉavar kaḷiṭattum kāmak kaḷḷāṭṭiṉ kaṇ mayaṅkic ceyta ceykaikaḷ cilavāmō; 
kāmak kaḷḷāṭṭiṭai - kāmamākiya kaḷḷai uṇṭavaḻi. aṟivai mayakkutalāṉum, veḷippaṭuntōṟum viruppattai yuṇṭākkutalāṉum kāmam kaḷḷiṉai yoppatāyiṟṟu; 1"kaḷittoṟuṅ kaḷḷuṇṭal vēṭṭaṟṟāṟ kāmam, veḷippaṭun tōṟu miṉitu" eṉpatu kāṇka. tēvarkaḷum mayaṅkic ceytaṉa pala veṉṟapaṭi.
90-98.	mātavaṉ maṭantaikku varuntu tuyar eyti āyirañ ceṅkaṇ amararkōṉ peṟṟatum - kautama muṉivar maṉaiviyākiya akalikaiyiṉ mēluḷḷa vēṭkaiyāl mikka tuyaruḻantu tēvarkkaracaṉākiya intiraṉ ammuṉivarāl āyirañ ceṅkaṇ aṭainta ceytiyum, mēruk kuṉṟattu ūrunīrc caravaṇattu-imayamalaiyiluḷḷa paranta nīriṉaiyuṭaiya caravaṇap poykaiyil, aruntiṟal muṉivarkku āraṇaṅkākiya - ariya vali poruntiya muṉivar eḻuvarkku ariya aṇaṅkupōlvārākiya, perum peyarp peṇṭir piṉpu uḷam pōkkiya - periya pukaḻaiyuṭaiya avar maṉaiviyar piṉṉē uḷattaic celuttiya, aṅki maṉaiyāḷ - akkiṉitēvaṉ maṉaivi, avaravar vaṭivāyt tāṅkā vēṭkaitaṉai avaṇ taṇittatūum - am muṉivar maṉaiviyariṉ vaṭivattait taṉittaṉi koṇṭu avaṉatu nīṅkāta vēṭkaiyait taṇitta ceytiyum, kēṭṭum aṟitiyō vāṭṭiṟal kuricil- kēṭṭum aṟiyāyō vāḷvaliyuṭaiya aracar peruntakaiyē;
tuyareyti avaḷaic cērntu ataṉāṟ peṟṟatum eṉa viritturaittuk koḷka. intiraṉ kautama muṉivar maṉaivi yākiya akalikaiyai virumpic cērntu am muṉivariṭṭa cāpattāl āyiraṅkaṇ peṟṟāṉ eṉpatu purāṇakkatai. mēru - imayam ; 1"imaiyavil vāṅkiya" eṉpatu kāṇka. ūrtal - parattal. caravaṇam - taruppaik kāṭu ; ataṉai yuṭaiyatoru poykaiyai uṇarttiṟṟu. muṉṉoru kālattil aṅkivāṉavaṉ eḻumuṉivar maṉaiviyarai virumpi avvētaṉai poṟukkalāṟṟātu kāṭṭiṟkucceṉṟapoḻutu, avaṉ eṇṇattai yaṟinta avaṉ maṉaiviyākiya cuvākātēvi eḻu muṉivar maṉaivikaḷuḷ aruntati yoḻintōr vaṭivattai muṟaiyē eṭuttu, vēṟuvēṟu kālaṅkaḷiṟ cērntu avaṉ vēṭkaiyait taṇittāḷ eṉpatum purāṇakkatai.  
98-102.	kaṉṉik kāvalum kaṭiyiṟ kāvalum - kaṉṉip paruvattiṟ kāvalum maṇattiṉ piṟkāvalum, taṉuṟukaṇavaṉ cāvuṟiṟ kāvalum-taṉṉai aṭainta kaṇavaṉ iṟappiṟ kāvalum ākiya ivaṟṟai, niṟaiyiṟ kāttup piṟarpiṟark kāṇātu-uḷattaik kaṟpu neṟiyil niṟuttalāṟ kāttu ayalārai nōkkāmal, koṇṭōṉ allatu teyvamum pēṇā - kaṇavaṉaiyaṉṟit teyvattaiyum toḻāta, peṇṭirtam kuṭiyil piṟantāḷ allaḷ - kulamakaḷirtam marapil tōṉṟiyavaḷ allaḷ ; 
kulamakaḷir kaṉṉi mutaliya munnilaiyilum niṟaiyāṟ ṟammaikkāt toḻukuva reṉṟataṉāl potumakaḷirkku an nilai vēṟupāṭum kāttalum illai yeṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu. 2"makaḷir, niṟaikākkuṅ kāppē talai," "teyvan toḻāaḷ koḻunaṟ ṟoḻuteḻuvāḷ" eṉpaṉa kāṇka.  
103-111.	nāṭavar kāṇa nal araṅ kēṟi āṭalum pāṭalum aḻakum kāṭṭi-ilakkaṇappaṭi amaitta nalla araṅku kaḷil ēṟi nāṭṭiṉar palarum kāṇum vaṇṇam āṭalaiyum pāṭalaiyum eḻilaiyum pulappaṭutti, curuppu nāṇ karuppuvil aruppukkaṇai tūva - vaṇṭukaḷākiya nāṇiṉaiyuṭaiya karumpuvillaikkoṇṭa anaṅkaṉ arumpukaḷākiya ampu kaḷaip poḻiya, cerukkayal neṭuṅkaṇ curukku valaip paṭuttu-pōrceyyuṅ kayalmīṉai yotta nīṇṭa kaṇ kaḷākiya curukku valaiyāl akappaṭutti, kaṇṭōr neñcam koṇṭu akampukku - tamatāṭal mutaliyavaṟṟaik kaṇṭōruṭaiya uḷḷattaik koḷḷaikoṇṭu maṉaiyiṟ ceṉṟu, paṇtēr moḻiyiṟ payaṉpala vāṅki - paṇpōlum iṉiya moḻikaḷāl poṉ āṭai aṇi mutaliya pala poruḷkaḷaiyum vāṅki, vaṇṭiṉ tuṟakkum koṇṭi makaḷirai - tēṉaiyuṇṭu malarait tuṟakkum vaṇṭiṉaip pōlap poruḷ koṭuttāraip piṉpu tuṟakkum parattaiyarai, pāṉmaiyil piṇittup paṭiṟṟurai aṭakkutal - taṉ vaya mākap piṇittu avartam poyyuraikaḷai aṭakkutal, kōlmuṟai aṉṟō kumaraṟku eṉṟalum - iḷaṅkōvukkuc ceṅkōṉmuṟaimai allavō eṉa uraittalum,  
nallaraṅku-1"eḻukō lakalat teṇkō ṉīḷat, toru kōluyarat tuṟuppiṉa tāki, uttarap palakaiyō ṭaraṅkiṉ palakai, vaitta viṭai nilam nāṟkō lāka, ēṟṟa vāyi liraṇṭuṭaṉ poliyat, tōṟṟiya varaṅku." nāṭaka makaḷirkku āṭal mutaliya mūṉṟiṉoṉṟuṅ kuṟaiyalākā teṉpatu, 2"āṭalum pāṭalu maḻaku meṉṟik, kūṟiya mūṉṟi ṉoṉṟukuṟai paṭāmal" eṉpataṉā ṉaṟika ; curumpu, karumpu, arumpu eṉpaṉa valittal peṟṟaṉa. arumpu - moṭṭaṟāmalar. cerukkayal - oṉṟai yoṉṟu etirntu pōr ceyyuṅ kayal. koṇṭi makaḷir-poruḷaik koḷḷum parattaiyar, kōṉmuṟai-aracanīti eṉṟumām. kumaraṟku:muṉṉilaiyiṟ paṭarkkai. kuṭiyiṟ piṟantāḷallaḷ. koṇṭi makaḷiruṭ paṭṭāḷ. avaḷaip piṇittu aṭakkutal kōṉmuṟai yaṉṟō veṉka. 
112-118.	utayakumaraṉ uḷḷam piṟaḻntu - utayakumaraṉ maṉam māṟutalaṭaintu, viraipari neṭuntēr mēṟceṉ ṟēṟi-virainta celaviṉaiyuṭaiya kutiraikaḷ pūṭṭappeṟṟa periya tēriṉmītu ēṟic ceṉṟu, āyiḻai irunta ampalam eyti - maṇimēkalaiyirunta ampalattai yaṭaintu, kāṭamar celvi kaṭippaci kaḷaiya ōṭukaik koṇṭu niṉṟu ūṭṭunaḷ pōla - pēykaḷiṉ paciyai nīkkutaṟku ōṭṭiṉaik kaiyiṟ koṇṭu niṉṟu ūṭṭukiṉṟa kāṭu kiḻāḷaip pōla, tīppaci mākkaṭkuc ceḻuñ cōṟu īttup pāttiram ēntiya pāvaiyaik kāṇṭalum - koṭiya paciyaiyuṭaiya makkaḷukkuc ceḻuñcōṟaḷittuk kaṭiñai ēnti niṉṟa maṇimēkalaiyaik kaṇṭa vaḷavil;
kaṭi - pēy. ūṭṭunaḷākiya celvi pōla veṉka. 
119-127.	iṭaṅkaḻi kāmamoṭu aṭaṅkāṉ āki - varampiṉṟip perukiya kāmattōṭu aṭaṅkātavaṉāy, uṭampōṭu eṉṟaṉ uḷḷakam pukuntu eṉ neñcam kavarnta vañcak  kaḷvi - uṭaluṭaṉ eṉṉuṭaiya neñciṉuṭ pukuntu eṉatu uḷḷattaik kavarnta vañcak kaḷviyākiya maṇimēkalai, nōṟṟūṇ vāḻkkaiyiṉ nocitavam tāṅki - virataṅkaḷāṟ paṭṭiṉi viṭṭuṇṇum vāḻkkaiyaiyuṭaiya nuṇṇiya tavattait tāṅki, ēṟṟūṇ virumpiya kāraṇam eṉeṉa - irantuṇṇutalai virumpiya kāraṇam yātu eṉṟu, tāṉē tamiyaḷ niṉṟōḷ muṉṉar - tāṉē taṉiyaḷāy niṉṟōḷmuṉ, yāṉē kēṭṭal iyalpu eṉac ceṉṟu - yāṉē nēriṟ ceṉṟu kēṭpatu naṉṟu eṉap pōy, nallāy eṉkol naṟṟavam purintatu collāy eṉṟu tuṇintuṭaṉ kēṭpa - naṅkāy nī naṟṟavam ceyya mēṟkoṇṭatu eṉṉa kāraṇam ataṉaik kūṟuvāyāka    eṉṟu tuṇintu kēṭka; 
iṭaṅkaḻi - varampu kaṭanta; 1"iṭaṅkaḻi neñcat tiḷamai yāṉai" eṉpataṉ urai kāṇka, nocitavam-uṭal iḷaittaṟ kētuvākiya tavameṉṟum, maṉam nuṇṇitātaṟ kētuvākiya tavameṉṟum ām; 2"nōṟṟuṇal yākkai nocitavattīr'' eṉṟār iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum. utayakumāraṉ ampalameytip pāvaiyaik kāṇṭalum aṭaṅkāṉāki, yāṉē kēṭṭal iyalpeṉac ceṉṟu, ‘purintatu eṉkol col lāy' eṉṟu kēṭpa eṉka. 
128-133.	eṉ amar kātalaṉ irākulaṉ īṅkivaṉ taṉ aṭi toḻutalum takaveṉa vaṇaṅki-ivaṉ eṉṉai virumpiya kaṇavaṉākiya irākulaṉākaliṉ ivaṉuṭaiya aṭikaḷai vaṇaṅkutalum takutiyē eṉap paṇintu, aṟaipōy neñcam avaṉpāl aṇu kiṉum-eṉ uḷḷam ōṭṭai pōy avaṉiṭam kiṭṭiṉum, iṟaivaḷai muṉ kai īṅkivaṉ paṟṟiṉum - iṟaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya vaḷai yaṇinta muṉ kaiyai ivaṉ paṟṟiṉum, toṉṟu kātalaṉcol etir maṟuttal - paḻampiṟappiṟ kaṇavaṉuṭaiya collai etir maṟuttal, naṉṟi aṉṟeṉa naṭuṅkiṉaḷ mayaṅki-naṉmai yaṉṟu eṉa naṭukkamuṟṟu mayaṅkiyavaḷāy; 
īṅkivaṉ : oru col. aṟaipōtal - kīḻaṟuttuc cellutal, aṇukiṉum paṟṟiṉum eṉpaṉa etirkālattil vantaṉa. maṟut talāvatu viṭaikūṟā toḻital. 
134-139.	kēṭṭatu moḻivēṉ kēḷviyāḷaril tōṭṭa ceviyai nī ākuvai āmeṉil-kēḷviyāḷarpōla nī tuḷaikkap paṭṭa ceviyuṭaiyai ākuvaiyāṉāl nī kēṭṭa taṟku maṟumoḻi kūṟuvēṉ; piṟattalum mūttalum piṇip paṭṭu iraṅkalum iṟattalum uṭaiyatu - piṟattalum mutumai yeytalum nōyppaṭṭu varuntutalum iṟattalum uṭaiyatu; iṭumpaik koḷkalam- tuṉpattiṟkuk koḷ kalamāṉatu; makkaḷ yākkai itu eṉa uṇarntu - makkaḷ uṭampākiya itu eṉa aṟintu, mikka nal aṟam virumputal purintēṉ-mēṉmaiyuṭaiya aruḷaṟattiṉai virumpu vēṉāyiṉēṉ;
ākuvai yāmeṉil moḻivēṉ eṉa māṟuka. kēḷvi yāḷar - uṇmaip poruḷaik kēṭṭuṇarpavar; 1"kēṭpiṉuṅ kēḷāt takaiyavē kēḷviyāl tōṭkap paṭāta cevi'' eṉpatu īṇṭu uṇartaṟ pālatu. makkaḷ yākkaiyākiya itu uṭaiyatu koḷkalam eṉa vuṇarnteṉka.
nallaṟam-aruḷaṟam: tayātarumam, 2"viṉaiyiṉ vantatu viṉaikku viḷaivāvatu,......, makkaḷ yākkai yituveṉa uṇarntu'' eṉa innūluḷ muṉṉar yākkaiyi ṉiḻipu kūṟappaṭṭiruttalum kāṇka. 
140-142.	maṇṭamar murukkum kaḷiṟu aṉaiyārkkup peṇṭir kūṟum pēraṟivu uṇṭō-mikkuc cellukiṉṟa pōrai yaḻikkum kaḷiṟṟiṉai yotta āṭavarkku makaḷir kūṟum pēraṟivum uṇṭō, kēṭṭaṉai āyiṉ vēṭṭatu cey keṉa - itaṉaik kēṭṭā yātaliṉ iṉi nī virumpiyataic ceyka eṉṟu kūṟi; 
amariṉkaṇ pakaivaraik kollum eṉṟumām. kaḷiṟaṉai yārkku eṉṟatu muṉṉilaippuṟam. "iḷamai nāṇi mutumai yeyti ......ceṟivaḷai makaḷir ceppalu muṇṭō" (4: 107 - 10) eṉa muṉṉarc cutamati utayakumāraṉukku nīti kūṟiyavāṟuṅ kāṇka.  
143-150.   vāḷtiṟal kuricilai maṭakkoṭi nīṅki -maṇimēkalai vāḷ valiyuṭaiya maṉṉavaṉ putalvaṉai nīṅki, muttai mutalvi mutiyāḷ irunta-aṉaivarukkum muṟpaṭṭa mutalvi yākiya campāpati amarntirukkiṉṟa, kuccarak kuṭikaitaṉ akam pukku āṅku - ciṟiya kōyiliṉuḷ nuḻaintu av viṭattil, āṭavar ceyti aṟikunar yār eṉa - āṇmakkaḷiṉ ceyalai aṟivōr yāvar eṉṟu eṇṇi, tōṭalar kōtaiyait toḻutaṉaḷ ētti - itaḻ virikiṉṟa malar mālaiyai yaṇinta campāpatiyai vaṇaṅkit tutittu, māya viñcai mantiram ōti - māyavittai yuṭaiya mantirattai ōti, kāyacaṇṭikai eṉum kārikai vaṭivāy- kāyacaṇṭikai eṉṉum viñcai makaḷiṉ vaṭivaṅkoṇṭu, maṇi mēkalai tāṉ vantu tōṉṟa - maṇimēkalai veḷiyē vantu tōṉṟa; 
 muttai: muntai yeṉpataṉ tiripu. kuccaram - kūrccara nāṭu; kuccarak kuṭikai - kūrccara nāṭṭup paṇiyamainta ciṟiya kōyil eṉpar. āṅku:acai. āṭavar ceykaiyai aṟikunar yār eṉṟatu maṇimēkalai taṉ aṟivuraiyaik kēṭṭa piṉṉum utayakumaraṉ kāmattaik kaṭakkalāṟṟātu taṉṉaip paṟṟutaluṅ kūṭumeṉa eṇṇiṉāḷ eṉṉum kuṟippiṟṟu. māyaviñcai mantiram eṉṟatu maṇimēkalā teyvam aruḷiya vēṟṟuru eytuvikkum mantirattai. 
151-158.    aṇimalart tārōṉ avaḷpāṟ pukku - aḻakiya malar mālaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya utayakumaraṉ kāyacaṇṭikai yuruk
koṇṭa naṅkaiyiṭañ ceṉṟu, kuccarak kuṭikaik kumariyai marī i-piṉpu kōyiliṉkaṇ uḷḷa camputteyvattaip porunti niṉṟu, piccaip pāttiram perumpaci uḻanta kāyacaṇṭikai taṉkaiyil kāṭṭi - taṉ kaiyilirunta tiruvōṭṭaip perumpaciyāl varuntiya kāyacaṇṭikaiyiṉ kaiyiṟ koṭuttu, māyaiyiṉ oḷitta maṇimēkalaitaṉai-māyaiyiṉāl maṟainta maṇimēkalaiyai, īṅkim maṇṇīṭṭu yār eṉa uṇarkēṉ - īṇṭuḷḷa ip pāvaikaḷuḷ yāreṉṟu aṟivēṉ, āṅkavaḷ ivaḷ eṉṟu    aruḷāy āyiṭiṉ -am maṇimēkalai iṉṉaḷeṉa aṟivit taruḷāy āyiṉ,palnāḷ āyiṉum pāṭukiṭappēṉ - palanāṭkaḷāyiṉum īṇṭu varaṅkiṭappēṉ ;
maṇṇīṭu-cutaiyāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa pāvai. "neṭunilai maṇṇīṭu niṉṟa vāyilum" (6: 47) eṉa innūluḷ muṉṉarum, 1"kaṇṇuḷ viṉaiñarum maṇṇīṭ ṭāḷarum" eṉac cilappatikārattum varutal kāṇka. āṅkavaḷ: oru col; 2"teḷivaṉ puṉaṟ ceṉṉi" eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷuraiyil, "iṅkivai uṅkuvai yeṉpaṉa oru col" eṉap pērāciriyaruraittamai kāṇka. pāṭu kiṭattal-varam vēṇṭik kiṭattal; karutiyatu kaikūṭum varai eḻātu kiṭattaleṉka; 3"pācaṇṭac cāttaṟkup pāṭukiṭan tāḷukku" eṉpatu kāṇka.  
159-172.	iṉṉum kēḷāy imaiyōr pāvāy-vāṉōr talaiviyē iṉṉuṅ kēṭpāyāka, pavaḷac cevvāyt tavaḷa vāḷ nakaiyum - pavaḷampōṟ civanta vāyiṉkaṇuḷḷa veḷḷoḷi poruntiya paṟkaḷum, añcaṉam cērāc ceṅkayal neṭuṅkaṇum- maiyeḻutappaṭāta civanta kayal pōlum nīṇṭa kaṇkaḷum, muṟintu kaṭai neriya varinta cilaip puruvamum-kaṭai neriyumāṟu vaḷaintu varinta villaip pōṉṟa puruvaṅkaḷum, kuvimuṭ karuviyum kōṇamum kūrnuṉaik kavaimuṭ karuviyum ākik kaṭikoḷa kaḷiṟṟiṉai yaṭakkum kuvinta muḷḷaiyuṭaiya karuviyum tōṭṭiyum kūriyamuṉaiyaiyuṭaiya kavaitta muṭkaḷālākiya parik kōlumākik kāval koḷḷa, kalvip pākaril kāppuvalai ōṭṭi-kalviyākiya pākarāl kāppiṉaiyuṭaiya valaiyai vīci, valvāy yāḻiṉ mellitiṉ viḷaṅka - col vaṉmaiyuṭaiya vāyākiya yāḻiṉāl iṉitāka viḷaṅku māṟu, mutukkuṟai mutumoḻi eṭuttukkāṭṭi - pēraṟivai viḷakkum mutumoḻikaḷai eṭuttukkāṭṭi, putukkōḷ yāṉai vēṭṭam vāytteṉa - putitākak koḷḷappaṭum yāṉai vēṭṭattiṉkaṇ akappaṭṭateṉṟu, mutiyāḷ uṉṟaṉ kōṭṭam pukunta - campāpatī niṉṉuṭaiya kōyiliṟ pukunta, mativāḷ mukattu maṇimēkalaitaṉai oḻiyap pōkēṉ - matipōlum oḷḷiya mukattiṉaiyuṭaiya maṇimēkalaiyai īṇṭu viṭuttuc cellēṉ ; uṉ aṭi toṭṭēṉ - niṉ tiruvaṭikaḷait toṭṭuc cūḷuṟṟēṉ ; itu kuṟai eṉṟaṉaṉ iṟaimakaṉ tāṉ eṉ - eṉakku vēṇṭum kāriyam ituveṉṟu utayakumaraṉ kūṟiṉa ṉeṉka.  
īṇṭu imaiyōr pāvāy eṉṟatu pōlap piṉṉar "vāṉōr pāvāy" (25-147) eṉac cintātēviyai viḷittaluṅ kāṇka. pikkuṇiyātaliṉ kaṇṇukku maitīṭṭiṟṟila ḷeṉka. varinta cilai - kaṭṭamainta vil; nāṇēṟṟiya villumām. kōṇam - tōṭṭiyeṉṉuṅ karuvi; kavaimuṭ karuvi - kavaitta muḷḷaiyuṭaiya parikkōl: 1"kōṇan tiṉṟa vaṭuvāḻ mukatta" 2"kavaimuṭ karuviyiṉ vaṭamoḻi payiṟṟi" eṉpaṉa kāṇka. 
nakai kuvimuṭ karuviyum, kaṇ kōṇamum, puruvam kavaimuṭ karuviyumāki eṉa niraṉiṟaiyākkuka. kāppu eṉṟatu valaikku aṭai. kalaiyākiya valaiyeṉa uruvakam viritturaikka. taṉ kalaittiṟaṅkaḷāl puṟampōkātu taṭuttāḷ eṉṟapaṭi. vāyai yāḻ eṉṟataṟkēṟpa mutumoḻiyai icaiyeṉa uruvakañ ceyka ; yāḻōcai pōla mellitiṉ-viḷaṅka vāyāl mutumoḻi yeṭuttuk kāṭṭi eṉṟalumām ; yāṉai yāḻicaikku vayamākumātaliṉ ivvāṟu kūṟiṉāṉ. 3"kāḻvarai nillāk kaṭuṅkaḷiṟ ṟoruttal, yāḻvarait taṅki yāṅku" eṉpataṉāl yāṉai yāḻukku vayamāta laṟika. mutumoḻi - mutuvōr moḻi; nītimoḻi. utayakumaraṉ taṉṉai yāṉai yākavum, maṇimēkalaiyiṉ nakai mutaliyavaṟṟai yāṉaiyai aṭakkuṅ karuvikaḷākavum uruvakañ ceytu, avaḷāl tāṉ piṇippuṇṭamai kūṟiṉaṉeṉka. cūḷuṟuvār aṭitoṭutal vaḻakkātalai 4"aṟava raṭitoṭiṉum" 5"kōṉaṭi toṭṭēṉ" 6"aṭalvali yeyiṉarniṉ aṭi toṭu kaṭaṉitu" eṉpavaṟṟāṉu maṟika.  
cittirāpati atu kēṭṭu eṟi veytuyirttuk kalaṅkit tīrppaleṉṟu kūttiyaṉ maṭantaiyar ellārkkuṅ kūṟum; aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟukiṉṟavaḷ, vañciṉañ cāṟṟi uyirttut tērntu pōkik kuṟukip porunti niṉṟu ētta, avaṉ, ‘tavaṟiṉṟō' eṉa, avaḷ, ‘vēntē! niṉ kaṇṇi vāḻka; kāñci ampalat tāyatu' eṉa, atu kēṭṭu avaṭku uraippōṉ, ‘eyyā maiyalēṉ' eṉṟu colla, cittirāpati ciṟunakai yeyti, paṭiṟṟurai yaṭakkutal kōṉmuṟai yaṉṟō kumaraṟku?' eṉṟalum, utayakumaraṉ uḷḷam piṟaḻntu ēṟi eytik kāṇṭalum aṭaṅ kāṉākic ceṉṟu tuṇintu kēṭpa, avar vaṇaṅki naṭuṅki mayaṅki, ‘vēṭṭatu ceyka' eṉṟu kūṟi nīṅkip pukkuttoḻutu ētti ōtik kāyacaṇṭikai vaṭivāy vantu tōṉṟa, tārōṉ pukku marīi, ‘itu kuṟai' eṉṟaṉaṉ eṉa, viṉai muṭivu ceyka.  
utayakumaraṉ ampalam pukka kātai muṟṟiṟṟu. 
19. ciṟaikkōṭṭam aṟakkōṭṭamākkiya kātai  
utayakumaraṉ campāpatiyai vaṇaṅkiiṅṅaṉam vañciṉaṅ kūṟukaiyil avaṉ kēṭkumpaṭi, "nī em perumāṭṭiyiṉ muṉ ārāyntu pārāmaṟ cūḷuṟavu moḻintaṉai ; ataṉāl yātoru payaṉumillai" eṉṟu āṅkuḷḷa cittiraṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟaip poruntiyuḷḷa teyvaṅ kūṟiṟṟu. avvurai kēṭṭu avaṉ maṉaṅ kalaṅki varunti, "maṇimēkalaiyai maṟappāyeṉṟu muṉṉaṅkūṟiya teyvattiṉ moḻiyum viyappait tarukiṉṟatu ; teyvattaṉmai yuḷḷatāka iruttaliṉ avaḷ ēntiya pāttiramum viyappait tarukiṉṟatu ; ic cittiram pēciyatum viyappai viḷaikkiṉṟatu ; ivaṟṟaiyellām maṇimēkalaiyiṉ ceytiyai aṟintukoṇṭa piṉpu aṟivōm" eṉṟu tuṇintu mīṇṭu taṉ iruppiṭattai yaṭaintāṉ. maṇimēkalai, "nam vaṭivattōṭu tirintāl utayakumaraṉ viṭṭu nīṅkāṉ ; ākaliṉ nām kāyacaṇṭikai vaṭivaṅkoḷḷutalē naṉṟu" eṉṟu eṇṇi av vaṭivukoṇṭu campāpatiyiṉ kōyililiruntu amutacurapiyaik kaiyilēntik koṇṭu yāṅkaṇum ceṉṟu pacittuvanta yāvarukkum uṇavaḷittu varupavaḷ, oru nāḷ an nakarattuḷḷa ciṟaikkōṭṭattiṟ pukuntu, aṅkē paciyāl varuntuvōrai aruḷuṭaṉ pārttu iṉiya moḻi kūṟi uṇpippāḷāyiṉaḷ. avaḷ oru pāttirattiliruntē palarukku uṇavaḷittalaik kaṇṭa kāvalāḷar mikka viyappaṭaintu, "ip pāttirattiṉ mēṉmaiyaiyum ivaḷ ceykai yaiyum aracaṉukkut terivippēm' eṉa niṉaintu ceṉṟu, cīrtti yeṉṉum irācamātēviyuṭaṉ oru maṇṭapattil mikka ciṟappuṭaṉ vīṟṟirukkum vēntaṉuṭaiya cevviyai nōkkic cēymaiyil vaṇaṅki niṉṟu, "māvaṇkiḷḷi ūḻitō ṟūḻi oḷiyoṭu vāḻi" eṉṟu vāḻtti, "yāṉaittī yeṉṉum nōyāl varunti uṭal melintu ip pakutiyilē tiriyum maṭantai yorutti ciṟaikkōṭṭattuḷḷē vantu niṉṉai vāḻttik kaiyiṉkaṇ piccaip pāttiram oṉṟē koṇṭu aṅku vantu moykkiṉṟa yāvarukkum uṇavu curantūṭṭu kiṉṟaṉaḷ; iv vaticayattait terivikkavē vantōm" eṉṟār. ataṉaik kēṭṭa aracaṉ, "am maṅkaiyai iṅkē aḻaittu varuka" eṉṟaṉaṉ. uṭaṉē kāvalāḷar vantu ataṉait terivikka, avaḷ ceṉṟu aracaṉaik kaṇṭu vāḻtti niṉṟaṉaḷ. aracaṉ, "ariya tavamuṭaiyāy ; nī yār ? kaiyilēntiya pāttiram eṅkē kiṭaittatu?" eṉṉa, avaḷ, "aracē! neṭuṅ kālam vāḻvāyāka; yāṉ viñcai makaḷ; ip pakutiyilē vēṟṟuruk koṇṭu tirintēṉ; itu piccaip pāttiram; itaṉai ampalattēyuḷḷa teyvamoṉṟu eṉakku aruḷiyatu ; itu teyvat taṉmaiyuṭaiyatu; yāṉaittī yeṉṉum tīrāp pacinōyait tīrttatu; paciyāl melintavarkaḷukku uyir maruntākavuḷḷatu" eṉṟu kūṟiṉaḷ. piṉ, aracaṉ, "yāṉ ceyya vēṇṭuvatu yātu?" eṉṟu kēṭpa, avaḷ, "ciṟaikkōṭṭattai yaḻittu aṟavōr vāḻuṅ kōṭṭamākac ceytal vēṇṭum" eṉṟāḷ. aracaṉ, avaḷ virumpiya vaṇṇamē ceyvittaṉaṉ. (itaṉkaṇ aracaṉatu poḻil viḷaiyāṭṭuk kūṟumiṭattuḷḷa iyaṟkaippuṉaivu mutaliyaṉa mikka iṉpam payappaṉa.
		mutiyāḷ tiruntaṭi mummaiyiṉ vaṇaṅki
matumalart tārōṉ vañciṉaṅ kūṟa
ēṭaviḻ tārōy eṅkō makaḷmuṉ
nāṭātu tuṇintunā nalkūrn taṉaiyeṉa.
5		vittakar iyaṟṟiya viḷaṅkiya kaiviṉaic
cittira moṉṟu teyvaṅ kūṟalum
utaya kumaraṉ uḷḷaṅ kalaṅkip
potiyaṟaip paṭṭōr pōṉṟumey varunti
aṅkavaḷ taṉṟiṟam ayarppā yeṉṟē
10    		ceṅkōl kāṭṭiya teyvamun tippiyam
paiyara valkul palarpaci kaḷaiyak
kaiyi lēntiya pāttiram tippiyam
muttai mutalvi aṭipiḻait tāyeṉac
cittira muraitta itūun tippiyam
15		innilai yellām iḷaṅkoṭi ceytiyiṉ
piṉṉaṟi vāmeṉap peyarvōṉ ṟaṉṉai
akalvāy ñālam āriru ḷuṇṇap
pakalara cōṭṭip paṇaiyeḻun tārppa
mālai neṟṟi vāṉpiṟaik kōṭṭu
20		nīla yāṉai mēlōr iṉṟik
kāmar ceṅkai nīṭṭi vaṇṭupaṭu
pūnāṟu kaṭāañ cerukkik kālkiḷarntu
niṟaiyaḻi tōṟṟamoṭu toṭara muṟaimaiyiṉ
nakara nampiyar vaḷaiyōr tammuṭaṉ
25		makara vīṇaiyiṉ kiḷainarampu vaṭitta
iḷipuṇa riṉcīr eḵkuḷaṅ kiḻippap
poṟāa neñciṟ pukaiyeri pottip
paṟāak kurukiṉ uyirttavaṉ pōyapiṉ
uṟaiyuṭ kuṭikai uḷvarik koṇṭa
30		maṟuvil ceykai maṇimē kalaitāṉ
mātavi makaḷāy maṉṟam tiritariṉ
kāvalaṉ makaṉō kaiviṭa līyāṉ
kāypaci yāṭṭi kāyacaṇ ṭikaiyeṉa
ūrmuḻu taṟiyum uruvaṅ koṇṭē
35		āṟṟā mākkaṭ kāṟṟun tuṇaiyāki
ēṟṟalum iṭutalum irappōr kaṭaṉavar
mēṟceṉ ṟaḷittal viḻuttakait teṉṟē
nūṟporuḷ uṇarntōr nuṉittaṉa rāmeṉa
mutiyāḷ kōṭṭat takavaiyiṉ irunta
40		amuta curapiyai aṅkaiyiṉ vāṅkip
patiyakan tiritarum paintoṭi naṅkai
atirkaḻal vēntaṉ aṭipiḻait tārai
oṟukkun taṇṭat tuṟuciṟaik kōṭṭam
viruppoṭum pukuntu veytuyirttup pulampi
45		āṅkup paciyuṟum āruyir mākkaḷai
vāṅku kaiyakam varuntaniṉ ṟūṭṭalum
ūṭṭiya pāttiram oṉṟeṉa viyantu
kōṭṭaṅ kāvalar kōmakaṉ ṟaṉakkip
pāttira tāṉamum paintoṭi ceytiyum
50		yāppuṭait tāka icaittumeṉ ṟēki
neṭiyōṉ kuṟaḷuru vāki nimirntutaṉ
aṭiyiṟ paṭiyai yaṭakkiya annāḷ
nīriṟ peyta mūri vārcilai
māvali marumāṉ cīrkeḻu tirumakaḷ
55		cīrtti yeṉṉun tiruttaku tēviyoṭu
pōtaviḻ pūmpoḻil pukuntaṉaṉ pukkuk
kompart tumpi kuḻalicai kāṭṭap
poṅkar vaṇṭiṉam nalliyāḻ ceyya
varikkuyil pāṭa māmayil āṭum
60		viraippūm pantar kaṇṭuḷañ ciṟantum
puṇartuṇai nīṅkiya poykai aṉṉamoṭu
maṭamayiṟ pēṭaiyun tōkaiyuṅ kūṭi 
iruciṟai virittāṅ keḻuntuṭaṉ koṭpaṉa
oruciṟaik kaṇṭāṅ kuṇmakiḻ veyti
65		māmaṇi vaṇṇaṉun tammuṉum piññaiyum
āṭiya kuravaiyiḵ tāmeṉa nōkkiyum
kōṅkalar cērnta māṅkaṉi taṉṉaip
pāṅkuṟa virunta palpoṟi maññaiyaic
cempoṟ ṟaṭṭil tīmpā lēntip
70		paiṅkiḷi yūṭṭumōr pāvaiyā meṉṟum
aṇimalarp pūmpoḻil akavaiyi ṉirunta
piṇavukkuraṅ kēṟṟip perumatar maḻaikkaṇ
maṭavōrk kiyaṟṟiya māmaṇi yūcal
kaṭuvaṉūk kuvatu kaṇṭunakai eytiyum
75		pācilai ceṟinta pacuṅkāṟ kaḻaiyoṭu
vālvīc ceṟinta marāaṅ kaṇṭu
neṭiyōṉ muṉṉoṭu niṉṟaṉa ṉāmeṉat
toṭicēr ceṅkaiyiṟ ṟoḻutuniṉ ṟēttiyum
āṭaṟ kūttiṉō ṭavinayan terivōr
80		nāṭakak kāppiya naṉṉūl nuṉippōr
paṇṇiyāḻ narampiṟ paṇṇumuṟai niṟuppōr
taṇṇumaik karuvik kaṇṇeṟi terivōr
kuḻaloṭu kaṇṭaṅ koḷaccīr niṟuppōr
85		āram parinta muttaṅ kōppōr
īram pularnta cāntan timirvōr
kuṅkuma varuṇaṅ koṅkaiyiṉ iḻaippōr
añceṅ kaḻunīr āyitaḻ piṇippōr
naṉṉeṭuṅ kūntal naṟuvirai kuṭaivōr
90		poṉṉiṉ āṭiyiṟ poruntupu niṟpōr
āṅkavar tammō ṭakaliru vāṉattu
vēntaṉiṟ ceṉṟu viḷaiyāṭ ṭayarntu
kuruntun taḷavun tiruntumalarc ceruntiyum
murukuviri mullaiyuṅ karuviḷam poṅkarum
95		poruntupu niṉṟu tiruntunakai ceytu
kuṟuṅkāl nakulamum neṭuñcevi muyalum
piṟaḻntupāy māṉum iṟumpakalā veṟiyum
vammeṉak kūuy makiḻtuṇai yoṭutaṉ
cemmalarc ceṅkai kāṭṭupu niṉṟu
100		maṉṉavaṉ ṟāṉum malarkkaṇai maintaṉum
iṉṉiḷa vēṉilum iḷaṅkāṟ celvaṉum
entirak kiṇaṟum iṭuṅkaṟ kuṉṟamum
vantuvī ḻaruviyum malarppūm pantarum
parappunīrp poykaiyuṅ karappunīrk kēṇiyum
105		oḷittuṟai yiṭaṅkaḷum paḷikkaṟaip paḷḷiyum
yāṅkaṇun tirintu tāḻntuviḷai yāṭi
makata viṉaiñarum marāṭṭak kammarum
avantik kollarum yavaṉat taccarum
taṇṭamiḻ viṉaiñar tammoṭu kūṭik
110		koṇṭiṉi tiyaṟṟiya kaṇkavar ceyviṉaip
pavaḷat tiraḷkāṟ paṉmaṇip pōtikait
tavaḷa nittilat tāman tāḻnta
kōṇac canti māṇviṉai vitāṉattut
tamaṉiyam vēynta vakaipeṟu vaṉappiṟ
115		paiñcēṟu meḻukāp pacumpoṉ maṇṭapattu
intira tiruvaṉ ceṉṟiṉi tēṟalum
vāyiluk kicaittu maṉṉavaṉ aruḷāl
cēynilat taṉṟiyuñ cevvitiṉ vaṇaṅki
eñcā maṇṇacaii ikaluḷan turappa
120		vañciyi ṉiruntu vañci cūṭi
muṟañcevi yāṉaiyun tērum māvum
maṟaṅkeḻu neṭuvāḷ vayavaru miṭainta
talaittārc cēṉaiyoṭu malaittuttalai vantōr
cilaikkayal neṭuṅkoṭi ceruvēṟ ṟaṭakkai
125		ārpuṉai teriyal iḷaṅkōṉ ṟaṉṉāl
kāriyāṟṟuk koṇṭa kāval veṇkuṭai
valikeḻu taṭakkai māvaṇ kiḷḷi
oḷiyoṭu vāḻi ūḻitō ṟūḻi
vāḻi yeṅkō maṉṉavar peruntakai
130		kēḷitu maṉṉō keṭukaniṉ pakaiñar
yāṉait tīnōyk kayarntumey vāṭiyim 
mānakart tiriyumōr vampa mātar
aruñciṟaik kōṭṭat takavayiṟ pukuntu
perumpeyar maṉṉa niṉpeyar vāḻtti
135		aiyap pāttiram oṉṟukoṇ ṭāṅku
moykoṇ mākkaṇ mocikka'd2vūṇ curantaṉaḷ
ūḻitō ṟūḻi ulakaṅ kāttu
vāḻi yeṅkō maṉṉava eṉṟalum
varuka varuka maṭakkoṭi tāṉeṉṟu
140		aruḷpuri neñcamo ṭaracaṉ kūṟaliṉ
vāyi lāḷariṉ maṭakkoṭi tāṉceṉṟu
āykaḻal vēntaṉ aruḷvā ḻiyaveṉat
tāṅkarun taṉmait tavattōy nīyār	
yāṅkā kiyativ vēntiya kaṭiñaiyeṉṟu
145		aracaṉ kūṟalum āyiḻai uraikkum
viraittār vēntē nīnīṭu vāḻi
viñcai makaḷyāṉ viḻavaṇi mūtūr
vañcan tirintēṉ vāḻiya peruntakai
vāṉam vāykka maṇvaḷam perukuka
150		tītiṉ ṟāka kōmakaṟ kīṅkītu
aiyak kaṭiñai ampala maruṅkōr
teyvan tantatu tippiya māyatu
yāṉait tīnōy arumpaci keṭuttatu
ūṉuṭai mākkaṭ kuyirmarun tituveṉa
155		yāṉceyaṟ pālateṉ iḷaṅkoṭik keṉṟu
vēntaṉ kūṟa melliyal uraikkum
ciṟaiyōr kōṭṭañ cīttaruḷ neñcattu
aṟavōrk kākkum atuvā ḻiyareṉa
aruñciṟai viṭṭāṅ kāyiḻai uraitta
160		peruntavar tammāṟ perumporu ḷeytak
kaṟaiyō rillāc ciṟaiyōr kōṭṭam
aṟavōrk kākkiṉaṉ aracāḷ vēnteṉ 
urai 
1-6.  		mutiyāḷ tiruntaṭi mummaiyiṉ vaṇaṅki - campāpatiyiṉ tiruntiya aṭikaḷai mummuṟai vaṇakkañceytu, matumalart tārōṉ vañciṉam kūṟa - tēṉ poruntiya malarmālai kaḷaiyuṭaiya utayakumaraṉ vañciṉaṅ kūṟutalum, ēṭu aviḻ tārōy eṅkōmakaḷ muṉ nāṭātu tuṇintu nā nal kūrntaṉai eṉa - itaḻvirikiṉṟa mālaiyai yuṭaiyavaṉē em talaimakaḷ muṉṉar ārāyātu tuṇivuṭaṉ cūḷuṟavu ceytu nā vaṟumai yuṟṟaṉai eṉṟu, vittakar iyaṟṟiya viḷaṅkiya kaiviṉaic cittiram oṉṟu teyvam kūṟalum-kaival ciṟparāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭu viḷaṅkiya toḻil amainta cittiram oṉṟiṟ poruntiya teyvam uraittalum ;
mummaiyiṉ - uḷam urai uṭal eṉṉum muk karaṇaṅkaḷāl eṉṟumām ; muṟaimaiyiṉ eṉpatum pāṭam. nā nalkūrtalāvatu payaṉila moḻital. vittakar-ciṟpam vallōr; "vittaka riyaṟṟiya viḷaṅkiya kaiviṉaic, cittirac ceykai" (3: 167-8) eṉa muṉṉar vantamaiyuṅ kāṇka. oṉṟu-poruntiya; atiṭṭitta.
7-16. 	utayakumaraṉ uḷḷam kalaṅki - utayakumaraṉ maṉak kalakka muṟṟu, potiyaṟaip paṭṭōrpōṉṟu mey varunti - cāḷaramillāta kīḻaṟaiyil akappaṭṭōraip pōla uṭal varunti, aṅkavaḷ taṉtiṟam ayarppāy eṉṟē - maṇimēkalaimītu koṇṭa eṇṇattai maṟappāy eṉṟē, ceṅkōlkāṭṭiya teyvamum tippiyam - ceṅkōṉ muṟaiyai eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya teyvaṅ kūṟiyatum viyappuṭaittu, paiyara valkul palar pacikaḷaiyak kaiyilēntiya pāttiram tippiyam - maṇimēkalai palaruṭaiya paciyaiyum nīkkutaṟkuk kaiyil ēntiya kaṭiñaiyum viyappuṭaittu, muttai mutalvi aṭipiḻaittāy eṉac cittiram uraitta itūum tippiyam-em talaiviyiṉ tiruvaṭiyil piḻai purintāy eṉṟu iv vōviyaṅ kūṟiya ituvum viyappuṭaittu, innilai ellām iḷaṅkoṭi ceytiyiṉ piṉ aṟivām eṉap peyarvōṉ taṉṉai - ivaṟṟiṉ uṇmaiyai yellām maṇimēkalaiyiṉ ceyalāl piṉpu aṟivōmeṉa eṇṇi aṅkuniṉṟum mīṇṭa utayakumaraṉai ;
potiyaṟai - puḻukkaṟai: ciṟu tuvāramumiṉṟi mūṭappaṭṭa kīḻaṟai; 1"pōtār piṟavip potiyaṟaiyōr" eṉpatu kāṇka. teyvam-maṇimēkalā teyvam. teyvam kūṟiyatumeṉka. tippiyam- īṇṭu viyappeṉṉum poruṭṭu; teyvattaṉmaiyumām. araviṉ pai yeṉa māṟuka. pai-paṭam. pāttiramum eṉa eṇṇummai virikka muttai : vikāram.
17-23. 	akalvāy ñālam āriruḷ uṇṇa-iṭamakaṉṟa pūmiyai niṟainta iruḷāṉatu uṇṇumāṟu, pakal aracu ōṭṭi - katiravaṉākiya aracaṉai ōṭṭi, paṇaieḻuntu ārppa - paṟaiyeḻuntu olikka, mālai neṟṟi vāṉpiṟaik kōṭṭu- antik kālamākiya neṟṟiyum vāṉiluḷḷa piṟaiyākiya maruppum uṭaiya, nīlayāṉai- kariya niṟamuṭaiya iravākiya yāṉai, mēlōr iṉṟi - pākar oruvarum iṉṟi, kāmar ceṅkainīṭṭi-viruppamākiya tutikkaiyai nīṭṭi, vaṇṭupaṭu pū nāṟu kaṭāam cerukki - vaṇṭu moykkum pūviṉ maṇam poruntiya matanīraic corintu kaḷittu, kāl kiḷarntu niṟaiyaḻi tōṟṟamoṭu toṭara - kāṟṟaip pōla eḻuntu kāvalaik kaṭanta tōṟṟattuṭaṉ toṭarntu vara ;
muṉṉuḷḷa vicēṭaṇaṅkaḷāl ‘nīlayāṉai' eṉṟatu iravāyiṟṟu. irāttiriyai yāṉaiyāka uruvakippavar ataṟ kiyaipupaṭap piṟavaṟṟaiyum uruvakañ ceykiṉṟār. "ñālam āriruḷ uṇṇa' eṉpatu 1"uṇṭaṟkuriya vallāp poruḷai, yuṇṭaṉa pōlak kūṟalumarapē" eṉpataṉāṉ amaikkappaṭum. kolkaḷiṟṟiṉ celkaiyai aṟivittaṟkup paṟaiyaṟaivit taluṇmaiyiṉ 'paṇaiyeḻuntārppa' eṉṟār. 2"verūup paṟai nuvalum parūup perun taṭakkai, veruvaru celaviṉ vekuḷi vēḻam" eṉpatu kāṇka; veṉṟi muracuṅ koḷka. mēlōriṉṟi eṉṟatu pākarai vīci yeṉṟapaṭi; 3"mēlōr vīci" eṉṟār piṟa cāṉṟōrum, kāmar - kāmam eṉṉum poruṭṭu. kaṭakkaḷiṟu kaivīcic cellumākaliṉ ' kāmar ceṅkai nīṭṭi' eṉṟār. pū - vēṅkaippū ; ēḻilaip pālaiyiṉ malarumām; yāṉaiyiṉ matam. appūkkaḷiṉ maṇamuṭaiyatātalai, 4"vilakkaruṅ karimatam vēṅkai nāṟuva" 5"pātta yāṉaiyiṟ pataṅkaḷiṟ paṭumata nāṟak, kātta vaṅkuca nimirntiṭak kālpiṭit tōṭip, pūtta vēḻilaip pālaiyaip poṭip poṭi yākak, kātti raṅkaḷāṟ ṟalat toṭun tēytta tōr kaḷiṟu" eṉpavaṟṟāṉaṟika. 6"kālkiḷarn taṉṉa vēḻamēl koṇṭu" eṉpataṉāl yāṉai kāṟṟuppōṟ kiḷarnteḻu meṉpataṟika.
peyarvōṉṟaṉṉai nīlayāṉai toṭara veṉka : iravu vara eṉṟavāṟāyiṟṟu.
23-28. 	muṟaimaiyiṉ nakara nampiyar vaḷaiyōr tammuṭaṉ - naka rattiluḷḷa kātalañcelvar makaḷir tammōṭu muṟaimai yāka, makara vīṇaiyiṉ kiḷai narampu vaṭitta iḷipuṇar iṉcīr eḵku uḷam kiḻippa - makarayāḻiṉ kiḷainarampu kaḷait teṟitta icaiyuṭaṉ cērnta iṉiya tāḷa voṟṟākiya vēl uḷattaikkiḻippa, poṟāa neñcil pukaieri potti-poṟukkalāṟṟāta uḷḷattiṉkaṇ pukaikiṉṟa kāmattīyāṉatu mūṭṭappaṭṭu, paṟāak kurukiṉ uyirttu avaṉ pōyapiṉ-kollaṉ ulai mūkkiṉaip pōla veytuyirttu iḷaṅkō ceṉṟapiṉṉar; 
vaḷaiyōr-vaḷaiyaṇinta makaḷir. nampiyarum vaḷaiyōrum vaṭitta iḷipuṇar iṉcīr eṉka. makara vīṇai - makarayāḻ; nālvakai yāḻuḷoṉṟu; patiṉēḻu narampukaḷaiyuṭaiyatu. kiḷai - aintām narampu; uḻai caṭcamākaliṉ, caṭcakkiramattil ataṟkaintāva tākiya kural kiḷaiyākum; aṅṅaṉamē iḷi, tuttam, viḷari, kaikkiḷai eṉpaṉa kural mutal oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu kiḷaiyākum; ākaliṉ ivvaintaṉaiyumē kiḷainaram peṉṟalumuṇṭu; "kiḷaiyeṉap paṭuva kiḷakkuṅ kālaik, kuralē yiḷiyē tuttam viḷari, kaikkiḷai yeṉavain tāku meṉpa" eṉṉum paḻaiya nūṟpāvuṅ kāṇka. vaṭittal - narampai uruvutal; teṟittaluk kāyiṟṟu. iḷiyeṉpatu ēḻunarampiṉuḷ oṉṟaṉ peyarātalaṉṟi, icaiyaik kuṟikkum potup peyarumām, cīruṭaṉkūṭiya iḷiyākiya eḵku eṉka. paṟāak kuruku - kollaṉ ulaimūkku; pūvā vañci mutaliyaṉa pōla veḷippaṭaiyeṉṉum
ilakkaṇattatu. avaṉ - utayakumaraṉ. 
29-35. 	uṟaiyuṭ kuṭikai uḷvarik koṇṭa - uṟaiviṭa
mākiya ciṟiya kōyiliṉkaṇ vēṟṟuruva meytiya, maṟuvil ceykai maṇimēkalaitāṉ - kuṟṟamaṟṟa ceykaiyaiyuṭaiya maṇimēkalai, mātavi makaḷāy maṉṟam tiritariṉ-mātaviyiṉ makaḷākavē ulaka aṟaviyiṟ cuṟṟikkoṇṭiruntāl, kāvalaṉ makaṉō kaiviṭalī yāṉ - maṉṉavaṉ putalvaṉ kaiviṭāṉ eṉa niṉaintu, kāypaci yāṭṭi kāyacaṇṭikai eṉa ūr muḻutaṟiyum uruvaṅkoṇṭē - ūriluḷḷōr aṉaivarālum naṉkaṟiyappaṭṭa yāṉaittī nōyiṉaiyuṭaiya kāyacaṇṭikaiyaip pōla uruvaṅkoṇṭu, āṟṟā mākkaṭku āṟṟum tuṇaiyāki-vaṟiyōrkku utavipuriyum aruntuṇaiyāki ; 
uḷvari - vēṟṟuru; poyvēṭam. viṭalīyāṉ: viṉaittiri col. kaiviṭēṉeṉa niṉaintu eṉa oru col varuvitturaikka.
36-41.	ēṟṟalum iṭutalum irappōr kaṭaṉ - vāṅkutalum koṭuttalum irappōr kaṭaṉām ākaliṉ, avar mēṟ ceṉṟu aḷittal viḻuttakaittu eṉṟē - avar ev viṭattum valintu ceṉṟu koṭuttal ciṟappuṭaiyatu eṉṟu, nūṟporuḷ uṇarntōr nuṉittaṉarāmeṉa-nūṟporuḷkaḷai aṟintōr ārāynturaittaṉar eṉṟu karuti, mutiyāḷ kōṭṭattu akavayiṉ irunta - campāpati kōyiliṉ uḷḷē irunta, amutacurapiyai aṅkaiyiṉ vāṅki - amutacurapiyai aḻakiya kaiyiṟkoṇṭu, patiyakam tiritarum paintoṭi naṅkai - nakarattiṉkaṇṇē cuṟṟukiṉṟa paciya vaḷaiyalkaḷai yaṇinta maṇimēkalai; 
mēṟceṉṟu - valitiṟ ceṉṟu. nuṉittal - nuṇṇitiṉ uṇartal. eṉa - eṉṟu karuti. paintoṭi maṭantai eṉpatum pāṭam.
42-46.	atirkaḻal vēntaṉ aṭipiḻaittārai - olikkiṉṟa vīrak kaḻalaiyuṭaiya maṉṉavaṉ aṭikkup piḻai ceytārai, oṟukkum taṇṭattu uṟuciṟaik kōṭṭam - taṇṭikkum taṇṭattiṉaiyuṭaiya ciṟaic cālaiyiṉkaṇ, viruppoṭum pukuntu - viruppattuṭaṉ ceṉṟu, veytuyirttup pulampi - veytāka uyirttu varunti, āṅkup paciyuṟum āruyir mākkaḷai-avviṭattilē paciyuṟṟirukkum ariya uyirai yuṭaiya makkaḷai, vāṅku kaiyakam varunta niṉṟu ūṭṭalum- vāṅkukiṉṟa kaiyiṉiṭam varuntumāṟu niṉṟu uṇpittalum;
iṟaivaṉukkum periyārkkum piḻaiceytārait tiruvaṭi piḻaittāreṉṟu kūṟutal marapu. taṇṭattu uṟu - taṇṭat tiṉaip porunti nukarum. paciyuṟṟu veytuyirttup pulampum mākkaḷeṉa māṟuka. āruyir mākkaḷ eṉṟatu, peṟutaṟkariya makkaḷuyiraip peṟṟār iṅṅaṉam varuntukiṉṟaṉarē yeṉa iraṅkik kūṟiyapaṭi. īṇṭuk kūṟiyavāṟu "vāṅkunar kaiyakam varuttuta lallatu" (11 : 49) "vāṅkukai varunta maṉṉuyi rōmpaliṉ" (14 : 23) "vāṅkukai varunta maṉṉuyirk kaḷittu" (17 : 5) eṉa muṉṉarp pōntamaiyuṅ kāṇka.
47-50.	ūṭṭiya pāttiram oṉṟeṉa viyantu-iṅṅaṉam palaraiyum uṇpitta pāttiram oṉṟē eṉa viyappuṟṟu, kōṭṭaṅ kāvalar - ciṟaikkōṭṭaṅ kāppavar, kōmakaṉ taṉakku - aracaṉukku, ip pāttira tāṉamum paintoṭi ceytiyum - ip pāttirattiṉ tāṉac ciṟappaiyum itaṉaik koṇṭa iḷaṅkoṭiyiṉ ceyalaiyum, yāppuṭaittāka icaittum eṉṟu ēki - uṟuti yuṭaittākak kūṟuvōm eṉac ceṉṟu ;
kōṭṭaṅ kāvalar viyantu tāṉamum ceytiyum kōmakaṉukku icaittumeṉ ṟēki yeṉka,
51-56.	neṭiyōṉ kuṟaḷ uruvāki nimirntu taṉ aṭiyiṟ paṭiyai aṭakkiya-tirumāl vāmaṉa vaṭivaṅkoṇṭu piṉ pēruruvamāyp pūmiyait taṉ tiruvaṭiyiṉkaṇ aṭakka, annāḷ-aṟṟaināḷil, nīriṟpeyta - pūmiyai nīruṭaṉ aḷitta, mūri vār cilai māvali marumāṉ cīrkeḻu tirumakaḷ-valiya periya villaiyuṭaiya māvaliyiṉ vaḻittōṉṟalākiya aracaṉuṭaiya pukaḻmikka tirumakaḷākiya, cīrtti eṉṉum tiruttaku tēviyoṭu - cīrtti eṉṉum peyaruṭaiya celvamikka tēviyuṭaṉ, pōtu aviḻpūmpoḻil pukuntaṉaṉpukku - pūkkaḷ virinta aḻakiya cōlaiyiṟ ceṉṟu pukuntu ;  
kuṟaḷ - kuṟiya vaṭivamuṭaiya vāmaṉaṉ ; kācipaṉukkum atitikkum piṟantavaṉ ; tirumāliṉ aintāvatu tōṟṟamāyavaṉ; ivaṉ māpaliyiṭattu mūṉṟaṭimaṇ irantu peṟṟu, puviyaiyum vāṉaiyum īraṭiyāl aḷantu, mūṉṟāmaṭiyaip paliyiṉ talaiyil vaittu avaṉaip pātalattil iruttiṉaṉ eṉpatu varalāṟu. aṭakkiya-aṭakka: ceyyiya veṉṉum viṉaiyeccam; peyareccamākkōṭalumām. nīriṟ peyta-nīruṭaṉ peyta; tārai vārttaḷitta eṉṟapaṭi. māvali: māpaliyeṉpataṉ tiripu. pāṇa kulattaracarkaḷ māvaliyiṉ vaḻiyiṉareṉak kūṟappaṭuvar. iṉṉōr vāṇakappāṭi mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu aracupurin tōrāvar. ak kulattaracaruḷoruvaṉ makaḷeṉka.
57-60. 	kompart tumpi kuḻalicai kāṭṭa - kompu kaḷiluḷḷa tumpikaḷ vēyṅkuḻa lōcaiyaik kāṭṭa, poṅkar vaṇṭiṉam nalliyāḻceyya - cōlaiyiluḷḷa vaṇṭiṉaṅkaḷ nalla yāḻiṉi caiyai murala, varikkuyil pāṭa māmayil āṭum - varikaḷaiyuṭaiya kuyilkaḷ pāṭa aḻakiya mayilkaḷ āṭukiṉṟa, viraippūm pantar kaṇṭu uḷam ciṟantum-maṇam poruntiya malarppantaraik kaṇṭu maṉamakiḻcci mikkum;
vari - kīṟṟu; varippāṭṭumām. pantaril āṭutalaik kaṇṭu eṉa māṟutalumām. ‘kuḻalicai tumpi koḷuttik kāṭṭa, maḻalai vaṇṭiṉam nalliyāḻ ceyya, veyiṉuḻai paṟiyāk kuyiṉuḻai potumpiṉ, mayilā ṭaraṅkiṉ' (4 : 3-6) eṉa muṉṉar vantiruttal aṟiyaṟ pālatu.
61-66.	puṇartuṇai nīṅkiya poykai aṉṉamoṭu - taṉ tuṇaiyākiya pēṭṭiṉaip pirinta kuḷattil vāḻum aṉṉappuḷḷuṭaṉ, maṭamayiṟ pēṭaiyum tōkaiyum kūṭi - maṭappam poruntiya mayiṟpēṭaiyum āṇ mayilum cērntu, iruciṟai virittāṅku. eḻuntuṭaṉ koṭpaṉa-iraṇṭu ciṟakukaḷaiyum virittu oruṅkē eḻuntu cuḻalvaṉavaṟṟai, oru ciṟaik kaṇṭāṅku uḷmakiḻvu eyti - āṇṭu oru pakkattiṟ kaṇṭu maṉak kaḷippuṟṟu, mā maṇi vaṇṇaṉum tam muṉum piññaiyum - nīla maṇipōṉṟa niṟamuṭaiya kaṇṇaṉum avaṉ muṉṉavaṉākiya palatēvaṉum nappiṉṉaiyum, āṭiya kuravai iḵtāmeṉa nōkkiyum - tuvarā patiyil erumaṉṟattē āṭiya kuravaiyākum iḵtu eṉṟu nōkki viyantum;
veṇṇiṟa muṭaimaiyāl āṇ aṉṉattiṟkup pala tēvaṉum, karuniṟamuṭaimaiyāl āṇ mayilukkum peṇ mayilukkum muṟaiyē kaṇṇaṉum nappiṉṉaiyum uvamaikaḷ. kuravai - viṉōtak kūttu  āṟiṉuḷ oṉṟu; kāmamum veṉṟiyum poruḷākak kuravaic ceyyuḷ pāṭṭāka eḻuvarēṉum eṇmarēṉum oṉpatiṉmarēṉum kaipiṇaintāṭuvatu. kaṇṇaṉ mutaliyōr kuravaiyāṭiyataṉai,
1"	māyavaṉṟam muṉṉiṉoṭum varivaḷaikkaip piṉṉaiyoṭum 
kōvalartañ ciṟumiyarkaḷ kuḻaṟkōtai puṟañcōra 
āyvaḷaiccīrk kaṭipeyarttiṭ ṭacōtaiyār toḻutēttat 
tāterumaṉ ṟattāṭuṅ kuravaiyō takavuṭaittē."
eṉpataṉāṉaṟika.
67-70. 	kōṅkalar cērnta māṅkaṉi taṉṉaip pāṅkuṟa irunta palpoṟi maññaiyai - kōṅkam pūviṉuṭaṉ cērntirunta māṅkaṉiyiṉ pakkattil irunta pala poṟikaḷaiyuṭaiya mayilai, cempoṉ taṭṭil tīmpāl ēnti-civanta poṟṟaṭṭil iṉiya pālai ēntik koṇṭu, paiṅkiḷi ūṭṭum ōr pāvaiyām eṉṟum - paciya kiḷiyai uṇpikkum oru aḻakiya peṇṇākum eṉṟum;
kōṅkalarkkup pālaiyuṭaiya poṟṟaṭṭum, māṅkaṉikkuk kiḷiyum, mayilukkup peṇṇum uvamaikaḷ. māṅkaṉikkuk kiḷi vaṭivum vaṇṇamum paṟṟi uvamaiyākum ; 1"vaṇṭaḷir mā attuk, kiḷi pōl kāya kiḷaittuṇar" eṉpatuṅ kāṇka. maṟṟum, māvaṭuviṟkuk kiḷi uvamaiyātalum, makaṭūu orutti poṉvaḷḷattilē pālaiyēntik kiḷiyai uṇpippatumākiya karuttukkaḷ 2"cēṭiyal vaḷḷattup peytapāl cila kāṭṭi, ūṭumeṉ ciṟukiḷi yuṇarppavaḷ mukampōla,...kaṭikayat tāmaraik kamaḻmukai karaimāviṉ, vaṭitīṇṭa vāyviṭūum vayalaṇi nallūra" eṉṉuñ cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷil amain tiruttal kāṇka.
71-74. 	aṇimalarp pūmpoḻil akavayiṉ irunta - aḻakiya malarkaḷaiyuṭaiya pūñcōlaiyil uḷḷiṭattirunta, piṇavuk kuraṅku ēṟṟip perumatar maḻaikkaṇ maṭavōrkku iyaṟṟiya māmaṇi ūcal - matartta nōkkamuṭaiya maḻai pōlum periya kaṇkaḷaiyuṭaiya makaḷirukkuc ceyyappaṭṭa aḻakiya maṇikaḷāṉākiya ūcalil peṇ kuraṅkiṉai ēṟṟi, kaṭuvaṉ ūkkuvatu kaṇṭu nakai eytiyum - āṇ kuraṅku āṭṭuvatu kaṇṭu makiḻcci yaṭaintum;
piṇavuk kuraṅku - peṇ kuraṅku; 3"paṉṟi pulvāy nāyeṉa mūṉṟum, oṉṟiya veṉpa piṇaviṉ peyark koṭai" eṉṉuñ cūttirattu, 'oṉṟiya' eṉṟataṉāl, piṇavu eṉpatu kuraṅkukkum koḷḷappaṭṭatu. kaṭuvaṉ ūcalil piṇavuk kuraṅkai yēṟṟi ūkkuvatu kaṇṭu nakaiyeytiyum eṉka. 
75-78.	pācilai ceṟinta pacuṅkāṟ kaḻaiyoṭu - paciya ilaikaḷ neruṅkiya paciya taṇṭiṉaiyuṭaiya mūṅkiluṭaṉ, vāl vīceṟinta marāam kaṇṭu-veḷḷiya pūkkaḷ neruṅkiya veṇkaṭampiṉaik kaṇṇuṟṟu, neṭiyōṉ muṉṉoṭu niṉṟaṉaṉ āmeṉa - kaṇṇaṉ taṉ muṉṉōṉoṭu niṟ kiṉṟaṉaṉām eṉṟu, toṭicēr ceṅkaiyil toḻutu niṉṟu ēttiyum-vīravaḷai yaṇinta civanta kaikaḷāṟ kumpiṭṭu niṉṟu tutittum ;
muṉ - tamaiyaṉ ; palatēvar. kaḻaikkuk kaṇṇaṉum veṇ kaṭampiṟkup palatēvarum uvamaikaḷ. veṇkaṭampiṟkup palatēvar uvamaiyātal 1"orukuḻai yoruvaṉpō liṇarcērnta marāamum" 2nāñcil valava ṉiṟampōlap pūñciṉaic, ceṅkāṉ marāan takaintaṉa" eṉpavaṟṟuḷḷum kāṇappaṭumāṟaṟika.
